# Changelog / 更新日志

Every notable change to this project, from all of us, in one place. If you want to know what
the project did before you joined a piece of it, read this file top to bottom.

Add an entry in the section that matches the area you changed, as a pair of lines: English
first, then 简体中文. Say what changed and why it mattered — a reader six weeks from now
cannot recover the reason from the diff. This is the only active team change record; the
existing [`work-packages/`](work-packages/) files are read-only historical archives. See
[`README.md`](README.md).

本项目所有重要变更的唯一汇总，来自我们每个人。想知道某块工作在你接手之前发生过什么，从头读这份
文件即可。

请在与改动领域对应的小节下追加条目，一条英文、一条简体中文成对出现。写清楚改了什么、以及为什么
重要——六周后的读者无法从 diff 里还原当时的理由。本文件是唯一仍在维护的团队变更记录；现有
[`work-packages/`](work-packages/) 文件仅作为只读历史档案保留。参见 [`README.md`](README.md)。

## [Unreleased] / [未发布]

### First monthly dependency updates: TypeScript held at 6.0, security lab kept on Python 3.12 / 第一批每月依赖更新：TypeScript 停在 6.0，安全实验室保持 Python 3.12

- The four grouped Dependabot pull requests (#244 to #247) are merged. Three needed a fix first. Ruff 0.17 adds RUF036 (`None` last in a type union), SQLAlchemy 2.1 deprecates `Row._tuple()` because a `Row` is now a tuple, and Alembic 1.20 renames `version_path_separator` to `path_separator`; the backend now follows all three. TypeScript 7 removes the entry point vue-tsc loads, so the typecheck could not run at all: TypeScript stays on `~6.0.3`, `tsconfig.json` drops the deprecated `baseUrl` and names `"types": ["node"]` (6.0 no longer loads it by default), and Dependabot skips TypeScript 7 until vue-tsc supports it. The security lab image was proposed on Python 3.14, where the pinned `pydantic-core` has no wheel and the slim image has no compiler; the lab should attack the backend on the Python CI and the server run anyway, so it moved to 3.12.15 instead and Dependabot now proposes only 3.12 patch releases for it. GitHub Actions moved to `checkout`, `setup-python` and `setup-node` v7, `cache` v6 and `upload-artifact` v7, and the CI runner image to 2.338.0, which takes effect when the runner is next rebuilt on the VPS.
- 四个按生态合并的 Dependabot PR（#244 到 #247）已合并，其中三个要先修。Ruff 0.17 新增 RUF036（类型联合里 `None` 放最后），SQLAlchemy 2.1 因为 `Row` 本身已经是 tuple 而弃用 `Row._tuple()`，Alembic 1.20 把 `version_path_separator` 改名为 `path_separator`，后端三处都已跟上。TypeScript 7 去掉了 vue-tsc 加载的入口，类型检查根本跑不起来：TypeScript 停在 `~6.0.3`，`tsconfig.json` 去掉已弃用的 `baseUrl` 并写明 `"types": ["node"]`（6.0 不再默认加载），Dependabot 在 vue-tsc 支持之前跳过 TypeScript 7。安全实验室镜像被提议升到 Python 3.14，但钉住的 `pydantic-core` 在 3.14 上没有 wheel，slim 镜像里又没有编译器；实验室本来就应该在 CI 和服务器用的 Python 上攻击后端，所以改为 3.12.15，Dependabot 以后只为它提 3.12 的补丁版本。GitHub Actions 升到 `checkout`、`setup-python`、`setup-node` v7，`cache` v6，`upload-artifact` v7；CI runner 镜像升到 2.338.0，要等下次在 VPS 上重建 runner 才生效。

### Team stickers centred, and every member's photo as large as the supervisor's / 团队贴纸居中，组员头像和导师一样大

- After #248 the member photos were in the small sticker, and on every tile the sticker, name and role sat in the left half of the left column with the right half empty, which looked odd. Every tile with a photo now uses the large sticker (8rem by 9.85rem), so the members' pictures are as clear as the supervisor's, and the sticker, name and role are centred in that column. Between 768 and 1023 px the member tiles sit two to a row and the left column (161 to 190 px) was too narrow for the large sticker, and some names and roles already broke onto two lines there, so in that range the member tiles stack like on a phone and names never wrap. Card widths, the right column, colours and fonts are unchanged. The four member tiles now run Shuoxun Wen, Junyan Li, Jason Huang, Tai Chen, as the group asked; the supervisor's tile stays first.
- #248 之后组员头像在小号贴纸里，而且每张卡的贴纸、名字和角色都挤在左栏的左半边，右半边空着，看着很怪。现在有照片的卡都用大号贴纸（8rem × 9.85rem），组员头像和导师的一样清楚，贴纸、名字和角色在左栏里居中。768 到 1023 px 之间组员卡一行两张，左栏只有 161 到 190 px，放不下大贴纸，有些名字和角色原本在这个宽度就会折成两行，所以这段宽度下组员卡改成和手机一样上下排列，名字也不再折行。卡片宽度、右栏、配色和字体都没变。四位组员的卡片顺序按组里的要求改成 Shuoxun Wen、Junyan Li、Jason Huang、Tai Chen，导师卡仍在最前面。

### Photos for the four team members on Contact us / Contact us 页加上四位组员的头像

- Only the supervisor's tile on Contact us had a picture, so the four student tiles showed initials and the page looked unfinished next to his portrait. Shuoxun Wen, Tai Chen, Junyan Li and Jason Huang now each have a square picture in `frontend/src/assets/people/` and served from the site's own origin like the supervisor's (the CSP blocks hot-linked images). The large sticker used to follow "has a photo", which would have made every member's sticker grow, so it now follows "is the supervisor" and the member stickers keep their size. In the enlarged profile the members' pictures are cropped from the middle, not the top, and on screens narrower than 768 px the box is square and shows the whole picture, because Tai Chen's picture is a logo with its name underneath and the short wide box on a phone cut that name in half. The browser test now checks that all five pictures load.
- Contact us 页原来只有导师的卡片有照片，四位组员只显示姓名缩写，放在导师照片旁边显得没做完。现在 Shuoxun Wen、Tai Chen、Junyan Li 和 Jason Huang 各有一张正方形头像，放在 `frontend/src/assets/people/`，和导师照片一样从网站自己的域名加载（CSP 会拦截外链图片）。大号贴纸原来按“有没有照片”判断，这样组员有图后贴纸都会变大，所以改成按“是不是导师”判断，组员贴纸尺寸不变。点开后的大图里组员头像从中间裁而不是从顶部裁；屏幕窄于 768 px 时图框变成正方形并显示整张图，因为 Tai Chen 的头像是下面带名字的 logo，手机上那个又矮又宽的框会把名字切掉一半。浏览器测试现在会检查五张图都能加载。

### Security policy, monthly dependency updates, and a deploy that checks the running commit / 安全策略、每月依赖更新，以及部署时核对正在运行的 commit

- There was no written way to report a vulnerability, nothing proposed dependency updates between the manual `pip-audit` and `npm audit` runs in CI (threat model F-12), and a deploy counted as successful whenever `/health/live` answered `ok`, even if the process answering was still the previous release. `SECURITY.md` now says to report privately to the team address or through a draft GitHub security advisory, what is in scope, and what not to do against `https://secureview.tech` (no other people's accounts, no destructive or load tests, no scanners without asking). `.github/dependabot.yml` opens at most one grouped pull request a month each for pip (`backend/`), npm (`frontend/`), GitHub Actions, and the two Dockerfiles. `/health/live` now also returns `commit`, taken from `APP_COMMIT` or, on the VPS, the `.mcs21-release` file `mcs21-deploy` already writes into each release; after the restart `mcs21-deploy` compares it with the SHA it deployed and restores the previous release if they differ, and `mcs21-deploy status` prints `live_commit`. The first deploy of this change already checks itself, because the new release is the one answering.
- 以前没有书面的漏洞报告途径；CI 里的 `pip-audit` 和 `npm audit` 之外没有任何东西会主动提出依赖更新（威胁模型 F-12）；而且只要 `/health/live` 回答 `ok` 部署就算成功，哪怕回答的进程仍是上一个 release。现在 `SECURITY.md` 说明了要私下报告给团队邮箱或通过 GitHub 草稿安全公告、哪些在范围内、对 `https://secureview.tech` 不能做什么（不用别人的账号、不做破坏性或压力测试、未经询问不跑扫描器）。`.github/dependabot.yml` 每月最多为 pip（`backend/`）、npm（`frontend/`）、GitHub Actions 和两个 Dockerfile 各开一个合并后的 PR。`/health/live` 现在还返回 `commit`，取自 `APP_COMMIT`，在 VPS 上则取自 `mcs21-deploy` 本来就写进每个 release 的 `.mcs21-release` 文件；重启后 `mcs21-deploy` 把它和刚部署的 SHA 比较，不一致就恢复上一个 release，`mcs21-deploy status` 也会打印 `live_commit`。这次改动的第一次部署就会检查自己，因为回答的正是新 release。

### Test counts refreshed to the 13 October CI run / 测试数量更新到 10 月 13 日的 CI

- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md`, its copy under `docs/test report/`, the test report README, the talking points and the "Verified" table in `TODO.md` quoted the CI run on `2ffc36c` (#222, 2026-10-09). Tests were added by #223 to #240 and the last run that executed them, on `1b1bcce` (#240, 2026-10-13), counts `backend` 718 passed (81.84 %), `mysql-gate` 786 passed with 0 skipped (86.64 %), `frontend` 899 passed and 78 skipped (77.36 % statements), `e2e-live` 977 passed with 0 skipped (79.72 % statements) and 57 browser tests. All of those records now quote that run, and the TODO table notes that a documentation-only push such as #241 skips the test jobs, so counts must be read from the newest run whose jobs actually ran. The final report uses the same figures.
- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md`、它在 `docs/test report/` 下的副本、测试报告 README、讲稿要点和 `TODO.md` 的“已核验”表引用的还是 `2ffc36c`（#222，2026-10-09）那次 CI。#223 到 #240 之间加了测试，最后一次真正跑测试的是 `1b1bcce`（#240，2026-10-13）：`backend` 718 通过（81.84%），`mysql-gate` 786 通过、0 跳过（86.64%），`frontend` 899 通过、78 跳过（语句 77.36%），`e2e-live` 977 通过、0 跳过（语句 79.72%），另有 57 个浏览器测试。这些记录现在都引用这次运行；TODO 表里注明了像 #241 这样只改文档的推送会跳过测试 Job，所以数字要从最近一次真正跑了测试的运行里读。期末报告用的是同一组数字。

- `docs/test report/` now holds copies of the testing summary, the end to end and performance testing pages, the 2026-10-09 load test report with its charts, server CSV and raw data, the 2026-10-12 ZAP scan, and the encryption and tamper evidence, with a README listing each file. They are copies so every existing link and CI keeps working; relative links inside the copies point back at the originals.

### Reconcile test no longer flakes on the MYT cutoff lines / 清理脚本的测试不再因 MYT 截止时间行而随机失败

- `test_the_report_prints_counts_and_never_a_name` runs the reconcile report twice and compares everything but the two cutoff lines, which it recognised by the old UTC `...Z  (` stamp. Since #231 those lines read MYT, so they were compared too, and the test failed whenever the second run landed in a different second (the `main` run after #239 did). The filter now drops the two lines by their labels.
- `test_the_report_prints_counts_and_never_a_name` 把清理脚本的报告跑两遍，比较除两行截止时间以外的内容，而它是靠旧的 UTC `...Z  (` 时间戳认出那两行的。#231 之后那两行显示 MYT，于是也被拿来比较，只要第二遍落在不同的一秒就失败（#239 合并后的 `main` 就撞上了）。现在改为按行首标签去掉那两行。

### Help videos at 1080p with a light caption bar / 帮助页视频改为 1080p，字幕条改浅色

- Full-screen on a laptop the 1280×720 videos looked soft, and the dark navy caption bar across the bottom, together with the site's own dimmed backdrop whenever a dialog is open, made them feel dark (measured, the frames were not: white 255, black 0). The page is now laid out and recorded at 1920×1080 (Playwright only scales a frame down into the video, never up, so the viewport has to be the video size), encoded at CRF 28, and the caption bar is white with dark text, smaller and narrower. The three videos were recorded again at `259e859`; the presentation backup video, which shares the overlay, is unchanged until it is next recorded.
- 1280×720 的视频在笔记本上全屏显得发虚，底部深蓝色字幕条再加上网站弹窗时自带的压暗背景，看起来就偏暗（实测画面并不暗：白是 255，黑是 0）。页面改为按 1920×1080 排版并以该尺寸录制（Playwright 只会把画面缩小进视频，不会放大，所以视口必须等于视频尺寸），编码 CRF 28；字幕条改为白底深字、更小更窄。三段视频在 `259e859` 上重录；共用这层字幕的答辩备用视频下次重录时才会跟着变。

The guide's step 5 reads "Unlock your key." instead of "if asked", since it is asked every time now. The Education animations are drawn by code and needed nothing.

### The passphrase is asked for on every decrypted download and every share / 每次解密下载和每次分享都要输入 passphrase

- Before, the account key stayed open in the browser after the first unlock (the sidebar said "Key unlocked in this browser"), and later downloads and shares in the same tab did not ask again until 15 minutes without activity. Li wants the passphrase to protect every single file, so someone holding a stolen login cannot use a tab the owner already unlocked. Now `downloadAndDecrypt` and `shareFileWithRecipient` drop the key as soon as they finish, whether they worked or failed, and the sidebar goes back to "Key locked". The key was only ever kept in page memory as a non-extractable `CryptoKey` (nothing in sessionStorage or IndexedDB), so that map is the only thing cleared. Pressing Share asks for the passphrase (or the `.pem` file) first, every time, and only then opens the share dialog; the key stays open while that dialog is up and is dropped after the share or when the dialog closes. A second share from the same dialog, or a file under an older key, asks again on top of the dialog. Download encrypted and upload are unchanged, since they never needed the private key. Registration no longer opens the key right after sign-in, because it would only sit there until the first download. Six new unit tests cover the success and failure paths; the live suites and the Playwright golden path unlock before each operation; the demo script now says Alice types her passphrase once and Bob twice.
- 以前第一次解锁之后，账户密钥一直在浏览器里开着（侧栏显示 "Key unlocked in this browser"），同一个标签页里之后的下载和分享都不再问，直到 15 分钟没有操作才重新锁上。Li 希望 passphrase 保护每一个文件，这样拿到被盗账号的人也没法利用主人已经解锁过的标签页。现在 `downloadAndDecrypt` 和 `shareFileWithRecipient` 一结束就丢掉密钥，不管成功还是失败，侧栏回到 "Key locked"。密钥本来就只以不可导出的 `CryptoKey` 放在页面内存里（sessionStorage 和 IndexedDB 里都没有），所以清掉的就是这一个 map。每次点 Share 都先弹出 passphrase（或 `.pem` 文件）对话框，验证通过才打开分享对话框；分享对话框开着的时候密钥保持打开，分享完成或关闭对话框就丢掉。在同一个对话框里再分享一次，或者文件用的是旧密钥，会在对话框上面再问一次。Download encrypted 和上传不变，它们本来就不需要私钥。注册后不再自动打开密钥，因为打开了也只是放着等第一次下载。新增 6 个单元测试覆盖成功和失败两种情况；live 测试和 Playwright golden path 在每次操作前都重新解锁；演示脚本改成 Alice 输入一次口令、Bob 输入两次。

### Access Records is now Activity Log, with account activity at the top / Access Records 改名为 Activity Log，账户操作放到最上面

- The `/audit-logs` page was called Access Records in the sidebar and File Access Records on the page, but it also lists account events (sign-ins, passphrase and key changes), and those sat at the very bottom under every file. The sidebar, page title, Workspace card, help assistant answers and the security agent's advice now say Activity Log (中文界面「活动日志」), the subtitle covers both account activity and file access, and the account entry is the first card in the list with the file records under it. The route stays `/audit-logs` so old links keep working.
- `/audit-logs` 页面在侧栏叫 Access Records，页面标题叫 File Access Records，但它也列出账户事件（登录、口令和密钥变更），而且这些放在所有文件下面的最底部。现在侧栏、页面标题、工作台卡片、帮助助手的回答和 security agent 的建议都改叫 Activity Log（中文界面「活动日志」），副标题同时说明账户操作和文件访问，账户那一项放在列表第一张卡片，文件记录接在下面。路由仍是 `/audit-logs`，旧链接不会失效。

### Records: removed the list naming AC2, AC3, AC4, AC5 and AC8 as the sign-off items / 文档：删除把 AC2、AC3、AC4、AC5、AC8 写成签核项的名单

- `docs/presentation/RTM_DEMO_MAP.md` 原来写着 “五个签核项是 AC2、AC3、AC4、AC5 和 AC8”，并在这几行标了 **签核**。这份名单并不是教学团队对现场演示项目的确认记录，读者却可能把它当成确认记录。中英文两版都删掉了这句话和五处标记，引入这张对照表的旧更新日志条目也不再重复这个说法。表格各行内容不变，包括 AC8 安全代理做什么。只改文档，没有改代码。

### Home page wording in plain English / 首页文案改成直白的说法

- Li's friends read the home page as obviously written by an AI, mostly because of its headings: paired slogans ("Sealed in your browser, unreadable to others."), a closing one-liner ("Not even our server can read it."), dashes in the step labels ("01 — Seal") and wordplay such as "Changed means refused". Only the text changed; the layout, colours and fonts are exactly as before. The hero now uses the poster's line, "Google can read your files. We cannot.", the steps section is "What happens to your file" with cards "01 · Encrypt / Encrypted before upload", "Shared with one person" and "Checked before download", and the picture guide is "Your first share, one screen at a time". The board-minutes.pdf card in the hero keeps its wording (SEALED, "Sealed for demo-bob") because slide 1 of the final presentation shows it. Chinese entries were added for every new line.
- Li 的朋友一看首页就觉得是 AI 写的，主要是标题的问题：对仗口号（"Sealed in your browser, unreadable to others."）、结尾金句（"Not even our server can read it."）、步骤标签里的破折号（"01 — Seal"），还有 "Changed means refused" 这种文字游戏。这次只改文字，布局、颜色和字体完全不变。首屏标题改用海报上的那句 "Google can read your files. We cannot."，步骤部分改成 "What happens to your file"，三张卡片是 "01 · Encrypt / Encrypted before upload"、"Shared with one person" 和 "Checked before download"，图片指南改成 "Your first share, one screen at a time"。首屏右边 board-minutes.pdf 那张卡片的文字保持不变（SEALED、"Sealed for demo-bob"），因为期末汇报 PPT 第 1 页用了这张卡片的截图。每句新文案都加了中文翻译。

### Workspace card text fills its lines / 工作台卡片说明文字排得更满

- After #232 the four Workspace card descriptions wrapped as plain paragraphs, but at four cards across each line ended well short of the card's edge ("Encrypt a new file in your", "or cancel one that is no"), which Li found ugly. Justified text and balanced wrapping were tried on screenshots: justify opened wide gaps between words and hyphenated "brows-er", balance only made every line short. The descriptions are now shorter, so the lines come out close to full width: "Encrypt a file in your browser with AES-256-GCM. Only the ciphertext is uploaded.", "Download, share, verify or delete the files you have already uploaded.", "The tamper-evident log of every upload, download and share on your account.", "Track your recovery requests, or cancel one you no longer need." The Chinese dictionary and `ui-strings.json` carry the new sentences. Checked locally in English and Chinese at desktop and phone widths.
- #232 之后工作台四张卡片的说明按普通段落换行，但一行放四张卡片时，每行都离卡片右边还差一截（“Encrypt a new file in your”、“or cancel one that is no”），Li 觉得难看。截图试过两端对齐和均衡换行：两端对齐让词之间空得很大，还把 “browser” 断成 “brows-er”；均衡换行只是把每行都变短。现在把说明改短，让每行接近排满：“Encrypt a file in your browser with AES-256-GCM. Only the ciphertext is uploaded.”、“Download, share, verify or delete the files you have already uploaded.”、“The tamper-evident log of every upload, download and share on your account.”、“Track your recovery requests, or cancel one you no longer need.”。中文词典和 `ui-strings.json` 同步更新。已在本地用中英文、桌面和手机宽度核对。

The descriptions now wrap as ordinary text, and hyphenated terms such as `AES-256-GCM` are kept on one line, which also stops a phone splitting it as "(AES-" / "256-GCM". ### Every time on the site is Kuala Lumpur time / 网站上所有时间统一为吉隆坡时间

- Times were shown in two zones at once. The security agent wrote its evidence in UTC ("Damage first seen 2026-10-12 18:19 UTC"), while every other page formatted times in the browser's own zone with no label, so on a Malaysian laptop Admin → Integrity said "Last scan 05:30:14" for the same night the agent called 21:30 UTC, and a presenter on another machine would see a third set of numbers. Storage, the API and backup set names stay UTC; only what a person reads changed. A new `frontend/src/utils/site-time.ts` formats every on-screen time as `2026-10-13 02:19:05 MYT` in Asia/Kuala_Lumpur whatever the browser's zone, and is now used by My Files, Access Records, the file history, sharing, Profile and every Admin tab (Users, Files, Recovery, Stats, Integrity, Security agent). The Access Records date filter reads a picked day as that day in Kuala Lumpur, to match the rows. On the server, the agent's evidence and advice use `app.core.time.site_stamp` (the backup advice adds that set names are UTC, 8 hours behind), `reconcile.py` prints its cutoffs in MYT, and the alert mail (`mcs21-alert`) stamps its time in MYT whatever the host's zone. The English lines with a Chinese line under each in the reported screenshot came from a browser translation extension, not from the site: the site's own Chinese replaces the English and words it differently.
- 时间之前同时用两个时区显示。Security agent 的证据写的是 UTC（“Damage first seen 2026-10-12 18:19 UTC”），其他页面则用浏览器自己的时区、且不标时区，所以在马来西亚的电脑上，Admin → Integrity 显示 “Last scan 05:30:14”，而 agent 把同一晚说成 21:30 UTC；换一台电脑演示又会是第三套数字。数据库、API 和备份集名称仍然用 UTC，只改给人看的部分。新增 `frontend/src/utils/site-time.ts`，不管浏览器在哪个时区，都把屏幕上的时间按 Asia/Kuala_Lumpur 显示成 `2026-10-13 02:19:05 MYT`，My Files、Access Records、文件历史、分享、Profile 和所有 Admin 页（Users、Files、Recovery、Stats、Integrity、Security agent）都改用它。Access Records 的日期筛选把选中的日期当作吉隆坡的那一天，与列表里的时间一致。服务器端：agent 的证据和建议改用 `app.core.time.site_stamp`（备份建议里注明备份集名称是 UTC，比 MYT 晚 8 小时），`reconcile.py` 的截止时间按 MYT 打印，告警邮件（`mcs21-alert`）不论主机时区如何都按 MYT 标时间。报告截图里每行英文下面那行中文来自浏览器翻译插件，不是网站自己的：网站自己的中文会直接替换英文，措辞也不同。

### The tamper demo can be flipped again after a restore from backup / 从备份恢复后演示文件可以再次篡改

- Rehearsing the defence means flipping `quarterly-results.txt` with `demo_tamper.py`, then putting it back with the command Admin → Integrity shows (`restore_file.py`, #229). `demo_tamper.py` only knew about its own `restore`, so its kept copy of the original stayed behind: `list` still marked the healthy file `TAMPERED` and the next `flip` answered "Already tampered; run restore first", leaving no way to prepare the file for the talk short of a `restore` that was no longer needed. Seen on staging on 2026-10-13. `flip`, `forge` and `list` now compare the stored object and the recorded digest with the kept original; when they match, the file was put back another way, the kept copy is discarded and the flip goes ahead. A file still tampered, or one whose digest was forged, is not mistaken for restored. `docs/presentation/TAMPER_DEMO.md` gains the live recovery steps from the Integrity tab.
- 彩排答辩要先用 `demo_tamper.py` 篡改 `quarterly-results.txt`，再用 Admin → Integrity 页面给的命令（`restore_file.py`，#229）恢复。`demo_tamper.py` 只认它自己的 `restore`，所以它保留的原件副本一直留着：`list` 仍把已经正常的文件标成 `TAMPERED`，下一次 `flip` 报 “Already tampered; run restore first”，除非再跑一次已经多余的 `restore`，否则没法为答辩重新篡改。2026-10-13 在 staging 上遇到。现在 `flip`、`forge` 和 `list` 会把存储的对象和记录的摘要与保留的原件比较；一致就说明文件已用别的方式恢复，丢弃保留副本，`flip` 照常进行。仍处于篡改状态的文件、或摘要被伪造的文件，不会被误判为已恢复。`docs/presentation/TAMPER_DEMO.md` 增加了从 Integrity 页现场恢复的步骤。

### The Integrity tab's restore command runs as pasted / Integrity 页上的恢复命令原样粘贴就能跑

- The command the Integrity tab showed for a damaged file ended in `--backup-set <backup set directory> --private-key <backup private key>`. Pasted as it was, bash answered `syntax error near unexpected token 'newline'`; filled in, it needed an encrypted off-site set downloaded from S3 and the custodian's private key, which nobody has at hand in a demonstration. The server already keeps a plaintext nightly tar of the object store in `/var/backups/mcs21-daily` (root-only, 14 days), so `scripts/restore_file.py` now uses that by default: newest archive first, checked against its `.sha256` manifest, the one object read out and compared with the recorded digest, an archive that already holds the damaged bytes skipped for an older one. `--backup-set` and `--private-key` remain, together, for the encrypted off-site set. Run as root it loads `/etc/fyp-secure-file-platform/backend.env` itself and hands the restored object and the quarantine copy back to the service account. The tab now shows `cd /opt/mcs21/current/backend && sudo .venv/bin/python scripts/restore_file.py --file-id <uuid>` and the same with `--apply`, and the agent's INTEGRITY_DAMAGE advice says the tab has the exact command. Checked on staging: the demo file was flipped, the pasted dry run answered in 1.5 s naming the nightly archive, `--apply` restored it, re-verified it VALID and left the object owned by `fypapp`.
- Integrity 页给受损文件显示的命令结尾是 `--backup-set <backup set directory> --private-key <backup private key>`。原样粘贴，bash 报 `syntax error near unexpected token 'newline'`；要真填上，得先从 S3 下载加密的异地备份集，再向保管人要私钥，演示时谁都没有这些。服务器本来就在 `/var/backups/mcs21-daily` 保存对象存储的明文每夜 tar（只有 root 可读，保留 14 天），所以 `scripts/restore_file.py` 现在默认用它：从最新归档开始，先对照自己的 `.sha256` 清单，再读出这一个对象与记录的摘要比对，已含受损字节的归档跳过改用更早的。`--backup-set` 和 `--private-key` 仍然保留、成对使用，对应加密的异地备份集。以 root 运行时脚本会自己加载 `/etc/fyp-secure-file-platform/backend.env`，并把恢复后的对象和隔离副本交还给服务账户。页面现在显示 `cd /opt/mcs21/current/backend && sudo .venv/bin/python scripts/restore_file.py --file-id <uuid>` 以及带 `--apply` 的同一条，Agent 的 INTEGRITY_DAMAGE 建议也改为指向页面上的命令。已在 staging 上验证：翻转演示文件一个字节，粘贴的演练命令 1.5 秒给出要用的每夜归档，`--apply` 恢复并重新校验为 VALID，对象仍归 `fypapp` 所有。

The seven images were captured again with the same script (`E2E_BROWSER=capture bash backend/tests/e2e/run_live_stack.sh` on a disposable local stack at `b44ec15`); the refused-download one now shows the sentence that names the next step. The other six changed only in timestamps and the generated account name.
