### A refused download says what happens next / 下载被拒时说明接下来会怎样

- When the server's integrity check refuses a download, My Files showed the server's sentence, "The encrypted file could not be verified", and nothing else. A recipient has no button for this (the fix is on the server side), so the page now says: the stored copy was changed and was not sent; the daily check alerts the administrator, who restores it from backup; or ask the owner to upload it again. The AC4a capture script looks for the new sentence; the AC4a screenshots in `docs/presentation/figures/` and the slide pack still show the old one.
- 服务器的完整性检查拒绝下载时，“我的文件”只显示服务器那句 “The encrypted file could not be verified”，别的什么都没有。接收方这边没有任何可点的按钮（修复在服务器那边），所以页面现在直接说明：存储副本被改动过，没有发送；每日检查会提醒管理员从备份恢复；或者请文件主人重新上传。AC4a 的截图脚本改为查找新句子；`docs/presentation/figures/` 和幻灯片包里的 AC4a 截图仍是旧句子。

### Recovery request on a changed file: a clear answer instead of "not found" / 对被改动文件申请恢复：给出明确回答，不再说“未找到”

- The tamper demo: after the stored copy of `quarterly-results.txt` was changed, the recipient saw the Modified status and the refused download as designed, then pressed Request recovery and got "The requested file was not found". The integrity sweep sets a changed file's status to CORRUPTED, and `RecoveryService.create_request` treated anything but ACTIVE as missing. A recovery request re-seals the file key to the requester's account key; it cannot repair content, which is the administrator's restore from backup. The server now answers a 409 that says so, after the grant check, so someone without access still sees "not found". On My Files the Request recovery item is disabled for a Modified file, with one line under it saying an administrator restores it from backup. `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` section 9 says the same.
- 篡改演示：`quarterly-results.txt` 在服务器上的副本被改一个字之后，接收方按设计看到了 Modified 状态和被拒绝的下载，接着点“申请恢复”，得到的却是“未找到所请求的文件”。完整性扫描会把被改动文件的状态设为 CORRUPTED，而 `RecoveryService.create_request` 把非 ACTIVE 的文件一律当作不存在。恢复申请做的是把文件密钥重新封装到申请人的账户密钥上，修不了内容，内容靠管理员从备份恢复。现在服务器在校验过授权之后返回 409 并把这件事说清楚，没有权限的人看到的仍是“未找到”。“我的文件”里，Modified 的文件上“申请恢复”变为禁用，下面一行说明由管理员从备份恢复。`docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` 第 9 节也补了这一段。

### OWASP ZAP baseline scan repeated for presentation week / 答辩周重跑 OWASP ZAP 基线扫描

- The home page's "0 FAIL in an OWASP ZAP scan" rested on the 2026-10-04 scan, eight days and 45 merged pull requests before the final presentation. The same passive baseline was run again on 2026-10-12 against release `1eb56d8`: 0 FAIL, 5 WARN, 62 PASS, the same five accepted or informational notes as after the nginx fix, so the claim stands on a current scan. Raw output is in `docs/security/scans-2026-10-12/`; README, the traceability table, the reflection and the talking points point at it.
- 首页的 "0 FAIL in an OWASP ZAP scan" 原来只靠 2026-10-04 那次扫描，离答辩已经过去八天、合并了 45 个 PR。2026-10-12 对版本 `1eb56d8` 又跑了一次同样的被动基线扫描：0 FAIL、5 WARN、62 PASS，五条和修好 nginx 之后的一样，都是已接受或仅供参考的提示，所以这个数字有了当前的依据。原始输出在 `docs/security/scans-2026-10-12/`；README、追溯表、反思和讲稿要点都指向它。

### Upload: the algorithm picker text is centred / 上传页：算法下拉框文字居中

- On the Upload page the "File contents" picker in the Encryption box had its text stuck to the left edge, which looked off next to everything else in that box. It is centred now. Chrome and Firefox only centre a closed select with `text-align-last`, so it has that as well as `text-center`. We checked WebKit (the engine Safari uses) too and it centres the same way. On a 360 px phone the option name is wider than the box, so it still starts from the left there, and the page still does not scroll sideways. The open list of options and the note card under it are left aligned like before, since centred paragraphs are harder to read.
- 上传页 Encryption 区块里的 “File contents” 下拉框，文字原来贴在左边，跟框里其他内容放在一起看着不太对。现在改成居中。Chrome 和 Firefox 收起状态的 select 只认 `text-align-last` 才会居中，所以除了 `text-center` 也加了它。我们也在 WebKit（Safari 用的引擎）里看过，一样是居中的。在 360 px 的手机上选项名称比框还宽，所以那里还是从左边开始显示，页面也仍然不会横向滚动。展开后的选项列表和下面的说明卡片保持左对齐，因为多行文字居中反而难读。

### Security agent: one address into many accounts is one finding, closed accounts get fitting advice, the 2026-10-10 alert explained; picture guide at 3 seconds a step / 安全 Agent：同一地址进多个账户只报一条、已注销账号给对应建议、解释 2026-10-10 的告警；图文指南每步 3 秒

- The daily check mailed at 06:00 on 2026-10-10: exit 3, 43 High findings. Every one of them was the 2026-10-09 load test. The audit log shows the `secureview-loadtest/1.0` user agent on each event, the tester's two home addresses, the five busiest accounts uploading and downloading 500 to 770 files in ten minutes during the 22:02 to 22:53 run, and, two hours earlier, the cleanup of the first attempt: 68 failed sign-ins at accounts already deleted, then 32 sign-ins to the accounts still there, all from one address. Those 32 sign-ins gave 32 `SIGN_IN_AFTER_GUESSING` findings quoting the same 68 failures, and every account had the `deleted-<hex>` tombstone name with advice to "disable the account on the Users tab". Nothing was changed on the server. Two rule changes: a sign-in to another account from the same address on the same run of failures now extends the one finding (the accounts are listed, Critical if one is an administrator), and a burst, guessing or takeover finding on an account that is not active now (closed or disabled) says so and gives advice that fits: Access Records for a burst, a password reset before re-enabling for guessing; address-keyed findings never carry the note; the repository reads `users.status` for that, still no metadata or user agents. Replayed read-only against the same audit window, the morning's report is 12 High findings instead of 43, with the five real bursts on top. `SECURITY_AGENT.md`, `PERFORMANCE_TESTING.md` and the load test README now say to expect the mail after a load test and how to confirm it.
- 2026-10-10 06:00 每日检查发了邮件：退出码 3，43 条 High 发现。全部是 2026-10-09 的压测。审计日志里每条事件都带 `secureview-loadtest/1.0` 的 User-Agent，来自测试者家里的两个地址；22:02 到 22:53 的正式压测里五个最忙的账号十分钟内各上传下载了 500 到 770 个文件；再往前两小时是第一次尝试的清理：对已删除账号的 68 次登录失败，然后登进还在的 32 个账号，全部来自同一个地址。这 32 次登录生成了 32 条引用同一批 68 次失败的 `SIGN_IN_AFTER_GUESSING`，每个账号都是 `deleted-<hex>` 的墓碑名，建议却是“到用户页停用该账户”。服务器上什么都没有改。规则改了两处：同一地址基于同一轮失败登进另一个账户，现在并入同一条发现（列出账户，有管理员则为 Critical）；现在已注销或被停用的账户，以它为主体的突增、猜密码和接管类发现会写明，并给出对应的建议（突增看访问记录页，猜密码则重新启用前先重置密码），以地址为主体的发现不带这句话；仓库为此多读 `users.status` 一列，仍不读元数据和 User-Agent。用新规则只读地回放同一个审计窗口，那天早上的报告是 12 条 High 而不是 43 条，五条真正的突增排在最上面。`SECURITY_AGENT.md`、`PERFORMANCE_TESTING.md` 和压测 README 现在都写明压测后要预料到这封邮件，以及怎么核实。
- The homepage picture guide ("See exactly what to click") moved on every 8 seconds, which was too long to wait for the next step; it is 3 seconds now. The progress bar, Pause, Back and Next are unchanged, so a step can still be held to read.
- 首页图文指南（“查看具体要点击的内容”）原来每 8 秒才切到下一步，等得太久；现在改为 3 秒。进度条、暂停、上一步和下一步都没有变，想细看某一步仍然可以停住。
- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md` and the "Verified" table in `TODO.md` now quote the CI run on `2ffc36c` (#222): 886 passed and 78 skipped with 74.97 % statements in `frontend`, 964 passed with 79.12 % in `e2e-live`; the backend numbers did not change. The summary had been left at `0bb6238` (#217) while five frontend tests were added by #218 to #222.
- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md` 和 `TODO.md` 的“已核验”表改为引用 `2ffc36c`（#222）上的 CI：`frontend` 886 通过、78 跳过、语句覆盖率 74.97%，`e2e-live` 964 通过、79.12%；后端数字没有变。之前总结页一直停在 `0bb6238`（#217），而 #218 到 #222 又加了五个前端测试。

### Help videos and the homepage picture guide re-shot for the current upload page / 按现在的上传页重录帮助页视频、重拍首页图文指南

- The two Help walkthroughs and the homepage picture guide were recorded on 2026-10-05, before PR #215. They showed the old algorithm dropdown without grades or the explanation box, the old "Encryption Complete" button, the Details link under the file name, and the sign-in page's 2026-10-03/04 figures. `encrypt-upload.mp4` and `share-open.mp4` were recorded again from a disposable local stack with made-up accounts, and all 24 picture-guide images (English, Chinese, phone) were captured again. The Help page's text steps under the upload video now mention the grade, the explanation box and the green status. `record-walkthroughs.mjs` pointed at the first combobox on the page, which is the header's language switcher, so the "pick the cipher" outline was on the wrong control (in the old video too); it now targets the File contents list, and it also points at the explanation box and the finished status. `how-it-works.mp4` and the presentation backup video are unchanged. AES-128-GCM's label in the algorithm list changes from "High · Standard" to "High · Widely used": "Standard" read like a grade of its own next to "High", while the point is that it is the common choice (HTTPS uses it); none of the recordings show the opened list, so nothing had to be re-shot for it. After that, the upload video and the picture guide were made to show the list itself, so people see there are six ciphers to choose from: the video opens the list, picks ChaCha20-Poly1305 and AES-128-GCM so the explanation box changes, points to Education in the sidebar, then goes back to AES-256-GCM and encrypts (now 98.1 s); the guide has a new second step, "Choose a cipher", with all seven steps' images re-captured because the step numbers are part of each image. A native `<select>` pop-up is drawn by the operating system and never appears in a recording, so for those few seconds both scripts show the same element as a list of its six options under its label and put it back afterwards; the site's own list is unchanged.
- 帮助页的两段演示视频和首页图文指南是 2026-10-05 录的，早于 PR #215：画面里还是没有等级和说明框的旧算法下拉框、旧的“Encryption Complete”按钮、文件名下面的 Details 链接，以及登录页 2026-10-03/04 的旧数字。现在用一次性本地环境和虚构账户重录了 `encrypt-upload.mp4` 和 `share-open.mp4`，并重拍了图文指南全部 24 张图（英文、中文、手机版）。帮助页上传视频下面的文字步骤也补上了等级、说明框和绿色完成状态。`record-walkthroughs.mjs` 原来指向页面上第一个下拉框，那其实是页头的语言切换，所以“选择算法”那一步的高亮框一直框错了地方（旧视频里也是）；现在改为指向“File contents”算法列表，并增加了指向说明框和完成状态的两步。`how-it-works.mp4` 和答辩备用视频没有改动。算法列表里 AES-128-GCM 的说明从 “高 · 标准” 改为 “高 · 广泛使用”：“标准” 放在 “高” 旁边像是另一个等级，而想说的是它最常用（HTTPS 就在用）；视频和图片都没有拍到展开的列表，所以不用为此重拍。之后又让上传视频和图文指南直接拍到这个列表，让人知道有六种算法可选：视频里展开列表，依次选中 ChaCha20-Poly1305 和 AES-128-GCM 让说明框跟着变，再指向侧栏的学习中心，最后切回 AES-256-GCM 加密（现在 98.1 秒）；图文指南新增第 2 步“选择加密算法”，因为步骤编号是拍在图里的，七步的图全部重拍。原生 `<select>` 的弹出菜单由操作系统绘制，录屏和截图都拍不到，所以两个脚本在那几秒里把同一个元素显示成标签下面的六行列表，拍完再恢复；网站本身的下拉框没有改。

### A restore by the server shows as "System (server script)", not as a deleted account / 服务器恢复的记录显示为"系统（服务器脚本）"，不再显示成已删除账户

- Access Records and a file's history showed every row without a username as "A deleted account". A row has no username in two different cases: it was written by a server job (`scripts/restore_file.py` recording `FILE_RESTORED_FROM_BACKUP`, which has no signed-in actor), or the account was deleted, which leaves the row under a `deleted-<hex>` username. So an operator's restore from backup read as the work of a removed user. `auditActorLabel` in `src/utils/audit-actions.ts` now tells them apart: no actor is "System (server script)", a `deleted-<32 hex>` username is "A deleted account", anything else is the username. Both labels are also translated now; the old text sat inside `translate="no"` with the username and never was.
- 访问记录和文件历史把所有没有用户名的记录都显示成"一个被删除的账户"。没有用户名其实有两种情况：一是服务器任务写的（`scripts/restore_file.py` 记录 `FILE_RESTORED_FROM_BACKUP`，没有登录的操作人），二是账户已被删除，记录里的用户名变成 `deleted-<hex>`。结果管理员从备份恢复文件，看起来像是某个已删除用户做的。现在 `src/utils/audit-actions.ts` 里的 `auditActorLabel` 把两者分开：没有操作人显示"系统（服务器脚本）"，`deleted-<32 位十六进制>` 显示"一个被删除的账户"，其余照常显示用户名。这两个文字现在也会被翻译；以前它和用户名一起放在 `translate="no"` 里，从来没翻译过。

### Home and sign-in pages quote the 2026-10-09 load test / 首页和登录页改用 2026-10-09 的压测数字

- The proof figures on the home page and beside the sign-in and registration forms still showed the 2026-10-03/04 run ("0 / 5,094" downloads failed their integrity check, "102 / 102" uploads at 100 users). They now show the after-fix run through the public URL: 0 of 7,444 downloads failed their integrity check, and 0 server errors with 100 concurrent users. Both figures come from `src/data/proof.ts`, which now points to the report and to `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md` so they are updated together.
- 首页以及登录、注册表单旁边的数字还是 2026-10-03/04 那次的（"0 / 5,094" 次下载未通过完整性检查、100 个用户时 "102 / 102" 次上传成功）。现在改成修复后经公网 URL 跑的结果：7,444 次下载里 0 次未通过完整性检查，100 个并发用户时 0 个服务器错误。这两个数字都来自 `src/data/proof.ts`，文件里注明了报告和 `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md`，以后一起更新。

### Load test re-run after the fixes, and one testing summary / 修复后重跑压测，加一页测试总结

- The 2026-10-03/04 load test went through an SSH tunnel and found uploads failing with HTTP 500 at 100 users. After the pool fix, the deadlock retry log and today's review fixes, the whole matrix except the long soaks was run again on 2026-10-09 through the public URL with nginx and its limits on, the path a real user takes: 18,256 operations, 7,444 SHA-256-verified downloads, 0 integrity failures and no server 5xx; 100 users now give 0 server errors (the 429s are one address hitting the 50-uploads-in-flight limit); 36 files across six types and six sizes round-trip byte-exact. Server CPU averaged 4.5 % and a single 500 MiB transfer ran at 87 to 98 Mbit/s, so the VPS's 100 Mbit/s port is the limit, not the code. The report is `docs/performance/REPORT-2026-10-09.md` (EN and zh) with raw data, server metrics and charts; the 10-03 report stays as the before-fix baseline.
- 2026-10-03/04 的压测走的是 SSH 隧道，100 个用户时上传出现 HTTP 500。修了连接池、加了死锁重试日志、合并了今天的复查修复之后，2026-10-09 经公网 URL 重新跑了除长时间压测以外的全部场景，nginx 和限流都开着，和真实用户走同一条路：18,256 次操作，7,444 次下载做了 SHA-256 校验，0 次完整性失败，服务器没有任何 5xx；100 个用户时 0 个服务器错误（429 是同一个地址碰到了"最多 50 个同时上传"的限制）；六种类型、六档大小的 36 个文件全部逐字节一致。服务器 CPU 平均 4.5%，单个 500 MiB 传输就能到 87 到 98 Mbit/s，所以瓶颈是 VPS 的 100 Mbit/s 网口，不是代码。报告在 `docs/performance/REPORT-2026-10-09.md`（中英文），附原始数据、服务器指标和图表；10-03 的报告保留作为修复前对照。
- The load tool gained `loadtest/run_staging.sh` (public URL by default, the 1 MiB to 500 MiB matrix only with `LOAD_LARGE_FILES=1`); the size matrix now uses one owner per file type and deletes each file once it is verified, so the per-account quotas stay on; cleanup waits and retries on 429 instead of leaving accounts behind (the first attempt left 32 of 100); and its envelope test no longer seals GCM-SIV and XChaCha20 vectors as AES-GCM, which had kept 6 of its tests red since #145.
- 压测工具新增 `loadtest/run_staging.sh`（默认走公网 URL，1 MiB 到 500 MiB 的大文件矩阵要加 `LOAD_LARGE_FILES=1` 才跑）；大文件矩阵按类型各用一个账号，每个文件验证完就删，所以每个账号的配额不用关；清理遇到 429 会等待重试，不再留下账号（第一次清理留下了 100 个里的 32 个）；信封测试不再把 GCM-SIV 和 XChaCha20 的向量当 AES-GCM 来封装，这个问题从 #145 起让它的 6 个测试一直是红的。
- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md` (EN and zh) puts every test count, coverage figure, load-test result and known gap on one page, from the CI on `0bb6238` (backend 691 at 81.67 %, MySQL gate 759 with no skips at 86.52 %, frontend 881, live stack 959 with no skips, browser 57). README, TALKING_POINTS, REFLECTION, TODO, PERFORMANCE_TESTING, PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY and the load tool README now quote these numbers.
- `docs/TESTING_SUMMARY.md`（中英文）把所有测试数量、覆盖率、压测结果和已知缺口放在一页，数字来自 `0bb6238` 上的 CI（后端 691，覆盖率 81.67%；MySQL 门禁 759，零跳过，覆盖率 86.52%；前端 881；连真实后端 959，零跳过；浏览器 57）。README、TALKING_POINTS、REFLECTION、TODO、PERFORMANCE_TESTING、PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY 和压测工具 README 都改成引用这些数字。

### Account safety: forced password change enforced by the server, the last admin stays, one email per share / 账户安全：强制改密由服务器执行，最后一个管理员不能删除自己，同一次分享只发一封邮件

- #212 only made the browser send a user with an admin-issued temporary password to Settings; the API still accepted that session everywhere, so whoever held the temporary password could delete the account. While `must_change_password` is set the server now answers 403 `password_change_required` on every authenticated route except `GET /auth/me`, change password, logout and logout-all (refresh issues tokens held to the same list). `GET /keys/me` is left out on purpose because it returns the encrypted private key bundle. The frontend sends any such 403 to the Settings password card. A test walks every authenticated route in the OpenAPI spec.
- #212 只让浏览器把拿着管理员临时密码的用户送到 Settings，API 仍然接受这个会话做任何事，拿到临时密码的人可以直接删号。现在只要 `must_change_password` 为真，服务器对所有需要登录的接口都返回 403 `password_change_required`，只放行 `GET /auth/me`、改密、登出和全部登出（refresh 签发的令牌同样受这个限制）。`GET /keys/me` 有意不放行，因为它会返回加密的私钥包。前端收到这个 403 会跳到 Settings 的改密卡片。有一个测试会按 OpenAPI 把所有需要登录的接口都过一遍。
- The last active administrator can no longer delete their own account or be disabled by another admin at the same moment: both answer 409 `last_admin`, after locking the admin rows in id order so two admins acting together run one after the other. Before this, losing the last admin meant editing the database to get one back.
- 最后一个有效的管理员不能再删除自己的账号，也不会在两个管理员互相停用时被同时停用：两种情况都返回 409 `last_admin`，并且会先按 id 顺序锁住管理员行，两个管理员同时操作时会一个接一个执行。以前如果没了最后一个管理员，只能直接改数据库才能恢复。
- Sharing a file again with someone who already has it no longer emails them again; only a new grant or a revoked one being restored sends the notice. Repeated re-sharing could spam a recipient and use up the site-wide daily notification cap.
- 再次分享给已经有权限的人时不再重复发邮件，只有新授权或恢复已撤销的授权才发通知。以前反复分享可以骚扰收件人，还会耗光全站每日的通知上限。
- Looking up a recipient by email or username after signing in stays as it is: sharing needs it. THREAT_MODEL 1.6 records it as T-S2a and F-21 (Accepted: needs a signed-in session, nginx allows 30 API requests a second per address, and it returns only the user id and public key). 1.6 also adds the forced password change as T-S8 and the last-admin rule as T-D5. TODO gains an open item to re-record the two backup videos once these changes are live.
- 登录后按邮箱或用户名查收件人保持不变，因为分享需要它。THREAT_MODEL 1.6 把它记为 T-S2a 和 F-21（接受：需要登录，nginx 限制每个地址每秒 30 次 API 请求，只返回用户 id 和公钥）。1.6 还把强制改密记为 T-S8，最后一个管理员规则记为 T-D5。TODO 新增一项：这些改动上线后重录两个备用视频。

### Repository review 2026-10-09: two information leaks closed, upload and Access Records edge cases, records brought up to date / 2026-10-09 仓库检查：堵住两处信息泄露，修上传页和访问记录的边角问题，文档记录对齐

- The Access Records visibility rule let a non-admin see any audit row whose resource id matched a file they owned, whatever the row was about. Upload file ids are chosen by the client, so someone could upload a file whose id was another user's account id and then read that user's sign-in failures, password changes, admin resets and account deletion. Only rows about a file (FILE, SHARE, INTEGRITY and file recovery actions) now count as "about your file". Tested on SQLite in the unit suite and on MySQL in `mysql-gate`.
- 访问记录的可见范围规则会让普通用户看到"resource id 等于自己某个文件 id"的任何审计行，不管这一行讲的是什么。上传时文件 id 由客户端决定，所以有人可以拿别人的账户 id 当文件 id 上传一个文件，然后读到对方的登录失败、改密、管理员重置和删号记录。现在只有关于文件的行（FILE、SHARE、INTEGRITY 和文件恢复操作）才算"关于你的文件"。单元测试用 SQLite 验证，`mysql-gate` 用 MySQL 验证。
- `POST /auth/verification-codes/send` still told registered and unregistered emails apart: the 60-second resend interval was only checked when a code was really sent, so a quick second request answered 200 for one and 429 for the other, which undid part of #212. The interval is now checked first on every path, and a withheld send records its time in an unusable INVALIDATED code row (no user, random digest) that the existing cleanup removes. No schema change.
- `POST /auth/verification-codes/send` 仍能区分邮箱是否已注册：60 秒重发间隔只在真正发码时检查，所以快速发第二次时一种情况返回 200、另一种返回 429，等于把 #212 修的问题又露了一半。现在每条路径都先检查间隔，不发码的情况也会写一条不能使用的 INVALIDATED 记录（没有用户，摘要是随机值）来记时间，现有清理任务会把它删掉。不改表结构。
- Account deletion: a full password-hashing pool now answers 429 instead of 500, every timestamp it writes is naive UTC to the second (the aware clock with microseconds was rounded by MySQL up to half a second ahead), and a storage clean-up failure after the account is already gone is logged instead of failing the request. The admin password reset uses the app's password manager and also answers 429 when the pool is full. The security agent's password-guessing rule now counts wrong passwords on account deletion as well as on password change.
- 删除账号：密码哈希并发满时返回 429 而不是 500；写入的时间统一是精确到秒的 naive UTC（原来带微秒的时间会被 MySQL 四舍五入到最多半秒之后）；账号已经删除后清理存储失败只记日志，不再让请求报错。管理员重置密码改用应用自己的 password manager，并发满时同样返回 429。安全代理的猜密码规则现在也统计删号时输错的密码。
- Upload page: files can no longer be removed or added while a batch is encrypting (removed ones used to upload anyway, added ones were left pending under a green "complete"), a failed upload closes the progress panel instead of leaving it spinning, and the same file can be chosen again after removing it. Access Records shows readable errors instead of "Request failed with status code 500", ignores stale responses when filters are applied quickly, and wraps long file names on phones. The Education AES demo says "Wrong password, or the ciphertext was changed." instead of showing nothing in Chrome and Safari. Smaller fixes: the remove button has a label, the receipt no longer floods screen readers, the Chinese share row reads "已分享 对象：junyan", and a must-change-password user is only sent to Settings from pages that need sign-in.
- 上传页：加密过程中不能再删或加文件（原来删掉的照样上传，新加的留在待上传，页面却显示绿色完成）；上传失败会关掉进度面板，不再一直转圈；删掉文件后可以重新选同一个文件。访问记录出错时显示可读的提示，不再是 "Request failed with status code 500"；快速切换筛选时丢弃过期的响应；手机上长文件名会换行。Education 的 AES 演示在 Chrome 和 Safari 输错密码时会提示"密码错误，或密文被改过"，不再什么都不显示。小修：删除按钮有了无障碍标签，加密回执不再刷屏读屏软件，中文分享记录显示为"已分享 对象：junyan"，必须改密的用户只在需要登录的页面才被带去 Settings。
- Records: TALKING_POINTS had the 10-07 test numbers and the pre-#210 nginx limits; it now quotes the c3569c8 CI run (backend 661, MySQL 728, frontend 875, live 953, browser 57) and 30 r/s, 60 credential checks a minute, 50 uploads in flight. THREAT_MODEL is version 1.5 (stale checkpoint and section references fixed, the missing Chinese T-T1a row added, the sweep timer confirmed running on staging). REFLECTION, SECURITY_FLOWS, README, backend README, ARCHITECTURE, TODO, PERFORMANCE_TESTING and E2E_TESTING no longer describe shipped work as missing, and the demo script says the backup videos predate #215. The full findings list, including the items left for a decision, is in the team's review notes for 2026-10-09.
- 文档：TALKING_POINTS 里还是 10 月 7 日的测试数字和 #210 之前的 nginx 限流，现在引用 c3569c8 的 CI（后端 661、MySQL 728、前端 875、连真实后端 953、浏览器 57）以及每秒 30 次、每分钟 60 次凭证检查、50 个并发上传。THREAT_MODEL 升到 1.5（修了过时的检查点说法和章节引用，补上中文缺的 T-T1a 行，确认 staging 上巡检定时任务在运行）。REFLECTION、SECURITY_FLOWS、README、后端 README、ARCHITECTURE、TODO、PERFORMANCE_TESTING 和 E2E_TESTING 不再把已上线的功能写成没做，演示脚本说明备用视频录于 #215 之前。完整的检查清单（包括留给用户决定的几项）在团队 2026-10-09 的检查记录里。

### Audit log: filters and real paging on the access records page / 审计日志：访问记录页加筛选和真正的分页

- `GET /audit-logs` takes optional `action` (whitelisted, anything else is a 422), `result` (`SUCCESS` or `FAIL`), `from` and `to` (ISO 8601 UTC, inclusive) and `q` (a keyword of at most 100 characters over the description and the resolved file name, with `%` and `_` taken literally). Every filter is applied on top of the existing visibility rule, so a non-admin who filters still sees only their own rows; a MySQL integration test proves a stranger searching for the owner's file name gets nothing of the owner's. The access records page gets a filter bar (event type, result, date range, keyword, Apply and Clear), loads 100 events at a time with a Load more button and says how many are shown, and its stat cards now say they cover the loaded events. It used to walk the whole trail before drawing anything: ten requests and a hard stop at a thousand rows on a long-lived account, with no way to look for one event. The demo shows this page, and a demo account has months of history.
- `GET /audit-logs` 新增可选参数 `action`（白名单校验，其它值返回 422）、`result`（`SUCCESS` 或 `FAIL`）、`from` 和 `to`（ISO 8601 UTC，闭区间）以及 `q`（最多 100 个字符的关键词，匹配描述和解析出的文件名，`%` 和 `_` 按字面匹配）。所有筛选都叠加在原有的可见范围规则之上，非管理员筛选后仍然只能看到自己的记录；MySQL 集成测试证明陌生人搜索文件所有者的文件名时拿不到所有者的任何记录。访问记录页新增筛选栏（事件类型、结果、日期范围、关键词，应用和清除按钮），每次加载 100 条并提供"加载更多"按钮和已显示数量，统计卡注明只统计已加载的事件。原来页面在渲染之前要把整条记录拉完：长期账号首屏要发 10 个请求，1000 条封顶，而且没有办法查找某一条事件。演示会展示这个页面，而演示账号有几个月的历史记录。

### Auth: registration stops leaking emails, account deletion is throttled, admin resets force a new password, dead crypto code removed / 认证：注册不再泄露邮箱、删号密码校验接入限流、管理员重置后强制改密、删除无用加密代码

- `POST /auth/register` no longer reveals whether an email address already has an account. It used to answer `409 account_identifier_unavailable` for a taken username or email *before* looking at the verification code, so anyone could probe addresses for free (THREAT_MODEL F-08). Now a taken username is still reported first with `409`, because usernames are public (sharing resolves them) and reporting it before the code is consumed saves the caller a fresh code; the code is consumed next; and only then is the email checked, answering the same `422 invalid_verification_code` a wrong code gets. The send endpoint never delivers a code to a registered address, so in practice that branch only closes the race, and the `IntegrityError` fallback maps the two unique keys the same way.
- `POST /auth/register` 不再暴露某个邮箱是否已注册。此前它会在校验验证码*之前*就对已占用的用户名或邮箱返回 `409 account_identifier_unavailable`，任何人都能免费探测地址（THREAT_MODEL F-08）。现在已占用的用户名仍先返回 `409`，因为用户名本来就是公开的（分享时按用户名解析），而且在消耗验证码之前就告知，调用方不必重新发码；接着消耗验证码；最后才查邮箱，查到已注册时返回与验证码错误完全相同的 `422 invalid_verification_code`。发码接口本来就不会给已注册邮箱发验证码，所以这一分支实际只是堵住竞态，`IntegrityError` 兜底也按同样方式映射两个唯一键。
- `POST /auth/delete-account` now counts a wrong password against the same `REAUTH_ACCOUNT` / `REAUTH_IP` throttle buckets as `POST /auth/change-password`, answers `429` with `Retry-After` while they are blocked, and writes `ACCOUNT_DELETION_PASSWORD_FAILED` to the audit log. Before, the one irreversible action in the application was the only place a signed-in session could guess the login password without limit or trace. `AuthService` exposes `begin_reauthentication`, `record_reauthentication_failure` and `clear_reauthentication_throttle` for this, and `AccountDeletionService` requires that dependency rather than defaulting it, so a wiring that forgot it cannot silently leave the endpoint open.
- `POST /auth/delete-account` 的密码校验失败现在计入与 `POST /auth/change-password` 相同的 `REAUTH_ACCOUNT` / `REAUTH_IP` 限流桶，被封禁期间返回带 `Retry-After` 的 `429`，并把 `ACCOUNT_DELETION_PASSWORD_FAILED` 写入审计日志。此前，应用里唯一不可逆的操作反而是登录会话可以无限次、无痕迹猜登录密码的唯一入口。`AuthService` 为此暴露了 `begin_reauthentication`、`record_reauthentication_failure` 和 `clear_reauthentication_throttle`，`AccountDeletionService` 把这个依赖设为必填而不是默认值，忘记接线的代码不会悄悄让接口失去保护。
- An administrator's password reset now marks the account `must_change_password` (migration `0011_must_change_password`, `users.must_change_password BOOL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`). Login and `GET /auth/me` return the flag; the frontend's router guard sends the holder to Settings, lands them on the Login password card with a notice, and keeps every other signed-in page off limits until `POST /auth/change-password` (or the emailed reset) clears it. The API refuses nothing because of the flag, so a holder can never be locked out of the one card that clears it. The temporary password has been seen by the administrator and travelled by hand; before this nothing stopped it from staying in use for months.
- 管理员重置密码现在会给账户打上 `must_change_password` 标记（迁移 `0011_must_change_password`，`users.must_change_password BOOL NOT NULL DEFAULT 0`）。登录和 `GET /auth/me` 都返回该字段；前端路由守卫把持有人带到设置页，定位到“登录密码”卡片并显示提示，在 `POST /auth/change-password`（或邮件重置）清除标记之前，其他登录后页面一律跳回。API 不会因为这个标记拒绝任何请求，所以持有人永远不会被锁在唯一能清除标记的卡片之外。临时密码管理员看到过、也经手传递过；在此之前没有任何机制阻止它被一直用下去。
- Removed code nothing called: `CryptoService.generate_account_key`, `decrypt_account_private_key`, `rewrap_key_protection_profile` and their helpers (the server-managed "format 2" experiment, kept since the revert to client-held custody), `GeneratedAccountKey`, `WatermarkService`, and the `app/utils` package (`file_utils`, `response`, `exceptions`). The AAD and HKDF layout builders stay as the documented byte format and gained a byte-exact test. Less unused crypto in the tree means less to audit and no risk of someone wiring the server-side path back in by accident.
- 删除了无人调用的代码：`CryptoService.generate_account_key`、`decrypt_account_private_key`、`rewrap_key_protection_profile` 及其辅助函数（服务端托管的 "format 2" 试验，自回退到客户端托管后一直留着）、`GeneratedAccountKey`、`WatermarkService`，以及 `app/utils` 包（`file_utils`、`response`、`exceptions`）。AAD 与 HKDF 布局构造函数作为文档化的字节格式保留，并补了逐字节测试。仓库里少一份没人用的加密代码，就少一份要审计的东西，也不会有人误把服务端路径接回去。

### Supervisor feedback: no double upload, graded algorithm list, quicker explainer / 导师反馈：不再重复上传、算法列表加等级、讲解动画更快

- After a batch finished, the upload button still said "Encryption Complete" but was still the Start Encryption button underneath, and `handleEncrypt` walked the whole file list again, so pressing it uploaded every file a second time. The page now sends only files that have not uploaded yet (`filesStillToUpload` in `upload.ts`), and once everything is up the button is replaced by a green status bar (the same green as the SEALED label) that cannot be pressed. "View Encrypted Files" stays, and adding a file brings the button back. The golden path browser test counts `POST /files/upload` requests and checks that clicking the status sends none; every other browser test and recording script now looks for the status by test id.
- 一批文件加密完成后，按钮显示 “Encryption Complete”，但它其实还是 Start Encryption 按钮，而 `handleEncrypt` 会把整个文件列表再走一遍，所以再点一下就会把所有文件又上传一次。现在页面只上传还没上传过的文件（`upload.ts` 里的 `filesStillToUpload`），全部上传完后按钮换成一个不能点的绿色状态条（和 SEALED 标签同色）。“View Encrypted Files” 保留，再选新文件时按钮会恢复。golden path 浏览器测试会统计 `POST /files/upload` 请求数，并检查点状态条不会再发请求；其他浏览器测试和录制脚本改用 test id 找这个状态条。
- The algorithm dropdown only listed names, and the supervisor pointed out that a normal user cannot tell which one to pick. Each option now has a short grade: AES-256-GCM is "Max · Recommended" (still the default), GCM-SIV "Extra safety net", XChaCha20 "Long nonce", ChaCha20 "Fast on phones", AES-192 "High" and AES-128 "High · Standard". A box under the dropdown explains the chosen algorithm in plain words. 128-bit AES is labelled High, not low, because it is still secure, and no speed numbers are claimed that we have not measured.
- 算法下拉框原来只有名字，导师指出普通用户根本不知道该选哪个。现在每一项后面都有一个简单等级：AES-256-GCM 是 “最高 · 推荐”（仍是默认），GCM-SIV 是 “额外保护”，XChaCha20 是 “长随机数”，ChaCha20 是 “手机上快”，AES-192 是 “高”，AES-128 是 “高 · 标准”。下拉框下面有一个说明框，用大白话解释当前选中的算法。128 位 AES 标成 “高” 而不是 “低”，因为它目前仍然安全；也没有写任何我们没实测过的速度数字。
- The "What happens to your file" explainer on the Upload page waited 7 seconds on every step, which felt slow. Each step now stays about 3 seconds, up to 3.5 for longer captions (`sceneDuration` in `security-story.ts`). Pause and the arrows work as before; the full-screen `?present` view used for recording keeps its fixed timing.
- 上传页的 “What happens to your file” 动画每一步都停 7 秒，看起来太慢。现在每步大约 3 秒，字多的步骤最多 3.5 秒（`security-story.ts` 里的 `sceneDuration`）。暂停和左右箭头不变；录视频用的全屏 `?present` 页面保持原来的固定时长。
- CI: the tamper drill failed with "Access denied for user 'root'" after an earlier run on the same self-hosted runner was cancelled, because that run's MySQL volume was still there with a different random password. `security-lab/run.sh` now removes any leftover lab containers and volumes before it starts.
- CI：同一台自托管 runner 上前一次运行被取消后，tamper drill 报 "Access denied for user 'root'"，因为那次的 MySQL 数据卷还在，密码是另一个随机值。`security-lab/run.sh` 现在启动前会先清掉残留的实验容器和数据卷。
- The Encrypted preview shows 8 rows (128 bytes) of ciphertext and stays as it is. The server already stores the full ciphertext and "Download encrypted file" hands all of it to the owner, and AES-GCM is built to stay safe when an attacker sees every byte of it. This is recorded as T-I10 in `THREAT_MODEL.md` (version 1.4).
- Encrypted preview 显示 8 行（128 字节）密文，保持不变。服务器本来就存着完整密文，“Download encrypted file” 也会把全部密文给所有者，而 AES-GCM 的设计前提就是攻击者能看到全部密文也安全。这一判断记录在 `THREAT_MODEL.md` 的 T-I10（版本 1.4）。

### Presentation: sharing sequence diagram, slide flow diagram, AC4a screenshots, requirement-to-demo map / 演示材料：分享时序图、幻灯片流程图、AC4a 截图、需求与演示对照表

- `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` has a sequence diagram of sharing that follows the calls in `share.ts`, `ShareDialog.vue` and `sharing_service.py` in order, including the unlock that comes before the DEK is unwrapped and the `GET /files/{file_id}` that fetches the envelope; rendered to `docs/presentation/diagrams/share-sequence.png`. The slides get one plain flow diagram (`docs/presentation/diagrams/secureview-flow.png` and `.svg`): upload, encrypt in the browser, share, authorise, decrypt. No other diagrams, on purpose.
- 在 `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` 里加了一张分享时序图，严格按 `share.ts`、`ShareDialog.vue` 和 `sharing_service.py` 的调用顺序画，包括解包 DEK 之前的解锁，以及取信封的 `GET /files/{file_id}`；另渲染成 `docs/presentation/diagrams/share-sequence.png`。幻灯片用一张直观的流程图（`docs/presentation/diagrams/secureview-flow.png` 和 `.svg`）：上传、浏览器加密、分享、授权下载、解密。有意不再画别的图。
- The AC4a recovery slide uses seven screenshots in `docs/presentation/screenshots/`, taken by Playwright on a local stack with the new `E2E_BROWSER=capture` mode of `run_live_stack.sh` (never used by CI), so they can be re-taken after a UI change. `docs/presentation/RTM_DEMO_MAP.md` maps AC1 to AC8 and AC4a to the demo step where each is seen, what is on screen and the tests behind it. The demo script gains the Education opening (AC6), an Access history step for the audit trail (AC5) and the morning email check that decides whether registration (AC1) is shown live.
- AC4a 恢复那页幻灯片用 `docs/presentation/screenshots/` 里的七张截图，由 Playwright 在本地 stack 上用 `run_live_stack.sh` 新增的 `E2E_BROWSER=capture` 模式截取（CI 不用它），界面改了可以重截。`docs/presentation/RTM_DEMO_MAP.md` 把 AC1 到 AC8 加 AC4a 对应到演示的哪一步、屏幕上看到什么以及背后的测试。演示脚本加了 Education 开场（AC6）、看审计记录的 Access history 一步（AC5），以及当天早上的邮件测试，用来决定注册（AC1）是否现场演示。
- Taking the screenshots showed that the owner's Details drawer for a tampered file said in red "The list of people could not be loaded": the server lists recipients only for an intact file and answers 404 otherwise. The drawer now says in grey that sharing details are hidden until the file is restored. Recipients never saw this.
- 截图时发现，被篡改文件的所有者打开 Details 抽屉会看到红色的 “The list of people could not be loaded”：服务器只为完好的文件列出接收方，否则返回 404。抽屉现在用灰字说明文件恢复前不显示分享信息。接收方本来就看不到这一处。
- Records: WBS 5.4 is Done on the evidence of informal real use (supervisor, team, teaching team, other students), stated as not a formal study; 5.3 stays In review and now says the missing piece is an independent external review. `THREAT_MODEL.md` is version 1.3 with a matching revision entry (its header said 1.1 while the history was at 1.2). `REFLECTION.md` row 6 now states the measured cause in the problem column, matching its status, and the "no diagrams" row is done.
- 记录：WBS 5.4 依据非正式真实使用（导师、组员、教学团队和其他同学）改为 Done，并写明不是正式研究；5.3 保持 In review，写清缺的是独立外部评审。`THREAT_MODEL.md` 升到 1.3 并补了对应修订记录（原来头部写 1.1，修订历史已到 1.2）。`REFLECTION.md` 第 6 行问题列改成实测原因，和状态列一致；“幻灯片没有图”一行标为完成。

### Crypto: AES-192-GCM in every browser, every suite shared end to end, vectors checked server-side / 加密：AES-192-GCM 在所有浏览器可用、六种套件都走完分享、后端也校验测试向量

- Chromium's WebCrypto has no AES-192, so Chrome and Edge users saw five upload suites while Firefox and Safari saw six, and the education pages had to hedge ("where the browser supports it"). The client now probes WebCrypto once per AES key length and, where it refuses, performs AES-GCM in `@noble/ciphers` (the library already used for GCM-SIV and the ChaCha suites). Standard GCM is standard GCM: the tests show the library's ciphertext and tag equal WebCrypto's byte for byte under the same key, nonce and AAD, each side opens what the other sealed, the 256-bit primitive vectors and the two 192-bit file envelopes from `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` reproduce, and with `importKey` mocked to refuse 24-byte keys all six suites are still offered and AES-192 runs without touching WebCrypto. AES-256-GCM never falls back: the account envelopes depend on it. The upload page note and the education pages now say what actually happens instead of "not available in every browser".
- Chromium 的 WebCrypto 没有 AES-192，所以 Chrome 和 Edge 用户上传页只有五种套件，Firefox 和 Safari 有六种，教育页也只能含糊地写“浏览器支持时提供”。现在客户端按 AES 密钥长度各探测一次 WebCrypto，被拒绝时改用 `@noble/ciphers` 做 AES-GCM（GCM-SIV 和 ChaCha 套件本来就用这个库）。标准 GCM 就是标准 GCM：测试证明同一密钥、nonce、AAD 下库的密文和 tag 与 WebCrypto 逐字节相等，一边封装另一边能打开，`docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` 里的 256 位原语向量和两个 192 位文件信封都能复现，把 `importKey` 模拟成拒绝 24 字节密钥后六种套件仍全部可选且 AES-192 完全不经过 WebCrypto。AES-256-GCM 不会回退：账户信封依赖它。上传页说明和教育页现在写的是实际行为，而不是“不是每个浏览器都可用”。
- The live stack suite shared only the ChaCha20-Poly1305 file. Sharing is where a suite's key length and library path meet a second account, so it now runs for each of the six: the owner shares, the recipient sees it in their list, decrypts it with their own passphrase to the same bytes, the server's integrity check reports VALID, the recipient cannot pass it on, and after revocation read, download and integrity check all return the uniform 404. 929 tests, none skipped.
- 实机测试套件原来只分享 ChaCha20-Poly1305 那一个文件。分享正是套件的密钥长度和库路径遇到第二个账户的地方，所以现在六种各跑一遍：所有者分享，接收方在列表里看到、用自己的口令解出相同字节、服务器完整性检查返回 VALID、接收方不能再转发，撤销后读取、下载和完整性检查都返回统一的 404。929 个测试，零跳过。
- `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` was generated by python-cryptography but only ever read by the frontend, so a regenerated or hand-edited vector would be caught only when TypeScript disagreed with it. `backend/tests/unit/test_crypto_vectors.py` now checks every list against the library that produced it: AES-256-GCM, ChaCha20-Poly1305, AES-256-GCM-SIV and XChaCha20-Poly1305 detached vectors, SHA-256, the public-key fingerprint through `CryptoService`, the file-content AAD layout (including NFC and lower-case media type normalisation), Argon2id KEKs through `argon2-cffi`, and the full file envelopes under all six suites. python-cryptography has no XChaCha20-Poly1305, so the test computes the HChaCha20 subkey itself (checked against the draft-irtf-cfrg-xchacha vector) and seals with the library's ChaCha20-Poly1305; nothing is skipped, which `mysql-gate` requires.
- `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` 由 python-cryptography 生成，却只有前端在读，所以重新生成或手改过的向量只有在 TypeScript 不一致时才会被发现。新增的 `backend/tests/unit/test_crypto_vectors.py` 用生成它的库逐条校验：AES-256-GCM、ChaCha20-Poly1305、AES-256-GCM-SIV 和 XChaCha20-Poly1305 的分离 tag 向量、SHA-256、经 `CryptoService` 计算的公钥指纹、文件内容 AAD 布局（含 NFC 和媒体类型小写归一化）、经 `argon2-cffi` 派生的 Argon2id KEK，以及六种套件下的完整文件信封。python-cryptography 没有 XChaCha20-Poly1305，测试自己算 HChaCha20 子密钥（先用 draft-irtf-cfrg-xchacha 的向量校验过），再用库里的 ChaCha20-Poly1305 封装；没有任何跳过，`mysql-gate` 要求这一点。
- `docs/crypto/FILE_ENVELOPE.md` said a client must decrypt the file with the DEK before sharing it, and `share.ts` never did, because that needs the whole ciphertext downloaded first. The document now describes what the client does and why it is enough: RSA-OAEP unwrapping is authenticated and `unwrapDek` checks the length, the rewrap cannot be checked without the recipient's key, and a wrong DEK only makes the recipient's download fail its tag check after the server's SHA-256 comparison, so a bad share is a grant that does not work, not a disclosure. `SECURITY_FLOWS.md` no longer asks for a self-test "where practical".
- `docs/crypto/FILE_ENVELOPE.md` 原来写分享前必须用 DEK 把文件解一遍，而 `share.ts` 从来没这么做，因为那要先把整份密文下载下来。文档现在写的是客户端实际做法和为什么够用：RSA-OAEP 解包本身带认证、`unwrapDek` 检查长度，rewrap 没有接收方私钥无法验证，错的 DEK 只会让接收方下载在服务器比对 SHA-256 之后过不了 tag 校验，所以分享错了是一个不生效的授权而不是泄露。`SECURITY_FLOWS.md` 也不再要求“可行时自测”。

### Per-address limits resized for a venue network / 按地址的限制按会场网络重新设定

- Every rate limit keyed on the client address treated one address as one person. At the final presentation the whole room shares one Wi-Fi and reaches the server as one IP, and with the old values the room would have throttled itself: nginx allowed 12 credential checks a minute and 10 uploads in flight per address, the backend allowed 10 registrations an hour per address, and fail2ban banned an address for an hour after 20 responses of 401 or 429 anywhere under `/api/v1/auth/`, which every idle tab produces once its access token expires. The per-address limits are now sized for about a hundred people behind one address (nginx 30 requests a second and 60 credential checks a minute, 50 uploads in flight, backend 500 failed sign-ins, 200 registrations and 300 codes per address; fail2ban 60 rejected credentials in ten minutes then a 15-minute ban, counting only a 401 on the routes that actually check a password or a code). Per-account limits are unchanged. The nginx auth zone now covers only the credential routes; refresh, `/auth/me` and logout are ordinary API traffic. The table and the reasoning are in THREAT_MODEL §11.6, the install commands in DEPLOYMENT.
- 所有按客户端地址计数的限流都把一个地址当成一个人。答辩时整个会场共用一个 Wi-Fi，到服务器只有一个 IP，按原来的数值会场会把自己限住：nginx 每地址每分钟只允许 12 次凭证校验、同时 10 个上传，后端每地址每小时只允许 10 次注册，fail2ban 在 `/api/v1/auth/` 下任意 20 次 401 或 429 之后封一小时，而每个闲置标签页在访问令牌过期后都会产生 401。现在按地址的限制按"一百人共用一个地址"设定（nginx 每秒 30 次请求、每分钟 60 次凭证校验、50 个并发上传，后端每地址 500 次登录失败、200 次注册、300 个验证码；fail2ban 十分钟内 60 次凭证被拒后封 15 分钟，而且只算真正校验密码或验证码的路由上的 401）。按账户的限制不变。nginx 的 auth 区现在只覆盖凭证路由；刷新、`/auth/me` 和登出按普通 API 流量处理。数值表和理由在 THREAT_MODEL 第 11.6 节，安装命令在 DEPLOYMENT。

### Demo readiness: errors look like errors, checks show progress, the key stays open while you work / 演示准备：错误按错误显示，检查有进度，用着的时候密钥不再锁

- On the Files page, a failed list load, delete or integrity check used to appear in the blue "done" style, and a 500 showed the raw "Request failed with status code 500". They now use the red error line with the API's own message. The status badge and the Details drawer's "Check integrity" button show a spinner and "Checking…" while the server re-hashes the file, which for a large file takes a few seconds and used to look like nothing happened. The list reloads quietly when the tab comes back to the front (at most once every five seconds), so a recipient sees a revocation or a restored file without pressing Refresh.
- 文件页里列表加载失败、删除失败、完整性检查失败原来都用蓝色"完成"样式显示，500 时直接露出 "Request failed with status code 500"。现在都走红色错误行并显示接口的实际信息。状态徽章和详情抽屉的 "Check integrity" 按钮在服务器重新算哈希期间显示转圈和 "Checking…"，大文件要几秒，以前看起来像没反应。标签页回到前台时列表会静默重新加载（最多每五秒一次），接收方不用按 Refresh 就能看到撤销或恢复后的状态。
- Sharing: only an HTTP 404 means "no such account"; a network error or a 500 is shown as what it is instead of "No account matches". Sharing a file with yourself is refused in the dialog as soon as the lookup returns your own account, with the Confirm button disabled, instead of the server's "canonical owner grant" message.
- 分享：只有 HTTP 404 才表示"没有这个账号"，网络错误或 500 按原样提示，不再一律显示 "No account matches"。分享给自己时，查到的是自己的账号就立刻在对话框里拒绝并禁用确认按钮，不再出现服务器的 "canonical owner grant" 那条提示。
- The unlocked account key relocked after fifteen minutes without a decryption, even while the person was clicking around, which on stage meant a surprise passphrase prompt. Any click, key press, pointer press or return to the tab now extends the fifteen minutes (checked at most once every thirty seconds); fifteen idle minutes still relock it. Covered by fake-timer unit tests.
- 解锁的账户密钥原来是 15 分钟内没有解密操作就重新锁上，哪怕人一直在点页面，演示时会突然弹出口令框。现在任何点击、按键、指针按下或切回标签页都会把 15 分钟续上（每 30 秒最多计一次），15 分钟完全没动才重锁。有假定时器的单元测试。
- Chinese wording fixes from the machine draft: "篡改明显审计日志" is now "防篡改审计日志", wrapped and sealed both read "封装", recovery officer reads "恢复审批人", "单向指纹识别" reads "单向指纹", two garbled sentences in the registration and passphrase-reset cards were rewritten, and English full stops inside Chinese sentences were replaced. The passphrase-too-short message no longer borrows the username key. The unused placeholder `FileTable.vue` is gone.
- 修正了机翻中文："篡改明显审计日志"改为"防篡改审计日志"，wrapped 和 sealed 统一译"封装"，recovery officer 译"恢复审批人"，"单向指纹识别"改"单向指纹"，注册页和口令重设卡片的两句病句重写，中文句子里的英文句号换成中文句号。口令过短的提示不再借用用户名的文案。没人用的占位组件 `FileTable.vue` 已删除。
- `docs/presentation/DEMO_SCRIPT.md`: the tamper step is now prepared the day before with `demo_tamper.py flip` (full commands, what the nightly sweep and the agent do to the flipped file, nothing restores it by itself), the stage shows one refused download and then the Security agent's Critical finding, and recovery is a slide with screenshots. The checklists add the recovery-key check, a small demo file, `npm ci` for a local build, and the new unlock behaviour.
- `docs/presentation/DEMO_SCRIPT.md`：篡改改为前一天用 `demo_tamper.py flip` 准备好（完整命令、夜间扫描和代理会对翻过字节的文件做什么、没有任何东西会自动恢复），现场只展示一次被拒的下载，紧接着看安全代理的 Critical 发现，恢复用截图放在幻灯片上。清单里补了恢复公钥检查、用小文件演示、本机构建先 `npm ci`，以及新的解锁行为。

### Ops: pre-deploy backup pruning installed and run on staging / 运维：部署前备份清理已安装并在 staging 上运行

- The #204 `mcs21-deploy` was installed in `/usr/local/sbin` on 2026-10-08 (the previous script kept in `/root`), and a forced redeploy of `f826165` ran the first prune: 175 old sets removed, 10 kept, `/var/backups/mcs21-deploy` 81 GB → 7.5 GB, root disk 69 % → 51 %. The deployment itself succeeded and the site stayed healthy; the audit log records `deploy_backups_pruned removed=175 kept=10`.
- #204 的 `mcs21-deploy` 已于 2026-10-08 安装到 `/usr/local/sbin`（旧脚本备份在 `/root`），并通过强制重新部署 `f826165` 完成第一次清理：删除 175 份旧备份、保留 10 份，`/var/backups/mcs21-deploy` 从 81 GB 降到 7.5 GB，根盘占用从 69% 降到 51%。部署本身成功、网站保持正常；审计日志记录为 `deploy_backups_pruned removed=175 kept=10`。

### Settings: the two top columns end at the same line / 设置页：上方两栏底边对齐

- The Settings page's top grid aligned its columns to the top, so the right column (Account, key fingerprint) ended well above the left (Appearance, Language) and left an empty band. Both columns now stretch to the same height; the fingerprint card fills the rest and puts the fingerprint, now in a framed box, at its bottom, level with the language buttons. Below `lg` the page is one column, unchanged.
- 设置页上方的网格原来是顶部对齐，右栏（账户、密钥指纹）比左栏（外观、语言）短一截，下面空出一块。现在两栏等高；密钥指纹卡片填满剩余高度，指纹改放在带边框的框里并贴在卡片底部，与左边的语言按钮齐平。`lg` 以下的单栏布局不变。

### Passphrase: reject common, patterned and account-derived passphrases (F-04) / 口令：拒绝常见、有规律或由账户信息构成的口令（F-04） ([PR #205](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/205))

- The encryption passphrase only had a 10-character minimum, so `password123` or `aaaaaaaaaa` passed, and the threat model measured that a list passphrase falls in minutes on one laptop despite Argon2id (F-04, §9.4). The passphrase never reaches the server, so only the browser can check it: registration, the passphrase change card and the key-file reset now refuse passphrases on a common list (the server's login deny-list at 10+ characters plus passphrase defaults), repeated or sequential characters, and ones that are mostly the username or email, and suggest four or more random words. A real phrase that merely contains the name still passes. F-04 is mitigated; it remains a client-side check that a modified client could skip.
- 加密口令原来只要求至少 10 个字符，`password123`、`aaaaaaaaaa` 都能通过；威胁模型实测，即使有 Argon2id，列表里的口令在一台笔记本上几分钟就会被猜中（F-04，第 9.4 节）。口令从不发到服务器，所以只能在浏览器里检查：注册、修改口令卡片和私钥文件重设现在都会拒绝常见列表中的口令（服务器登录拒绝列表里 10 位以上的条目，加上常见口令默认值）、重复或连续的字符，以及主要由用户名或邮箱构成的口令，并建议使用四个或更多随机单词。只是包含名字的正常短语仍可通过。F-04 已缓解；它仍是客户端检查，被改过的客户端可以绕过。

### Log each MySQL deadlock retry / 每次 MySQL 死锁重试都写日志 ([PR #202](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/202))

- `run_with_deadlock_retry` (#139) now logs one warning on `secureview.db` each time it retries a deadlock (1213) or lock-wait timeout (1205), with the error code and attempt number only, never the statement or its parameters. The #140 load test could prove no deadlock reached a user but not whether a retry ever fired; the next run can answer that from the journal.
- `run_with_deadlock_retry`（#139）每次重试死锁（1213）或锁等待超时（1205）时，都会在 `secureview.db` 上写一条 warning，只含错误码和第几次尝试，从不包含 SQL 语句或参数。#140 压测只能证明没有死锁影响到用户，却无法证明重试是否发生过；下一次压测可以直接从日志里看到。

### Settings: change the encryption passphrase with the current one / 设置页：凭当前口令修改加密口令 ([PR #201](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/201))

- Until now the only way to a new encryption passphrase was the private-key file (#80), so someone who simply wanted a stronger passphrase had to dig out a file that should stay locked away. Settings now has a "Change your encryption passphrase" card: the current passphrase opens the stored key in the browser, and the same path as the key-file reset seals it again under a new account master key, signs the replacement with the key, and reads it back to check it opens. A wrong current passphrase is refused before anything is sealed or sent; neither passphrase leaves the browser, and a stolen session alone still cannot change it. No backend or schema change. Covered by unit tests, the live passphrase-reset suite (changes it for real and decrypts a file uploaded before) and a golden-path browser step.
- 以前想换加密口令只能用私钥文件（#80），只是想换个更强口令的人也得把本该锁好的文件翻出来。设置页新增“修改加密口令”卡片：当前口令在浏览器中打开已存的私钥，再走与私钥文件重设相同的路径，用新的账户主密钥重新封装、用私钥签名后提交，并读回确认能打开。当前口令错误时，任何东西都不会被封装或发送；两个口令都不离开浏览器，仅凭偷来的会话仍然改不了。后端和 Schema 均无改动。由单元测试、实时口令重设测试（真实修改并解密修改前上传的文件）和主流程浏览器测试覆盖。

### Deploy: keep only the newest 10 pre-deploy backup sets / 部署：只保留最新 10 份部署前备份 ([PR #204](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/204))

- `mcs21-deploy` dumps the database and archives every ciphertext object before each deployment, and nothing ever deleted them: 178 sets and 76 GB on 2026-10-08, with the shared root disk 65 % full. After a healthy deployment it now keeps the newest `MCS21_KEEP_DEPLOY_BACKUPS` (default 10), only ever considers directories with the names it creates, refuses to keep fewer than 3, and audits how many it removed. A failed deployment never prunes, so the rollback history is never shortened by a bad release. Off-server copies remain the job of `mcs21-offsite-backup`. Takes effect once the script is reinstalled in `/usr/local/sbin`.
- `mcs21-deploy` 每次部署前都会导出数据库并打包所有密文对象，而这些备份从未被删除：2026-10-08 已有 178 份、76 GB，共用的根盘已用 65%。现在部署健康后只保留最新的 `MCS21_KEEP_DEPLOY_BACKUPS` 份（默认 10），只处理脚本自己创建的目录名，拒绝保留少于 3 份，并在审计日志里记下删了几份。部署失败时从不清理，所以坏发布不会缩短可回滚的历史。异地副本仍由 `mcs21-offsite-backup` 负责。需要把脚本重新安装到 `/usr/local/sbin` 后生效。

### Upload: a close button for the seal receipt (AC7) / 上传：封存回执加上关闭按钮（AC7） ([PR #200](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/200))

- The seal receipt on the Upload page (read, encrypt and self-check, wrap the file key, upload, then the bytes before and after) now has a close button once nothing is running. AC7 in the proposal asks for a *dismissible* progress pipeline; the four stages shipped in #156, and this was the missing half, so every product acceptance criterion and milestone M7 are now Done. The golden-path browser test closes the receipt and checks it leaves the page.
- 上传页的封存回执（读取、加密并自检、包装文件密钥、上传，以及加密前后的字节对比）在没有任务运行时多了一个关闭按钮。提案的 AC7 要求进度流程*可关闭*；四个阶段已在 #156 上线，缺的就是这一半，所以现在所有产品验收标准和里程碑 M7 都是 Done。主流程浏览器测试会关闭回执并确认它从页面上消失。

### Dependencies: Vue 3.5.43 and source-map-js 1.2.2 for two new high-severity advisories / 依赖：为两条新的高危公告升级到 Vue 3.5.43 与 source-map-js 1.2.2

- On 2026-10-08 `npm audit` began reporting GHSA-g2v6-rqmx-r4w6 (XSS in `@vue/server-renderer` before 3.5.42) and an event-loop denial of service in `source-map-js` before 1.2.2, and the CI audit gate failed every frontend pull request. The app does not server-render, so the Vue advisory was not reachable here, but the gate is what keeps a reachable one out, so the fix is the upgrade rather than an exception. Lockfile and the `vue` floor only; typecheck, 810 unit tests and the build pass.
- 2026-10-08 起 `npm audit` 报告 GHSA-g2v6-rqmx-r4w6（3.5.42 之前的 `@vue/server-renderer` 存在 XSS）以及 1.2.2 之前 `source-map-js` 的事件循环拒绝服务，CI 的依赖审计门禁因此拦下了所有前端 PR。本应用不做服务端渲染，所以 Vue 那条在这里无法被利用；但门禁的作用就是挡住真正可利用的公告，所以处理方式是升级而不是加例外。只改了 lockfile 和 `vue` 的最低版本；类型检查、810 个单元测试和构建均通过。

### Documentation second pass: both languages say the same thing, and open items re-checked on staging / 文档第二轮：中英两版内容一致，未完成事项在 staging 上重新核对

- `backend/README` now carries the same content in both languages (the Chinese lacked the unused format-2 envelope section, the English the passphrase-loss paragraph), the load-test report has a Chinese summary, and statements that had gone stale were checked on the host: the off-site backup is installed and ran on 2026-10-08 (README said "not yet installed"), external audit checkpoints are verified (the threat model's asset table still said "not yet verified"), `quarterly-results.txt` is pre-shared for the tamper demo, and the redesign item is closed. The pre-deploy dumps that `mcs21-deploy` never prunes have grown to 178 sets and 76 GB, recorded in BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.
- `backend/README` 中英两版内容对齐（中文缺未使用的 format 2 信封一节，英文缺 passphrase 遗失一段），压测报告新增中文摘要，并在服务器上核对了已经过时的说法：异地备份已安装并于 2026-10-08 运行成功（README 写的是“尚未安装”）、外部审计检查点已验证（威胁模型资产表仍写“未核实”）、篡改演示用的 `quarterly-results.txt` 已预先分享、改版事项已完成。`mcs21-deploy` 从不清理的发布前备份已增至 178 份、76 GB，已记入 BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY。

### Documentation brought in line with the code and the live system / 文档与代码和线上系统对齐

- Every living document was checked against the code, the CI run on `e4b3f13` and the staging host on 2026-10-07. The worst gap was the claim, in `PROJECT_SCOPE`, that a forgotten passphrase cannot be reset: since #110 the private key file resets it, and only losing both is unrecoverable. Also corrected: the login-password rule (8–128 characters and two character classes, not 15–128), one cipher instead of six, schema revision `0004`/`0007` instead of `0010`, the 13- instead of 15-table runtime grant, missing folder/avatar/integrity/agent endpoints, an integrity check described without the audit-anchored digest and external checkpoints, sharing without the fingerprint confirmation, and pages described as they were before the "Sealed" redesign.
- 对照代码、`e4b3f13` 上的 CI 运行和 2026-10-07 的 staging 主机，逐份核对了所有描述现状的文档。最严重的问题是 `PROJECT_SCOPE` 声称忘记 passphrase 无法重设：自 #110 起私钥文件就能重设，只有两者都丢失才无法恢复。同时更正：登录密码规则（8–128 位且至少两类字符，而不是 15–128 位）、只写了一种加密算法而实际有六种、Schema revision 写成 `0004`/`0007` 而实际为 `0010`、运行时授权写成 13 张表而实际 15 张、缺少文件夹/头像/完整性/安全 Agent 接口、完整性检查没写审计锚定摘要和外部检查点、分享流程缺少指纹确认，以及若干页面仍按“Sealed”改版前描述。
- Added the missing Chinese versions (`AGENTS`, `E2E_TESTING`, `I18N`, `PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY`, `loadtest/README`, the walkthrough and video READMEs), a 2026-10-07 verification table in TODO with the real test counts, a CI-gate section in `E2E_TESTING`, and the bilingual and presentation-records rules in `AGENTS.md` and the PR template, so the next change keeps them current. Dated records (load-test and scan reports, past changelog entries) were left as they were.
- 补齐缺失的中文版（`AGENTS`、`E2E_TESTING`、`I18N`、`PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY`、`loadtest/README`、截图与视频说明），在 TODO 中加入带真实测试数字的 2026-10-07 核验表，在 `E2E_TESTING` 中加入 CI 门禁一节，并把中英双版和答辩材料同步的规矩写进 `AGENTS.md` 和 PR 模板，让以后的改动保持同步。带日期的记录（压测和扫描报告、过去的 changelog 条目）保持原样。

### Admin: account details and login-password reset; change your login password in Settings / 管理员：查看账户信息、重置登录密码；设置页可修改登录密码 ([PR #197](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/197))

- The admin console's user list now shows email, and a Details dialog shows one account: email and whether it is verified, role, status, created, last sign-in, when the password last changed, and counts only (files, stored bytes, shares sent and received, sessions open) from `GET /admin/users/{id}`. No password is ever shown: the login password is stored only as a one-way hash, and the encryption passphrase never reaches the server, so there is nothing to show.
- 管理后台的用户列表新增邮箱一列；“详情”对话框显示单个账户：邮箱及是否验证、角色、状态、创建时间、最近登录、密码最近修改时间，以及只含数字的统计（文件数、占用空间、分享出去和收到的数量、在线会话数），数据来自 `GET /admin/users/{id}`。界面上永远不显示密码：登录密码只以单向哈希保存，加密口令从来不会到达服务器，所以本来就没有可以显示的东西。
- `POST /admin/users/{id}/reset-password` sets a random temporary login password (four groups of four characters, no look-alike characters, checked against the password policy), returns it once with `no-store`, stores only its hash, signs the account out everywhere, writes an `ADMIN_PASSWORD_RESET` audit entry without the password, and emails the holder that it changed (the email does not contain it). It refuses the administrator's own account and other administrators. Files and the encryption passphrase are untouched. No schema change.
- `POST /admin/users/{id}/reset-password` 会生成一个随机临时登录密码（四组、每组四个字符，去掉了容易看错的字符，并按密码规则检查），只在响应里返回一次（`no-store`），数据库只存哈希；同时让该账户在所有设备上退出登录，写一条不含密码的 `ADMIN_PASSWORD_RESET` 审计记录，并发邮件告诉本人密码被改了（邮件里不含密码）。不能重置管理员自己或其他管理员。文件和加密口令都不受影响。不需要改数据库结构。
- Settings has a "Login password" card. `POST /auth/change-password` existed but nothing in the app called it, so after a reset the holder had nowhere to choose their own password. Changing it signs out every session, so the page goes to sign-in with a notice.
- 设置页新增“登录密码”卡片。后端早就有 `POST /auth/change-password`，但前端没有任何地方调用它，所以管理员重置后用户没地方改成自己的密码。修改后所有会话都会退出，页面会跳到登录页并给出提示。
- Checked locally end to end on a throwaway account: after the reset the old password fails and the temporary one works; after the holder changes it in Settings the temporary one fails and their own works; no console errors, Chinese view checked. New backend tests cover the reset (hash only, sessions revoked, audit without the password, refusals for self, other admins, missing and deleted accounts, non-admins), the temporary password format and policy, the notice email, the count queries, and that both routes refuse non-administrators with `no-store`. 503 backend unit tests and 810 frontend tests pass.
- 在本地用一次性测试账号完整跑过：重置后旧密码登录失败、临时密码可以登录；本人在设置里改密后，临时密码失效、新密码可以登录；控制台没有报错，中文界面也核对过。新的后端测试覆盖：重置只存哈希、撤销会话、审计不含密码，拒绝重置自己、其他管理员、不存在和已删除的账户以及非管理员；临时密码的格式和规则；通知邮件；统计查询；两个新接口对非管理员返回 403 且带 `no-store`。后端 503 个单元测试、前端 810 个测试全部通过。

### Deploy: a stalled dependency download no longer hangs the release / 部署：依赖下载卡住不再拖住发布 ([PR #196](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/196))

- On 2026-10-05 the deploy of #195 sat for over seven minutes because one pip connection trickled at ~29 kB/s; it was only unblocked by killing pip and running `mcs21-auto-deploy retry`. `mcs21-deploy` now gives each `pip install` attempt a wall-clock limit (240 s by default) on top of pip's `--timeout 30 --retries 5`, retries a stalled or failed attempt up to three times, and removes the `.build-*` directory when it finally gives up (two had been left on the server). The limits can be overridden with `MCS21_PIP_ATTEMPTS`, `MCS21_PIP_ATTEMPT_SECONDS` and `MCS21_PIP_RETRY_PAUSE`. New tests run the real function against a fake pip that hangs once or always fails.
- 2026-10-05 部署 #195 时，一个 pip 连接以约 29 kB/s 的速度慢慢下载，部署卡了七分多钟，最后是手动结束 pip 再运行 `mcs21-auto-deploy retry` 才过去。现在 `mcs21-deploy` 给每次 `pip install` 加了总时长上限（默认 240 秒），pip 自身也加了 `--timeout 30 --retries 5`；卡住或失败会重试，最多三次；最终放弃时会删掉 `.build-*` 目录（服务器上之前留下了两个）。上限可以用 `MCS21_PIP_ATTEMPTS`、`MCS21_PIP_ATTEMPT_SECONDS`、`MCS21_PIP_RETRY_PAUSE` 调整。新测试用一个“卡一次”或“一直失败”的假 pip 实际运行这个函数。

### Headings and intros: one line where they fit, breaks only between sentences / 标题和简介：放得下就一行，换行只在句与句之间 ([PR #195](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/195))

- A text audit (`frontend/scripts/text-audit.mjs`) measured every heading and short paragraph on 16 pages at 1440, 1280, 1100, 1024, 768 and 390 px in English and Chinese, looking for four things: text capped narrower than its space, a sentence broken mid-way although it would fit on a line of its own, headings made of two sentences, and one or two words left alone on a last line.
- 新增文字巡检脚本（`frontend/scripts/text-audit.mjs`）：在 1440、1280、1100、1024、768、390 px、中英文下量 16 个页面的每个标题和短段落，找四类问题：文字被限宽、本来能单独放一行的句子被从中间折断、由两句话组成的标题、最后一行只剩一两个词。
- Two-sentence intros now break only between sentences, through a new `SentenceLines` component: each sentence is an inline-block, so the text stays on one line when it fits and otherwise starts the next sentence on a new line ("Your first secure share in six screenshots." / "Follow at your own pace, …"). Used by every `PageHeader` subtitle and by the home hero, the picture guide, the Dashboard cards and checklist, My Files, Profile, the 404 page and the How it works tips. In Chinese, `dom-translate.ts` translates the whole text as one entry and gives one Chinese sentence (split at 。) to each block.
- 两句话的简介现在只在句与句之间换行：新增 `SentenceLines` 组件，每句是一个 inline-block，整段放得下就一行，放不下时下一句整句换到新行（“Your first secure share in six screenshots.” / “Follow at your own pace, …”）。所有 `PageHeader` 副标题，以及首页第一屏、图文指南、Dashboard 卡片和新手清单、我的文件、个人资料、404 页和 How it works 的提示都用它。中文由 `dom-translate.ts` 整句查词典，再按“。”把每一句分给对应的块。
- Headings rewritten as one sentence: the home hero and sign-in panel now read "Sealed in your browser, / unreadable to others." (each clause one line from 1024 to 1600 px), "Three moves. The server is trusted with none of them." became "Three steps, no trust in the server.", and How it works is "Locked before it leaves your computer". The home cards are "Sealed before it leaves", "Only to a checked key" and "Changed means refused", sit in three columns from 1024 px (one column below) and show a four-group fingerprint, so none of them wraps. The picture guide's step titles are one short sentence each ("Choose a file to protect.", "Click Share on your file." instead of "Find your file. Click its Share button.").
- 标题改成一句话：首页第一屏和登录页左栏改为 “Sealed in your browser, / unreadable to others.”（1024–1600 px 每半句各占一行）；“Three moves. The server is trusted with none of them.” 改为 “Three steps, no trust in the server.”；How it works 标题改为 “Locked before it leaves your computer”。首页三张卡片的标题改为 “Sealed before it leaves”“Only to a checked key”“Changed means refused”，1024 px 起三列（以下单列），指纹只显示四组，所以都不再折行。图文指南每一步的标题改成一句短话（“Choose a file to protect.”、“Click Share on your file.”，原来是 “Find your file. Click its Share button.”）。
- Running text uses `text-wrap: pretty` (no single word left on a last line) and h4 joins h1–h3 on `text-wrap: balance`. Dashboard cards are two per row until 1280 px so "Upload & Encrypt" and "Recovery requests" stay on one line.
- 正文统一用 `text-wrap: pretty`（最后一行不再只剩一个词），h4 也和 h1–h3 一样用 `text-wrap: balance`。Dashboard 的卡片在 1280 px 以下每行两张，“Upload & Encrypt”“Recovery requests”不再折行。
- Upload: the drop zone grows to the height of the Encryption column, so its bottom edge lines up with Start Encryption. File details: under 1 KB the exact byte count is no longer repeated next to the size ("146 Bytes 146 bytes").
- 上传页：拖放区随右侧加密设置一栏一起拉高，底边与 Start Encryption 按钮对齐。文件详情：小于 1 KB 时不再在大小旁边重复一遍字节数（“146 Bytes 146 bytes”）。

### Home page: a comparison table that reads as questions and answers, and a footer without a gap / 首页：对比表读起来是“问题—两种回答”，页脚中间不再空一块 ([PR #194](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/194))

- The comparison with ordinary cloud storage had an empty top-left cell, and the question column looked like a third answer. It now has a small monospace "Question" label, numbered questions (01–05) in semibold, and a rule between the questions and the two answer columns. The column headings stay left-aligned so the cloud and shield icons line up with the ✗ and ✓ below them; centring them would leave the headings floating over left-aligned answers. The phone cards are unchanged.
- 与普通网盘的对比表原来左上角是空的，问题那一列看上去像第三列答案。现在左上角有一个小号等宽的“Question”标签，问题加粗并编号（01–05），问题列和两列答案之间加了一条竖线。列标题保持左对齐，这样云朵和盾牌图标正好对齐下面的 ✗ 和 ✓；居中的话，标题会悬在左对齐的答案上方。手机上的卡片不变。
- The footer left an empty grid track between the brand block and the link columns (the blurb was also capped at `max-w-md`), and "The project" squeezed one sentence into five lines. The brand block and the three columns now fill all twelve tracks, the cap is gone, and "The project" lists short facts in the same rhythm as Product and Learn: Monash University, FIT3161 / FIT3162, Team MCS21, the supervisor, and "Meet the team →". On a tablet the brand block has its own row and the three columns share the next one. English and Chinese strings are in `en.json` and `zh-CN.json`.
- 页脚在品牌介绍和链接栏之间空着一整列网格（介绍文字还被 `max-w-md` 限了宽），“The project”一栏把一句话挤成了五行。现在品牌区和三栏铺满十二列，去掉了限宽；“The project”改成和 Product、Learn 同样节奏的短条目：莫纳什大学、FIT3161 / FIT3162、MCS21 团队、指导老师，以及“Meet the team →”。平板上品牌区独占一行，三栏在下一行。中英文文案在 `en.json` 和 `zh-CN.json` 里。
- Checked with screenshots at 1440 (light, dark, Chinese), 768 and 390 px: no horizontal overflow, no console errors. Typecheck, the translation check and 806 unit tests pass.
- 在 1440（浅色、深色、中文）、768 和 390 px 下截图检查：没有横向溢出，控制台没有报错。类型检查、中文词典检查和 806 个单元测试都通过。

### My Files: an obvious way into the details panel, and a panel that sits beside the list / 我的文件：详情入口更明显，详情栏改为贴在列表旁边 ([PR #193](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/pull/193))

- The details panel (cipher, exact sizes, ciphertext SHA-256, who can open the file, every action) was only reachable through a small grey "Details" link under the file name, which read as a caption. Now the whole row opens it (blank space in the row; its own controls keep their jobs), the file name is a button that opens it, and the Actions column has a bordered Details button: labelled from 1400 px, icon-only between 768 and 1399 px, and a full-width button under the actions on a phone. The open file's row stays highlighted. The old link is gone.
- 详情栏（算法、精确大小、密文 SHA-256、谁能打开、全部操作）原来只能通过文件名下面一行灰色小字“Details”打开，看上去像说明文字。现在点整行的空白处就能打开（行里自己的控件照常工作），文件名本身也是打开详情的按钮，操作列多了一个带边框的 Details 按钮：1400 px 及以上带文字，768–1399 px 只显示图标，手机上是操作按钮下面一整行的按钮。打开的那一行保持高亮。原来的小字链接删掉了。
- The panel is no longer modal from 768 px up: no grey backdrop, the list stays readable, and clicking another row switches the panel to that file. A click elsewhere on the page or Esc closes it. On a phone it is a bottom sheet over a backdrop, as a modal. We kept it as a side panel rather than a centred dialog: it describes one row, it is long, and the list beside it is the context; dialogs stay for steps that need an answer (unlock, confirm a share).
- 从 768 px 起详情栏不再是模态：没有灰色遮罩，列表保持可读，点另一行就切换到那个文件。点页面其他地方或按 Esc 关闭。手机上是带遮罩的底部弹出面板，仍是模态。我们保留侧边栏而不改成居中弹框：它描述的是某一行，内容长，旁边的列表就是上下文；弹框留给需要用户作答的步骤（解锁、确认分享）。
- Making room for the button: between 1024 and 1279 px the Encrypted At column is hidden (the date is in the panel) and Share is icon-only, which also fixes headings that overlapped and the Verified badge touching the download icon at about 1100 px on main. On a tablet, Access history moves into the panel. Checked with screenshots at 1440, 1280, 1100, 768 and 390 px, light, dark and Chinese: no horizontal page overflow and no console errors. The table at 768 px still scrolls 18 px sideways, as on main. Typecheck, the translation check and 806 unit tests pass.
- 为这个按钮腾地方：1024–1279 px 隐藏 Encrypted At 列（日期在详情栏里有），Share 只显示图标，这也顺带修好了 main 上约 1100 px 时列标题重叠、Verified 徽章压到下载图标的问题。平板上 Access history 收进详情栏。在 1440、1280、1100、768、390 px，浅色、深色和中文下截图检查：页面没有横向溢出，控制台没有报错。768 px 时表格仍可横向滚动 18 px，与 main 一致。类型检查、中文词典检查和 806 个单元测试都通过。

### UI redesign step 4: re-shot picture guide, Help videos and the demo backup video / 界面改版第 4 步：重拍图文指南、帮助页视频和演示备用视频 ([#186](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/issues/186))

- The home page's six-step picture guide is re-captured from the new interface, in English and in Simplified Chinese (`<name>.webp` and `<name>-zh.webp`; the guide shows the set for the language chosen in the header). The capture script now finds the upload and file cards by test id (`encrypt-card`, `files-card`) instead of the old `rounded-3xl` class, and takes both languages from one page state by switching the language in place. The guide's text no longer calls the Share button blue (it is black), and the same fix is made in the Help assistant's sharing steps and their Chinese text.
- 首页六步图文指南已按新界面重拍，英文和简体中文各一套（`<name>.webp` 和 `<name>-zh.webp`；指南按页头里选的语言显示对应那一套）。截图脚本现在用 test id（`encrypt-card`、`files-card`）而不是旧的 `rounded-3xl` 类名来定位上传和文件卡片，并在同一个页面状态里原地切换语言，一次得到两种语言。指南文字不再说“分享”按钮是蓝色（现在是黑色），帮助助手里分享步骤的英文和中文也一并改了。
- The three Help-page videos (how it works, encrypt and upload, share and open) and the presentation's `demo-backup.mp4` (2 min 23 s, with its poster) are re-recorded on a local stack with the documented test accounts, and checked frame by frame: the new sign-in page, sidebar, centred headers, two-column Settings (Bob reading his fingerprint), the seal receipt and the Modified state after the tamper step. The demo still follows `DEMO_SCRIPT.md` step for step.
- 帮助页的三个视频（how it works、encrypt and upload、share and open）和演示用的 `demo-backup.mp4`（2 分 23 秒，含海报图）已用文档里的测试账号在本地环境重新录制，并逐段看过：新的登录页、侧边栏、居中页头、双栏设置页（Bob 读自己的指纹）、封存回执，以及篡改一步之后的 Modified 状态。演示视频仍与 `DEMO_SCRIPT.md` 的步骤一一对应。
- Found while re-shooting: a tab opened before a deploy could not open a lazy page afterwards (the click did nothing, because the page's file had been replaced); the router now reloads the target address once. The algorithm note on Upload was untranslated and its Chinese sentence said the file key and file are "not protected" (the opposite of the English); both are fixed. "Total 1 encrypted file" no longer shows "file" in English inside the Chinese text, and the Status chip no longer wraps.
- 重拍时发现的问题：部署之前打开的标签页，之后无法打开懒加载页面（点了没反应，因为那个页面的文件已被替换）；路由现在会对目标地址整页重载一次。上传页的算法说明没有翻译，而且它的中文句子把意思说反了（写成文件密钥和文件“没有受到保护”）；两处都已修正。“Total 1 encrypted file”在中文里不再夹着英文“file”，“状态”标签也不再折行。

### My Files: centred table, Access history beside Delete / 我的文件：表格居中、访问历史放到删除旁边

- The My Files table is centred from md up: the column headings and every cell, including the file name block (a small file icon before the name, with Details and Encrypted preview centred under it). The Actions column is wider so five controls fit. On a phone the card layout is unchanged.
- 我的文件的表格从 md 起居中：列标题和每个单元格都居中，包括文件名那一块（名字前有一个小文件图标，Details 和 Encrypted preview 在下面居中）。操作列变宽，放得下五个控件。手机上的卡片布局不变。
- "Access history" moved out of the More menu into its own icon button between Delete and More, so one file's history is a single click; the menu keeps Download encrypted file and Request recovery. The phone browser test now taps the button on the card instead of opening the menu.
- “Access history”从“更多”菜单里移出来，成为删除和更多之间的一个图标按钮，查看某个文件的历史只需点一下；菜单里保留 Download encrypted file 和 Request recovery。手机浏览器测试改为点卡片上的这个按钮，不再打开菜单。

### UI fixes P2: near-white primary in dark mode, no pill labels, split sign-in layout / 界面修正 P2：深色主色改近白、去掉胶囊标签、登录页分栏 ([#184](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/issues/184))

- Dark mode `--primary` is now near-white (`#EEF0F3`, text `#07090D`) instead of mint, so Login, Start Encryption, Share and every other primary button is no longer green; mint stays on `--success` (verified, sealed, done). Uses of primary that meant "success" (the check marks in the passphrase reset, the ciphertext preview, the admin panels, the getting-started steps and the algorithm list, the integrity coverage figure) now use the success tokens. A scan of every interactive element on all pages in dark mode finds no mint-coloured control. The home hero keeps its own colours.
- 深色模式的 `--primary` 改为近白（`#EEF0F3`，文字 `#07090D`），不再是薄荷绿，所以“Login”“Start Encryption”“Share”等主按钮都不再是绿色；薄荷绿只留给 `--success`（已验证、已封存、已完成）。原来用主色表示“成功”的地方（重置口令的对勾、密文预览、管理台面板、入门清单步骤、算法列表、完整性覆盖率数字）改用成功色。在深色模式下扫描所有页面的全部可交互元素，没有发现薄荷绿的控件。首页第一屏保留自己的配色。
- The rounded pills above headings (Encryption Education, Product Help, the algorithm type, the demo kind, the supervisor role) are now the small monospace eyebrow; the eyebrows that were in the primary colour are muted. Status badges and filter chips stay.
- 标题上方的圆角胶囊（Encryption Education、Product Help、算法类型、演示类型、导师角色）改成了小号等宽小标题；原来用主色的小标题改为弱色。状态徽章和筛选标签保留。
- Sign in, Register and Forgot password share one `AuthShell`: from lg up a dark brand panel on the left (the home hero's ciphertext wall, drawn by the same `byteRows` seed function, now in `utils/byte-rows.ts` and `CipherWall.vue`, plus the one-line positioning and three measured results from `data/proof.ts`, the same list the home page uses) and the form about 420px wide on the right; below lg only the form. The wall stops animating under `prefers-reduced-motion`. Labels, the `Login` button and test ids are unchanged. The browser suite caught that the single-column grid track let the Register page grow past a phone's width; it is fixed with a `minmax(0, 1fr)` track.
- 登录、注册、找回密码共用一个 `AuthShell`：lg 及以上左边是深色品牌区（首页第一屏的密文墙，由同一个 `byteRows` 种子函数绘制，现在放在 `utils/byte-rows.ts` 和 `CipherWall.vue` 里，再加一句话定位和三个实测结果，数据来自 `data/proof.ts`，与首页是同一份），右边是约 420px 宽的表单；lg 以下只显示表单。开启 `prefers-reduced-motion` 时密文墙不再动。字段标签、`Login` 按钮和 test id 都没变。浏览器测试发现单列网格会让注册页在手机上被撑宽，已改用 `minmax(0, 1fr)` 轨道修复。
- The Argon2 benchmark page already uses `PageHeader` and the shared container from #183. Typecheck, the translation check, 812 unit tests and the 57-test Chromium suite pass.
- Argon2 基准页已经在 #183 里改用 `PageHeader` 和统一容器。类型检查、中文词典检查、812 个单元测试和 57 个 Chromium 测试都通过。

### UI fixes P3: no failed requests on Access Records, bigger step-dot targets, no 768px overflow / 界面修正 P3：访问记录不再有失败请求、步骤点更好点、768px 不再溢出 ([#185](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/issues/185))

- Access Records no longer produces a 4xx or a console error. An ordinary user's page used to call `POST /audit-logs/verify-chain` (administrators only) on every visit and got a 403, and every deleted file was looked up with `GET /files/{id}` and got a 404. The chain check now runs only for administrators, and `GET /audit-logs` and `GET /audit-logs/file/{id}` carry `file_name` and `file_deleted` on rows about a file (one query for the whole page; a deleted file is a soft delete, so its name is still in the `files` row and the hash chain is untouched). The page shows the name with a "Deleted" tag, or "Deleted file" if even the row is gone, and the per-file lookup code (`utils/file-names.ts`) is removed. Access to rows is unchanged: a name is added only to rows the caller could already read.
- 访问记录页不再产生任何 4xx 或控制台错误。以前普通用户每次打开都会调用只有管理员能用的 `POST /audit-logs/verify-chain`，返回 403；每个已删除的文件还会被 `GET /files/{id}` 逐个查询，返回 404。现在哈希链校验只对管理员运行；`GET /audit-logs` 和 `GET /audit-logs/file/{id}` 在与文件有关的行上带上 `file_name` 和 `file_deleted`（整页只查一次；删除文件是软删除，名字仍在 `files` 表里，哈希链不受影响）。页面显示文件名并加“Deleted”标记，连这一行都没有时显示“Deleted file”；逐个查文件名的代码（`utils/file-names.ts`）已删除。能看到哪些行没有变：只给调用者本来就能读的行补上名字。
- The seven step dots in the animated explainer (How it works, and "Under the hood" on Upload) are still small to look at but are now buttons at least 24px wide and 32px tall, still keyboard-focusable and labelled "Step n: title". Chromium at 768px found the sideways overflow: the footer's project column is two grid tracks wide there and the unbreakable "(FIT3161/FIT3162)" pushed the page 12px (4px at some heights) sideways; the footer columns now shrink and break words. The layout audit reports `overflowX` 0 at 1440, 768 and 390 and, with the 100-view run, no errors.
- 动画讲解里的七个步骤点（How it works，以及上传页的“Under the hood”）看起来仍然小，但现在是至少 24px 宽、32px 高的按钮，仍可用键盘聚焦，标签是“Step n: 标题”。768px 的横向溢出是在 Chromium 里查到的：页脚的“项目”列在这个宽度只有两格网格宽，里面不能断开的“(FIT3161/FIT3162)”把整页撑宽了 12px（某些高度下是 4px）；页脚各列现在可以收缩并换行。巡检在 1440、768 和 390 下 `overflowX` 都是 0，100 个页面视图没有任何错误。
- Item 13: on a local API from the latest `main`, My Files → Details shows "Ciphertext SHA-256" with the Copy button (`file-sha256`). The same check on https://secureview.tech is left to the project lead, who signs in as `demo-alice`; nothing here signs in to the live site. Verified: backend 553 unit and API tests, 17 MySQL file-lifecycle tests (one new), frontend typecheck, 806 unit tests, the live-stack suites 862 passed with no skips, and the 57-test Chromium suite.
- 第 13 项：在用最新 `main` 启动的本地 API 上，“我的文件”→ Details 显示“Ciphertext SHA-256”和 Copy 按钮（`file-sha256`）。线上站点 https://secureview.tech 的同一项检查留给项目负责人用 `demo-alice` 登录确认；这里没有任何登录线上站点的操作。已验证：后端 553 个单元和 API 测试、17 个 MySQL 文件生命周期测试（新增 1 个）、前端类型检查、806 个单元测试、live-stack 套件 862 个通过且无跳过，以及 57 个 Chromium 测试。

### UI fixes P1: one centred page header, one content width, two-column Settings and Profile, sidebar on every signed-in page / 界面修正 P1：统一居中页头、统一内容宽度、双栏设置和资料页、登录后所有页面保留侧边栏 ([#183](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/issues/183))

- New `PageHeader` component replaces every page's own title block and the old `.product-heading` CSS: centred, an optional small monospace eyebrow (plain text, not a pill), a 48px title (32px on a phone), a one-sentence subtitle that is never capped (a paragraph keeps a centred 70ch measure), and an actions slot. It is used by Your Workspace, Upload, My Files, Access Records, Recovery requests / Admin console, Profile, Settings, Education, Help, Contact us, How it works and the Argon2 benchmark; the home page's "Three moves" heading is centred too. This reverses the left-aligned headings of #182: a left-aligned title above centred content looked wrong, and the project lead asked for centred headers on one centre line with the content. App pages share one container (`.page-container`, 72rem) instead of each page choosing its own width; Access Records, Recovery requests, Settings and Profile were full width or a narrow strip before.
- 新增 `PageHeader` 组件，替换各页面自己写的标题块和旧的 `.product-heading` 样式：居中，可选的小号等宽小标题（纯文字，不是胶囊），48px 标题（手机 32px），一句话简介不限宽（长段落保留居中的 70ch 行宽），以及一个操作插槽。工作区、上传、我的文件、访问记录、恢复请求 / 管理台、个人资料、设置、教育、帮助、联系我们、How it works 和 Argon2 基准页都用它；首页“Three moves”标题也改为居中。这推翻了 #182 的左对齐标题：左对齐标题配居中内容看起来很怪，项目负责人要求页头居中并与内容同一条中线。应用页统一用同一个容器（`.page-container`，72rem），不再每页各写一套宽度；访问记录、恢复请求、设置和个人资料之前要么满宽，要么是窄窄一条。
- Settings is two columns from lg up (Appearance and Language on the left, Account and the key fingerprint on the right, Reset passphrase and Delete account full width below); Profile has an identity card (initials or picture, name, email, role, verified badge, member since) beside the details and actions; Recovery requests gets a proper empty state with an icon. Help, Education and Contact us no longer drop the sidebar when you are signed in: the sidebar shows on every page except the home page and the sign-in pages, and the current page is highlighted. Card body text in Upload, Workspace and the getting-started card is 14px instead of 12px.
- 设置页在 lg 及以上分两栏（左边外观和语言，右边账户和密钥指纹，重置口令和删除账户各占整行放在下面）；个人资料页左边是身份卡（首字母或头像、用户名、邮箱、角色、已验证徽章、注册时间），右边是详情和操作；恢复请求没有内容时有像样的空状态（图标加说明）。登录后点“帮助 / 教育 / 联系我们”不再丢掉侧边栏：除首页和登录类页面外都显示侧边栏，并高亮当前页。上传页、工作区和入门卡片里的说明文字从 12px 改为 14px。
- `frontend/scripts/layout-audit.mjs` is committed: it opens every page in five configurations (1440 light, 1440 dark, 768, 390, 1440 Chinese), takes full-page screenshots and reports squeezed headings, horizontal overflow, clipped text, tiny text, small targets, untranslated English and console or HTTP errors. It reads accounts from a local file through `ACCOUNTS` and contains no credentials. After this change `wraps` is 0 on every page in English, and `overflowX` is 0 at 1440 and 390 (the 768 overflow and the Access Records 403 are #185). Typecheck, the translation check and the unit tests pass.
- 提交了 `frontend/scripts/layout-audit.mjs`：它在五种配置（1440 浅色、1440 深色、768、390、1440 中文）下打开每个页面，截全页图，并报告被挤成多行的标题、横向溢出、被截断的文字、过小文字、过小点击区域、未翻译的英文以及控制台或 HTTP 错误。账号通过 `ACCOUNTS` 从本地文件读取，脚本里不含任何账号信息。改动后英文下所有页面的 `wraps` 均为 0，1440 和 390 下 `overflowX` 为 0（768 的溢出和访问记录的 403 属于 #185）。类型检查、中文词典检查和单元测试都通过。

### Test accounts recorded in `docs/TEST_ACCOUNTS.md` / 测试账号记录在 `docs/TEST_ACCOUNTS.md`

### Home page: "Three moves" heading on one line / 首页“三步”标题改为一行

- The heading above the Seal, Share and Verify cards sat in a box capped at 56 rem, so it broke onto two lines with empty space on its right. The cap is removed; it now takes the full width and is one line at desktop widths (checked in a browser at 1440 px).
- “封存、分享、验证”三张卡片上方的标题放在一个最大宽度 56 rem 的容器里，所以被折成两行，右边空出一大块。现已去掉这个限制，标题占满整行，桌面宽度下是一行（已在 1440 px 的真实浏览器里确认）。

Access records: the heading, refresh button and "Hash chain verified" line are left-aligned, the verified line is a green badge, the four statistics lose their coloured tiles, the file groups use the full width instead of a centred column, event rows use monospace for who and when, and "Failed" uses the destructive tint. All texts, buttons and test ids are unchanged.
- 工作区：标题改为与卡片左对齐，不再居中；四张入口卡片去掉了带底色的图标块，悬停时不再出现阴影而是边框变深；最近文件的大小改用等宽字体；页面更矮。访问记录：标题、刷新按钮和“Hash chain verified”一行都左对齐，“已验证”那一行改成绿色徽章，四项统计去掉彩色底块，文件分组占满宽度而不是居中窄栏，事件行里“谁”和“何时”改用等宽字体，“Failed”使用危险色底。所有文字、按钮和 test id 都没有变。
