### UI redesign, step 3b: the upload page and the seal receipt / 界面改版第 3b 步：上传页和封存回执

- The encryption progress panel is now a "Seal receipt": the file name with a SEALED (or Sealing…) badge, the four real steps with their measured times and green ticks, and the first bytes of the file next to what the server receives in a dark panel with the ciphertext in mint. It is the same data and the same animation as before (`data-testid`s, step titles and the "Your file" and "What the server receives" labels are unchanged); only the look changes. The upload page itself gets a larger drop zone with a dashed outline, a monospace cipher selector, a calmer heading size on every product page (`.product-heading`), smaller corner radii and a success tick instead of the brand colour.
- 加密进度面板现在是一张“封存回执”：文件名加上“SEALED（或 Sealing…）”徽章，四个真实步骤带着实测耗时和绿色对勾，以及文件开头的字节与服务器收到的内容的对比，放在深色面板里，密文用薄荷绿显示。数据和动画都和以前完全一样（`data-testid`、各步骤标题以及“Your file”“What the server receives”两个标签都没变），只换了外观。上传页本身的拖放区更大，用虚线轮廓，算法选择器改用等宽字体，所有产品页的标题（`.product-heading`）字号更克制，圆角更小，成功状态用绿色对勾而不是品牌色。
- Checked against a local stack in light and dark with a real upload. Typecheck and 812 unit tests pass. The upload page's other parts (the "Under the hood" animation below, the progress logic) are untouched. Workspace cards and the access-record and settings pages are still to restyle.
- 已在本地环境用真实上传在浅色和深色下检查。类型检查和 812 个单元测试通过。上传页的其他部分（下方的“Under the hood”动画、进度逻辑）没有改动。工作区卡片、访问记录页和设置页仍待改。

### UI redesign, step 3a: a sidebar for the signed-in app and a file detail panel / 界面改版第 3a 步：登录后应用的侧边栏和文件详情栏

- Signed in, the app pages (`/dashboard`, `/upload`, `/files`, `/audit-logs`, `/admin`, `/profile`, `/settings`) now have a left sidebar from the lg breakpoint up, instead of the marketing header with its links. It carries the same links in the same words (Dashboard, Upload, My Files, Access Records, the recovery or admin console, Help, Education, Contact us), highlights the current page, and shows at the bottom whether this browser currently holds the account key ("Key unlocked in this browser" or "Key locked") with the first four groups of its fingerprint, plus the user name and log out. The header keeps the language switch and the account menu and, below lg, the menu button, so phones are unchanged; the large footer is hidden beside the sidebar. Public pages and the home page keep the header.
- 登录后，应用页面（`/dashboard`、`/upload`、`/files`、`/audit-logs`、`/admin`、`/profile`、`/settings`）在 lg 断点及以上改用左侧边栏，不再使用带链接的营销页页头。侧边栏的链接和文字与原来相同（Dashboard、Upload、My Files、Access Records、恢复或管理控制台、Help、Education、Contact us），会高亮当前页面，底部显示这个浏览器当前是否持有账户密钥（“Key unlocked in this browser”或“Key locked”）以及密钥指纹的前四组，还有用户名和退出。页头保留语言切换和账户菜单，lg 以下仍是菜单按钮，所以手机端不变；侧边栏旁边隐藏了体积很大的页脚。公开页和首页仍然使用页头。
- My Files gets a "Details" button on every file, which opens a panel from the right with what the list already knows plus who can open the file: the cipher, original and stored size with exact byte counts, type, upload time, owner, the verification status, the people with access from `GET /files/{id}/shared-users` (owner only; a recipient sees who shared it), and a note that the key is also sealed for the recovery key. Its buttons (Download and decrypt, Share, Check integrity, Access history, Delete) call the page's own handlers and close the panel first, so they behave exactly as they do from the row. The ciphertext SHA-256 (new field `ciphertext_sha256`, see the backend PR) is shown with a copy button when the server returns it. No other new data. Escape and the backdrop close it, focus moves to its close button, and it is a labelled modal dialog.
- My Files 里每个文件多了一个“Details”按钮，点开后从右侧滑出一个面板，显示列表已有的信息以及谁能打开这个文件：加密算法、原始大小和存储大小（含精确字节数）、类型、上传时间、所有者、校验状态、有访问权限的人（来自 `GET /files/{id}/shared-users`，仅所有者可见；接收方看到的是谁分享给自己的），以及密钥也为恢复密钥封存了一份的说明。面板里的按钮（Download and decrypt、Share、Check integrity、Access history、Delete）调用页面自己原来的处理函数，并先关闭面板，所以行为和在行里点击完全一样。服务器返回时（新字段 `ciphertext_sha256`，见后端 PR）会显示密文的 SHA-256，并带复制按钮；没有其他新数据。按 Esc 或点击遮罩可关闭，焦点会移到关闭按钮，面板是带标签的模态对话框。
- Checked in light and dark and at 390 px (no horizontal scroll). Typecheck and 812 unit tests pass. The browser specs find the sidebar's links by the same roles and names as before (the header's own navigation is hidden beside the sidebar), and CI runs them. Upload receipt, workspace cards and the remaining app pages are still to restyle.
- 已在浅色、深色和 390 px 宽度下检查（没有横向滚动）。类型检查和 812 个单元测试通过。浏览器测试按原来相同的角色和名称查找侧边栏里的链接（页头自己的导航在侧边栏旁边是隐藏的），由 CI 运行。上传回执、工作区卡片和其余应用页仍待改。

### The file list returns each file's ciphertext SHA-256 / 文件列表返回每个文件的密文 SHA-256

- `FileSummaryOut` (the rows of `GET /files` and the head of `GET /files/{id}`) gains `ciphertext_sha256`: the SHA-256 of the stored ciphertext as recorded at upload. It is not a secret: the ciphertext preview endpoint already returns it to anyone with view access, and the integrity checks compare against it. It lets the file detail panel show the fingerprint of what the server holds, so a person can compare it with the audit record or hash the downloaded ciphertext themselves. One field, no new endpoint, no new query (the column is already loaded), covered by a unit test; the frontend type is updated and the panel follows in the UI redesign PR.
- `FileSummaryOut`（`GET /files` 的每一行以及 `GET /files/{id}` 的头部）新增 `ciphertext_sha256`：上传时记录的、已存储密文的 SHA-256。它不是秘密：密文预览接口早已把它返回给任何有查看权限的人，完整性检查也是拿它来对比的。有了它，文件详情栏就能显示服务器所存内容的指纹，让人可以与审计记录对照，或自己对下载下来的密文做哈希。只加一个字段，没有新接口，也没有新查询（这一列本来就会被读出），有单元测试；前端类型已同步，详情栏的显示随界面改版 PR 一起提交。

### UI redesign, step 2: the home page and the public chrome / 界面改版第 2 步：首页和公开页的外壳

- Home hero: a dark scene (the only dark section; it stays dark in both themes) with a wall of flickering ciphertext, a scan line and a floating "SEALED" card that shows the real first bytes of a PDF next to what the server receives, plus a key fingerprint. The headline is now "Sealed in your browser. Unreadable to everyone else." Motion stops under `prefers-reduced-motion`. The two calls to action keep their text ("Get started" or "Encrypt a file", "See how it works").
- 首页首屏：一个深色场景（全站唯一的深色区块，浅色和深色主题下都保持深色）：一面闪烁的密文墙、一条扫描线，以及一张悬浮的“SEALED”文件卡，卡里显示一个 PDF 的真实开头字节和服务器收到的内容的对比，以及一个密钥指纹。标题改为“Sealed in your browser. Unreadable to everyone else.”。系统设置了“减少动态效果”时动画会停止。两个按钮的文字不变（“Get started”或“Encrypt a file”、“See how it works”）。
- The scrolling wall of icon cards ("Core Features") is replaced, with the same information, by a proof strip and the three moves Seal, Share and Verify, each with the real artefact it produces, plus a line of what else is included. The strip only quotes numbers that have evidence in the repository: 0 of 5,094 downloads failed an integrity check, 102 of 102 uploads at 100 concurrent users, 0 failures in the ZAP scan, 500 MB per file. The header and footer "Product" links still land on it (`#features`). The "Under the hood" animation, the six-step picture guide and the animated explainer are unchanged apart from colours.
- 滚动的图标卡片墙（“Core Features”）被替换，信息一条不丢：现在是一条证据数据条，加上“封存、分享、验证”三步，每步都配上它真实产生的东西，再加一行“还包含什么”。数据条只引用仓库里有证据的数字：5,094 次下载 0 次未通过完整性检查、100 个并发用户下 102 次上传全部成功、ZAP 扫描 0 项失败、每个文件 500 MB。页头和页脚的“Product”链接仍然落在这里（`#features`）。“Under the hood”动画、六步图文指南和动画讲解除了颜色之外都没有改动。
- The header is flat instead of a floating rounded pill, so it no longer covers a section title when the page scrolls to an anchor (`scroll-padding-top` added). The comparison table marks yes in green and no in red instead of the brand colour, section eyebrows use the mono face, and the removed components are `features-section.vue` and `FeatureLane.vue`. New Chinese strings were written by hand to pass the glossary test and can be regenerated with the translation pipeline.
- 页头改成扁平的，不再是悬浮的圆角胶囊，所以页面滚动到锚点时不会再盖住章节标题（新增 `scroll-padding-top`）。对比表里“是”用绿色、“否”用红色，不再借用品牌色；各章节的小标题改用等宽字体；删除了 `features-section.vue` 和 `FeatureLane.vue`。新增的中文是手写的，用来通过术语表测试，之后可以用翻译流程重新生成。

### UI redesign, step 1: a global "Sealed" style / 界面改版第 1 步：全局“Sealed”风格

- The interface read as a generic AI-made site. Step 1 changes only the look, in one place (`src/style.css`) plus a few components, and no feature: warm-paper background, near-black ink for buttons and text, a restrained 8 px radius, and one colour per meaning, so green now only says verified/sealed and red only says changed. The dark theme is near-black with a single mint accent. New tokens `success`, `success-soft` and `destructive-soft` back the Verified and Modified badges in My Files, which used to borrow the brand colour. The soft radial glows behind the home hero, the Contact us / team pages and every product page, and the tinted team-card gradients, are removed.
- 界面看起来像一个套了模板的 AI 网站。第 1 步只改外观，集中在 `src/style.css` 加少数几个组件，不动任何功能：暖白纸色背景、按钮和文字用近黑墨色、更克制的 8 px 圆角，并且“一种颜色只表示一种含义”——绿色现在只表示已验证/已封存，红色只表示被改动。深色主题改为近黑底加唯一的薄荷绿强调色。新增 `success`、`success-soft`、`destructive-soft` 三个颜色变量，供 My Files 的“Verified/Modified”徽章使用（此前它借用品牌色）。首页首屏、Contact us/团队页和各产品页背后的柔和径向光晕，以及团队卡片的彩色渐变，都已去掉。
- Fonts: the stylesheet named Geist but never loaded it, so the site used the system font. `@fontsource-variable/geist` and `geist-mono` are now bundled and served from our own origin, which the production CSP (`font-src 'self'`) allows; Google Fonts would have been blocked. Headings get tighter tracking and balanced wrapping; code, hashes and fingerprints use Geist Mono. Checked in light and dark, English, at 1440 and 390 px; typecheck, 812 unit tests and the build pass. Steps 2–4 (public pages, app pages, re-recorded screenshots and videos) follow.
- 字体：样式表里写了 Geist，但从来没有加载，所以网站一直在用系统字体。现在打包了 `@fontsource-variable/geist` 和 `geist-mono`，从我们自己的域名提供，生产环境的 CSP（`font-src 'self'`）允许这样做；如果引用 Google Fonts 会被拦截。标题的字距更紧并均衡换行；代码、哈希和指纹用 Geist Mono。已在浅色和深色、英文、1440 和 390 px 下检查；类型检查、812 个单元测试和构建通过。第 2–4 步（公开页、应用页、重拍截图和视频）随后进行。

### Remove the usability study kit / 删除可用性研究套件

- The project owner decided not to run a formal usability study (#115 was already closed as not planned), so the kit that supported it is deleted: `docs/usability/` (study plan in both languages, consent sheet, screener, task cards, SUS questionnaire, CSV templates, `RESULTS.md`), `backend/scripts/usability_analysis.py` and its test. It remains in the git history (PR #114). NFR2 is still described as informal real use with no usability score; TODO, `PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY`, REFLECTION and TALKING_POINTS (both languages) no longer link to the deleted files.
- 项目负责人决定不做正式的可用性研究（#115 此前已按 not planned 关闭），所以支撑它的整套材料一并删除：`docs/usability/`（中英文研究方案、同意书、筛选问卷、任务卡、SUS 问卷、CSV 模板、`RESULTS.md`）、`backend/scripts/usability_analysis.py` 及其测试。这些仍可在 git 历史（PR #114）里找回。NFR2 仍然写作“非正式的真实使用，没有可用性分数”；TODO、`PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY`、REFLECTION 和 TALKING_POINTS（中英文）不再链接已删除的文件。

### Backup video for the live demo, and re-recorded Help videos / 现场演示备用视频，以及重录的帮助视频

- The live demo had no backup if the network or staging failed. `frontend/scripts/record-demo.mjs` records it from the real product: Alice's and Bob's browsers are recorded side by side and cut together in the order of `DEMO_SCRIPT.md` (upload with the timed steps and byte comparison, share after checking the fingerprint, Bob decrypts, and the tamper step), with a label for whose screen it is. Step 4 really changes the stored file through `demo_tamper.py`, the card shows that command's real output, and the file is restored at the end. Result: `docs/presentation/media/demo-backup.mp4`, 2 min 23 s. It was recorded on a disposable local stack running the same code, because the recorder signs in automatically.
- 现场演示原来没有备份，断网或 staging 出问题就没办法。`frontend/scripts/record-demo.mjs` 从真实产品里录制：Alice 和 Bob 两个浏览器同时录，再按 `DEMO_SCRIPT.md` 的顺序剪在一起（上传并显示计时步骤和字节对比、核对指纹后分享、Bob 解密、篡改），并标明当前是谁的屏幕。第 4 步通过 `demo_tamper.py` 真的改动存储的文件，卡片里显示的是该命令的真实输出，录完自动还原。成品：`docs/presentation/media/demo-backup.mp4`，2 分 23 秒。因为录制脚本会自动登录，所以是在运行同样代码的本地一次性环境上录的。
- The Help page's `encrypt-upload` and `share-open` videos still showed the old upload and the one-step share. They are re-recorded: the upload now shows the timed encryption steps and the byte comparison, and the share stops at the recipient's key fingerprint before "Confirm and share". The caption, outline and sign-in helpers moved to `frontend/scripts/lib/video.mjs`, shared by both recorders.
- 帮助页的 `encrypt-upload` 和 `share-open` 视频还是旧的上传界面和一步分享。现已重录：上传会显示计时的加密步骤和字节对比，分享会先停在接收方的密钥指纹，再点“确认分享”。字幕、高亮框和登录这些辅助函数移到了 `frontend/scripts/lib/video.mjs`，两个录制脚本共用。

### Staging re-run of the 100-user step, the demo accounts and the post-fix ZAP scan / staging 重跑 100 用户压测、演示账号和修复后的 ZAP 复扫

- Re-ran the #140 `steps` on staging after the pool fix (same parameters as 2026-10-03, with auto-deploy, CI runners and backups paused, the sshd tunnel rule reverted afterwards, test accounts deleted). At 100 concurrent users uploads went from 23 of 103 succeeded (78 % HTTP 500) to **102 of 102 succeeded**; 102 downloads were decrypted and SHA-256-matched with 0 failures; upload throughput rose from 0.19 to 0.86 per second. Latency is still bounded by the generator's home link, and four dropped connections in the first 5-user step were not investigated. Evidence in `docs/performance/pool-fix-2026-10-04/staging-rerun/`.
- 修复连接池之后在 staging 上重跑了 #140 的 `steps`（参数与 2026-10-03 相同；期间暂停了自动部署、CI runner 和备份，结束后撤销了 sshd 隧道规则、删除了测试账号）。100 个并发用户时，上传从 103 次成功 23 次（78% 返回 HTTP 500）变为 **102 次全部成功**；102 次下载解密并与原文 SHA-256 比对，0 次失败；上传吞吐从每秒 0.19 次升到 0.86 次。延迟仍然受测试机家用网络限制；第一档 5 用户出现的 4 次连接中断没有深入排查。证据在 `docs/performance/pool-fix-2026-10-04/staging-rerun/`。
- The updated nginx file was installed on the VPS by the user and ZAP re-run: 0 FAIL, 5 WARN, 62 PASS (was 8 WARN, 59 PASS); the missing-header alerts are gone. `demo-alice` and `demo-bob` were created on staging with the seeder (no email code needed) for the live demo.
- 用户已把更新后的 nginx 文件装到 VPS，并重跑 ZAP：0 FAIL、5 WARN、62 PASS（之前 8 个 WARN、59 PASS），缺失响应头的告警已消失。另外用种子脚本在 staging 上建了 `demo-alice` 和 `demo-bob`（不需要邮箱验证码），供现场演示使用。

### CI: judge a docs-only push against `deploy`, so it cannot strand a code commit / CI：纯文档推送相对 `deploy` 判断，不再让代码 commit 卡住不部署

- A push to `main` cancels the run still in progress for `main`. On 2026-10-04 the docs-only #168 was merged three minutes after #169, cancelled #169's run, and then skipped the tests because it changed only documentation; `promote` was skipped with them, so `deploy` did not move to #169. Here no served code was involved, but the same order after a code merge would have left that code undeployed until the next code commit. The `changes` job now compares a push with the `deploy` branch (the last promoted commit) instead of the previous push, so unpromoted code is still in the diff and every job runs. A structural test in `test_deploy_gate.py` keeps it that way. Pull requests are unchanged.
- 推送到 `main` 会取消 `main` 上仍在运行的 CI。2026-10-04，只改文档的 #168 在 #169 之后三分钟合并，取消了 #169 的运行，又因为只改了文档而跳过测试；`promote` 也随之跳过，`deploy` 没有推进到 #169。这次没有涉及线上代码，但如果是代码合并后紧接着出现同样的顺序，那段代码就要等下一个代码 commit 才会部署。现在 `changes` 作业把推送与 `deploy` 分支（上一次推进的 commit）比较，而不是与上一次推送比较，所以尚未推进的代码仍在差异里，全部作业都会运行。`test_deploy_gate.py` 中新增的结构测试保证这一点不被改回。PR 的判断方式不变。

### Usability: informal real use instead of the formal study (#115 closed) / 可用性：以非正式真实使用代替正式研究（#115 已关闭）

- The team decided not to run the formal non-expert usability study, and #115 was closed as not planned. The supervisor, the four team members, the FIT3162 Teaching Team and other students have used the live site with their own accounts instead. The documents that still said "study pending" now say this: TODO, the proposal traceability table (WBS 5.4 stays In progress, with informal use as its only evidence), the study plan's status, TALKING_POINTS §10 and the reflection table. They also say what must not be claimed: a usability study, a SUS score, a completion rate or a user count nobody measured. `RESULTS.md` stays empty and the study kit stays in `docs/usability/`.
- 团队决定不做正式的非专业用户可用性研究，#115 已按 not planned 关闭。取而代之的是：导师、团队 4 人、FIT3162 Teaching Team 和其他同学都用自己的账号在线上站点用过。仍写着“研究待进行”的文档现已改为这一说法：TODO、提案对照表（WBS 5.4 仍为 In progress，唯一依据是非正式使用）、研究计划的状态、TALKING_POINTS 第 10 条和反思表。这些文档也写明了不能声称的内容：做过可用性研究、SUS 分数、完成率，以及没人统计过的使用人数。`RESULTS.md` 保持为空，研究套件仍保留在 `docs/usability/`。

### CI: a self-hosted runner on the VPS, and documentation-only changes skip the test jobs / CI：改用 VPS 上的自建 runner，只改文档时跳过测试任务

- Every CI job now runs on the project's own runner on the VPS (`deployment/ci-runner/`), so CI no longer depends on GitHub's monthly minutes. It is isolated from the production stack on the same machine: its own Docker daemon (dind), so job containers never see the host's containers or ports; a firewall rule that stops the CI subnet opening connections to the host; CPU and memory caps with a low CPU weight, so production wins when both want the CPU; and no production path or secret mounted. Three independent runner pairs take jobs in parallel, so a full run still takes about 5 minutes. The remaining risk, a container escape from dind's privileged mode, is written down and was accepted for a private five-person repository.
- 所有 CI 任务现在都在 VPS 上项目自建的 runner 上运行（`deployment/ci-runner/`），CI 不再依赖 GitHub 的每月分钟数。它与同一台机器上的线上环境隔离：有独立的 Docker（dind），任务容器看不到宿主机的容器和端口；防火墙规则阻止 CI 网段主动连接宿主机；限制了 CPU 和内存，并设了较低的 CPU 权重，两者抢 CPU 时线上优先；不挂载任何线上路径或密钥。三组相互独立的 runner 并行接任务，完整 CI 仍约 5 分钟。剩余风险（dind 特权模式下的容器逃逸）已写明，并在私有、5 人团队的前提下接受。

- The repository's free GitHub Actions allowance (2,000 minutes a month for a private repository) ran out on 2026-10-04 after 145 runs in four days, at about 13 billed minutes each, and the promote job for #166 could not start. A new `changes` job now looks at what a pull request or push changed: if every file is Markdown, or under `docs/` except `docs/crypto/` (whose test vectors the frontend and loadtest suites read), the five test jobs and `promote` are skipped and the run costs about a minute. Anything it cannot decide (no earlier commit, a failed diff) runs everything.
- 仓库的 GitHub Actions 免费额度（私有仓库每月 2,000 分钟）在 2026-10-04 用完：四天里跑了 145 次，每次约 13 个计费分钟，#166 的 promote 作业因此没能启动。新增的 `changes` 作业会检查一次 PR 或推送改了什么：如果所有文件都是 Markdown，或都在 `docs/` 下（`docs/crypto/` 除外，前端和压测测试会读取其中的测试向量），就跳过五个测试作业和 `promote`，这次运行只花约一分钟。凡是判断不了的情况（没有可比较的上一个 commit、diff 失败）一律照常全跑。

### Four-minute demo script and an honest usability statement / 4 分钟演示脚本与如实的可用性说明

- The 12-minute presentation has four minutes of demo, and a live demo can lose the network or hang. `docs/presentation/DEMO_SCRIPT.md` gives a timed four-step script (upload with the real encryption steps, share with the key-fingerprint check, the recipient opens it, the live tamper), the lines to say, a day-before and 30-minutes-before checklist, what to do when each step fails, and how to record the backup video. The `demo-alice` / `demo-bob` accounts and the video are left to the user.
- 12 分钟展示里只有 4 分钟演示，现场可能断网或卡住。`docs/presentation/DEMO_SCRIPT.md` 给出带时间的四步脚本（上传并展示真实的加密步骤、核对密钥指纹后分享、接收方打开、现场篡改）、讲解词、前一天和开始前 30 分钟的检查清单、每一步出问题时怎么办，以及怎么录备用视频。`demo-alice` / `demo-bob` 账号和视频由用户来准备。
- NFR2 (usability) has no result because no study session has run. TALKING_POINTS §10 now gives the exact wording: not verified yet, the pass criteria were fixed in advance, and what the team walkthrough changed; and what must not be said (no "easy to use", no SUS number).
- NFR2（可用性）还没有结果，因为一场研究都没跑。TALKING_POINTS 第 10 条现在写明了该怎么说：尚未验证、及格标准事先已定好，以及团队走查改进了什么；也写明了哪些话不能说（不能说“好用”，不能报 SUS 分数）。

### Language switch in the header bar, and a refreshed picture guide / 语言切换移到页头栏，首页图文指南截图更新

- The language switch was inside the gear menu (signed out) or the avatar menu (signed in), so a visitor who cannot read the current language had to guess which icon hides it. It now sits in the header bar itself, to the left of the gear (signed out) or of Dashboard (signed in), and first in the phone menu; the gear and avatar menus keep only the theme. Checked at 1400, 1100 and 1024 px (the narrowest desktop layout, in Chinese) with no overflow, and at 375 px in the phone menu. The language-switch browser spec no longer opens the gear.
- 语言切换原来藏在齿轮菜单（未登录）或头像菜单（已登录）里，看不懂当前语言的访客只能猜哪个图标里有它。现在它直接放在页头栏上：未登录时在齿轮左边，登录后在“仪表盘”左边；手机菜单里排在第一位；齿轮和头像菜单只保留主题。已在 1400、1100 和 1024 px（最窄的桌面布局，中文界面）下检查，没有溢出；375 px 手机菜单也已检查。语言切换浏览器测试不再需要先打开齿轮。
- The homepage picture guide still showed the old six-icon file row. All twelve screenshots are re-captured from the current build: the row with Share labelled, and step 5 now stops at the recipient confirmation showing the key fingerprint. `capture-workflow.cjs` had drifted from the UI and is fixed: new card selectors, fixtures for folders and the tab totals, a recipient-lookup fixture, and an example account key sealed with the app's own `sealAccountKey` (through `jiti`) so the Unlock dialog really opens it. Alt text for step 5 updated in English and Chinese.
- 首页图文指南的截图还是旧的六个图标那一行。现在 12 张截图全部按当前版本重新截取：文件行里“分享”带文字，第 5 步改为停在显示密钥指纹的接收方确认。`capture-workflow.cjs` 已经跟不上界面，现已修好：更新卡片选择器，补上文件夹和标签页计数的模拟数据、接收方查找的模拟数据，并用应用自己的 `sealAccountKey`（通过 `jiti` 加载）封装示例账户密钥，让“解锁”对话框真的能打开它。第 5 步的替代文字中英文都已更新。

### Third-party security scans and the headers they found missing / 第三方安全扫描及其发现的缺失响应头

- "Secure" rested only on our own word. An OWASP ZAP baseline scan of `https://secureview.tech` (passive, 1-minute spider) found no High-risk issue: 0 FAIL, 8 WARN, 59 PASS. It did find a real gap: the hashed JS/CSS bundle under `/assets/` was served without `Strict-Transport-Security` or `X-Content-Type-Options`, because nginx drops every server-level `add_header` in a location that adds its own, and `/assets/` adds `Cache-Control`. The headers are now repeated there; `Permissions-Policy` (camera, microphone, location, payment, USB off) and `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy` / `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin` are added; a unit test fails if any location that adds a header drops a security header. COEP and `style-src 'unsafe-inline'` are accepted with reasons.
- “安全”此前只有我们自己的说法。用 OWASP ZAP 对 `https://secureview.tech` 做了基线扫描（被动规则，爬取 1 分钟），没有高风险问题：0 FAIL、8 WARN、59 PASS。但它确实发现了一个真实漏洞：`/assets/` 下带哈希的 JS/CSS 代码包返回时没有 `Strict-Transport-Security` 和 `X-Content-Type-Options`。原因是 nginx 的规则：某个 location 只要自己写了 `add_header`，server 级别的 `add_header` 就全部失效，而 `/assets/` 加了 `Cache-Control`。现在在那里重复写了这些头；新增 `Permissions-Policy`（关闭摄像头、麦克风、定位、支付和 USB），以及 `Cross-Origin-Opener-Policy` / `Cross-Origin-Resource-Policy: same-origin`；新增单元测试：任何自己加响应头的 location 如果漏掉安全头，测试就会失败。COEP 和 `style-src 'unsafe-inline'` 写明理由后接受。
- `pip-audit` found nothing in the runtime dependencies and `npm audit` found nothing in the frontend; the test-only `httpx2` 2.7.0 had six advisories and is upgraded to 2.12.0 (570 backend unit and API tests pass). Report and raw output in `docs/security/scans-2026-10-04/` (English and 简体中文). The tracked nginx file must still be installed on the VPS by someone with root, then the scan re-run.
- `pip-audit` 在运行时依赖中没有发现问题，`npm audit` 在前端也没有发现问题；仅测试用的 `httpx2` 2.7.0 有 6 条公告，已升级到 2.12.0（后端 570 个单元和 API 测试通过）。报告和原始输出在 `docs/security/scans-2026-10-04/`（中英文）。仓库里的 nginx 文件还需要有 root 权限的人装到 VPS 上，然后重新扫描。

### A scripted live tamper demonstration / 可脚本化的现场篡改演示

- "We encrypt and check every file" had no moment the audience could watch. `backend/scripts/demo_tamper.py` changes one byte of a demo account's stored ciphertext on the server (`flip`), or changes it and rewrites the recorded digest and size to match as an attacker with database access would (`forge`), and puts everything back (`restore`); `list` prints the account's files and ids. It only touches accounts named `demo-*`, checks the named owner really owns the file, keeps the original bytes and row values (mode 0600) until restore, and refuses a second tamper. `flip` is refused by the server's SHA-256 check before streaming; `forge` is refused by the upload digest recorded in the hash-chained audit log. Steps, lines to say and cautions are in `docs/presentation/TAMPER_DEMO.md` (English and 简体中文).
- “我们加密并校验每一个文件”一直没有观众能亲眼看到的时刻。`backend/scripts/demo_tamper.py` 可以在服务器上把演示账号存储的密文改掉一个字节（`flip`）；或者改字节的同时把数据库里记录的摘要和大小改成一致，模拟拿到数据库权限的攻击者（`forge`）；再全部还原（`restore`）；`list` 列出该账号的文件和 id。它只动名为 `demo-*` 的账号，会核对指定账号确实是文件所有者，还原前一直保留原始字节和数据库原值（权限 0600），并拒绝重复篡改。`flip` 会被服务器发送前的 SHA-256 检查拦下；`forge` 会被上传时写进哈希链审计日志的摘要拦下。步骤、讲解词和注意事项见 `docs/presentation/TAMPER_DEMO.md`（中英文）。
- Rehearsing it found that a refused download only said "Download failed": downloads ask for bytes, so the server's JSON error arrived as an ArrayBuffer and its code and message were never read. `api/http.ts` now decodes a byte error body (`failureBody`), and My Files shows the server's reason and marks the row **Modified** when the code is `integrity_verification_failed`. Unit test `http-error-body.test.ts`; Chinese string added. Checked locally against MySQL 8.4 with the real frontend: flip → refused and Modified; restore → decrypted to the original text; forge → refused; restore → works, and clicking the badge returns it to Verified.
- 预演时发现，下载被拒时界面只显示“Download failed”：下载请求要求返回字节，所以服务器的 JSON 错误是以 ArrayBuffer 形式到达的，其中的错误码和原因从来没被读出来。现在 `api/http.ts` 会解析字节形式的错误内容（`failureBody`）；当错误码是 `integrity_verification_failed` 时，My Files 会显示服务器给出的原因，并把这一行标为 **Modified**。新增单元测试 `http-error-body.test.ts`，并补了中文翻译。已在本地 MySQL 8.4 + 真实前端上验证：flip → 被拒并显示 Modified；restore → 解密出原文；forge → 被拒；restore → 恢复正常，点状态标签后变回 Verified。

### Fix the 100-user database-pool failure / 修复 100 用户时数据库连接池耗尽的问题

- The #140 staging run found 65–78 % of uploads failing with HTTP 500 at 100 concurrent users (`QueuePool limit ... timeout 30.00`) while CPU averaged 3 %. The report's guess, that file transfers hold their connection, was measured and is wrong: three slow 30 MB downloads left no open transaction. The failure reproduced locally at 100 users (80 % failed uploads, 0.33 ops/s), with every timeout in authentication. The cause: the async upload endpoint kept the connection from authentication and ran its database calls on the event loop while awaiting the thread pool once per chunk to stage the file, and every pool thread was a request waiting for a connection. A first fix that only released the connection made 50 users slow too, because the upload then waited for a connection on the event loop, and was not shipped. The fix: `FileService.upload` checks in the thread pool and releases its connection, stages with no connection held, and records in the thread pool, with the same rollback and compensation; `get_current_actor` ends its read-only transaction. Locally the 100-user step went to 0 % failures at 56 ops/s (upload p95 60.5 s → 0.97 s). Before/after summaries, charts and the write-up are in `docs/performance/pool-fix-2026-10-04/` (EN and zh-CN).
- #140 的 staging 压测发现，100 个并发用户时 65–78% 的上传返回 HTTP 500（`QueuePool limit ... timeout 30.00`），而 CPU 平均只有 3%。报告里"文件传输期间占着连接"的推测经过实测被证明是错的：三个很慢的 30 MB 下载期间，没有任何未结束的事务。问题在本地 100 用户时复现（80% 上传失败，每秒 0.33 次操作），所有超时都发生在认证这一步。原因是：异步的上传接口把认证时拿到的连接一直占着，在事件循环上直接执行数据库调用，同时每写一块暂存文件就等一次线程池，而线程池的每个线程又都是在等连接的请求。第一次修复只是归还连接，结果让 50 用户也变慢了：上传改成了在事件循环上等连接；这一版没有上线。最终修复：`FileService.upload` 在线程池里做检查并归还连接，暂存时不持有连接，再在线程池里写入记录，回滚和补偿逻辑保持不变；`get_current_actor` 会结束它的只读事务。本地 100 用户那一档变为零失败、每秒 56 次操作（上传 p95 从 60.5 秒降到 0.97 秒）。修复前后的汇总、图表和说明在 `docs/performance/pool-fix-2026-10-04/`（中英文）。
- Tests: a unit test that the connection is released before staging; the fault-injection test now kills the connection at the commit that writes the file row rather than at the first commit; backend 633 passed on MySQL 8.4, 85.28 % coverage. The #140 report, `PERFORMANCE_TESTING`, TALKING_POINTS, REFLECTION and TODO (both languages) are updated; the staging re-run is still to do, and the rarely used async avatar endpoints were left as they are.
- 测试：新增单元测试，检查暂存之前已经归还连接；故障注入测试改为在写入文件记录的那次提交时杀掉连接，而不是第一次提交；后端在 MySQL 8.4 上 633 个测试全部通过，覆盖率 85.28%。#140 报告、`PERFORMANCE_TESTING`、TALKING_POINTS、REFLECTION 和 TODO（中英文）均已更新；staging 上的重测还没做，很少使用的异步头像接口这次没有改动。

### A getting-started checklist on the dashboard / 仪表盘上的新手清单

- A first sign-in gave no hint that the product is three moves: upload, share, and someone opening the file. The dashboard now shows "Getting started" with those three steps (encrypt and upload a file; share it with someone, checking their key fingerprint; download and decrypt a file, yours or one shared with you), each linking to where it is done. A step ticks itself from the account's own audit trail (`FILE_UPLOADED`, `FILE_SHARED`, `FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED`), so it reflects what really happened, not a button press. The card can be hidden per account and stays hidden; once all three are done it says so. It also gives the live demo a script to follow.
- 第一次登录时，界面没有提示这个产品的核心其实就是三步：上传、分享、对方打开文件。现在仪表盘会显示“新手入门”，列出这三步：加密并上传一个文件；把它分享给别人（并核对对方的密钥指纹）；下载并解密一个文件（自己的或别人分享的）。每一步都链接到对应的页面，并根据账号自己的审计记录（`FILE_UPLOADED`、`FILE_SHARED`、`FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED`）自动打勾，反映的是真实发生的操作，而不是点过某个按钮。卡片可以按账号隐藏，隐藏后不会再出现；三步都完成后会给出提示。它也正好可以当作现场演示的流程。
- `utils/getting-started.ts` holds the rule and the per-account hide flag, with unit tests; `GettingStartedCard.vue` waits for the signed-in user before reading the trail, which is fetched after the page loads. Checked by hand against a local API: a new account showed 0 of 3, then 1 of 3 after an upload, and hiding survived a reload; strings translated in `zh-CN.dict.json`.
- 判断规则和按账号隐藏的标记放在 `utils/getting-started.ts`，配有单元测试；`GettingStartedCard.vue` 会等登录用户信息加载完成后再读取审计记录（用户信息是在页面加载之后才取回的）。已在本地 API 上手动验证：新账号显示 0/3，上传后变为 1/3，隐藏后刷新页面仍保持隐藏；新增文字已翻译进 `zh-CN.dict.json`。

### Register in two steps and sign the new account straight in / 注册拆成两步，注册后直接登录

- The first-time-user walkthrough found registration the hardest part: one form asked for a username, a login password and its confirmation, an encryption passphrase with different rules, an email and a code, then a page about the private key, then a second sign-in. Registration is now two steps. Step 1 "Your account" is username, password, email and code, checked when Next is pressed. Step 2 "Your encryption passphrase" is the passphrase alone, under a short note on why there is a second password: the login password is checked by the server, the passphrase never leaves the browser and locks the key that opens the files. After registering, the page signs the account in and opens this session's account key with the private key it has just generated, so "Continue to your workspace" goes straight to the dashboard and the first upload or share does not ask for the passphrase. If either step fails, the old "Continue to sign in" appears instead.
- 新用户走查发现注册是最难的一步：一张表单要填用户名、登录密码及确认、规则不同的加密口令、邮箱和验证码，接着是一整页私钥说明，最后还要再登录一次。现在注册分成两步。第 1 步“你的账号”：用户名、密码、邮箱和验证码，点击“下一步”时检查。第 2 步“你的加密口令”：只填加密口令，上方有一段说明，解释为什么还要第二个密码：登录密码由服务器验证，加密口令永远不离开浏览器，锁住的是打开文件的那把密钥。注册成功后，页面会自动登录，并用刚生成的私钥解锁本次会话的账户密钥，所以“进入你的工作区”会直接打开仪表盘，第一次上传或分享也不会再要求输入口令。如果这两步中任何一步失败，就退回原来的“继续登录”。
- New keyed messages in `en.json` and `zh-CN.json`; the Help assistant's registration steps are rewritten (Chinese updated, four replaced sentences removed). The three browser specs that register through the page (`golden-path`, `tamper-visibility`, `help-assistant`) now press Next before the passphrase; golden-path checks that registering lands on Your Workspace, signed in. Checked by hand against a local API: pressing Next on an empty step 1 names the first problems, step 2 focuses the passphrase, and a new account reached the dashboard without a second sign-in.
- `en.json` 和 `zh-CN.json` 新增了对应的带 key 文案；帮助助手里的注册步骤已重写（中文同步更新，删除了被替换的四句旧翻译）。三个通过页面注册的浏览器测试（`golden-path`、`tamper-visibility`、`help-assistant`）现在会先点“下一步”再填口令；golden-path 会检查注册后是否已登录并进入“你的工作区”。已在本地 API 上手动验证：第 1 步什么都不填直接点“下一步”，会逐项指出问题；第 2 步光标自动落在口令框；新账号注册后无需再次登录就进入了仪表盘。

### Confirm the recipient and their key fingerprint before sharing (threat model T-S5) / 分享前确认接收方及其密钥指纹（威胁模型 T-S5）

- Sharing used to happen the moment a name was entered, so a typo shared with a stranger, and the browser wrapped the file key to whatever public key the server returned (threat model T-S5, accepted until now). The share dialog now has a second step: it names the recipient and shows the fingerprint of the key the file will be sealed to, computed in the browser from the PEM (a key whose server-stated fingerprint disagrees is refused). "Confirm and share" wraps to exactly that key with no second lookup. The browser remembers each recipient's fingerprint and warns when it differs from the last share, and Settings shows the user's own fingerprint so two people can compare. This is the control §10.2 of the threat model recommended for T-S5; T-S5 and the recipient half of F-06 are now mitigated against a data writer, while a malicious code supplier (N1) and a first share on a new device remain open.
- 以前一输入名字就立刻分享，打错一个字就会发给陌生人，而且浏览器会把文件密钥包装给服务器返回的任何公钥（威胁模型 T-S5，此前为"接受"）。现在分享对话框多了第二步：写明接收方是谁，并显示文件将被加密给的那把公钥的指纹。指纹在浏览器里根据 PEM 计算；如果服务器声明的指纹与公钥不符，就拒绝使用。点击"确认分享"后，只包装给被确认的那把公钥，中间不会再查第二次。浏览器会记住每个接收方的指纹，与上次分享时不同就发出警告；"设置"页显示用户自己的指纹，方便两人核对。这正是威胁模型第 10.2 节为 T-S5 推荐的控制措施；T-S5 和 F-06 的接收方部分现已针对数据写入者缓解，恶意代码提供者（N1）和新设备上的首次分享仍未解决。
- `crypto/share.ts` gains `lookupRecipient` and accepts a confirmed `RecipientCandidate`; new `crypto/fingerprint.ts`, `utils/known-keys.ts` and `KeyFingerprintCard.vue`. Unit tests cover the fingerprint format, the mismatch refusal, a failed lookup and the remembered keys; the golden-path browser spec now confirms the share. Checked by hand against a local API: the sender's dialog and the recipient's Settings both showed `F79E 8110 CCFA 80CF 3397 A1F4 4A6E B0DD`, and an altered remembered fingerprint raised the warning; English and 简体中文. Help text and `THREAT_MODEL` (T-S5, F-06, the §10.2 decision) updated in both languages.
- `crypto/share.ts` 新增 `lookupRecipient`，并支持传入已确认的 `RecipientCandidate`；新增 `crypto/fingerprint.ts`、`utils/known-keys.ts` 和 `KeyFingerprintCard.vue`。单元测试覆盖了指纹格式、指纹不符时拒绝、查找失败，以及记住的指纹；golden-path 浏览器测试现在会点击确认分享。已在本地 API 上手动验证：发送方对话框和接收方"设置"页显示的都是 `F79E 8110 CCFA 80CF 3397 A1F4 4A6E B0DD`；篡改记住的指纹后会弹出警告；中英文界面都已检查。帮助文字和 `THREAT_MODEL`（T-S5、F-06、第 10.2 节的决定）中英文版本均已更新。

### File rows: Download, Share and Delete visible with labels, the rest under More / 文件行：下载、分享、删除直接显示并带标签，其余收进"更多"

- A first-time-user walkthrough found each file row had six unlabelled icons: Share, the action a demo depends on, was one of them; Share, Delete and Access history had no accessible name; and at about 800 px the Help assistant button covered Delete. The row now shows Download (decrypt), a labelled blue Share button and Delete, plus More (⋯) holding Download encrypted file, Request recovery and Access history. A recipient's row shows Download and More only, as before for Share and Delete. Every control has an accessible name. The menu is teleported to `<body>` so the table's horizontal scroll box cannot clip it, opens upwards near the bottom of the window, and closes on Escape, an outside click, scroll or resize.
- 以新用户身份走查时发现，每个文件行有六个没有文字的小图标：演示最需要的"分享"只是其中一个；分享、删除、访问记录没有无障碍名称；在约 800 px 宽度下，帮助助手按钮会压住删除图标。现在每行显示下载（解密）、带文字的蓝色"分享"按钮和删除，外加"更多"（⋯），里面是下载加密文件、请求恢复和访问历史。接收者的行只显示下载和"更多"（分享和删除本来就不对接收者显示）。每个控件都有无障碍名称。菜单通过 Teleport 挂到 `<body>`，不会被表格的横向滚动容器裁掉；靠近窗口底部时向上弹出；按 Esc、点击外部、滚动或缩放窗口都会关闭。
- The Help assistant answers and the picture-guide captions that described "the connected-dots icon" and "the lifebuoy" now say the Share button and More; their Chinese is updated and the six replaced sentences are removed from the dictionary. The two browser specs that clicked the old icons (`mobile-file-flow`, `help-assistant`) now use the visible buttons and the menu. Checked by hand at 1280, 800 and 375 px: nothing covered, no horizontal scroll, the menu fits the window. The picture guide's screenshots still show the old row and are re-captured with the demo material.
- 帮助助手的回答和图文指南里写着"连接点图标""救生圈"的说明，已改为"分享"按钮和"更多"菜单，中文同步更新，词典里被替换掉的六句旧翻译已删除。原来直接点击旧图标的两个浏览器测试（`mobile-file-flow`、`help-assistant`）已改为点击新按钮和菜单。已在 1280、800、375 px 宽度下手动检查：没有按钮被遮挡，没有横向滚动，菜单完整显示在窗口内。图文指南的截图仍是旧版界面，会和演示材料一起重新截取。

### Limit upload abuse, and warn before saving a shared program (threat model T-D2 / F-09) / 限制恶意上传，并在保存他人分享的程序前给出警告（威胁模型 T-D2 / F-09）

- One verified account could keep uploading until the disk filled, and the disk is shared with other services; the 85 % alert was the only control. The backend now refuses, before anything is stored, an upload that would take an account over 2 GiB of stored ciphertext (413 `storage_quota_exceeded`), over 5 GiB uploaded in the last 24 hours with deleted files counted so delete-and-reupload does not reset it (429 `upload_daily_limit`), or that would leave the storage filesystem below a free-space floor (507 `server_storage_low`). All three are settings (`USER_STORAGE_QUOTA_BYTES`, `USER_DAILY_UPLOAD_BYTES`, `UPLOAD_MIN_FREE_DISK_BYTES`), 0 turns one off, and staging sets the floor to 20 GiB. No staging account was over 2 GiB when this shipped (largest: 479 MiB).
- 以前一个通过验证的账户就能一直上传直到磁盘写满，而这块磁盘还和其他服务共用，唯一的控制是 85% 告警。现在后端会在写入任何内容之前拒绝以下上传：会让账户存储的密文超过 2 GiB 的（413 `storage_quota_exceeded`）；会让 24 小时内上传总量超过 5 GiB 的，已删除文件也计入，防止"删了再传"重置额度（429 `upload_daily_limit`）；以及会让存储所在文件系统剩余空间低于底线的（507 `server_storage_low`）。三项都可配置（`USER_STORAGE_QUOTA_BYTES`、`USER_DAILY_UPLOAD_BYTES`、`UPLOAD_MIN_FREE_DISK_BYTES`），设为 0 即关闭；staging 的磁盘底线设为 20 GiB。上线时 staging 上没有账户超过 2 GiB（最大 479 MiB）。
- nginx now allows ten uploads in flight per address on `/api/v1/files/upload` (`limit_conn`; not lower, because a campus NAT puts many people behind one address), and the security agent has an `UPLOAD_BURST` rule (30+ uploads by one account in 10 minutes = Medium, 100+ = High). Recipients who download a shared program, script or macro-capable Office file (`.exe`, `.bat`, `.js`, `.docm`, …) are first told that SecureView cannot scan it, since the server only holds ciphertext, and asked to confirm.
- nginx 现在对 `/api/v1/files/upload` 限制每个地址同时最多 10 个上传（`limit_conn`；没有设得更低，因为校园网 NAT 会让很多人共用一个地址），安全 Agent 新增 `UPLOAD_BURST` 规则（单个账户 10 分钟内上传 30 次以上 = Medium，100 次以上 = High）。接收者下载别人分享的程序、脚本或可能带宏的 Office 文件（`.exe`、`.bat`、`.js`、`.docm` 等）之前，会先被告知 SecureView 无法扫描它（服务器只保存密文），并需要确认。
- Tests: unit tests for each limit (refused before storage, exactly-at-quota allowed, the 24-hour window), the agent rule's grades, and the risky-extension check; backend 569 and frontend 802 pass. Checked by hand against a local API: the quota message appears on the upload page, and the warning appears in English and 简体中文 for a shared `setup.exe`. `THREAT_MODEL` T-D2 and F-09 are now Mitigated, and `PERFORMANCE_TESTING` says to turn the account limits off for a capacity run.
- 测试：每项限制都有单元测试（在写入存储前拒绝、恰好用满配额时允许、24 小时窗口），也测试了 Agent 规则的分级和危险扩展名判断；后端 569 个、前端 802 个测试全部通过。已在本地 API 上手动验证：上传页面会显示配额提示；对于别人分享的 `setup.exe`，中英文界面都会弹出警告。`THREAT_MODEL` 中的 T-D2 和 F-09 已改为"已缓解"，`PERFORMANCE_TESTING` 也写明了做容量测试时要关闭账户限额。

### Presentation talking points in English, a reflection document, and README/TODO brought up to date / 答辩素材英文版、反思文档，以及 README/TODO 同步更新

- `docs/presentation/TALKING_POINTS.md` is the English version of the talking points, which now open with how to spend the 12 minutes along the nine parts the unit asked for (Ed #66) and treat the test-night operations as engineering evidence rather than project management. New `docs/presentation/REFLECTION.md` / `.zh-CN.md` record what is still missing (from a first-time-user walkthrough, the #140 numbers, the threat model and the presentation guidance), each with its fix, priority and status, and the order decided on 2026-10-04: file-row actions, share confirmation with key fingerprint, two-step registration, first-login checklist, the DB-pool fix and re-measure, a tamper demo, a ZAP scan, then the demo script, slides and the NFR2 statement. The usability study needs the supervisor's ethics answer and a pilot first, as #115 already says.

- README (both languages) now describes the upload encryption steps and the measured staging capacity and links the presentation documents. TODO (both languages) marks #139 and #140 done with their evidence and adds a "Final presentation and FYP Award" section with the prioritised items; the upload limits in PR #157 stay unchecked until merged and deployed. Documentation only.
- README（中英文）补充了上传时的加密步骤展示和 staging 实测容量，并链接了答辩文档。TODO（中英文）把 #139 和 #140 标为完成并附上证据，新增"Final Presentation 与 FYP Award"一节，按优先级列出待办；PR #157 的上传限额在合并部署之前保持未勾选。本次只改文档。

### Show the real encryption steps while a file uploads / 上传时展示真实的加密步骤

- Encrypting a few MB in the browser takes tens of milliseconds, so the upload used to finish the moment the button was pressed and looked as if nothing had been encrypted. The upload page now shows each real step for the file in progress (read in the browser, encrypt with the chosen algorithm and self-check, wrap the file key with RSA-OAEP for the owner and the recovery key, upload the ciphertext with a real progress bar), each with its measured duration, and then the file's first 16 bytes beside the first 16 bytes the server receives. Nothing is slowed down to look busy: each step stays on screen for at least 0.35 s (batches of five files or fewer only), and the times shown exclude that pause.
- 在浏览器里加密几 MB 的文件只要几十毫秒，所以以前一按按钮上传就完成了，看起来像根本没加密。现在上传页会展示当前文件的每个真实步骤（在浏览器中读取、用所选算法加密并自检、用 RSA-OAEP 把文件密钥包装给本人和恢复密钥、上传密文并显示真实进度），每一步标出实测耗时，最后把文件的前 16 个字节和服务器收到的前 16 个字节并排显示。没有为了“看起来在忙”而故意放慢：每一步最少停留 0.35 秒（只对 5 个文件以内的批次生效），显示的耗时不包含这段停留。
- `prepareUpload` / `encryptAndUpload` take an optional event listener (`stage`, `timed`, `sample`, `upload-progress`) and `fileApi.uploadEncrypted` reports axios upload progress; existing callers are unchanged and the button labels the browser tests use are kept. The byte sample is a 16-byte copy that stays in the browser. A unit test checks the stage order and that the sample is the real plaintext and ciphertext; checked by hand in English and 简体中文, at desktop and 375 px widths. New strings are in `ui-strings.json` and `zh-CN.dict.json`.
- `prepareUpload` / `encryptAndUpload` 新增可选的事件监听参数（`stage`、`timed`、`sample`、`upload-progress`），`fileApi.uploadEncrypted` 会上报 axios 的上传进度；现有调用方不受影响，浏览器测试依赖的按钮文字保持不变。字节样本是 16 字节的副本，只留在浏览器里。单元测试检查了阶段顺序，以及样本确实是真实的明文和密文；已在英文和简体中文、桌面和 375 px 宽度下手动验证。新增文字已加入 `ui-strings.json` 和 `zh-CN.dict.json`。
- Added `docs/presentation/TALKING_POINTS.zh-CN.md`: what to say about the encryption, the #140 capacity numbers, the #139 fix, monitoring, upload abuse and why the server cannot scan for viruses, with the evidence for each and the questions judges are likely to ask. Items not yet done are marked as planned.
- 新增 `docs/presentation/TALKING_POINTS.zh-CN.md`：整理了加密、#140 容量数据、#139 修复、监控告警、防恶意上传，以及服务器为什么不能扫毒这几部分该怎么讲，每条都附上证据和评委可能问的问题。尚未完成的项目标为“计划中”。

### Staging capacity and performance measured (#140) / 在 staging 上实测容量与性能（#140）

- Ran the full `loadtest/` matrix against staging on the night of 2026-10-03/04 (release `c041880`, including the #139 retry) and committed the evidence under `docs/performance/`: the report, charts, every run's `summary.json`, the server sampler CSV and the run logs. 22,264 operations; 5,094 downloads decrypted and SHA-256-compared with the original, 0 integrity failures; every file type up to 500 MiB round-trips byte-exact. This answers meeting items 4–6 with measurements instead of "it supports many users"; the conclusions are filled in at the end of `docs/PERFORMANCE_TESTING.md` (EN and zh-CN).
- 2026-10-03 至 04 夜间在 staging（发布版本 `c041880`，已包含 #139 的重试）上跑完了完整的 `loadtest/` 测试矩阵，证据提交在 `docs/performance/`：报告、图表、每次运行的 `summary.json`、服务器采样 CSV 和运行日志。共 22,264 次操作；5,094 次下载全部解密并与原文件做 SHA-256 比对，完整性失败 0 次；所有文件类型在 500 MiB 以内都能逐字节一致地往返。会议第 4–6 项因此有了实测数据，而不是“支持多人同时使用”；结论已填入 `docs/PERFORMANCE_TESTING.md` 末尾（中英文）。
- The first limit is the API's 15-connection database pool, not the hardware: at 100 concurrent users 65–78% of uploads fail with HTTP 500 (`QueuePool limit ... timeout 30.00`) while server CPU averages 3%; up to 50 users the server returned no error, and the load recovered to 0 errors by itself when it dropped back. Every transfer figure is bounded by the generator's home link (~24 Mbit/s up, ~13 Mbit/s down; the VPS port itself is ~100 Mbit/s), and the report's "Test conditions" section says which failures are the generator's SSH tunnel rather than the server. No deadlock (1213) or lock-wait timeout (1205) appeared in 28,540 requests.
- 第一个瓶颈是 API 的 15 个数据库连接，而不是硬件：100 个并发用户时 65–78% 的上传返回 HTTP 500（`QueuePool limit ... timeout 30.00`），而服务器 CPU 平均只有 3%；50 个用户以内服务器没有返回任何错误，负载降回后自动恢复到零错误。所有传输类数字都受压测端家庭网络限制（上行约 24 Mbit/s、下行约 13 Mbit/s；VPS 网口本身约 100 Mbit/s），报告的“Test conditions”一节说明了哪些失败来自压测端的 SSH 隧道而不是服务器。28,540 个请求中没有出现死锁（1213）或锁等待超时（1205）。
- Server-side changes were temporary and are reverted: a temporary sshd `PermitOpen 127.0.0.1:8000` exception for the tunnel; the backup, offsite backup, backup-verify, reconcile, integrity-sweep, security-agent and auto-deploy timers paused for the run. All 100 test accounts and their files were deleted through the API, the backup and offsite backup were then run by hand, every timer was restarted and the sshd exception removed. Two follow-ups are recorded in the report: check whether file transfers hold a DB connection for their whole duration, and log when `db_retry` retries so the next run can see it.
- 服务器上的改动都是临时的，已全部恢复：为隧道加的 sshd `PermitOpen 127.0.0.1:8000` 例外；压测期间暂停的备份、异地备份、备份校验、reconcile、完整性巡检、安全 Agent 和自动部署定时器。100 个测试账号及其文件已通过 API 全部删除，随后手动补跑了备份和异地备份，所有定时器已重新启动，sshd 例外已撤销。报告里记录了两项后续工作：确认文件传输是否在整个过程中占用数据库连接；在 `db_retry` 重试时打日志，让下次压测能观察到重试。

### Retry the two MySQL deadlocks the load-test tool found (#139) / 为压测工具发现的两个 MySQL 死锁加上重试（#139）

- Both deadlocks reported in #139 aborted the whole transaction and reached the user as HTTP 500: two brand-new accounts signing in at the same instant could deadlock on the gap lock the session-limit check takes when neither has a session row yet, and a download racing a share could deadlock between the audit chain head and a `users` row. Both are now caught and retried: `app/core/db_retry.py` recognizes MySQL 1213 (deadlock) and 1205 (lock-wait timeout) on an `OperationalError`, rolls the session back, and reruns the whole attempt — up to three tries — since a deadlock aborts everything InnoDB had done, not just the one statement that lost. `AuthService.login`, `IntegrityService.open_verified_for_download`, and `SharingService.share` now go through it.
- #139 报告的两个死锁都会让整个事务被回滚，用户看到的是 HTTP 500：两个全新账户同时登录时，若双方都还没有会话行，会在会话上限检查所加的间隙锁上死锁；下载和分享竞争时，可能在审计链头与某条 `users` 行之间死锁。现在两者都会被捕获并重试：`app/core/db_retry.py` 识别 `OperationalError` 中的 MySQL 1213（死锁）和 1205（锁等待超时），回滚会话后重新执行整次尝试（最多三次）——因为死锁回滚的是 InnoDB 已做的全部工作，而不只是输掉的那条语句。`AuthService.login`、`IntegrityService.open_verified_for_download` 与 `SharingService.share` 现在都经过这一层重试。
- The download retry only redoes the small transactional tail (recording the integrity check, moving file status, appending to the audit chain); it never re-reads or re-verifies the ciphertext object, which can be up to 500 MB. Login and share redo the whole attempt, including the cheap throttle/row checks, trading a rare extra Argon2id verification for a simple, obviously-correct retry boundary.
- 下载的重试只重做很小的事务尾段（记录完整性检查结果、更新文件状态、写入审计链），绝不会重新读取或重新校验密文对象（可能高达 500 MB）。登录与分享则重做整次尝试，包括开销不大的限流/行检查，用极少数情况下多一次 Argon2id 校验换取一个简单、明显正确的重试边界。
- `tests/unit/test_db_retry.py` covers the retry helper in isolation (retries exactly on 1213/1205, gives up after the attempt budget, never touches an unrelated exception). `tests/integration/test_concurrency_mysql.py::test_two_simultaneous_fresh_account_logins_never_surface_a_deadlock` races two fresh accounts' logins against real MySQL 8.4 in a loop; on the unfixed code this reproduced the deadlock in roughly 7 of 10 iterations, and after the fix it is clean every time. The download-vs-share deadlock stayed below 1% even before the fix (matching #139's own measurement) and was not reliably forced in a test; the retry mechanism covers it by construction and the full `loadtest/` `mixed` scenario on staging (#140) is the real-load check.
- `tests/unit/test_db_retry.py` 单独测试了重试辅助函数（只在 1213/1205 上重试、超过次数预算后放弃、从不处理无关异常）。`tests/integration/test_concurrency_mysql.py::test_two_simultaneous_fresh_account_logins_never_surface_a_deadlock` 在真实 MySQL 8.4 上循环让两个全新账户并发登录：未修复前约 7/10 次会复现死锁，修复后每次都干净通过。下载与分享之间的死锁在修复前发生率本就低于 1%（与 #139 自己的测量一致），未能在测试中稳定强制复现；重试机制从设计上就覆盖了它，而 staging 上完整的 `loadtest/` `mixed` 场景（#140）才是真实负载下的验证。
- Backend-only change: no API contract, migration, or deployment change. `IntegrityService.check` and `SharingService.revoke` share the same append-then-commit shape and carry the same theoretical risk; they were not touched here since neither has been observed to deadlock, and are left for #140's staging run to confirm one way or the other.
- 仅涉及后端：不涉及 API 约定、迁移或部署变更。`IntegrityService.check` 与 `SharingService.revoke` 有相同的“写入后提交”结构，理论上存在同样的风险，但由于尚未观测到它们实际死锁，本次未一并改动，留待 #140 的 staging 压测来验证是否需要处理。

### UI layout and navigation consistency (Junyan#9) / 界面排版与导航一致性（Junyan#9）

- Shortened bilingual encryption copy and aligned feature cards to equal heights; extracted `FeatureLane.vue` so Core Features keeps autoplay while also supporting touch pan, mouse drag and keyboard focus/scroll, without a visible native scrollbar, so visitors can inspect each card deliberately.
- 精简中英文加密说明并对齐等高功能卡片；抽出 `FeatureLane.vue`，核心功能区在保留自动滚动的同时支持触屏滑动、鼠标拖动与键盘聚焦/浏览，且不显示浏览器原生滚动条，方便逐张查看。
- Reordered hero highlights with single-line captions (`Encrypted before upload` / `Standard ciphers` / `0 Key` / size), moved the file lifecycle explainer (`SecurityStory`) from the home page to below Upload, and applied shared `.product-page` / `.product-heading` Contact us styling to Upload, Education, Help, Access Records and My Files.
- 调整首页指标顺序并保持说明单行（上传前加密 / 标准算法 / 0 密钥 / 大小），将文件生命周期说明（`SecurityStory`）从首页移到上传区下方，并为上传、教育、帮助、访问记录与我的文件统一采用联系我们页的 `.product-page` / `.product-heading` 风格。
- Grouped language and theme: signed-in users get both in the account menu; signed-out users open a settings icon (`appearance-settings`) that holds language plus Light/Dark/System. The language-switch browser test follows that guest control. File table rows use vertical middle alignment on desktop.
- 归组语言与主题：已登录用户在账户菜单中同时切换；未登录用户通过设置图标（`appearance-settings`）打开含语言与 Light/Dark/System 的面板。语言切换浏览器测试跟随该访客控件。桌面端文件表行改为垂直居中对齐。
- Synced `ui-strings.json` / `zh-CN.dict.json` for the shortened feature and hero copy so Chinese still matches the new English.
- 同步更新 `ui-strings.json` / `zh-CN.dict.json` 中缩短后的功能与首页文案，确保中文与新英文一致。
- Validation: frontend typecheck and production build passed; all 13 i18n tests passed. Local Chrome checks covered 1440px/390px layouts, equal feature-card heights, keyboard scrolling, guest language settings and signed-in menus/pages with API fixtures. Fixtures are not live integration or deployment evidence.
- 验证：前端类型检查与生产构建通过，13 项国际化测试通过；本地 Chrome 检查覆盖 1440px/390px 布局、功能卡片等高、键盘滚动、访客语言设置，以及使用 API 模拟数据的登录菜单和页面。模拟数据检查不作为真实集成或部署证据。
- Local UI change only (Junyan#9): no API, migration, secret or deployment changes; no Issue is closed without deployment evidence.
- 本次仅修改本地界面（Junyan#9）：不涉及 API、迁移、密钥或部署变更；未在缺少部署证据时关闭任何 Issue。

### 简体中文: the pages that stayed English, and the team's own names (#74) / 简体中文：补齐仍显示英文的页面，并使用团队自己的中文名（#74）

- After #150 went live, the Education articles and algorithm details, the four quick-start steps and several styled sentences still showed English, and the team page used machine transliterations. Three causes, each fixed: articles are Markdown rendered into many small nodes, so they are now translated whole before rendering (lines, lists and tables kept); sentences built with `'…' + '…'` and sentences with a bold or italic word were collected in pieces, so they are now collected and translated as one sentence; and list items with a `•` or step number in front now match their entry.
- #150 上线后，Education 的文章与算法详情、快速上手的四个步骤以及若干带加粗/斜体的句子仍显示英文，团队页的人名是机器音译。原因有三，均已修复：文章是被渲染成许多小节点的 Markdown，现在在渲染前整篇翻译（保留换行、列表和表格）；用 `'…' + '…'` 拼接的句子和含加粗/斜体词的句子过去被拆成碎片收集，现在作为整句收集和翻译；带 `•` 或步骤编号前缀的列表项现在也能匹配到对应条目。
- The team page now reads 项目导师, 马来西亚莫纳什大学信息技术学院, and the members' own names (温硕勋, 李俊言, 黄建幸, 陈泰); the supervisor's name stays Asad Malik 博士 rather than a transliteration. These are in the translation glossary, so future strings use them too. Field placeholders (the Contact us message box) are now translated.
- 团队页现在使用“项目导师”“马来西亚莫纳什大学信息技术学院”以及成员本人的中文名（温硕勋、李俊言、黄建幸、陈泰）；导师姓名保留为 “Asad Malik 博士”，不做音译。这些已写入翻译术语表，以后的新文本也会沿用。输入框的提示文字（如 Contact us 的留言框）现在也会翻译。
- `scripts/find-untranslated.mjs` opens every public page in Chinese, presses each button that reveals content, and lists the English still showing: after this change only names, algorithm identifiers, GitHub handles and email addresses remain, which are meant to stay as written.
- 新增 `scripts/find-untranslated.mjs`：以中文打开每个公开页面，点开所有会展开内容的按钮，列出仍显示的英文。改动之后只剩人名、算法标识、GitHub 用户名和邮箱地址，这些本就应保持原样。

### English / 简体中文 language switch (#74), and #76 closed / English / 简体中文 语言切换（#74），以及关闭 #76

- The interface can be switched between English and 简体中文 from the header, the phone menu and Settings. The choice is remembered on the device and restored before the first render, so a Chinese visitor never sees the English flash past; an unknown saved value falls back to English. Navigation, sign-in, registration, password reset, Settings and the account dialogs use keyed vue-i18n messages; the rest of the interface is shown in Chinese from a dictionary that swaps whole text nodes, chosen over rewriting every component because it covers the whole product for a fraction of the change.
- 界面可在页头、手机菜单和设置页切换 English / 简体中文。选择按设备记住，并在首次渲染前恢复，中文用户不会先看到英文闪过；无法识别的保存值回退为英文。导航、登录、注册、重置密码、设置和账户相关弹框使用 vue-i18n 键值消息；其余界面通过词典整段替换文字节点显示中文——之所以不逐个组件改写，是因为这样只需很小的改动就能覆盖整个产品。
- The Chinese is drafted at build time by Zhipu `glm-4-flash` (`frontend/scripts/translate_ui_strings.py`, run on the staging VPS where the key already is); the browser never calls a translation service and no user text is sent. Every reply is checked for placeholders, algorithm names and the security glossary from #74 (加密口令, 私钥, 撤销, 密文…), and unit tests check the same plus key parity and that every message compiles. File names, usernames and other user content are marked `translate="no"` and never change. The Chinese has not had a full human review; see `docs/I18N.md`.
- 中文在构建阶段由智谱 `glm-4-flash` 起草（`frontend/scripts/translate_ui_strings.py`，在已有 key 的 staging VPS 上运行）；浏览器从不调用翻译服务，也不发送任何用户文本。每条译文都会校验占位符、算法名称以及 #74 规定的安全术语（加密口令、私钥、撤销、密文……），单元测试同样检查这些，并检查键是否齐全、每条消息能否编译。文件名、用户名等用户内容标记为 `translate="no"`，始终原样显示。中文尚未经过完整人工审校；见 `docs/I18N.md`。
- #76 (phone usability) is closed: its last open criterion, Android Chrome and iOS Safari, was checked by running the phone specs in WebKit as an iPhone 13 and in Chromium as a Pixel 7 (all pass). No physical phone was used; that limit is recorded on the Issue and in `docs/E2E_TESTING.md`.
- #76（手机可用性）已关闭：最后一条未满足的标准“Android Chrome 与 iOS Safari”，通过在 WebKit（模拟 iPhone 13）和 Chromium（模拟 Pixel 7）下运行手机测试完成核对（全部通过）。没有使用实体手机；这一局限已记录在 Issue 和 `docs/E2E_TESTING.md` 中。

### Help assistant v2: questions about your own account (#148) / Help assistant v2：回答关于自己账户的问题（#148）

- Signed in, the Help assistant now answers from the visitor's own account: "who has access to budget.xlsx?", "when was it last downloaded?", "which of my files have I shared?", "what has been shared with me?", "are any of my files marked Modified?", "any failed sign-ins?" and "what is in my account?". v1 could only explain where to look; v2 looks, and links to the file in My Files (`/files?q=<name>&tab=shared` now opens the right tab searched for it). A question that names no file, or matches several, gets a short list of the visitor's newest files to pick from.
- 登录后，Help assistant 现在能根据访客自己的账户作答：“who has access to budget.xlsx?”“它最近一次是什么时候被下载的？”“我分享过哪些文件？”“别人分享了什么给我？”“我的文件有没有被标记为 Modified？”“有没有失败的登录？”以及“我的账户里有什么？”。v1 只能告诉用户去哪里看；v2 直接查，并链接到 My Files 里的对应文件（`/files?q=<名称>&tab=shared` 现在会打开正确的标签页并搜索该文件）。没有说明是哪个文件、或匹配到多个文件时，会列出访客最新的几个文件供选择。
- Every check is read-only and uses only the requests My Files and Access Records already make, under the visitor's own session, so it can never show more than those pages; recipients and full history are only looked up for files the visitor owns. There is still no language model and the question is not sent: at most a file name goes to the existing `GET /files?search=`. An account question runs at once; a general question with a check attached ("who downloaded my file?") shows the general answer and a button, so nothing is read until asked. Results are not stored, and signing out clears the remembered questions so the next person at the tab does not see them.
- 每项检查都是只读的，只使用 My Files 和 Access Records 已有的请求，并在访客自己的会话下发出，因此不可能显示超出这些页面的内容；接收者列表和完整历史只对访客自己拥有的文件查询。仍然不使用大语言模型，问题本身也不会发出去：最多只把文件名交给现有的 `GET /files?search=`。账户类问题会立即检查；附带检查的一般性问题（“who downloaded my file?”）先显示一般回答和一个按钮，用户点了才读取。结果不保存；退出登录会清除记住的问题，同一标签页的下一位用户看不到。
- An LLM rewording layer was considered for v2 and not built, because it would send questions to a third party. Every v1 phrasing test still passes, and 18 new check tests against a fake API and a browser test (ask about the uploaded file at 360 and 320 px, follow the link, pick a file from the list) cover v2.
- v2 曾考虑加入大语言模型改写层，但没有做，因为那会把问题发给第三方。v1 原有的问法测试全部仍然通过；另有 18 个基于模拟 API 的检查测试和一个浏览器测试（在 360 和 320 px 下询问已上传的文件、点击链接、从列表中选择文件）覆盖 v2。

### Help assistant: a floating helper that answers how-to, how-it-works and comparison questions (#146) / Help assistant：右下角悬浮助手，回答操作、原理和对比类问题（#146）

- A round button at the bottom right of every page opens the Help assistant: ask "how do I share a file?", "I forgot my passphrase" or "why not just use Google Drive?" and get a two-to-four-sentence answer, numbered steps for a how-to, and one to three links to the right page or video. New users had to find the right page among the explainer, the videos and the Education articles; the assistant answers in place and points there. It is named Help assistant, not agent or AI, so it stays distinct from the security agent the supervisor's feedback was about.
- 每个页面右下角新增圆形按钮，点开即是 Help assistant：问“怎么分享文件”“忘了加密口令怎么办”“为什么不直接用 Google Drive”，会得到二到四句的回答、操作类问题的编号步骤，以及一到三个指向对应页面或视频的链接。此前新用户要在讲解动画、视频和 Education 文章之间自己找答案；助手就地回答并指向原页面。它叫 Help assistant 而不是 agent 或 AI，以便与导师反馈所针对的安全 Agent 区分开。
- No language model and no network call: questions are matched in the browser (normalising, light stemming, synonyms, typo correction and BM25) against 29 curated intents plus the existing Education articles, algorithm pages, explainer scenes and picture-guide steps, read rather than copied. Nothing is sent or logged, and the panel says so; only the open state and the last five questions are kept, in `sessionStorage`. A question it cannot match gets "I'm not sure", the closest three answers, and links to Help and Contact us, rather than a confident wrong answer.
- 不使用大语言模型、不发任何网络请求：问题在浏览器内（规范化、轻量词干化、同义词、拼写纠错、BM25）与 29 条人工整理的意图以及现有的 Education 文章、算法页、讲解场景和图文引导步骤进行匹配，这些内容是直接读取而非复制。问题不会被发送或记录，面板上也写明了这一点；只在 `sessionStorage` 中保存面板开关状态和最近五个问题。匹配不上的问题会得到 “I'm not sure”、最接近的三条答案以及 Help 和 Contact us 链接，而不是一个自信但错误的回答。
- The Google Drive, WhatsApp and email comparisons state only what their own documentation says, and link to it: Drive encrypts in transit and at rest with keys Google manages, client-side encryption is limited to some paid Workspace editions, and WhatsApp is end-to-end encrypted with the Signal protocol, so the difference is per-file revoke, access history and opt-in encrypted backups, not secrecy. None claims SecureView is "more secure", and each keeps the limit that a recipient keeps a copy already downloaded.
- 与 Google Drive、WhatsApp 和电子邮件的对比只陈述对方官方文档写明的内容，并附上链接：Drive 在传输和存储时加密但密钥由 Google 管理，客户端加密仅限部分付费 Workspace 版本；WhatsApp 使用 Signal 协议端到端加密，因此区别在于按文件撤销、访问记录和需手动开启的加密备份，而不在于保密性。所有对比都不声称 SecureView “更安全”，并保留“接收者已下载的副本无法收回”这一局限。
- Answers link straight to the right place: `/help?video=<id>#videos` opens the Help page on that video (the video list moved to `src/data/help-videos.ts`), and `/education?article=<id>#article-<id>` and `?algorithm=<id>#algorithm-detail` open the Education page on that article or algorithm. Signed out, a link to a page behind sign-in says "(sign in first)". The route table moved to `src/router/routes.ts` so a unit test can resolve every answer link against the real router.
- 回答直接链接到对应位置：`/help?video=<id>#videos` 在 Help 页打开对应视频（视频列表移到 `src/data/help-videos.ts`），`/education?article=<id>#article-<id>` 和 `?algorithm=<id>#algorithm-detail` 在 Education 页打开对应文章或算法。未登录时，指向需登录页面的链接会标注 “(sign in first)”。路由表移到 `src/router/routes.ts`，以便单元测试用真实路由校验每一个回答链接。
- Tests: 337 unit tests check that every intent's phrasings find it, that 29 realistic questions (typos included) find the expected answer, that six off-topic questions get the fallback, and that every link is a real route, anchor, article, algorithm or video. Seven browser tests cover ask-read-follow-close, keyboard-only use with a focus trap, and 360 and 320 px phones, including that the button never covers Start Encryption or the My Files card actions. How to add an intent: `docs/HELP_ASSISTANT.md`.
- 测试：337 个单元测试验证每条意图的示例问法都能命中自己、29 个真实问题（含拼写错误）命中预期答案、六个无关问题得到兜底回复，以及每个链接都是真实的路由、锚点、文章、算法或视频。七个浏览器测试覆盖“提问—阅读—跳转—关闭”、仅键盘操作与焦点锁定，以及 360 和 320 px 手机宽度，包括按钮不会遮挡 Start Encryption 和 My Files 卡片操作。新增意图的方法见 `docs/HELP_ASSISTANT.md`。

### My Files: folders for shared files, server-side tabs, tidy actions, re-recorded videos / 我的文件：分享文件可放入文件夹、分页改到服务端、操作按钮对齐、重录视频

- Files shared with you can now be filed in your own folders. Folders used to hold only files you uploaded, because placement lived on the file row, which belongs to its owner; with many people sharing with one account, "Shared with me" could not be organised at all. A recipient's placement now lives on their own access grant (`file_access_grants.folder_id`, migration `0010_shared_file_folders`), so each person files a shared file where they like without changing where its owner keeps it. The Shared tab gets the folder bar, the Folder column and per-tab folder counts; deleting a folder leaves its shared files in no folder, as it already did for your own.
- 分享给你的文件现在可以放进你自己的文件夹。此前文件夹只能放你上传的文件，因为归属信息存放在属于文件所有者的文件记录上；当很多人向同一账户分享时，“Shared with me” 完全无法整理。现在接收者的归属存放在其自己的访问授权上（`file_access_grants.folder_id`，迁移 `0010_shared_file_folders`），每个人都可以按自己的方式归类共享文件，而不会改变所有者的归类。Shared 标签页有了文件夹栏、Folder 列和分标签的文件夹计数；删除文件夹后，其中的共享文件变为“无文件夹”，与自己的文件一致。
- The two tabs are now split on the server. The page used to fetch one mixed list and divide it in the browser, so "Shared with me" showed only the shared files that happened to be on the current page, both tab counts were counts of that page, and "Total 15 encrypted files" on the Shared tab counted your own files too. `GET /files` takes `scope=mine|shared` and returns `owned_total` and `shared_total` for the tab labels.
- 两个标签页现在在服务端拆分。此前页面先取回一份混合列表、再在浏览器里拆分，所以 “Shared with me” 只显示恰好落在当前页的共享文件，两个标签的计数也只是当前页的数量，而 Shared 标签上的 “Total 15 encrypted files” 把你自己的文件也算了进去。`GET /files` 新增 `scope=mine|shared` 参数，并返回供标签使用的 `owned_total` 与 `shared_total`。
- A shared file's four actions now sit in a 2×2 block (your own files keep 3×2) instead of wrapping as three and one, and the empty space where the Folder column used to be missing on the Shared tab is gone.
- 共享文件的四个操作按钮现在排成 2×2（自己的文件仍是 3×2），不再是三个加一个的换行；Shared 标签页原先因缺少 Folder 列而留下的空白也消失了。
- The Help page walkthrough videos were re-recorded from the real product: the cipher step names all six choices, and the share video now ends with the recipient filing the shared file in a folder of their own.
- Help 页面的演示视频已从真实产品重新录制：选择加密算法的步骤列出全部六种选择，分享视频最后增加了接收者把共享文件放入自己文件夹的步骤。

### Two more file encryption choices: AES-256-GCM-SIV and XChaCha20-Poly1305 / 新增两种文件加密：AES-256-GCM-SIV 与 XChaCha20-Poly1305

- Meeting item 3 asked for more encryption algorithms. The upload page now offers six authenticated (AEAD) choices: AES-256-GCM (still the default), AES-256-GCM-SIV (RFC 8452, survives a repeated nonce), ChaCha20-Poly1305, XChaCha20-Poly1305 (24-byte nonce), AES-192-GCM and AES-128-GCM. The two new ones come from `@noble/ciphers`, already a dependency, so nothing new is installed. Both are checked byte for byte against independent implementations (OpenSSL, libsodium) and the RFC 8452 vectors in `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json`.
- 会议第 3 项要求增加加密算法。上传页现在提供六种认证加密（AEAD）选择：AES-256-GCM（仍为默认）、AES-256-GCM-SIV（RFC 8452，可承受 nonce 重复）、ChaCha20-Poly1305、XChaCha20-Poly1305（24 字节 nonce）、AES-192-GCM 与 AES-128-GCM。两种新算法来自已有依赖 `@noble/ciphers`，无需安装新库，并在 `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` 中与独立实现（OpenSSL、libsodium）及 RFC 8452 向量逐字节比对。
- XChaCha20-Poly1305 is the first suite with a nonce other than 12 bytes, so the nonce length is now per suite: the API rejects a nonce of the wrong length for the chosen algorithm, and migration `0009_more_aead_suites` makes the `files` CHECK constraint say the same (24 bytes for XChaCha20, 12 for the rest; `files.iv` was already wide enough). Its downgrade refuses while any file uses a new suite, rather than delete intact files.
- XChaCha20-Poly1305 是第一个 nonce 不是 12 字节的算法，所以 nonce 长度改为按算法而定：API 会拒绝与所选算法长度不符的 nonce，迁移 `0009_more_aead_suites` 让 `files` 的 CHECK 约束表达同样的规则（XChaCha20 为 24 字节，其余为 12 字节；`files.iv` 原本就够宽）。只要还有文件使用新算法，降级就会拒绝执行，而不是删除完好的文件。
- Stated honestly in the UI and docs: every file already gets its own random key, so a nonce can never repeat in SecureView whichever option is picked; the two new modes are an extra margin and a comparison, not a fix. AES-256-GCM-SIV runs in JavaScript and is about ten times slower than the browser's built-in AES-GCM (0.51 s against 0.07 s for 20 MiB on an Apple M4), which the upload page now says instead of "a little slower".
- 界面和文档如实说明：每个文件本来就有自己的随机密钥，所以无论选哪种，SecureView 里都不会出现 nonce 重复；两种新模式是额外的安全余量和对比，而不是修复某个问题。AES-256-GCM-SIV 在 JavaScript 中运行，比浏览器内置的 AES-GCM 慢约十倍（Apple M4 上 20 MiB 为 0.51 秒对 0.07 秒），上传页现在照实说明，而不是写“稍慢一点”。

- On a phone, the longer cipher names made the upload page scroll sideways (by 10 px at 360 px wide, caught by the phone test in CI): a grid column cannot shrink below its widest unbreakable content, and the dropdown's longest option set that. The columns are now `minmax(0, 1fr)` and the dropdown may shrink, which the phone test confirms down to 320 px.
- 在手机上，更长的算法名让上传页出现横向滚动（360 px 宽时多出 10 px，被 CI 中的手机测试发现）：网格列不能比其中最宽的不可断行内容更窄，而下拉框最长的选项决定了这个宽度。现在列宽改为 `minmax(0, 1fr)`，下拉框也可以收窄，手机测试确认在 320 px 宽度下同样正常。

### Education: the new algorithms, a cipher guide, and corrections / Education：新增算法、加密选择指南及内容更正

- The algorithm explorer gains AES-256-GCM-SIV and XChaCha20-Poly1305, each with how it works, strengths, limits, facts and its own animation (synthetic IV; extended nonce), and ChaCha20 and XChaCha20 are grouped as one family. A new article, "Which File Encryption Should I Choose?", compares all six options (key, nonce, where it runs, when to pick it) and says plainly that the default is the right choice when unsure.
- 算法浏览器新增 AES-256-GCM-SIV 与 XChaCha20-Poly1305，各有原理、优点、局限、要点和专属动画（合成 IV；扩展 nonce），ChaCha20 与 XChaCha20 归为同一族。新文章 “Which File Encryption Should I Choose?” 对比全部六种选择（密钥、nonce、运行位置、适用场景），并明确说明拿不准时保持默认即可。
- Corrections: the SHA steps said SHA-512 used 1024-bit blocks and then described "each 512-bit chunk"; they now give block sizes and round counts for both. "A matching hash proves the file is unaltered" now says it holds only if the reference digest is trusted (SecureView also keeps it in the audit log). The hashing table no longer says ciphertext is "input + padding" (GCM and ChaCha20 do not pad). Key advice no longer says every AES key needs 256 bits, and passphrase advice favours length over symbol rules. The RSA entry mentions that SecureView also signs with RSA-PSS.
- 内容更正：SHA 步骤先说 SHA-512 用 1024 位分块，后面又写成“每个 512 位分块”，现在分别给出两者的分块大小和轮数；“哈希一致就证明文件未被修改”现在注明前提是参考哈希可信（SecureView 也把它记入审计日志）；哈希对比表不再说密文是“输入 + 填充”（GCM 与 ChaCha20 不填充）；密钥建议不再说所有 AES 密钥都需要 256 位，口令建议改为长度优先于符号规则；RSA 条目补充 SecureView 也使用 RSA-PSS 签名。

### Site-wide accuracy pass: navigation, home, footer, Contact us and Education / 全站表述准确性检查：导航、首页、页脚、联系我们与 Education

- Every page was rendered and its visible text checked against what the product does. The header now reads Product, How it works, Help, Education, Contact us (signed in: Upload, My Files, Access Records, Help, Education, Contact us), and the footer mirrors it. The home hero says what SecureView is ("Share files no server can read") instead of "Encryption and Sharing"; its four figures replace "Easy Use · Architecture" with ones that mean something; signed out, its main button is Get started (to registration) because Encrypt Your Files only bounced visitors to sign in; Learn More became See how it works.
- 逐页渲染并对照产品的实际行为检查了所有可见文字。页眉改为 Product、How it works、Help、Education、Contact us（登录后为 Upload、My Files、Access Records、Help、Education、Contact us），页脚与之一致。首页主标题从 “Encryption and Sharing” 改为说明 SecureView 是什么的 “Share files no server can read”；四个数字指标把 “Easy Use · Architecture” 换成了有实际含义的内容；未登录时主按钮改为 Get started（去注册），因为原来的 Encrypt Your Files 只会把访客转到登录页；Learn More 改为 See how it works。
- The footer's Privacy Policy, Terms of Service, GitHub, X and Discord links all pointed at "#" (a click landed on whatever page the router chose); they are gone, replaced by real entries (the explainer, videos, picture guide, Meet the team, Send feedback, Back to top), with the marketing line ("enterprise-grade … cutting-edge … always secure") replaced by what the product does, three concrete guarantees, and who built it.
- 页脚的 Privacy Policy、Terms of Service、GitHub、X、Discord 链接都只指向 “#”（点了会落到路由随便选的页面），已删除，换成真实入口（原理动画、视频、截图教程、Meet the team、Send feedback、Back to top）；原来 “enterprise-grade … cutting-edge … always secure” 的宣传语换成产品实际做的事、三条具体保证，以及团队信息。
- The Contact us form pretended to send: it showed "Our team has received it" while nothing left the page. It now opens the visitor's email app with the note addressed to the project lead (the address already on his card), and says so.
- Contact us 的反馈表单此前是假提交：页面显示 “Our team has received it”，但实际上什么都没发出去。现在会打开访客自己的邮件应用，把留言填好发给项目负责人（地址本来就印在他的卡片上），页面也如实说明。
- Education corrections: AES-128 is an upload option (it said it was not); AES-192 is offered where the browser supports it (two pages called it "out of scope for now"); AES-GCM decryption runs AES forwards over a counter, not inverse rounds; RSA's security is factoring the product of two primes, and real RSA always uses padding (SecureView: 3072-bit RSA-OAEP); SHA-256 is not for passwords; Signal does not use ChaCha20 (OpenSSH does); a signature is not "the hash encrypted with the private key". The "Secure File Sharing Workflow" article described link expiry, download limits and sending keys by phone — none of which exist here — and now describes SecureView's real sharing. The SHA-256 article now says what is hashed (the stored ciphertext) and what proves the decrypted file (the authentication tag), and the Playground notes that the product never derives a file key from a password.
- Education 的更正：AES-128 是可选的上传算法（原文说没有）；AES-192 在浏览器支持时提供（两处写着“暂不涉及”）；AES-GCM 解密是对计数器正向运行 AES，不是逆向轮函数；RSA 的安全性在于分解两个素数的乘积，且实际的 RSA 必须加填充（SecureView 用 3072 位 RSA-OAEP）；SHA-256 不适合存密码；Signal 并不使用 ChaCha20（OpenSSH 用）；签名并不是“用私钥加密哈希”。“Secure File Sharing Workflow” 一文描述的链接过期、下载次数限制、电话发送密钥在本产品中都不存在，已改为 SecureView 实际的分享方式。SHA-256 一文现在说明哈希的是存储的密文、证明解密后文件完整的是认证标签；Playground 也注明本产品从不由密码派生文件密钥。
- Smaller fixes: the upload page said the server "never receives your file" (it receives the encrypted one) and that "neither key nor plaintext leaves this browser" (the sealed key does); the home features now say AES-192 depends on the browser; the quick start says the private key file lets you reset a forgotten passphrase yourself, and that losing both means nobody can recover the files.
- 其他小修正：上传页写着服务器 “never receives your file”（它收到的是加密后的文件）、“neither key nor plaintext leaves this browser”（封装后的密钥会离开）；首页功能说明注明 AES-192 取决于浏览器；快速上手说明私钥文件可让你自行重置忘记的口令，两者都丢失则任何人都无法恢复文件。

### Explaining SecureView: an animated explainer, real walkthrough videos, and an honest comparison / 讲清 SecureView：原理动画、真实操作视频与如实对比

- New `/how-it-works` page and a home-page section with an animated explainer: one file followed from Alice's browser through the server to Bob's in seven scenes (encrypted in the browser, the key sealed with her public key and an offline recovery copy, only noise on the server even for an intruder, sharing by re-sealing the key, Bob opening it with his own passphrase, a changed byte refused and restored from backup). It plays when scrolled into view, can be paused and stepped with buttons or the arrow keys, never autoplays with reduced motion, and has a full-screen `?present` mode for presentations. The captions and the claims live in one data file, so the animation and the video cannot drift apart. The site had screenshots of what to click but nothing that showed why the design is safe.
- 新增 `/how-it-works` 页面和首页板块，放了一段原理动画：用七个场景跟随一个文件从 Alice 的浏览器经过服务器到达 Bob 的浏览器（在浏览器里加密；密钥用她的公钥封装，另有一份离线恢复副本；服务器上即使入侵者也只看到乱码；分享靠重新封装密钥；Bob 用自己的口令打开；改动一个字节就会被拒绝，并可从备份恢复）。滚动到可见时自动播放，可用按钮或方向键暂停、逐步查看；开启“减少动态效果”时不自动播放；还有全屏 `?present` 模式供答辩演示。字幕和各项说法放在同一个数据文件里，动画和视频不会说法不一致。此前网站只有“点哪里”的截图，没有任何内容说明这个设计为什么安全。
- The Help page's two empty video slots ("Video demo still in development") now hold three captioned videos of about a minute each, recorded from the real product on a local stack with made-up accounts: the explainer, encrypt-and-upload, and share-then-open (the recipient decrypts with their own passphrase). Every step is also listed beside the video as text. `frontend/scripts/record-walkthroughs.mjs` re-records them after a UI change.
- Help 页原本两个空着的视频位（“Video demo still in development”）现在放了三段约一分钟、带字幕的视频，都是在本地环境用虚构账号从真实产品录制的：原理动画、加密上传、分享并打开（接收方用自己的口令解密）。每一步也以文字列在视频旁边。界面改动后可用 `frontend/scripts/record-walkthroughs.mjs` 重新录制。
- A comparison with ordinary cloud storage (who can read the file, what a breach exposes, how sharing works, whether a change is noticed, whether access is visible) on the home page and `/how-it-works`, plus a four-step quick start that explains the two secrets (the password signs you in; the passphrase opens your files and cannot be reset by us). The home page's feature cards also claimed "secure links with expiration times" and "custom passwords" as keys; neither exists, so they now describe what the product does. Signed out, the header's first link is How it works instead of "Product", which only redirected to sign in.
- 首页和 `/how-it-works` 加入与普通网盘的对比（谁能读文件、泄露会暴露什么、怎么分享、文件被改能否发现、访问记录是否可见），以及一个四步快速上手，讲清两个秘密的区别（密码用于登录；口令用于打开文件，我们无法重置）。首页功能卡片原先写着“带过期时间的安全链接”和用“自定义密码”作密钥，这两样都不存在，现已改为产品实际的做法。未登录时页眉第一个链接改为 How it works，取代只会跳转到登录页的 “Product”。

### Admin console: one tidy row of sections, a header link, filters that hide deleted records / 管理员控制台：分区整齐排成一行、页眉入口、默认隐藏已删除记录

- The eight admin tabs wrapped onto two lines with "Recovery key" alone on the second. They are now grouped as Monitor (Overview, Security agent, Integrity, Audit chain), Manage (Users, Files) and Recovery (Requests, Recovery key) in one row that fits a laptop and scrolls sideways, with an edge fade, on narrow screens instead of wrapping. The chosen section is kept in the URL (`/admin?tab=agent`), so refresh, the back button and shared links land on it, and each "needs attention" line on the Overview links to the section that resolves it.
- 管理员的八个标签页会折成两行，“Recovery key” 单独落在第二行。现在分成 Monitor（概览、安全 Agent、完整性、审计链）、Manage（用户、文件）、Recovery（请求、恢复密钥）三组排成一行，笔记本屏幕放得下；窄屏上改为横向滚动并在边缘淡出提示，不再折行。当前分区记在 URL 里（`/admin?tab=agent`），刷新、后退和分享链接都会回到同一分区；概览上每条“需要处理”的提示都能直接跳到处理它的分区。
- Administrators could sign in and see nothing different: the console was only reachable through an avatar-menu item called "Operations" that every user has. Administrators now get an "Admin" link in the header (recovery officers get "Recovery"), the page and menu are named for the role (Admin console, Recovery review, Recovery requests), and the header switches to the menu button below 1024px, where signed in its seven links slid under the Dashboard button and signed out "Contact us" ran into the theme menu at 768px.
- 管理员登录后看起来和普通用户没有区别：控制台只能从头像菜单里一个所有人都有的 “Operations” 进入。现在管理员在页眉有 “Admin” 入口（恢复专员是 “Recovery”），页面和菜单按角色命名（Admin console、Recovery review、Recovery requests）；页眉在 1024px 以下改用菜单按钮：以前登录后七个链接会滑到 Dashboard 按钮下面，未登录时 768px 下 “Contact us” 会撞上主题菜单。
- The Users and Files lists showed every deleted account and file (30 of 42 accounts and over 5,000 files on the local test database), and offered "Enable" on deleted accounts, which the server then refused. Both lists now default to current records with Active / Disabled (or Healthy / Corrupted) / Deleted / All filters, backed by a new optional `status` query parameter on `GET /admin/users` and `/admin/files`; they also gain a search over the loaded rows and coloured status badges. Disabling an account now asks for confirmation, and neither your own account nor a deleted one offers an action.
- 用户和文件列表会把所有已删除的账户和文件都列出来（本地测试库 42 个账户里有 30 个、文件超过 5,000 个），还对已删除账户显示服务器会拒绝的 “Enable” 按钮。现在两个列表默认只显示当前记录，并可按 Active / Disabled（或 Healthy / Corrupted）/ Deleted / All 筛选，由 `GET /admin/users` 和 `/admin/files` 新增的可选 `status` 查询参数支持；同时加入对已加载记录的搜索和彩色状态标签。禁用账户前会先确认，自己的账户和已删除账户不再显示操作按钮。


### Load and capacity testing tool, and two deadlocks it found (#140, #139) / 压测与容量测试工具，以及它发现的两个死锁（#140、#139）

- New `loadtest/` tool for the supervisor's capacity questions (meeting items 4–6). It signs in as many test accounts and encrypts every upload exactly as the browser does (pinned to the browser library's known-answer vectors), decrypts and SHA-256-verifies every download, and times each step. Scenarios: stepped concurrent users, every file type from 1 MB to 500 MB, parallel tasks per user, mixed and sustained load, overload and recovery, and simultaneous sign-ins. `server_sampler.py` records the server's CPU, memory, disk, network and connections, and the report lines them up with each step. `backend/scripts/seed_load_users.py` creates the accounts through the normal registration path; `cleanup` deletes them and their files through the API. The staging run has not happened yet: the server is busy with another workload and numbers taken alongside it would be wrong (#140).
- 新增 `loadtest/` 工具，用来回答导师的容量问题（会议行动项 4–6）。它用多个测试账号登录，每次上传都和浏览器完全一样地加密（与浏览器加密库的已知答案向量对齐），每次下载都解密并做 SHA-256 比对，并记录每一步的耗时。场景包括：逐级增加并发用户、1 MB 到 500 MB 的各种文件类型、单用户并行任务、混合与持续负载、超载与恢复，以及同时登录。`server_sampler.py` 记录服务器的 CPU、内存、磁盘、网络和连接数，报告会按每一档对齐。`backend/scripts/seed_load_users.py` 通过正常注册流程创建测试账号；`cleanup` 通过 API 删除账号及其文件。staging 正式测试还没有进行：服务器正在跑另一项任务，和它同时测出的数字是错的（#140）。
- The first local runs found two concurrency bugs, both MySQL deadlocks (1213) that reach the user as HTTP 500: two simultaneous sign-ins of accounts with no active session deadlock on the gap lock taken by the session-limit `SELECT … FOR UPDATE`, and a download and a share can deadlock between the audit chain head and a `users` row (0.3% of operations in a 6-user mixed load). They are backend changes, so they are written up with InnoDB's own deadlock report in #139 rather than changed here. The tool also confirmed that the server verifies only two passwords at a time and answers a third simultaneous sign-in with 429 instead of queueing it.
- 最初几次本地运行就发现了两个并发 bug，都是 MySQL 死锁（1213），用户看到的是 HTTP 500：两个没有活动会话的账户同时登录时，会在会话上限检查的 `SELECT … FOR UPDATE` 所加的间隙锁上死锁；下载和分享可能在审计链头与 `users` 行之间死锁（6 用户混合负载下占操作的 0.3%）。这些属于后端改动，所以连同 InnoDB 自己的死锁报告写进了 #139，没有在这里改。工具还证实服务器同一时间只验证两个密码，第三个同时登录直接返回 429 而不是排队。


### Admin overview: failed checks, events and failures were capped at 1 / 管理员概览：失败检查、事件与失败数被限制为 1

- The Overview tab's "Failed, 24 hours" (integrity), "Events, 24 hours" and "Failed, 24 hours" (audit) showed at most 1 however many there were. They summed a comparison (`SUM(result <> 'VALID')`), which SQLAlchemy types as Boolean, so the driver's answer went through `bool()` and any total above zero came back as 1. On the local test database with 7 failed checks, 45 events and 8 failures in the window, it reported 1, 1 and 1. They now sum a `CASE`, which is an integer. The tests that should have caught it started from an empty database, where 0 and 1 are both right; a new MySQL test compares every figure with a plain `COUNT(*)` and a unit test pins the `CASE`.
