- 概览页的 “Failed, 24 hours”（完整性）、“Events, 24 hours” 与 “Failed, 24 hours”（审计）无论实际多少都最多显示 1。它们对一个比较表达式求和（`SUM(result <> 'VALID')`），SQLAlchemy 把它当作 Boolean，驱动返回的结果会经过 `bool()`，任何大于 0 的总数都变成 1。在本地测试库里窗口内实际有 7 次失败检查、45 个事件、8 次失败时，它报告的是 1、1、1。现在改为对 `CASE` 求和，得到整数。本应抓住它的测试都从空数据库开始，此时 0 和 1 都正确；新的 MySQL 测试把每个数字与普通 `COUNT(*)` 比较，单元测试固定使用 `CASE`。

### Security agent v2: daily alerts, guessing that worked, throttle blocks (#137) / 安全 Agent v2：每日告警、猜测得手、限流封禁（#137）

- The agent now also runs every morning at 06:00 (after the 05:30 integrity sweep) through a new `mcs21-security-agent` timer, and mails the team through the existing failure alert when anything in the last 24 hours is Critical or High. Before, it only analysed when an administrator happened to open the tab, so a takeover or a tampered file could wait days for someone to look. The check prints counts and rule ids only, never names, addresses or file names, and the mail carries no journal content, just a pointer to Admin → Security agent; an unresolved Critical finding mails daily until it is fixed, by design.
- Agent 现在还会通过新的 `mcs21-security-agent` 定时器每天早上 06:00（05:30 完整性扫描之后）运行一次，最近 24 小时内有 Critical 或 High 发现时，经现有的失败告警邮件通知团队。此前它只在管理员碰巧打开标签页时才分析，账户被接管或文件被篡改可能要等好几天才有人看到。检查只输出数量和规则 id，从不输出用户名、地址或文件名；邮件不含任何 journal 内容，只提示去 Admin → Security agent 查看。未解决的 Critical 发现会每天发邮件直到修好，这是有意的设计。
- New rule `SIGN_IN_AFTER_GUESSING`: a successful sign-in preceded within 15 minutes by 5 or more failures on the same account or from the same address is High, and Critical for an administrator. Guessing that succeeded is a likely account takeover, the one outcome v1 could not tell apart from guessing that failed. Counting by address as well catches credential stuffing; a second sign-in on the same failures joins the first finding instead of adding another.
- 新规则 `SIGN_IN_AFTER_GUESSING`：一次成功登录之前 15 分钟内，针对同一账户或来自同一地址有 5 次以上失败，即为 High，管理员账户为 Critical。猜中的猜测很可能就是账户被接管，而 v1 分不清猜中和没猜中。同时按地址统计能抓到撞库；基于同一批失败的第二次登录会并入第一个发现，不会再多报一个。
- New rule `SIGN_IN_THROTTLE_ACTIVE` reports how many addresses and accounts the sign-in throttle is blocking right now (any address = Medium, accounts only = Low), read from `auth_login_throttles` as counts only. Attempts the throttle refuses with 429 are deliberately not audited, so the agent could not see an attacker who kept going after being blocked; auditing every 429 instead would let an attacker flood the hash-chained log. It is a separate rule, not evidence on the guessing findings, because the throttle keys are hashed and cannot be tied to any one finding.
- 新规则 `SIGN_IN_THROTTLE_ACTIVE` 报告登录限流此刻封禁了多少地址和账户（有地址 = Medium，只有账户 = Low），从 `auth_login_throttles` 只读取计数。被限流以 429 拒绝的尝试有意不写审计日志，所以 Agent 看不到被封后仍在继续的攻击者；而改成每个 429 都写审计，攻击者就能灌满哈希链日志。它单独成为一条规则而不是猜测类发现的附加证据，因为限流的键是哈希值，无法对应到任何一个发现。
- `mcs21-deploy`'s closed list of ops units is now exactly `(mcs21-integrity-sweep mcs21-security-agent)`, and the alert mail adds one fixed sentence for the agent's unit. Both scripts are installed on the server by hand, so they need reinstalling once after this merges (`docs/AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`); the agent's analysis gained a system entry point for the script, while the HTTP route keeps its administrator check, and a test fails if any route reaches the system path.
- `mcs21-deploy` 固定的运维 unit 名单现在恰好是 `(mcs21-integrity-sweep mcs21-security-agent)`，告警邮件为 Agent 的 unit 多加一句固定提示。这两个脚本都是在服务器上手动安装的，合并后需要重新安装一次（见 `docs/AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`）。Agent 的分析为脚本新增了一个系统入口，HTTP 路由的管理员检查不变，任何路由调用到系统入口都会让测试失败。

### Security agent v1: three fixes from a review / 安全 Agent v1：评审后的三处修正

- One address guessing one account was reported twice, as a source finding and an account finding, so a single incident counted as two Medium findings. It is now reported once by the source rule, which names the accounts it tried and how many attempts went to names with no account; the account rule now fires only for guessing spread across two or more addresses, which is the case the per-address view cannot see.
- 同一地址猜同一账户此前会被报告两次（来源发现和账户发现），一起事件算成两个 Medium。现在只由来源规则报告一次，并列出它尝试过的账户，以及有多少次尝试针对不存在的用户名；账户规则只在猜测分散于两个或以上地址时触发，这正是按地址看无法发现的情况。
- Events were read with one shared cap of 20,000, newest first, so a flood of one kind (a bulk download) could push every other kind out of the read and the report would still look complete. The cap is now 5,000 per event type, and the report says which types hit it.
- 此前所有事件共用一个 20,000 条的读取上限（最新优先），一种事件的洪流（如批量下载）可能把其他所有事件挤出读取范围，报告看起来却仍然完整。现在每种事件各有 5,000 条上限，报告会写明哪些类型触顶。
- The newest-check join for damaged files now aliases its inner table and states `file_id = files.id`, which removes a SQLAlchemy cartesian-product warning (the SQL result is unchanged). The live-stack suite in CI now drives the agent end to end: ten real failed sign-ins against the running API become one ranked finding with evidence and an action, and an ordinary user is refused.
- 受损文件的“最新检查”连接现在为内层表起了别名并写明 `file_id = files.id`，消除了 SQLAlchemy 的笛卡尔积警告（SQL 结果不变）。CI 的 live-stack 套件现在端到端驱动 Agent：对运行中的 API 做十次真实的登录失败，得到一条带证据和建议动作的排序发现；普通用户被拒绝。

### The daily integrity scan installs itself / 每日完整性扫描自动安装

- After each healthy release `mcs21-deploy` now installs and enables `mcs21-integrity-sweep.timer` from the release, so nobody has to remember to. The list of units it may install is closed (this one only), because a file in `/etc/systemd/system` runs as the account it names; before installing it checks that the service runs as `fypapp`, starts only `scripts/<name>.py` from the release's virtualenv, has no `+`/`!` privileged hooks, and that the timer starts that same service. A failure warns and is audited but never fails a deployment that is already healthy. `test_ops_units.py` runs the script's own check against the shipped unit and against variants that must be refused. It takes effect once the updated `mcs21-deploy` is installed on the VPS (one `install` command, in `docs/AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`); the other ops units, some of which run as root or delete data, stay manual installs.
- 每次健康的发布之后，`mcs21-deploy` 现在会从该发布中安装并启用 `mcs21-integrity-sweep.timer`，不再需要有人记得去装。它能安装的 unit 名单是固定的（只有这一个），因为放进 `/etc/systemd/system` 的文件会以它指定的账户运行；安装前会检查服务以 `fypapp` 运行、只启动发布虚拟环境里的 `scripts/<名称>.py`、没有 `+`/`!` 特权钩子、timer 启动的是同一服务。失败只会警告并记审计，不会让已经健康的部署失败。`test_ops_units.py` 用脚本自己的检查去验证随附的 unit 以及必须被拒绝的变体。VPS 上装上更新后的 `mcs21-deploy` 后生效（一条 `install` 命令，见 `docs/AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md`）；其余运维 unit 有的以 root 运行、有的会删除数据，仍是手动安装。

### Security agent v1 / 安全 Agent v1

- New **Security agent** tab for administrators (`GET /api/v1/admin/agent/report`). It reads the audit log, the audit hash chain, integrity-check history and recovery requests and returns ranked Critical / High / Medium / Low findings, each with the evidence that raised it and a recommended action. Ten rules cover damaged stored files, a broken audit chain, a stopped integrity scan, password guessing (by source and by account), reused session tokens, bulk downloading, bursts of sharing, failed password changes and recovery activity. Bursts use a sliding window, so 40 failures spread over a day are not one. For a damaged file the action names the dry-run restore command and which backup sets can hold the original, worked out from the file's own check history.
- 管理员新增 **Security agent** 标签（`GET /api/v1/admin/agent/report`）。它读取审计日志、审计哈希链、完整性检查历史和恢复请求，返回按 Critical / High / Medium / Low 排序的发现，每条都带触发它的证据和建议动作。十条规则覆盖：受损的存储文件、审计链断裂、完整性扫描停止、猜密码（按来源和按账户）、会话令牌被重复使用、批量下载、分享突增、改密失败和恢复活动。突发用滑动窗口判断，所以分散在一天里的 40 次失败不算一次突发。对受损文件，建议动作会给出演练模式的恢复命令，并根据该文件自己的检查历史推算哪些备份集可能持有原件。
- Deliberately rule-based, read-only and metadata-only: no language model is asked to judge anything, nothing leaves the server, it cannot change a file or run a recovery, and a test fails if its modules gain a database write path. Every report says how much it read, and a clean report says it is not proof that nothing happened. Inputs, rules, output format and boundaries are in `docs/SECURITY_AGENT.md`.
- 有意设计为基于规则、只读、只用元数据：没有语言模型被用来判断任何事，没有任何东西离开服务器，它不能改动文件或执行恢复，且一旦它的模块出现数据库写入路径就会有测试失败。每份报告都说明读了多少数据，结果为空时也说明这不等于什么都没发生。输入、规则、输出格式与边界见 `docs/SECURITY_AGENT.md`。

### Integrity and recovery: find a modified file, then put the original back / 完整性与恢复：发现被修改的文件，并把原件找回来

- Until now a modified stored file was only noticed when someone downloaded it or clicked the integrity check, and there was no way to recover it short of restoring the whole service. Now a daily sweep (`scripts/integrity_sweep.py`, `mcs21-integrity-sweep.timer`) hashes every stored object, records the findings and exits non-zero so the failure alert fires; Admin gets an **Integrity** tab listing damaged files, how much of the store has been checked and a "Scan now" button.
- 此前被修改的存储文件只有在有人下载或点击完整性检查时才会被发现，而且除了恢复整个服务之外没有办法找回原件。现在每日扫描（`scripts/integrity_sweep.py`、`mcs21-integrity-sweep.timer`）会对每个存储对象做哈希、记录发现并以非零退出码触发失败告警；管理员页面新增 **Integrity** 标签，列出受损文件、已检查的覆盖情况，并提供 “Scan now” 按钮。
- `scripts/restore_file.py` puts one object back from an encrypted backup set. Objects are write-once, so a backup copy whose SHA-256 equals the recorded digest *is* the original; there is no version history to maintain. It refuses a set that does not authenticate, does not hold the object, or holds different bytes; keeps the damaged copy in quarantine; replaces the object atomically; re-verifies; and writes `FILE_RESTORED_FROM_BACKUP` to the audit chain. It needs the offline backup key, so there is deliberately no web button for it.
- `scripts/restore_file.py` 从加密备份集中把一个对象放回去。对象只写一次，所以 SHA-256 等于记录摘要的备份副本*就是*原件，无需维护版本历史。它会拒绝无法认证、不含该对象或字节不同的备份集；把受损副本保存到隔离目录；原子替换；重新验证；并把 `FILE_RESTORED_FROM_BACKUP` 写入审计链。它需要离线备份密钥，因此有意不提供网页按钮。
- The upload's audit row now records the ciphertext SHA-256, and every check and download compares against it. Before, rewriting an object *and* `files.encrypted_sha256` together left them agreeing with each other, so nothing flagged it; now the audit chain disagrees. Files uploaded earlier have no audit digest and still rely on the column.
- 上传的审计行现在记录密文 SHA-256，每次检查和下载都会与它比对。此前把对象*和* `files.encrypted_sha256` 一起改写会让两者互相一致，没有任何东西报警；现在审计链会不一致。更早上传的文件没有审计摘要，仍依赖该列。
- The CI backup drill now tampers one object, requires the sweep to exit 3 and mark it corrupted, refuses the wrong key, and then requires a byte-identical restore with the damaged copy quarantined, the file `ACTIVE` again and the audit row written. Limits (a file newer than the last nightly backup cannot be restored; the timer is not installed until enabled deliberately) are in `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` §9.
- CI 的备份演练现在会篡改一个对象，要求扫描退出码为 3 并标记为受损，拒绝错误的密钥，然后要求逐字节一致的恢复，且受损副本被隔离、文件重新为 `ACTIVE`、审计行已写入。限制（比最近一次夜间备份更新的文件无法恢复；定时器需有意启用才会安装）见 `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` 第 9 节。

### Contact us: uniform team tiles, the supervisor's full introduction, a plainer heading / 联系我们：统一大小的团队瓦片、导师完整介绍、更自然的标题

- The supervisor tile is back to carrying everything the earlier version had, in the same sticker-tile style: portrait, role, name, title (Lecturer and Cybersecurity Group Leader), affiliation (School of Information Technology, Monash University Malaysia), what he guides the project on, research interests and the Monash profile link. The previous tile showed only a one-line intro, so his position and affiliation had dropped out of sight.
- 导师瓦片恢复为之前版本的完整内容，沿用同样的贴纸瓦片风格：照片、角色、姓名、职位（Lecturer and Cybersecurity Group Leader）、单位（School of Information Technology, Monash University Malaysia）、指导内容、研究方向与 Monash 个人主页链接。上一版瓦片只剩一句简介，职位和单位反而看不到了。
- The four member tiles are now the same size. The mosaic gave them different column and row spans, so they came out in different heights and widths; the supervisor is one wide tile above them because he has more to say, and the resting tilt is gone so the rows line up. Links sit at the bottom of every tile so they line up whatever the length of the intro.
- 四位成员的瓦片现在大小一致。之前的拼贴给它们设了不同的列宽、行高，所以高矮宽窄不一；导师因为内容更多，单独占上方一整行，并去掉了静止时的倾斜，让每一行对齐。链接固定在每个瓦片底部，不论简介长短都对齐。
- The details no longer hide behind a two-second hover. Each tile now shows the full description on its face, and the large profile opens on a click, tap or Enter, with a visible "Click or tap to enlarge" line on every tile. The two-second hover is gone: nothing told a visitor it existed, and it also opened a pop-up by accident whenever the pointer rested on a tile.
- 详细介绍不再藏在“悬停两秒”之后。每个瓦片现在直接显示完整描述，大图资料通过点击、轻触或回车打开，并在每个瓦片上有一行常驻的 “Click or tap to enlarge” 提示。悬停两秒已去掉：没有任何提示告诉访客它的存在，而且鼠标只是停在瓦片上就会误弹窗。
- The enlarged profile is now actually larger than the tile. It had the same portrait size and the same text as the tile, so opening it showed nothing new. It now has a large portrait that runs the full height of the card, with the role badge, name, title, affiliation, description, research interests and the Monash profile button beside it (the layout of the earlier supervisor card). Team members get the same layout with their initials in place of a photo, plus their GitHub and email as buttons.
- 放大后的资料现在真的比瓦片大了。之前它的头像尺寸和文字都与瓦片相同，点开后看不到任何新内容。现在左侧是贯通整张卡片高度的大头像，右侧依次是角色标签、姓名、职位、单位、介绍、研究方向和 Monash 主页按钮（即之前导师卡片的版式）。团队成员使用同样的版式，以首字母代替照片，并以按钮形式给出 GitHub 和邮箱。
- "Meet the people" is now "The people behind SecureView", and the line under it no longer says "decentralized", which this platform is not (it has a server that stores ciphertext).
- “Meet the people” 改为 “The people behind SecureView”，其下说明也不再写 “decentralized（去中心化）”——本平台有保存密文的服务器，并非去中心化。

### Upload page wording: two things that read wrong, and one left alone on purpose / 上传页措辞：两处读起来不对，另有一处有意不改

- The page said "Files are encrypted in your browser with AES-256-GCM" while offering four algorithms in the dropdown beside it. It now says "with the algorithm you choose below (AES-256-GCM by default)".
- 页面写着 “Files are encrypted in your browser with AES-256-GCM”，旁边的下拉框却提供四种算法。现在改为 “with the algorithm you choose below (AES-256-GCM by default)”。
- "File key wrapping: RSA-OAEP-SHA256" made a reader ask why a key-wrapping step involves SHA-256. It is one name, not two steps: RSA public-key encryption with OAEP padding, which uses SHA-256 inside the padding; it is not a checksum of the file (the SHA-256 that detects tampering is a different one, computed over the stored ciphertext). The row now reads "RSA-OAEP (SHA-256)" with a hover explanation, and the note beneath says the same in a sentence. Display text only: the algorithm identifier the API and the envelope carry is still `RSA-OAEP-SHA256`.
- “File key wrapping: RSA-OAEP-SHA256” 会让人疑惑：密钥包装为什么会牵涉 SHA-256。它是一个整体的名字，而不是两个步骤：带 OAEP 填充的 RSA 公钥加密，SHA-256 是填充内部使用的哈希，并不是文件的校验和（用来检测篡改的 SHA-256 是另一个，计算对象是存储的密文）。这一行现在写作 “RSA-OAEP (SHA-256)”并带悬停说明，下面的注释也用一句话说明了同样的事。只改显示文字：API 和信封里携带的算法标识仍是 `RSA-OAEP-SHA256`。
- "Maximum size 500 MB" stays "500 MB" on purpose, and the backend limit is unchanged. The limit is 524,288,000 bytes, so a file of 500 MB under either convention fits: 500,000,000 bytes (macOS and most websites) and 524,288,000 bytes (Windows Explorer). Calling it "500 MiB" would be exact but is a term most people do not know, and lowering the limit to a round 500,000,000 bytes would reject a file Windows calls 500 MB. A comment beside the constant records this so nobody tidies it later.
- “Maximum size 500 MB” 有意保持写作 “500 MB”，后端上限不变。上限是 524,288,000 字节，所以按任何一种习惯算的 500 MB 文件都能放得下：500,000,000 字节（macOS 和大多数网站）与 524,288,000 字节（Windows 资源管理器）。写成 “500 MiB” 虽然精确，但大多数人不认识这个词；把上限降到整齐的 500,000,000 字节，则会拒绝 Windows 所说的 500 MB 文件。常量旁边的注释记录了这一点，免得以后有人把它“整理”掉。

### Contact us: Junyan's sticker-tile design restored, with the new content / Contact us：恢复 Junyan 的贴纸瓦片设计，并保留新内容

- Put back the tilted sticker tiles, the hold-to-enlarge pop-up and the tile layout that Junyan Li designed for the team section. The previous change (#125) replaced them with a plain card grid because I read "fill in the details from the presentation" as "make it look like the presentation". The request was to keep his design and correct the information on it; the layout and styling are now his original code again.
- 恢复了 Junyan Li 为团队部分设计的倾斜贴纸瓦片、长按放大弹窗和瓦片布局。上一次改动（#125）把它们换成了朴素的卡片网格，因为我把“按演示文稿填写资料”理解成了“做成演示文稿的样子”。真正的要求是保留他的设计、只更正上面的信息；现在布局和样式又是他原来的代码。
- What stays from #125: the tidier top of the page (the feedback pitch beside the form, instead of a narrow card with the right half empty). What the tiles now carry: names written given name first as the presentation does (Shuoxun Wen, Tai Chen, Junyan Li, Jason Huang; the old page mixed "Wen Shuoxun" with "Jason Huang"), the presentation's roles and one-line responsibilities, each member's GitHub profile and Monash student email (emails break after the `@` so a narrow tile does not cut the domain in half), and a supervisor tile for Dr. Asad Malik with his portrait in the sticker, title, research interests and a link to his Monash research profile.
- 保留 #125 的部分：页面顶部更整齐的布局（反馈说明在表单旁边，而不是右半边空着的窄卡片）。瓦片现在承载的内容：按演示文稿的写法名在前（Shuoxun Wen、Tai Chen、Junyan Li、Jason Huang；旧页面把“Wen Shuoxun”和“Jason Huang”混着写）、演示文稿里的角色与一句话职责、每位成员的 GitHub 主页和 Monash 学生邮箱（邮箱在 `@` 之后换行，窄瓦片不会把域名从中间切开），以及 Dr. Asad Malik 的导师瓦片：贴纸里是他的照片，并带有职位、研究方向和指向其 Monash 研究主页的链接。

### Phone and narrow-screen usability (Issue #76), measured and fixed / 手机与窄屏可用性（Issue #76）：先测量，再修复

- Audited 23 screens (public pages, sign-in and registration, upload, My Files and its five dialogs, access records, profile, settings, the administrator pages) at 360 and 320 px with an automated browser check that measures sideways overflow, controls past the screen edge, tap targets under 32 px and text under 11.5 px, rather than judging by eye. Findings: the Education page scrolled sideways at 562 px wide because one wide demo stretched its grid column; at 320 px the registration and forgot-password "Send code" button left the screen and the upload card was wider than the screen when a long file name was chosen; the footer's GitHub, X and Discord links were 9–50 px wide and 20 px tall on every page; and My Files, a seven-column table, either squeezed the file name to a few characters per line or pushed its action buttons off the right edge.
- 用自动化浏览器检查对 23 个界面（公开页面、登录与注册、上传、My Files 及其五个弹框、访问记录、个人资料、设置、管理员页面）在 360 与 320 px 下做了审计，测量横向溢出、超出屏幕边缘的控件、小于 32 px 的点击目标和小于 11.5 px 的文字，而不是凭肉眼判断。发现：教育页在 562 px 宽处横向滚动，因为一个很宽的演示撑开了网格列；在 320 px 下，注册与忘记密码页的 “Send code” 按钮跑出屏幕，选了长文件名后上传卡片比屏幕还宽；页脚的 GitHub、X、Discord 链接在每个页面上宽 9–50 px、高 20 px；My Files 这张七列表格要么把文件名挤成每行几个字，要么把操作按钮推出右边缘。
- Fixes. Below 768 px My Files is a **card list**: the name (wrapping), sizes and the encrypted-preview toggle, then the folder dropdown and status, then the six actions in a row of 40 px buttons; from 768 px up it is the table it was. The Education grid column can now shrink and its demo scrolls inside itself. The code field and button on registration and forgot-password fit 320 px, a long upload name wraps, and footer, sign-in, registration, "See all", "Forgotten your passphrase?" and the two acknowledgement checkboxes are 40 px or larger to tap. Landing-page guide labels went from 10–11 px to 12 px. The encrypted-preview panel's header wraps instead of squeezing its text into a sliver.
- 修复。768 px 以下 My Files 是**卡片列表**：名称（自动换行）、大小和加密预览开关，然后是文件夹下拉框和状态，再是一排 40 px 的六个操作按钮；768 px 及以上仍是原来的表格。教育页的网格列现在可以收缩，演示内容在自身内部滚动。注册与忘记密码页的验证码输入框和按钮能放进 320 px，上传时长文件名会换行，页脚、登录、注册、“See all”、“Forgotten your passphrase?”以及两个确认勾选框的点击目标都不小于 40 px。落地页引导的标签从 10–11 px 提到 12 px。加密预览面板的标题栏改为换行，而不是把文字挤成一条窄缝。
- Two behaviours of mobile Safari were fixed ahead of time rather than observed: on touch screens a form field is never smaller than 16 px (iOS zooms the page when a smaller one takes focus), and dialog heights use `dvh` so the address bar does not push a dialog off the bottom. **No test has been run on a real Android phone or in iOS Safari**, so Issue #76's device criterion stays open; `docs/E2E_TESTING.md` says so.
- 有两个移动端 Safari 的行为是提前修复的，而不是观察到的：在触屏上表单字段不小于 16 px（字段更小时 iOS 会放大页面），弹框高度使用 `dvh`，避免地址栏把弹框挤出底部。**没有在真实 Android 手机或 iOS Safari 上运行过任何测试**，所以 Issue #76 的设备验证一条仍未完成；`docs/E2E_TESTING.md` 已如实说明。
- Repeatable evidence: three Chromium specs (`mobile-public-pages`, `mobile-file-flow`, `team-page`) with shared checks in `browser-tests/viewport.ts`. The public-pages spec fails with six errors against the code as it was before these fixes and passes after. `mobile-file-flow` runs the primary file flow at 360 px against the live stack (register, upload a long-named file, tap every action, open the preview and the recovery, history, delete, unlock and share dialogs, download the original bytes back); it runs in `e2e-live` and was not run locally.
- 可重复的证据：三个 Chromium 测试（`mobile-public-pages`、`mobile-file-flow`、`team-page`），公共检查放在 `browser-tests/viewport.ts`。公开页面测试在这些修复之前的代码上会以六个错误失败，修复后通过。`mobile-file-flow` 在 360 px 下对真实后端运行主要文件流程（注册、上传长文件名的文件、点按每个操作、打开预览以及恢复、历史、删除、解锁和分享弹框、把原始字节下载回来）；它在 `e2e-live` 中运行，我没有在本地运行。

### Access Records: say that a file can be opened for its history / 访问记录：提示文件可以点开查看历史

- The Access Records page showed each file as a card but gave no sign that the card opens a full history, so the detail most people came for (who uploaded, shared or downloaded a file, and when) stayed hidden behind a click nobody knew to make. A line above the list now says "Select a file below to open its full access history: who uploaded, shared or downloaded it, and when", and each card ends in a **View history** / **Hide history** control with a chevron (the chevron alone on a narrow screen), exposed to assistive technology through `aria-expanded`. The card was already a button; it now looks like one.
- 访问记录页把每个文件显示为一张卡片，却没有任何迹象表明卡片可以点开查看完整历史，所以大多数人最想看的内容（谁上传、分享或下载了文件，以及时间）一直藏在一个没人知道要点的地方。列表上方现在有一行提示：“Select a file below to open its full access history: who uploaded, shared or downloaded it, and when”，每张卡片末尾有 **View history** / **Hide history** 控件和箭头（窄屏上只显示箭头），并通过 `aria-expanded` 暴露给辅助技术。这张卡片本来就是一个按钮，现在它看起来也像按钮了。

### Contact us: a tidier page, the supervisor's profile, and the team as the group presents it / Contact us：更整齐的页面、导师简介，以及按小组展示口径的团队

- Rebuilt the Contact us page. The feedback form used to sit in a narrow card on the left of a wide screen with the right half empty, and the team was a set of tilted tiles that only opened a profile after hovering for two seconds. It is now three plain sections: feedback (the pitch and topic hints on the left, the form on the right, stacked on a phone), the supervisor, and four equal team cards. The tilted mosaic, the two-second hover and its pop-up are gone, along with about 300 lines of CSS that only they used. Checked in Chromium at desktop, phone and dark theme: no sideways scrolling, and the photo loads.
- 重做了 Contact us 页面。以前反馈表单是宽屏左侧的一张窄卡片，右半边一片空白；团队是一组倾斜的瓦片，要悬停两秒才会打开简介。现在是三个朴素的区块：反馈（左边是说明和主题提示，右边是表单，手机上上下堆叠）、导师，以及四张等大的团队卡片。倾斜的拼贴、两秒悬停及其弹窗都已移除，连同只有它们使用的约 300 行 CSS。已在 Chromium 的桌面、手机和深色主题下检查：没有横向滚动，头像正常加载。
- Added **Dr. Asad Malik**, the project supervisor, with his title (Lecturer and Cybersecurity Group Leader, School of Information Technology, Monash University Malaysia), his research interests (cybersecurity, multimedia forensics, DeepFake detection, trustworthy AI), a link to his Monash research profile, and his portrait from that profile. Everything on his card is what that profile states (the first version of this entry, and of the card, wrongly wrote "Malaysia School of Information Technology"; the profile says "School of Information Technology, Monash University Malaysia", and the card now does too), and the page says nothing more about him than it does. The photo is bundled with the site rather than linked, because the site's content security policy only allows images from its own origin, so a linked photo would load nowhere. Worth confirming with Dr. Malik that he is happy for his profile photo to be used.
- 新增项目导师 **Dr. Asad Malik**：职位（Lecturer and Cybersecurity Group Leader，School of Information Technology，Monash University Malaysia）、研究方向（网络安全、多媒体取证、DeepFake 检测、可信 AI）、指向其 Monash 研究主页的链接，以及取自该主页的头像。他的卡片上的一切都是该主页所写（这条记录和卡片的第一版曾把学院误写成 “Malaysia School of Information Technology”；主页上写的是 “School of Information Technology, Monash University Malaysia”，卡片现在也是这样），页面没有多说一个字。头像随站点一起打包而不是外链，因为站点的内容安全策略只允许来自本站的图片，外链的头像在任何地方都加载不出来。建议向 Dr. Malik 确认他同意使用自己的主页头像。
- The four members now use the roles and one-line responsibilities from the group's MCS21 presentation (Project Lead; Backend and APIs; Frontend and Interface; Cryptography), with the names in the order given name then family name, as the presentation writes them. Each card also links the member's GitHub profile and Monash student email (a `mailto:` link), as supplied by the project owner; student numbers from the presentation are deliberately not published, and the project owner should know the four addresses are now public on the site. A new Chromium test (`team-page.browser.ts`, no account needed) checks the supervisor card and link, that the photo loads, the four members and their roles, and that a 360 px screen does not scroll sideways.
- 四位成员现在使用小组 MCS21 演示文稿里的角色和一句话职责（Project Lead；Backend and APIs；Frontend and Interface；Cryptography），姓名按演示文稿的写法为名在前、姓在后。每张卡片还链接了该成员的 GitHub 主页和 Monash 学生邮箱（`mailto:` 链接），均由项目负责人提供；演示文稿里的学号有意不公开，并请项目负责人知悉这四个邮箱现在在站点上是公开的。新增的 Chromium 测试（`team-page.browser.ts`，无需账号）检查导师卡片与链接、头像是否加载、四位成员及其角色，以及 360 px 的屏幕不会横向滚动。

### Dialogs that fit long file names, and "No folder" instead of "Unfiled" / 弹框适配长文件名，以及用 “No folder” 取代 “Unfiled”

- A file with a long name and no spaces ran straight out of the Request file recovery dialog, because a browser has nowhere to break an unbroken string. Every dialog that prints a file name (recovery request, share, access history, confirm-delete and confirm-revoke) now wraps it anywhere, and each dialog panel is capped at 90 % of the viewport height and scrolls inside itself, so tall content cannot run off a small screen either. Checked in Chromium with a 112-character name at desktop and phone width: the name stays inside the dialog at both.
- 文件名很长又没有空格时，会直接冲出 Request file recovery 弹框，因为浏览器对一整串不间断的字符没有地方断行。现在所有显示文件名的弹框（恢复请求、分享、访问历史、确认删除、确认撤销）都允许在任意位置换行，并且每个弹框面板高度不超过视口的 90%，超出时在内部滚动，所以内容过高也不会跑出小屏幕。已在 Chromium 中用 112 个字符的文件名在桌面和手机宽度下检查：两种宽度下文件名都留在弹框内。
- "Unfiled" read as an odd, unfinished word for "in no folder", so the filter chip, the per-file dropdown and the messages now say **No folder** (the message is "<file> is no longer in a folder"; deleting a folder says its files are no longer in any folder). No data or API value changed: a null `folder_id` still means no folder.
- “Unfiled” 作为“不在任何文件夹中”的说法既生僻又像没写完，所以筛选标签、每个文件的下拉框和提示信息现在都写作 **No folder**（提示为 “<文件> is no longer in a folder”；删除文件夹时说明其中的文件不再位于任何文件夹）。没有任何数据或 API 取值改变：`folder_id` 为 null 仍表示没有文件夹。
- Fixed a side effect of the file-table layout change in #120: on a phone the file-name column was squeezed to a few characters per line. The table now has a minimum width, so a narrow screen scrolls sideways and the name column keeps about 200 px. A proper mobile layout is still Issue #76.
- 修复了 #120 里文件表格布局改动的一个副作用：在手机上文件名列被压成每行几个字符。表格现在有最小宽度，窄屏改为横向滚动，文件名列保持约 200 px。真正的手机端布局仍属 Issue #76。

### Fix: moving a file into a folder failed with "Input should be an instance of UUID" / 修复：把文件移入文件夹报 “Input should be an instance of UUID”

- Choosing a folder in the My Files folder dropdown answered 422 with `folder_id: Input should be an instance of UUID`, so a file could be created into "Unfiled" but never filed into any folder. `FileMoveIn` inherited strict validation, and FastAPI validates a request body from the already-parsed JSON, where a UUID can only be text; strict mode refuses text for a UUID. The field now opts out of strictness (`Field(strict=False)`) and is still validated as a UUID, `null` still means "unfiled", an unknown field or a missing `folder_id` is still refused. Nothing else in the model is loosened.
- 在 My Files 的文件夹下拉框里选择任何文件夹，都会返回 422 `folder_id: Input should be an instance of UUID`，所以文件只能留在“未分类”，无法放进任何文件夹。`FileMoveIn` 继承了严格校验，而 FastAPI 是对已经解析好的 JSON 做校验，JSON 里的 UUID 只能是文本，严格模式拒绝文本形式的 UUID。现在该字段单独关闭严格模式（`Field(strict=False)`），仍按 UUID 校验；`null` 仍表示“未分类”，未知字段或缺少 `folder_id` 仍会被拒绝，模型的其他部分没有放宽。
- Why it shipped: the folder feature (#103) was only exercised through unit tests that build the model from a `UUID` object or a raw JSON string, and neither takes the path an HTTP request takes. Two tests now do: an HTTP-level contract test (`test_file_move_contract.py`, which fails with exactly the user's error when the fix is reverted) and a step in the Chromium golden path that creates a folder, files a file into it and unfiles it again.
- 为什么会漏掉：文件夹功能（#103）只被单元测试覆盖，这些测试用 `UUID` 对象或原始 JSON 字符串构造模型，都不走 HTTP 请求的路径。现在有两个测试走这条路径：HTTP 层的契约测试（`test_file_move_contract.py`，撤销修复时会以与用户所见完全相同的错误失败），以及 Chromium 黄金路径里的一步：创建文件夹、把文件放进去、再移出来。

### Administrator overview with platform statistics / 带平台统计的管理员概览

- The Operations page now opens on a new **Overview** tab for administrators, answering "how many users and files does the platform have, and is it healthy?": accounts by status (total, active, disabled, deleted, administrators, new in 7 days, signed in within 24 hours), encrypted files and their health, total bytes stored, the algorithm mix, active shares, the last day's integrity checks and failures, recovery requests awaiting review, and audit totals with the five newest failed events. Anything that needs attention (a corrupted file, a failed integrity check, a pending recovery request, failed events) is listed first, in red when it means damaged data. A supervisor asked whether the administrator can see how many users and files the site has; the page had only enable/disable controls.
- 管理员打开 Operations 页面时，现在先看到新增的 **Overview** 标签页，回答“平台有多少用户和文件、是否健康”：按状态统计的账户（总数、活跃、禁用、已删除、管理员、7 天内新增、24 小时内登录）、加密文件及其健康度、已存储的总字节数、各算法占比、有效分享数、最近一天的完整性检查与失败数、待审核的恢复请求，以及审计总数和最新的五条失败事件。需要关注的事项（文件损坏、完整性检查失败、待处理恢复请求、失败事件）置顶列出，涉及数据损坏的用红色显示。导师问过管理员能否看到网站有多少用户和文件；此前该页面只有启用/禁用控件。
- `GET /admin/stats` returns **counts only**: no file name, e-mail, IP address, key material or event metadata is selectable by any of its queries, and the one list it carries (recent failures) is limited to action, time and actor name. Each count is a single aggregate query, so the dashboard cannot be used to read anything the Users, Files and Audit tabs do not already gate. A non-administrator is refused before any count is read (unit and API tests). Eleven backend tests, including compiled-SQL checks that deleted files and the owner's own grant are excluded, and a real-MySQL test that registers users, uploads, corrupts an object and disables an account, then asserts every number moved by exactly the right amount.
- `GET /admin/stats` 只返回**数字**：它的任何查询都不会选出文件名、邮箱、IP 地址、密钥材料或事件元数据，唯一包含的列表（最近的失败事件）也只限于动作、时间和行为者用户名。每个数字都是一次聚合查询，所以仪表盘不可能被用来读取用户、文件、审计标签页没有放行的内容。非管理员会在读取任何数字之前被拒绝（单元测试与 API 测试）。十一个后端测试，其中包括检查编译后 SQL 排除了已删除文件和所有者自己那条授权，以及一个真实 MySQL 测试：注册用户、上传、破坏一个对象、禁用一个账户，然后断言每个数字都恰好变化了应有的量。

### Encrypted preview, evidence that files are really encrypted, and a tidier file table / 密文预览、加密真实性证据，以及更整齐的文件表格

- Added **Encrypted preview** under every file in My Files, for files you own and files shared with you. It shows the algorithm, both sizes in exact bytes, the SHA-256 recorded at upload and a hex dump of the first 128 bytes of the ciphertext the server stores, and checks that those bytes do not begin like an unencrypted file of the same type (a `.pdf` starting `25 50 44 46`, a `.docx` starting `50 4b 03 04`), turning red if they do. A supervisor looking at the list saw identical original and encrypted sizes and asked whether anything was encrypted or only the name changed; this lets a user look at the stored bytes instead of taking it on trust, and completes the preview half of #71 that had been closed without being built.
- 在 My Files 的每个文件下方新增 **Encrypted preview**，自己的文件和别人分享给你的文件都有。它显示算法、以精确字节表示的两个大小、上传时记录的 SHA-256，以及服务器所存密文前 128 字节的十六进制转储，并检查这些字节是否不是以同类型未加密文件的典型开头开始（`.pdf` 以 `25 50 44 46` 开头、`.docx` 以 `50 4b 03 04` 开头），若是则变红。导师在列表里看到原始大小与加密后大小完全一样，问到底有没有加密、还是只改了文件名；现在用户可以直接查看存储的字节，而不是凭信任，同时补上了 #71 里曾在未实现的情况下被关闭的预览那一半。
- The new `POST /files/{id}/ciphertext-preview` needs the same download capability as the encrypted download and exposes strictly less: a sample whose size is fixed by the server at 128 bytes (never chosen by the caller), plus metadata the caller already receives. It reads only the head of the object, so it costs nothing on a 500 MiB file, and for the same reason it is **not** integrity-verified; the response and the panel say so and point at the status badge and the integrity check. It is audited as `FILE_CIPHERTEXT_PREVIEWED`, deliberately not as a download, so previews never inflate the download count. Ten backend tests and ten frontend tests cover it (plus the four 20 MiB evidence tests below), and the Chromium golden-path test now opens the preview after an upload and asserts the sizes match to the byte, the dump has eight rows, and the stored bytes do not begin like the plaintext.
- 新增的 `POST /files/{id}/ciphertext-preview` 需要与下载密文相同的下载权限，并且暴露的更少：由服务器固定为 128 字节的样本（绝不由调用方指定），外加调用方本来就能拿到的元数据。它只读取对象的开头，所以对 500 MiB 的文件也没有成本；正因如此它**没有**做完整性校验，响应和面板都明说了这一点，并指向状态徽标和完整性检查。它以 `FILE_CIPHERTEXT_PREVIEWED` 记入审计，有意不算下载，所以预览不会抬高下载计数。十个后端测试和十个前端测试覆盖了它（另有下面四个 20 MiB 证据测试），Chromium 黄金路径测试现在会在上传后打开预览，并断言两个大小逐字节相同、转储有八行、存储的字节不是以明文的开头开始。
- Added `docs/ENCRYPTION_EVIDENCE.md` (and 简体中文) explaining why the two sizes match: AES-GCM and ChaCha20-Poly1305 add no padding, and the 12-byte nonce and 16-byte tag are stored beside the file, so equal size is expected and unequal size would be the bug. A new CI test, `encryption-evidence.test.ts`, pushes a 20 MiB structured file through the real upload path with all four algorithms and checks same length, entropy 4.4 → 7.99999 bits/byte, chance-level byte agreement, different hash, exact round-trip, and that flipping one byte fails authentication. The document says plainly what this does not prove: it is not cryptanalysis, and the figures come from Node rather than the browser.
- 新增 `docs/ENCRYPTION_EVIDENCE.md`（及简体中文版），解释两个大小为什么一样：AES-GCM 与 ChaCha20-Poly1305 不做填充，12 字节 nonce 和 16 字节标签存放在文件旁边，所以大小相同是预期，大小不同才是 bug。新增的 CI 测试 `encryption-evidence.test.ts` 把一个 20 MiB 的结构化文件用四种算法经由真实上传流程处理，并检查长度一致、熵从 4.4 升到 7.99999 比特/字节、字节重合率处于随机水平、哈希不同、能精确还原，以及改动一个字节会让认证失败。文档也明确写了它不能证明什么：这不是密码分析，数字来自 Node 而不是浏览器。
- Fixed the My Files table, whose header cells were squeezed together because the file-name column took whatever its longest name asked for. The table now uses a fixed layout: the name column is bounded and long names wrap, the other columns keep room for their own headings, the six row actions sit in a 3×2 block instead of wrapping raggedly, and hovering a size shows its exact byte count. The preview row spans exactly the columns on screen, because a cell spanning more makes browsers invent extra ones.
- 修复了 My Files 表格：表头挤在一起，原因是文件名列会占用最长文件名所需的全部宽度。表格现在采用固定布局：文件名列有上限且长文件名自动换行，其他列保留各自表头所需的空间，每行六个操作整齐排成 3×2，而不是参差不齐地换行，鼠标悬停在大小上会显示精确字节数。预览行恰好跨越屏幕上实际存在的列，因为跨越更多列会让浏览器凭空生成额外的列。

### Scope of #54 and #71 recorded, and the deploy gate confirmed live / 记录 #54 与 #71 的实际范围，并确认部署门禁已上线

- Wrote down what the two Issues closed in September actually delivered, on the Issues and in `TODO.md`/`TODO.zh-CN.md`/README. #54 delivered the key-file passphrase reset (#80) but not changing the passphrase while the old one is known, browser successor-key rotation, the account-envelope specification or browser E2E for the reset; #71 delivered the ciphertext download (#78, #79) but not the bounded preview. The project owner chose to record this and leave them closed rather than reopen them, so the TODO now marks both done *for what shipped*, lists the unbuilt parts, and says they are not tracked as open work. Without this, a closed Issue read as full completion of criteria that were never built.
- 把九月关闭的两个 Issue 实际交付了什么写了下来：写在 Issue 上，也写进 `TODO.md`/`TODO.zh-CN.md`/README。#54 交付了用密钥文件重置 passphrase（#80），但没有在记得旧 passphrase 时修改 passphrase、浏览器端后继密钥轮换、账户信封规范，以及重置流程的浏览器 E2E；#71 交付了密文下载（#78、#79），但没有有限长度的预览。项目负责人选择如实记录并保持关闭，而不是重新打开，所以 TODO 现在按“已交付的部分”把两者标为完成，列出未实现的部分，并说明它们不作为未完成工作被跟踪。否则，一个已关闭的 Issue 会被读成对从未实现的验收标准的完全达成。
- Confirmed the CI-gated deploy (#117) is live: the `promote` job created `deploy` on its own merge commit, including a commit that edits a workflow file, the new script was installed on the VPS, and its first run reported the running release already matches the latest CI-passed commit. Threat-model F-01 and T-E5 are now *mitigated* (v1.2). The severity is left as the author rated it; the residual is that no review is enforced and `mcs21-deploy all main` still bypasses CI by design.
- 确认以 CI 为门槛的部署（#117）已上线：`promote` 作业在自己的合并提交上创建了 `deploy`（该提交还修改了一个 workflow 文件），新脚本已安装到 VPS，首次运行报告运行中的 release 已与最新通过 CI 的 commit 一致。威胁模型 F-01 与 T-E5 现为*已缓解*（v1.2）。严重度保持作者原先的评级；残留的是不强制评审，以及 `mcs21-deploy all main` 仍按设计绕过 CI。

### Usability study: the answer key for losing both secrets / 可用性研究：同时丢失两个秘密的标准答案

- Settled the open question in the study plan: with the passphrase or the private key file an account can be reopened, but with neither the files cannot be recovered by anyone, and the only way forward is to delete the account and register again. This matches `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` (*Request recovery* only rewraps a file key to a registered key the requester can still use) and the registration copy, so no wording changes; Probe 2 now has an answer key, and a second probe checks the half people should get right (key file plus forgotten passphrase). What remains for the study to answer is whether a *Request recovery* button on every file row makes people think they are safe.
- 敲定了研究计划里悬而未决的问题：有 passphrase 或私钥文件，账户都能重新打开；两者都没有，则任何人都无法恢复文件，唯一的出路是注销账户后重新注册。这与 `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md`（“Request recovery”只是把文件密钥重新包给请求者仍能使用的已登记密钥）以及注册页文案一致，因此无需改文案；探针 2 现在有了标准答案，并新增一个探针检查人们应当答对的另一半（有密钥文件但忘了 passphrase）。留给研究回答的是：每个文件行上都有“Request recovery”按钮，会不会让人觉得自己是安全的。

### Deploy only commits that passed CI / 只部署通过 CI 的提交

- The staging server no longer deploys `main` directly. A new `promote` job in `.github/workflows/ci.yml` moves a `deploy` branch to a commit of `main` only after `backend`, `frontend`, `mysql-gate`, `e2e-live` and `tamper-drill` have all passed on it, and `mcs21-auto-deploy` deploys that branch. Before this, a direct push or a merge with red CI reached production within about two minutes (threat-model finding F-01, rated High); GitHub branch protection could not close it because the repository is private on the free plan.
- 预发布服务器不再直接部署 `main`。`.github/workflows/ci.yml` 中新增的 `promote` 作业，只有在 `backend`、`frontend`、`mysql-gate`、`e2e-live`、`tamper-drill` 在某个 `main` commit 上全部通过之后，才把 `deploy` 分支移动到它，而 `mcs21-auto-deploy` 部署的是这个分支。此前，直接推送或 CI 为红的合并会在约两分钟内进入生产（威胁模型发现 F-01，高危）；由于仓库是免费套餐下的私有仓库，GitHub 分支保护无法解决这个问题。
- Design choices worth knowing. The ref is moved through the REST API, not `git push`, because `GITHUB_TOKEN` may not push commits that change workflow files and this change itself edits one. The branch only moves forward and a diverged history fails the job instead of forcing. If CI has promoted nothing yet the server deploys nothing and raises no failed unit. A manual `mcs21-deploy all main` is kept as a deliberate escape hatch, and the timer will not walk a release back behind it. Seven structural tests (`test_deploy_gate.py`) fail if a new CI job is not in `promote.needs`, if the server script starts reading `main` again, or if the promote job ever uses `git push` or `--force`.
- 设计上值得知道的几点。移动引用用的是 REST API 而不是 `git push`，因为 `GITHUB_TOKEN` 不能推送修改了 workflow 文件的提交，而本改动本身就改了一个。分支只向前移动，历史分叉时作业直接失败而不是强制覆盖。CI 尚未推进任何提交时，服务器什么都不部署，也不会产生失败的 unit。手动 `mcs21-deploy all main` 作为有意保留的应急入口，timer 也不会把 release 退回到它之后。七个结构性测试（`test_deploy_gate.py`）会在以下情况失败：新增的 CI Job 没有列入 `promote.needs`、服务器脚本又开始读取 `main`、或 promote 作业使用了 `git push` 或 `--force`。
- What it does not do: it does not require a review, and it adds latency — a merge now reaches staging after CI finishes (about five minutes) plus up to two for the timer. The server script is deployed by hand (`deployment/server/` is a reference copy), so this PR alone changes nothing on the server until it is installed after the first successful promotion.
- 它不做什么：不要求评审，并且增加了延迟——现在合并后要等 CI 结束（约五分钟）再加最多两分钟的 timer。服务器脚本需要手动安装（`deployment/server/` 只是参考副本），所以本 PR 单独合并不会改变服务器行为，要等第一次成功推进之后再安装。

### Records reconciled with the live system on 2026-10-01 / 2026-10-01 记录与线上现状对账

- Brought `TODO.md`, `TODO.zh-CN.md`, `docs/PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY.md`, the threat-model findings register (now v1.1, both languages) and the staging evidence table in the observability runbook into line with what was observed on `main` at `70fcb6d` and on staging: a verified-gates table, #57 and #59 marked done on their recorded evidence (seven consecutive daily reconcile runs; the 2026-09-25 checkpoint, redaction and alert checks), WBS 5.1 Done, 5.3 In review and 5.4 In progress, and the eight `<pending>` runbook rows filled from the Issue #59 record rather than left to look unfinished.
- 将 `TODO.md`、`TODO.zh-CN.md`、`docs/PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY.md`、威胁模型发现登记表（现为 v1.1，中英文）以及可观测性运行手册中的 staging 证据表，与 `70fcb6d` 上的 `main` 和 staging 实际观察到的情况对齐：新增已核验门禁表；#57 与 #59 依据已记录的证据标为完成（连续七次每日对账运行；2026-09-25 的 checkpoint、脱敏与告警检查）；WBS 5.1 为 Done、5.3 为 In review、5.4 为 In progress；运行手册里八个 `<pending>` 行依据 Issue #59 的记录填写，而不是继续显得未完成。
- Corrected two things the records had wrong, and recorded what could not be done. The WBS summary said 20 Done / 3 In progress / 1 Backlog while its own table already showed 21 / 2 / 1; it now reads 22 Done, 1 In review, 1 In progress (97.5 %). Branch protection, which TODO and threat-model F-01 told the team to ask an admin to enable, is not available at all: the repository is private on GitHub's free tier and the API refuses it, so the recommendation is now a CI-gated deploy or a plan change.
- 纠正了记录中两处错误，并写明做不到的事。WBS 摘要写的是 20 Done / 3 In progress / 1 Backlog，而其自身的表格已经是 21 / 2 / 1；现在是 22 Done、1 In review、1 In progress（97.5%）。TODO 与威胁模型 F-01 要求团队请管理员启用的分支保护，实际上根本无法使用：仓库是 GitHub 免费版的私有仓库，API 会拒绝，所以建议改为以 CI 为门槛的部署，或更换套餐。
- Flagged, without changing GitHub, two Issues closed as completed before their acceptance criteria were met: #54 (no browser passphrase change, no successor-key rotation, no account-envelope specification in `frontend/src`) and #71 (the ciphertext download shipped; the bounded preview did not). TODO keeps them unchecked and says so; reopening or splitting them is the team's call. A new Issue, #115, tracks running the usability study.
- 标出了两个在验收标准尚未满足时就被标为已完成关闭的 Issue，但没有改动 GitHub：#54（`frontend/src` 中没有浏览器端修改 passphrase、后继密钥轮换与账户信封规范）和 #71（密文下载已交付，有限长度的预览没有）。TODO 保持它们未勾选并如实说明；重新打开或拆分由团队决定。新增 Issue #115 跟踪可用性研究的实施。
- Statuses that depend on #112, #113 and #114 (dependency audit, not-found page and large-upload warning, usability kit) describe those PRs as merged; this PR is meant to merge after them.
- 依赖 #112、#113、#114（依赖审计、404 页与大文件提示、可用性套件）的状态，是按这些 PR 已合并来描述的；本 PR 应在它们之后合并。

### Non-expert usability study kit (WBS 5.4, NFR2) / 非专业用户可用性研究套件（WBS 5.4、NFR2）

- Added `docs/usability/`: a study plan, bilingual consent sheet, screener, ten task cards, SUS and open-question questionnaire, and CSV templates. WBS 5.4 was the one Backlog package with no artefact at all, and NFR2 ("users can complete all operations without any specialized knowledge") is the only High-priority requirement that only people can verify. **No session has been run and `RESULTS.md` is deliberately empty**: the kit makes the claim testable, it does not make it true, and nothing may quote a result until real participants have produced one.
- 新增 `docs/usability/`：研究计划、中英双语同意书、筛选表、十张任务卡、SUS 与开放题问卷，以及 CSV 模板。WBS 5.4 是唯一一个没有任何产物的 Backlog 工作包，而 NFR2（“用户无需任何专业知识即可完成所有操作”）是唯一只能由真人来验证的高优先级需求。**尚未进行任何场次，`RESULTS.md` 有意留空**：该套件让这个主张变得可检验，并不会让它成真；在真实参与者给出结果之前，任何地方都不得引用结果。
- The success criteria are fixed in the plan before any session and encoded in `backend/scripts/usability_analysis.py` (22 unit tests): every task completed unaided by at least 80 % of at least five participants, no task with a critical error for more than one participant, and mean SUS of at least 68. Fixing them first is the point: it stops the verdict being argued after the data is in, and a result that misses them is recorded as a result rather than a threshold being moved.
- 成功标准在任何场次之前就写入计划，并编码在 `backend/scripts/usability_analysis.py`（22 个单元测试）：至少五名参与者中，每个任务都有至少 80% 无提示完成，没有任何任务的关键错误涉及一名以上参与者，且平均 SUS 不低于 68。先固定标准正是关键：避免数据出来后再争论结论；未达标的结果就如实记为结果，而不是挪动阈值。
- The plan lists what must be settled first rather than assuming it: whether Monash needs ethics review or an exemption (to be answered in writing by the supervisor; not verified here), and a contradiction in the product's own wording that decides the right answer to one comprehension question — registration says losing both the passphrase and the key file means files "cannot be recovered", while each file row offers *Request recovery* and the platform has recovery officers. It also records five expert observations from reading the interface (jargon beside the upload picker, "AES-256-GCM" copy beside a four-algorithm dropdown, icon-only row actions, password versus passphrase, the `.pem` file) as hypotheses for the sessions to confirm or drop, not as findings.
- 计划列出了必须先敲定的事项，而不是预先假定：Monash 是否需要伦理审查或豁免（须由导师书面答复；此处未核实），以及产品自身措辞里的一处矛盾——它决定一道理解题的正确答案：注册页说同时丢失 passphrase 和密钥文件意味着文件“无法恢复”，而每个文件行都有 *Request recovery*，平台也设有恢复官。计划还把阅读界面得到的五条专家观察（上传选择框旁的术语、四种算法下拉框旁写着“AES-256-GCM”的文案、只有图标的行操作、密码与 passphrase 的区别、`.pem` 文件）记为供场次证实或排除的假设，而不是发现。

### A not-found page, and a warning before a large upload fails / 404 页面，以及大文件上传失败前的提示

- Unknown URLs now render a "We can't find that page" screen with links home and to Help, instead of a blank page. The blank page was recorded as threat-model finding F-20: to a non-expert visitor it reads as a broken site, not a mistyped link. The server still answers such URLs with HTTP 200 because nginx serves the single-page app for every path; the page says "404" but the status line does not, which is acceptable for a signed-in tool and is noted rather than hidden. A Chromium test (`not-found.browser.ts`) covers it in the `e2e-live` job.
- 未知 URL 现在会显示“找不到该页面”，并提供回到首页与 Help 的链接，不再是一片空白。空白页已被登记为威胁模型 F-20：对非专业访问者来说，它看起来像网站坏了，而不是网址输错了。服务器对这类 URL 仍然返回 HTTP 200，因为 nginx 对每个路径都返回单页应用；页面上写着“404”而状态行不是，对登录后使用的工具这是可以接受的，在此注明而不是隐瞒。`e2e-live` Job 中新增的 Chromium 测试（`not-found.browser.ts`）覆盖此行为。
- The upload page now tells the user about size problems when the file is chosen, not minutes into encryption. From 100 MiB a file shows an amber note that encryption needs about four times its size in memory and may fail on a phone; above the 500 MiB limit it shows an error and "Start Encryption" stays disabled until the file is removed. If the browser still cannot allocate the buffer, the bare `RangeError` is replaced with "too large for this browser to encrypt … close other tabs, or try a desktop browser". This closes the user-facing half of finding F-19; the limit itself is deliberately unchanged, because 500 MiB was chosen in #70 and a desktop browser handles it.
- 上传页现在在选择文件时就告知大小问题，而不是加密了几分钟之后。从 100 MiB 起，文件会显示一条琥珀色提示：加密需要约其四倍大小的内存，在手机上可能失败；超过 500 MiB 限制则显示错误，并在移除该文件之前保持“开始加密”禁用。若浏览器仍然无法分配缓冲区，原先裸露的 `RangeError` 会被替换为“该文件对此浏览器而言过大，无法加密……请关闭其他标签页，或改用桌面浏览器”。这解决了发现 F-19 面向用户的那一半；上限本身有意不变，因为 500 MiB 是 #70 中定下的，桌面浏览器可以处理。

### Dependency vulnerability gates / 依赖漏洞门禁

- CI now audits dependencies on every pull request: `pip-audit -r requirements.txt` in the `backend` job and `npm audit --audit-level=high` in the `frontend` job. The 2026-10-01 project check found a high-severity `axios` advisory sitting in the lockfile that nothing was positioned to notice; the threat model had already recorded the gap as F-12 ("no automated vulnerability scanning"). The browser build uses axios' fetch/XHR adapters, so the Node-only advisories were not reachable from the shipped bundle, but a known-vulnerable HTTP client in a security product is not something to carry by argument.
- CI 现在在每个 PR 上审计依赖：`backend` Job 运行 `pip-audit -r requirements.txt`，`frontend` Job 运行 `npm audit --audit-level=high`。2026-10-01 的项目检查发现 lockfile 里躺着一个高危的 `axios` 公告，而当时没有任何环节会发现它；威胁模型已把这一缺口登记为 F-12（“没有自动化漏洞扫描”）。浏览器构建使用 axios 的 fetch/XHR 适配器，这些仅针对 Node 的公告在已发布的 bundle 中无法触发，但安全产品里留着一个已知有漏洞的 HTTP 客户端，不该靠“推理上不可达”来交代。
- Upgraded `axios` from 1.18.1 to 1.20.0 (lockfile and the `package.json` floor, so a fresh resolve cannot fall back below it); `npm audit` reports 0 vulnerabilities and the unit tests, typecheck and build are unchanged. The trade-off to know about: a newly published advisory can now turn an unrelated PR red until the package is upgraded or the finding is triaged. That is the intended behaviour of the gate, and the fix is normally a one-line lockfile bump.
- 将 `axios` 从 1.18.1 升级到 1.20.0（lockfile 与 `package.json` 下限一并更新，避免重新解析时回落到更低版本）；`npm audit` 报告 0 个漏洞，单元测试、类型检查与构建均不受影响。需要知道的代价：新发布的公告可能让一个无关的 PR 变红，直到升级该包或完成问题研判。这正是门禁的预期行为，通常只需把 lockfile 升一行。

### Argon2id measured, and what the passphrase minimum is worth / Argon2id 实测，以及 passphrase 最短长度的实际价值

- Measured the key derivation behind every unlock on real clients, for step 2 of Issue #60. A new public page, `/benchmark/argon2`, runs the production `deriveKek` — the same code, hash-wasm build and parameters as the unlock dialog — and reports timings a user can copy. On Chrome and Safari on an Apple Silicon Mac and on an iPhone, the production cost (Argon2id, 64 MiB, 3 passes) takes **100–151 ms**. The unlock the dialog calls "slow on purpose" is a tenth of a second; the cost does not burden users.
- 为 Issue #60 第 2 步，在真实客户端上实测了每次解锁背后的密钥派生。新增的公开页面 `/benchmark/argon2` 运行生产环境的 `deriveKek`——与解锁对话框相同的代码、hash-wasm 构建和参数——并给出可供复制的耗时。在一台 Apple Silicon Mac 上的 Chrome、Safari 以及一部 iPhone 上，生产环境成本（Argon2id，64 MiB，3 轮）耗时 **100–151 ms**。解锁对话框口中「故意放慢」的那一步只需十分之一秒，这个成本不会给用户造成负担。
- Measured the attacker too. `backend/scripts/benchmark_argon2_offline.py` runs the native C reference implementation at the same parameters, and new `argon2id_kek` known-answer vectors prove the browser derives byte-for-byte the same key, so the attacker's figures apply to real stolen envelopes. One 10-core laptop manages 53–58 guesses per second per account. Ten cores buy only 5.3×, because each guess holds 64 MiB and memory bandwidth, not core count, is the limit.
- 同时也测量了攻击者一侧。`backend/scripts/benchmark_argon2_offline.py` 用原生 C 参考实现以相同参数运行，新增的 `argon2id_kek` 已知答案向量证明浏览器派生出的密钥与之逐字节一致，因此攻击方的数据适用于真实被盗的信封。一台 10 核笔记本对单个账户每秒可猜 53–58 次。十个核心只带来 5.3 倍加速，因为每次猜测要占用 64 MiB，瓶颈在内存带宽而非核心数。
- The verdict, now in `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` §9: **the KDF cost is not the weak link; the passphrase check is.** Four random words survive a million laptops for about two years. But the check is length-only, so `aaaaaaaaaa` and `password12` pass it, and a passphrase from any leaked-password list falls in under two days on one laptop. Because the passphrase never reaches the server, only the browser can reject weak choices. The document recommends doing so (R-1), and records measured headroom to quadruple the cost at under 0.6 s (R-2), deferred until a low-end Android device is measured. Neither change is made here.
- 结论已写入 `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` 第 9 节：**薄弱环节不是 KDF 成本，而是 passphrase 检查。** 4 个随机单词面对一百万台笔记本也能撑约两年；但检查只看长度，因此 `aaaaaaaaaa` 和 `password12` 都能通过，任何泄露密码库中的 passphrase 在一台笔记本上不到两天就会被攻破。由于 passphrase 从不到达服务器，只有浏览器能拒绝弱选择。文档建议这样做（R-1），并记录了实测余量：可以把成本提高到四倍，同时解锁仍低于 0.6 秒（R-2），但要推迟到测过低端 Android 设备之后。这两项改动本次都未实施。
- The coverage gap is stated rather than hidden: no Android device, no Firefox and no low-end hardware were measured, and Safari was unexplainedly six times slower at 128 MiB than at 256 MiB. Raw results are kept in `docs/benchmarks/argon2id-2026-09-30.json`.
- 覆盖面上的缺口如实写明而不加掩饰：没有测量任何 Android 设备、Firefox 或低端硬件；Safari 在 128 MiB 时比 256 MiB 时还慢六倍，原因不明。原始结果保存在 `docs/benchmarks/argon2id-2026-09-30.json`。

### A written threat model / 书面威胁模型

- Completed the threat model for Issue #60 as v1.0. §11 reviews what the issue listed last. Dependencies: the frontend is lockfile-exact, but backend transitive dependencies are unpinned and nothing scans automatically; point-in-time `npm audit` and `pip-audit` found no known vulnerabilities on 2026-09-30. The section also covers each CSP relaxation and why it exists, the absence of a per-user storage quota, why tokens stay in `localStorage` at this scope, and the custody around offline recovery. §12 gathers everything into one register of 20 findings, each with severity, disposition, owner and evidence. §13 records honestly that the review was a self-assessment plus one peer review: no independent external review took place, and nothing is certified.
- 以 v1.0 完成了 Issue #60 的威胁模型。第 11 节评审 issue 最后列出的几项。依赖方面：前端依赖由锁文件精确固定，但后端的传递依赖未固定，也没有自动扫描；2026-09-30 的时间点 `npm audit` 与 `pip-audit` 均未发现已知漏洞。该节还说明了每一处 CSP 放宽及其理由、没有按用户存储配额的问题、为何在此范围内令牌仍放在 `localStorage`，以及离线恢复周边的保管问题。第 12 节把一切汇总为一张含 20 项发现的登记表，每项都有严重度、处置、负责人与证据。第 13 节如实记录：这次评审是一份自评加一次同行评审，没有进行过独立的外部评审，也不构成任何认证。
- Raised one earlier finding. `/docs` loads `swagger-ui-dist@5` from jsDelivr with a floating version and no Subresource Integrity, on `https://secureview.tech` — the same origin whose `localStorage` holds every signed-in user's tokens. A compromised release of that package could read the tokens of anyone signed in who opens `/docs`. It could not decrypt their files, but it could take over their sessions. v0.1 had rated the public docs low; they are now Medium (F-03), and the fix is configuration only: `APP_DOCS_ENABLED=false` on staging.
- 上调了一项早先的发现。`/docs` 从 jsDelivr 加载 `swagger-ui-dist@5`，版本浮动且没有子资源完整性校验，运行在 `https://secureview.tech` 上——正是这个源的 `localStorage` 保存着每个已登录用户的令牌。这个包一旦有某次发布被攻破，任何打开 `/docs` 的已登录用户的令牌都可能被读取；它无法解密文件，但能接管会话。v0.1 曾把公开文档评为「低」，现上调为「中」（F-03），修复只需改配置：在预发布环境设置 `APP_DOCS_ENABLED=false`。
- Four findings are *Recommended* and not implemented in the review: branch protection with CI-gated deploys (F-01, High), moving the staging recovery private key out of a workstation into the proposed offline custody (F-02, High), disabling `/docs` on staging (F-03, Medium), and rejecting weak passphrases in the browser (F-04, Medium). None needs a schema change; three are configuration or custody actions rather than code.
- 有四项发现标为*建议修复*，但未在本次评审中实施：启用分支保护并以 CI 作为部署门槛（F-01，高）；把预发布环境的恢复私钥从工作站移入提议的离线保管（F-02，高）；在预发布环境关闭 `/docs`（F-03，中）；在浏览器中拒绝弱 passphrase（F-04，中）。这几项都不需要 Schema 变更；其中三项是配置或保管方面的操作，而非代码改动。
- Recorded the step 3 decisions of Issue #60 in `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` §10, each taken deliberately at FYP scope, since none of these items is in `TODO.md` or any Issue. The plaintext `.pem` export stays: it is the only recovery factor independent of the passphrase, and the section now spells out what losing each credential costs. Losing both the passphrase and the key file is unrecoverable, and admin recovery cannot help, because it rewraps to the very key that was lost. Filenames and MIME types stay in cleartext, because server-side search and listing depend on them; their integrity is still bound into AAD-3, and a table lists exactly what the server can observe, including exact file sizes, since the ciphertext is never padded.
- 在 `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` 第 10 节中记录了 Issue #60 第 3 步的各项决定。这些决定都有意在 FYP 范围内作出，因为其中没有一项出现在 `TODO.md` 或任何 Issue 中。明文 `.pem` 导出予以保留：它是唯一独立于 passphrase 的恢复因子；该节写明了丢失各项凭据的后果。passphrase 与密钥文件都丢失时无法恢复，管理员恢复也帮不上忙，因为它重新包装给的恰恰是丢失的那把密钥。文件名与 MIME 类型保持明文，因为服务端搜索与列表依赖它们；它们的完整性仍被绑定进 AAD-3。另有一张表如实列出服务器能观察到的全部内容，其中包括精确的文件大小，因为密文从不做填充。
- Public-key substitution is analysed against a new adversary, X8: a compromised backend or database writer who can rewrite any row but not the code browsers run, because the release tree is owned by `mcs21-deploy` and only readable by the backend's `fypapp`. Against X8, a check in the browser genuinely helps; against a malicious code supplier, nothing does. The recovery, recipient and owner key substitutions (T-S4, T-S5, T-S7) and rollback (T-T4) are accepted at FYP scope, with the cheapest effective control recorded for each. If the project continues, the first to build is a recovery-key fingerprint compiled into the frontend, and the section notes that any build-time pin is only as strong as T-E5 lets the path from Git to production be.
- 针对新增的对手 X8 分析了公钥替换问题。X8 指被攻破的后端或数据库写入者，它能改写任意数据行，却改不了浏览器运行的代码，因为发布目录归 `mcs21-deploy` 所有，后端的 `fypapp` 只能读取。面对 X8，浏览器中的检查确实有用；面对恶意的代码提供方，什么都没用。恢复公钥、接收方公钥与拥有者公钥被替换（T-S4、T-S5、T-S7）以及回滚（T-T4）均在 FYP 范围内接受，并为每一项记录了最省事且有效的控制。若项目继续开发，首先应实现的是编译进前端的恢复公钥指纹；该节还指出，任何构建时固定的强度，都取决于 T-E5 所决定的那条从 Git 到生产环境的路径。
- Corrected the registration page, which still told users their encryption passphrase "cannot be reset" and that losing it made files unrecoverable. Both stopped being true when the key-file reset shipped. It now says the saved key file can set a new passphrase in Settings, and that only losing both the passphrase and the file is final.
- 修正了注册页的文案：它仍在告诉用户加密 passphrase「无法重置」、丢失它文件就无法恢复。自从密钥文件重置功能上线，这两句话都已不再成立。现在它写明：保存好的密钥文件可以在 Settings 中设置新 passphrase，只有 passphrase 与该文件都丢失才无可挽回。
- Corrected the threat model after a teammate's review of v0.1 found it described stronger controls than the code provides; all ten points held when checked against the implementation. The sharing dialog does **not** show the recipient's key fingerprint, though v0.1 listed that as a control (T-S5). A direct push to `main` does **not** skip CI; the real gap is that the server-pull deployment neither waits for CI nor requires it to pass (T-E5). `FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED` is written before streaming, so it proves an authorized request, not receipt (T-R1). The KEK is derived per unlock and never stored, so it is no longer described as "stored wrapped". O1 is now scoped to trusted client code, TB0 lists everything that actually crosses the boundary, including passwords, tokens and metadata, and N2 no longer implies that timestamps or the sharing graph are covered by the file tag. Owner-key substitution is added as T-S7, beside the recovery- and recipient-key cases.
- 根据队友对 v0.1 的评审修正了威胁模型：评审指出文档描述的控制比代码实际提供的更强，对照实现逐条核实后，十点全部成立。共享对话框**并不**显示接收方的公钥指纹，而 v0.1 把它列为一项控制（T-S5）。直接推送到 `main` **并不会**跳过 CI；真正的缺口在于服务器拉取式部署既不等待 CI，也不要求 CI 通过（T-E5）。`FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED` 在开始传输前写入，因此它证明的是一次获批准的请求，而非已接收（T-R1）。KEK 在每次解锁时派生、从不存储，因此不再被描述为「以包装形式存储」。O1 现在限定于客户端代码可信的前提；TB0 列出实际跨越边界的全部内容，包括密码、令牌与元数据；N2 不再暗示时间戳或共享关系图受文件 tag 保护。拥有者公钥被替换的情形作为 T-S7 加入，与恢复公钥、接收方公钥的情形并列。
- Adopted a rule the review exposed: **implemented is not deployed.** A control that exists in the repository but whose staging operation is unverified is no longer marked Mitigated. Applying it downgraded four rows beyond the reviewer's list, each against the pending items in the staging evidence tables: the external audit checkpoints have not yet uploaded from staging (T-T6), the 30-day backup bucket lock is not verified in the installation record (TB7), and the loopback bind (TB2) and log-redaction check (T-I5) are still pending. Recovery-key custody now separates the proposed offline design from the staging reality of an unencrypted PEM on a workstation.
- 采纳了这次评审揭示的一条原则：**已实现不等于已部署。**存在于仓库中、但在预发布环境上的运行尚未核实的控制，不再标为「已缓解」。按此原则，对照预发布环境证据表中的待定项，另有四行被下调，超出了评审者列出的范围：外部审计检查点尚未从预发布环境上传（T-T6）；30 天备份存储桶锁定未在安装记录中核实（TB7）；回环绑定（TB2）与日志脱敏核查（T-I5）仍为待定。恢复私钥保管现在区分了提议中的离线设计，与预发布环境中「未加密 PEM 存放在工作站上」的现状。
- Added `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` and its Chinese twin, step 1 of Issue #60. The security design had been specified flow by flow in `SECURITY_FLOWS.md`, but nothing stated in one place what the system protects, from whom, and where it stops: six objectives, seven explicit non-goals, fourteen assets, twelve actors, nine trust boundaries, a STRIDE table in which every threat has a stable ID for the later steps, and six worked abuse cases. Non-goals are listed first on purpose — the largest is that a server willing to ship hostile JavaScript can capture the passphrase, so zero-knowledge holds at the API boundary rather than against the code supplier — so a reader does not credit the system with protections it never claimed.
- 新增 `docs/THREAT_MODEL.md` 及其中文版，这是 Issue #60 的第 1 步。此前安全设计在 `SECURITY_FLOWS.md` 中按流程逐一规定，但没有一处集中说明系统保护什么、防范谁、边界止于何处：本文给出六项目标、七项明确的非目标、十四项资产、十二类参与者、九道信任边界、一张每条威胁都有稳定 ID 以供后续步骤引用的 STRIDE 表，以及六个完整推演的滥用场景。非目标被有意放在前面——其中最大的一项是：愿意下发恶意 JavaScript 的服务器可以截获 passphrase，因此零知识成立于 API 边界，而不针对代码提供方——以免读者把系统从未声称的保护归功于它。
- Every control the model credits was checked against code or the live deployment rather than recalled, and three of the first draft's claims did not survive that. Registration was listed as enumeration-resistant; in fact `POST /auth/register` answers `409 account_identifier_unavailable` for a taken username or email *before* checking the verification code, so existence can be probed without one. "Vue escapes by default" was replaced with the verified statement that both `v-html` sinks render only bundled content. "Role checks on every route" was narrowed to the guards that actually exist.
- 模型中认定的每项控制都对照代码或线上部署核实过，而非凭记忆，初稿中有三处说法没有通过核实。注册原被列为能抵御枚举；实际上 `POST /auth/register` 会在校验验证码*之前*，就对已被占用的用户名或邮箱返回 `409 account_identifier_unavailable`，因此无需验证码即可探测账户是否存在。「Vue 默认转义」被替换为经核实的表述：两个 `v-html` 输出点都只渲染打包进来的内容。「每条路由都有角色检查」被收窄为实际存在的那些守卫。
- The model records open findings rather than smoothing them over, each tagged with the step that will resolve it: staging serves `/docs`, `/redoc` and `/openapi.json` publicly under a relaxed CSP that loads script from `cdn.jsdelivr.net`; `main` has no branch protection, so a direct push skips review and CI and still auto-deploys; nothing pins the recovery public key or rejects a rolled-back key bundle; and the staging recovery private key is held as an unencrypted PEM on a workstation, not in the offline custody `BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` proposes. The document states on its first line that it is a self-assessment by the author of much of the cryptography it reviews, and certifies nothing.
- 模型如实记录尚未解决的发现，而不加粉饰，并为每项标注将由哪一步解决：预发布环境在放宽的 CSP 下公开提供 `/docs`、`/redoc` 与 `/openapi.json`，该 CSP 会从 `cdn.jsdelivr.net` 加载脚本；`main` 没有分支保护，直接推送可跳过评审与 CI 并照样自动部署；没有任何机制固定恢复公钥或拒绝回滚后的私钥包；预发布环境的恢复私钥以未加密 PEM 形式存放在一台工作站上，而非 `BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` 所提议的离线保管。文档第一行即写明：本文由其所评审的大部分密码学实现的作者自评，不构成任何认证。

### Link previews for secureview.tech / secureview.tech 的链接预览

- Added Open Graph and Twitter card tags to `frontend/index.html`, with a 1200×630 `og-image.png` in `public/`. Link-preview crawlers such as LinkedIn read the static HTML without running the SPA, so the page previously offered only its `<title>` and no description or image. A Vitest check keeps the tags present and the image URL absolute and matching the shipped PNG's dimensions.
- 在 `frontend/index.html` 中加入 Open Graph 与 Twitter card 标签，并在 `public/` 中放入 1200×630 的 `og-image.png`。LinkedIn 等链接预览爬虫只读静态 HTML、不执行 SPA，因此此前页面只提供 `<title>`，没有描述和图片。新增的 Vitest 检查确保标签存在、图片 URL 为绝对地址，并与随包发布的 PNG 尺寸一致。
- Re-rendered `og-image.png` at 2400×1260 with larger text. LinkedIn re-compresses the image and upscales it on high-density screens, so the 1200×630 original previewed visibly blurred. The URL now carries `?v=2` so LinkedIn fetches the new image rather than its cached copy.
- 将 `og-image.png` 以 2400×1260 重新渲染并放大文字。LinkedIn 会重新压缩图片，并在高分屏上放大显示，原先 1200×630 的预览明显模糊。图片 URL 增加 `?v=2`，让 LinkedIn 抓取新图而不是沿用缓存。
- Recorded the #73 staging rollout: the merge of #103 was quarantined by `mcs21-auto-deploy` because `03-verify.sql` expected `0007_file_folders` while staging was still on `0006`. The same code-first ordering had quarantined #101 earlier. `mcs21-deploy` never changes the schema, so revision `0007` must be applied by an administrator before the release, as `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` "Applying a schema revision to a running deployment" describes.
- 记录 #73 在 staging 上线的问题：#103 合并后被 `mcs21-auto-deploy` 隔离，原因是 `03-verify.sql` 期待 `0007_file_folders`，而 staging 数据库仍在 `0006`。此前 #101 也因同样的「先代码后数据库」顺序被隔离。`mcs21-deploy` 从不修改 schema，revision `0007` 必须由管理员在发布前手动应用，见 `docs/DEPLOYMENT.md` 的 “Applying a schema revision to a running deployment”。

### Validated user avatar upload and display / 经验证的用户头像上传与展示

- Completed #75: accounts can upload a validated PNG/JPEG profile picture (`POST /auth/avatar/upload`), serve it with `nosniff` (`GET /auth/avatar`), and delete it (`POST /auth/avatar/delete`). `/auth/me` and login snapshots include `has_avatar`; the header menu and `/profile` show the image when present and fall back to initials otherwise. Bytes live under a dedicated avatar root (outside ciphertext storage), are size-capped and magic-sniffed, rate-limited between updates, and cleared when the account is deleted.
- 完成 #75：账户可上传经验证的 PNG/JPEG 头像（`POST /auth/avatar/upload`），以 `nosniff` 提供（`GET /auth/avatar`），并删除（`POST /auth/avatar/delete`）。`/auth/me` 与登录快照含 `has_avatar`；页头菜单与 `/profile` 有图则显示图片，否则回退首字母。字节存放在独立头像根目录（不进入密文存储），有大小上限与魔数校验、更新间隔限流，注销账户时一并清理。
- Kept the real-MySQL authentication fixture at the `0008_avatars` schema by adding the avatar columns and shape constraint to its temporary `users` table; the gate now exercises authentication against the same user shape the ORM queries instead of failing before the flow begins.
- 为真实 MySQL 身份验证夹具的临时 `users` 表补齐头像字段及形状约束，使其与 `0008_avatars` Schema 保持一致；门禁现在会针对 ORM 实际查询的用户结构执行身份验证流程，而不会在流程开始前失败。
- Made the MySQL release drill derive both the current head and migration count from Alembic metadata, so adding `0008_avatars` (and future revisions) cannot leave the recovery and concurrency checks rejecting a correctly migrated database because of stale constants.
- MySQL 发布演练现在会从 Alembic 元数据推导当前 head 与迁移数量，因此新增 `0008_avatars`（以及未来 revision）后，恢复与并发检查不会再因过期常量而误判已正确迁移的数据库。
- Made avatar editing unmistakable on `/profile`: the picture now carries a camera action and a labelled primary Upload/Change button, with a visible busy state, accessible success/error announcements, and a separate Remove picture action when an avatar exists.
- 强化 `/profile` 的头像编辑入口：头像右下角新增相机操作，并提供带文字的高对比度“上传/更换头像”主按钮；上传过程有明确忙碌状态，成功与错误提示可被辅助技术播报，已有头像时另设“移除头像”操作。
- Restored #75 on top of the #73 folders work. The first merge (#101/#102) was removed from `main` after its release was quarantined, because both features had claimed revision `0007`. The avatar revision is now `0008_avatars`, revising `0007_file_folders`, and `03-verify.sql` counts the five avatar columns and `ck_users_avatar_shape` (213 columns, 37 CHECK constraints); the original branch had left those totals at the `0006` values, so its release would have failed verification even after the SQL ran.
- 在 #73 文件夹功能之上恢复 #75。首次合并（#101/#102）的发布被隔离后已从 `main` 移除，原因是两项功能都占用了 revision `0007`。头像 revision 现为 `0008_avatars`，基于 `0007_file_folders`；`03-verify.sql` 也计入了五个头像字段与 `ck_users_avatar_shape`（213 列、37 个 CHECK 约束）。原分支仍保留 `0006` 时的总数，因此即使执行了 SQL，其发布也会在校验阶段失败。

### Per-user file folders / 用户文件夹分类

- Implemented the repository side of #73 with account-scoped folders in My Files: owners can create, rename, delete, filter by, and move files between folders or Unfiled. Folder assignment is server-persisted across devices, recipients cannot reorganize another owner's shared files, and deleting a folder only clears its classification rather than deleting ciphertext. Revision `0007_file_folders` adds the ownership and assignment constraints so one account cannot name or move another account's folders. Staging deployment and affected-flow evidence remain before the Issue can close.
- 已完成 #73 的仓库侧实现，在 My Files 中加入按账户隔离的文件夹：所有者可创建、重命名、删除、筛选文件夹，并在文件夹与“未分类”之间移动文件。分类由服务端持久化，可跨设备同步；接收者不能重组他人分享的文件；删除文件夹只清除分类，不会删除密文。Revision `0007_file_folders` 增加所有权与归属约束，防止一个账户引用或移动另一个账户的文件夹。Issue 关闭前仍需 staging 部署与受影响流程证据。
- Fixed the release-gate race assertion to derive the migration count from Alembic history. Adding revision `0007` correctly made one concurrent deployer apply seven upgrades, but the gate still expected the previous six and rejected a healthy serialized migration; future revisions no longer require another manual count change. Also formatted the SQL deployment test that blocked the backend Ruff job.
- 修复发布门禁的并发断言，改为从 Alembic history 推导迁移数量。新增 `0007` 后，一个并发部署者正确执行了七次升级，但门禁仍期待旧的六次，因而误判正常的串行迁移失败；今后新增 revision 不再需要手动同步计数。同时格式化了阻断 backend Ruff Job 的 SQL 部署测试。

### Secondary confirmation for destructive actions / 破坏性操作二次确认

- Completed #72 for the product surfaces that still fired on one click: deleting a file in My Files and revoking a share now open a dedicated dialog that names the target and states whether the action is recoverable. Account deletion remains behind the existing password + typed-`DELETE` dialog, now also reachable from `/profile` as well as Settings.
- 完成 #72 中仍为一键触发的产品面：My Files 删除文件、分享对话框撤销访问均改为独立确认弹窗，写明目标与是否可恢复。注销账户仍使用既有的「登录密码 + 输入 DELETE」弹窗，并在 `/profile` 与 Settings 均可进入。

### Server-revoking logout and role-aware operations UI / 会撤销服务端会话的登出与角色感知运维界面

- Fixed #89: intentional **Log out** now calls `POST /auth/logout` while the bearer is still present, then clears the browser. Until this, the UI only wiped local tokens and left the server session family (and its refresh credential) valid. Session expiry and account deletion still clear locally only, because those paths already have no usable session or have already revoked every family server-side.
- 修复 #89：用户主动 **Log out** 时先在仍持有 bearer 的情况下调用 `POST /auth/logout`，再清理浏览器。此前界面只擦本地 token，服务端会话族及其 refresh 凭据仍然有效。会话过期与注销账户仍只清本地——那些路径要么已无可用会话，要么服务端已撤销全部会话族。
- Completed #55: `/admin` is now a role-aware Operations surface. ADMIN can list/enable/disable users, browse file metadata, verify the audit hash chain, register/revoke the recovery public key, and review recovery requests. RECOVERY_OFFICER gets recovery review plus offline execution-package download and rewrapped-key paste-back (the recovery private key is never accepted in the browser). Ordinary users can open a recovery request from My Files and track or cancel it on the same page.
- 完成 #55：`/admin` 现为角色感知的 Operations 界面。ADMIN 可列出/启用/禁用用户、浏览文件元数据、验证审计哈希链、登记/撤销恢复公钥，并复核恢复请求。RECOVERY_OFFICER 可做恢复复核，以及下载离线执行包并回填重封装后的密钥（浏览器从不接受恢复私钥）。普通用户可从 My Files 发起恢复请求，并在同一页面跟踪或取消。
- Added unit coverage for the new `adminApi` / `recoveryApi` clients and auth role getters so the live e2e function-coverage floor (63%) is not diluted by unexercised wrappers.
- 为新增的 `adminApi` / `recoveryApi` 客户端与 auth 角色 getter 补充单元覆盖，避免未调用的封装函数把 live e2e 的函数覆盖率门槛（63%）稀释掉。

### Redacted observability, incident response, and external audit anchors / 脱敏可观测性、事故响应与外部审计锚点

- Implemented the repository side of #59: security/operations events are structured JSON with route templates, redacted client networks, and field-name redaction; a loopback-only metrics snapshot is sampled every five minutes against explicit staging thresholds. Unexpected exception text, request bodies, filenames/content, credentials, tokens, passphrases and key material are deliberately excluded so detection does not create a second sensitive-data store.
- 完成 #59 的仓库侧实现：安全/运维事件改为结构化 JSON，只记录路由模板、脱敏后的客户端网段，并按敏感字段名再次脱敏；仅 loopback 可访问的 metrics snapshot 每五分钟按明确的 staging 阈值采样。未预期异常原文、请求正文、文件名/内容、凭据、令牌、passphrase 与密钥材料均被刻意排除，避免为了检测异常而制造第二份敏感数据存储。
- Added hourly HMAC-SHA256 audit-chain checkpoints for S3-compatible off-server storage. Each checkpoint authenticates only the deployed Git SHA and audit-chain head; the root-only HMAC key never enters the application environment. Offline `verify` and read-only `verify-live` detect a database rewrite that is internally self-consistent but no longer contains an older external anchor.
- 新增每小时发布到 S3 兼容异地存储的 HMAC-SHA256 audit-chain checkpoint。checkpoint 只认证已部署 Git SHA 与审计链头；仅 root 可读的 HMAC key 不进入应用环境。离线 `verify` 与只读 `verify-live` 能发现“数据库内部仍自洽、但已不再包含旧外部锚点”的整体重写。
- Hardened operational alerting and network boundaries: alert email no longer contains raw journal lines, Uvicorn's existing proxy trust is pinned to loopback nginx, `/internal/` is explicitly 404 on the public origin, and new observer/checkpoint units alert through the same sparse mail path. The paired incident runbooks cover outage, storage corruption, credential compromise, migration/schema mismatch, audit-chain failure, and recovery-key incidents. Staging activation and independent external-checkpoint/redaction evidence remain required before #59 closes.
- 收紧运维告警与网络边界：告警邮件不再携带原始 journal 行；Uvicorn 现有 proxy trust 明确固定为 loopback nginx；公网 origin 对 `/internal/` 显式返回 404；新的 observer/checkpoint unit 复用同一条最小化邮件告警链路。双语事故 runbook 覆盖宕机、存储损坏、凭据泄露、迁移/Schema 不一致、审计链失败与 recovery-key 事故。#59 关闭前仍必须完成 staging 启用，以及外部 checkpoint 独立验证和脱敏实证。

### Isolated VPS adversarial tamper drill / 隔离 VPS 对抗性篡改演练

- Added a default-stopped, resource-limited attacker container and disposable MySQL 8.4/object volumes for #90. The repeatable drill proves byte tampering becomes `MODIFIED`/`CORRUPTED`, download is refused, missing storage does not permanently poison status, and restoration heals the file, without exposing the Docker socket, host network, production secrets, database, or user objects.
- 为 #90 新增默认停止且资源受限的攻击容器，以及一次性 MySQL 8.4/对象卷。可重复演练证明字节篡改会变成 `MODIFIED`/`CORRUPTED`、下载被拒、存储暂时缺失不会永久污染状态、恢复密文后文件自愈；整个过程不会暴露 Docker socket、宿主机网络、生产 Secret、数据库或用户对象。
- The drill also demonstrates the residual trust boundary instead of overstating SHA-256: simultaneous database-and-object compromise can replace the recorded digest and pass the server comparison, while the original AES-GCM tag still rejects the forgery during client authentication. CI now runs the whole drill and retains only a hygiene-scanned JSON report; all lab credentials, keys, database state, and volumes are destroyed on exit.
- 演练同时展示剩余信任边界，不夸大 SHA-256：若数据库与对象存储同时失陷，攻击者可替换记录摘要并通过服务端比较，但原始 AES-GCM 标签仍会在客户端认证时拒绝伪造。CI 现会运行完整演练，只保留经过泄密扫描的 JSON 报告；所有实验凭据、密钥、数据库状态与卷都会在退出时销毁。
- Hardened the lab build after the first VPS run exposed a 102 MB context: `backend/.dockerignore` excludes environments, logs, caches, local object/staging directories, and reports, while the Dockerfile independently allowlists only application source, migrations, scripts, and tests. This prevents deployment-local material from entering either attacker image even if one layer of exclusion later regresses.
- 首次 VPS 运行发现构建上下文达到 102 MB 后，进一步收紧实验镜像：`backend/.dockerignore` 排除环境文件、日志、缓存、本地对象/暂存目录与报告；Dockerfile 同时独立采用白名单，只复制应用源码、迁移、脚本与测试。即使今后其中一层排除规则退化，部署机本地材料也不会进入攻击镜像。

### A real-browser smoke test of the golden path / 黄金路径的真实浏览器冒烟测试

- Added `frontend/browser-tests/golden-path.browser.ts`, a Playwright test in which Chromium clicks the built pages through register, emailed-code verification, sign-in, upload, unlocking the account key, download (bytes compared with the original), sharing, the recipient seeing and decrypting the file with their own passphrase, revocation, the file leaving the recipient's list, and logout. The Node live suites prove the crypto and API contract but cannot see a button wired to the wrong handler; with the Share button's handler disabled, the Node suites still passed all 313 tests and this test failed. It closes the last deferred item of #58.
- 新增 `frontend/browser-tests/golden-path.browser.ts`：Playwright 驱动 Chromium 在构建后的页面上依次点击完成注册、邮箱验证码验证、登录、上传、解锁账户密钥、下载（与原文件逐字节比较）、分享、接收方看到文件并用自己的 passphrase 解密、撤销、文件从接收方列表消失、登出。Node 真实后端测试能证明加密与 API 契约，却看不出按钮接错了处理函数；把 Share 按钮的处理函数改为空操作后，Node 测试 313 项仍全部通过，而本测试失败。它完成了 #58 最后一项遗留工作。
- It runs in the existing `e2e-live` job after the Node suites, against the same disposable API and MySQL (`run_live_stack.sh` with `E2E_BROWSER=1`, or `only` to skip the Node suites locally), on a production build served by `vite preview`, with one worker, no retries, failure on any flaky pass, and `check_no_skips.py` over its JUnit report. Only Chromium is installed, and its download is cached by Playwright version. No Vue component was changed; every selector is a role, label or visible text.
- 它在现有 `e2e-live` Job 中、Node 测试之后运行，使用同一套一次性 API 与 MySQL（`run_live_stack.sh` 加 `E2E_BROWSER=1`；本地可用 `only` 跳过 Node 测试），页面是由 `vite preview` 提供的生产构建。单 worker、不重试、出现 flaky 通过即失败，并用 `check_no_skips.py` 检查其 JUnit 报告。只安装 Chromium，下载按 Playwright 版本缓存。没有修改任何 Vue 组件；所有选择器都是 role、label 或可见文本。
- CI records no Playwright traces: a trace keeps every request header and body and DOM snapshot, including bearer tokens and the private key the register page shows once. The HTML report is uploaded only when the job fails, and it holds only throwaway data the test mints itself (`@example.com` accounts, "Throwaway" passwords, random file bytes). `check_report_hygiene.py` now unpacks ZIP attachments and the report's embedded base64 results, which a byte-level scan could not read, and refuses any Playwright trace outright.
- CI 不记录 Playwright trace：trace 会保存每个请求的 header、body 与 DOM 快照，其中包括 Bearer 令牌和注册页只显示一次的私钥。HTML 报告只在 Job 失败时上传，其中只有测试自己生成的一次性数据（`@example.com` 账户、"Throwaway" 密码、随机文件字节）。`check_report_hygiene.py` 现在会解开 ZIP 附件与报告内嵌的 base64 结果（逐字节扫描读不到这些内容），并直接拒绝任何 Playwright trace。

### Encrypted off-server backups and the reconciliation timer live on staging / 加密异地备份与对账 timer 已在 staging 上线

- Installed #56 and #57 on staging on 2026-09-24. Nightly encrypted sets now go to Cloudflare R2 (35-day lifecycle, 30-day bucket lock) through a read-only `fyp_backup_user`, and the reconcile timer runs at 05:00 after the same-host backup; every backup and cleanup unit emails on failure. Until now nothing left the VPS and no failure reached a person.
- 2026-09-24 在 staging 上安装了 #56 与 #57。每晚的加密备份集现在通过只读的 `fyp_backup_user` 传到 Cloudflare R2（35 天生命周期、30 天 bucket lock）；对账 timer 在同机备份之后的 05:00 运行；所有备份与清理 unit 失败时都会发邮件。此前没有任何数据离开 VPS，也没有任何失败会通知到人。
- A real off-server set was restored on a separate machine with the offline key in 6.97 s, and all 17 tables (`CHECKSUM TABLE`) and all 24 objects (SHA-256) matched the live system. This is the evidence #56 asked for: a restore from the real set with the real key, not only CI's throwaway keys.
- 一个真实的异地备份集已在另一台机器上用离线私钥恢复，用时 6.97 秒，17 张表（`CHECKSUM TABLE`）与 24 个对象（SHA-256）全部与线上一致。这正是 #56 要求的证据：用真实密钥恢复真实备份集，而不只是 CI 中的一次性密钥。
- The backup account had to be created for host `<docker-gateway>`, not `localhost` as `04-backup-account.sql` writes it: staging's MySQL runs in Docker, so host TCP connections arrive from the Docker gateway. The installation records in `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` and `docs/RECONCILIATION_RUNBOOK.md` note this and the other host facts.
- 备份账号必须以 host `<docker-gateway>` 创建，而不是 `04-backup-account.sql` 中写的 `localhost`：staging 的 MySQL 运行在 Docker 中，主机发起的 TCP 连接来自 Docker 网关。`docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` 与 `docs/RECONCILIATION_RUNBOOK.md` 的安装记录写明了这一点及其他主机事实。

### Making the reconciliation timer safe to enable, and alerts that reach a person / 让对账 timer 可以安全启用，并让告警真正通知到人

- `backend/scripts/reconcile.py` now removes an empty per-file directory only once its mtime is older than the storage grace (issue #57). Before, `--apply` removed every empty directory at once, so it could delete one that `publish` had just created and fail an upload between the `mkdir` and the `link`. A client retrying with the same `file_id` makes that window real. A directory the run itself empties waits for the next run.
- `backend/scripts/reconcile.py` 现在只在空的按文件目录 mtime 超过存储宽限期后才删除它（issue #57）。此前 `--apply` 会立即删除所有空目录，可能删掉 `publish` 刚创建的目录，让处于 `mkdir` 与 `link` 之间的上传失败；客户端用同一个 `file_id` 重试时这个窗口是真实存在的。本次运行自己清空的目录留到下一次运行再删。
- Added a volume guard. With `--apply`, a run that would remove more than 1000 entries (`--max-deletions`) or more than 10 ciphertext objects (`--max-object-deletions`) deletes nothing and exits 3, so the unit fails and alerts. The staging store holds about two dozen objects, so a burst of orphans means a wrong directory or database, not a real backlog. A daily run normally removes tens to a few hundred rows.
- 新增数量保护：带 `--apply` 时，若一次运行将删除超过 1000 个条目（`--max-deletions`）或超过 10 个密文对象（`--max-object-deletions`），则什么都不删并以 3 退出，unit 失败并告警。staging 存储总共只有约二十几个对象，一批孤立对象说明目录或数据库配错了，而不是真正的积压；每日运行通常只删除几十到几百行。
- The report now shows every category from both sides of its cutoff: eligible, crossed in the last 7 days, and crossing in the next 7 days. It prints counts only. Issue #57 requires reviewing candidates on both sides of every boundary before enabling the timer, and the old report showed only one side.
- 报告现在从截止线两侧展示每个类别：可删除数量、最近 7 天越过截止线的数量、未来 7 天将越过的数量，且只输出数量。issue #57 要求启用 timer 前审查每条边界两侧的候选项，而旧报告只显示一侧。
