- Added `backend/tests/integration/test_reconcile_mysql.py`. Against real MySQL 8.4, it proves both-sides counts, that a refused run deletes nothing, and that a second apply finds nothing. It also captures every SQL statement and fails if one names an audit, identity, key-custody, or recovery table. Unit tests cover the directory grace and the guard.
- 新增 `backend/tests/integration/test_reconcile_mysql.py`，在真实 MySQL 8.4 上证明：两侧计数正确、被拒绝的运行不删除任何东西、第二次 apply 什么也找不到。它还捕获每条 SQL 语句，只要有一条引用审计、身份、密钥保管或恢复相关的表就失败。单元测试覆盖目录宽限期与数量保护。
- `deployment/server/mcs21-reconcile.service` could not have run on staging: it named a non-existent `fyp-backend` user and storage paths under the release directory. It now runs as `fypapp` with `ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/fyp-secure-file-platform`. The timer moved from midnight to 05:00 Asia/Kuala_Lumpur, after the 04:15 same-host backup, and the service refuses to start without a backup from the last 3 hours, so every scheduled deletion has a rollback point.
- `deployment/server/mcs21-reconcile.service` 原本在 staging 上根本无法运行：它引用了不存在的 `fyp-backend` 用户和 release 目录下的存储路径。现在它以 `fypapp` 运行，`ReadWritePaths=/var/lib/fyp-secure-file-platform`。timer 从午夜改到 05:00（Asia/Kuala_Lumpur），在 04:15 的同机备份之后；若没有 3 小时内的备份，服务拒绝启动，因此每次计划删除都有回滚点。
- Added `mcs21-alert` and `mcs21-alert@.service`. They email the failed unit, host, time, and last 30 journal lines through the app's Resend account to the recipients in a root-only `alert.env`. The API key reaches `curl` only through a `0600` header file. The reconcile, off-server backup, same-host backup, and restore-check units all alert. Before, failures reached only the journal, which is how the next item went unnoticed for six weeks.
- 新增 `mcs21-alert` 与 `mcs21-alert@.service`：通过应用的 Resend 账户，把失败的 unit、主机、时间和最近 30 行 journal 发给仅 root 可读的 `alert.env` 中的收件人。API key 只通过 `0600` 的 header 文件传给 `curl`。对账、异地备份、同机备份与恢复校验四个 unit 都会告警。此前失败只进入 journal，下一条问题正是因此六周无人察觉。
- `mcs21-alert@.service` now passes the failed unit's name with `%i`, not `%I`. `%I` unescapes `-` to `/`, so installing it on staging turned `mcs21-backup-verify.service` into `mcs21/backup/verify.service`, and the alert would have mailed an empty journal excerpt for every one of these units.
- `mcs21-alert@.service` 现在用 `%i` 而非 `%I` 传递失败 unit 的名称。`%I` 会把 `-` 反转义成 `/`，在 staging 安装后 `mcs21-backup-verify.service` 变成了 `mcs21/backup/verify.service`，这些 unit 的告警邮件里 journal 摘录都会是空的。
- Put the server-only `mcs21-backup` and `mcs21-backup-verify` under version control, and fixed the restore check. It hardcoded `14|0002_auth_identity_sessions|1`, stale since migration 0003, so it had failed every Sunday since 2026-08-16. It now expects the deployed release's alembic head, and the live database's table count at that version.
- 把仅存在于服务器上的 `mcs21-backup` 与 `mcs21-backup-verify` 纳入版本管理，并修复了恢复校验：它写死了 `14|0002_auth_identity_sessions|1`，自迁移 0003 起就已过时，因此从 2026-08-16 起每周日都失败。现在它期望已部署 release 的 alembic head，以及线上数据库在该版本下的表数量。
- Added `docs/RECONCILIATION_RUNBOOK.md` (with a 简体中文 twin). It covers what the job deletes and what it never touches, with grant, foreign-key, and authentication evidence, plus installation, the report-only run, the boundary review, manual runs, disabling, rollback from `/var/backups/mcs21-daily`, the incident procedure, and alert testing. It ends in an installation record the operator fills in; no staging result is claimed yet.
- 新增 `docs/RECONCILIATION_RUNBOOK.md`（附简体中文版），说明任务删除什么、永不触碰什么（附授权、外键与认证方面的证据），以及安装、report-only 运行、边界审查、手动运行、停用、从 `/var/backups/mcs21-daily` 回滚、事故处理与告警测试。文末是一份由运维人员填写的安装记录；目前尚未声称任何 staging 结果。

### Encrypted off-server backups and a proven clean-host restore / 加密异地备份与经过验证的洁净主机恢复

- Added `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md` (with a 简体中文 twin) defining RPO, RTO, and retention for MySQL metadata, ciphertext objects, configuration, host secrets, and recovery keys (issue #56). Before this, the only safety nets were provider snapshots and a plaintext same-host dump taken at deploy time; neither is off-server, and neither had ever been restored. The numbers are sized for a small staging deployment: 24 h RPO, 1 h data-restore RTO, 4 h whole-service RTO, and 7 daily + 4 weekly sets. Each one states what backs it up, and what has not been timed yet is listed separately.
- 新增 `docs/BACKUP_AND_RECOVERY.md`（附简体中文版），为 MySQL 元数据、密文对象、配置、主机 Secret 与恢复密钥定义 RPO、RTO 与保留策略（issue #56）。此前唯一的保障是云厂商快照，以及部署时在同一主机上生成的明文 dump；两者都不在异地，也从未做过恢复。这些数字按小型预发布环境设定：RPO 24 小时，数据恢复 RTO 1 小时，整体服务 RTO 4 小时，保留 7 个每日 + 4 个每周备份集。每个数字都写明由什么支撑，尚未计时的部分单独列出。
- Added `backend/scripts/backup_encrypted.py`. It takes one `mysqldump --single-transaction` and one archive of the ciphertext object store under a shared manifest id, embedded in the dump's first line, the archive, and the manifest. It then checks that every `files` row that could be ACTIVE in the snapshot has its object, with the right `encrypted_sha256`, before shipping anything; otherwise a delete racing the backup could leave metadata pointing at a missing object. Each artifact is sealed with AES-256-GCM under a fresh key wrapped by RSA-OAEP to a backup **public** key, with the manifest id and role bound into the tag, so the backup host can never read its own backups and artifacts from two sets cannot be mixed. Destinations are a local/mounted directory (the stand-in used in CI), rsync over SSH, or S3; the two remote forms are wired but not yet tested against a real target.
- 新增 `backend/scripts/backup_encrypted.py`。它在同一个 manifest id 下生成一份 `mysqldump --single-transaction` 和一份密文对象存储归档，该 id 同时写入 dump 第一行、归档和 manifest。在送出任何内容之前，它会核对快照中可能为 ACTIVE 的每一行 `files` 都有对应对象，且 `encrypted_sha256` 正确；否则与备份同时发生的删除可能让元数据指向不存在的对象。每个产物用新生成的密钥做 AES-256-GCM 加密，该密钥再用 RSA-OAEP 包裹给备份**公钥**，manifest id 与角色绑定进认证标签。这样备份主机永远读不了自己的备份，两个备份集的产物也无法混用。目标可以是本地/挂载目录（CI 中用作替身）、经 SSH 的 rsync 或 S3；后两种已接好，但尚未对真实目标测试。
- Added `backend/scripts/restore_drill.py`, the clean-host restore procedure and its drill. It refuses, and cleans up after itself, if an artifact fails its hash or authentication, if any embedded manifest id disagrees, if the dump is truncated, if an ACTIVE row's object is missing or wrong, if the restored revision differs from the recorded one, or if `alembic check` finds drift. It records per-step timings. Measured locally: 945 MB of objects restored onto a second, empty MySQL 8.4 server in 4.27 s, with data and schema digests identical to the source and an identical object tree.
- 新增 `backend/scripts/restore_drill.py`，即洁净主机恢复流程及其演练。以下任一情况都会拒绝恢复并自行清理：产物哈希或认证不通过、任何嵌入的 manifest id 不一致、dump 被截断、ACTIVE 行的对象缺失或不符、恢复出的 revision 与记录不同、`alembic check` 发现差异。它会记录每一步的耗时。本地实测：945 MB 对象恢复到第二个空的 MySQL 8.4 服务器用时 4.27 秒，数据与 Schema 摘要和源端完全一致，对象树也完全相同。
- Added `database/mysql/04-backup-account.sql`, a separate, re-runnable file that creates `fyp_backup_user` with `SELECT` on the application database only. The previous draft put these grants in `02-schema.sql`, which would have aborted every fresh install half-way (MySQL error 1410: the account is never created there). `check_runtime_grants.py` now connects as the backup account in CI and proves it cannot write, lock, run DDL, grant, create users, or read `mysql.*`; a deliberately widened grant makes the check fail.
- 新增 `database/mysql/04-backup-account.sql`，一个独立、可重复执行的文件，创建仅对应用数据库拥有 `SELECT` 权限的 `fyp_backup_user`。此前的草稿把这些授权放进了 `02-schema.sql`，这会让每一次全新安装在中途中止（MySQL 错误 1410：该账号从未在那里创建）。`check_runtime_grants.py` 现在在 CI 中以备份账号连接，证明它不能写入、加锁、执行 DDL、授权、创建用户或读取 `mysql.*`；故意放宽授权会让检查失败。
- Added `backend/scripts/backup_restore_drill.sh` to the `mysql-gate` CI job. On every pull request it generates a throwaway key and backup account, then runs the encrypted backup and a restore into a database that does not exist yet. It checks the destination holds no plaintext or private key, and proves six restore refusals plus the backup's own refusal. The restore report (ids, counts, timings) is kept as a workflow artifact.
- 在 `mysql-gate` CI Job 中加入 `backend/scripts/backup_restore_drill.sh`。它在每个 PR 上生成一次性密钥和备份账号，执行加密备份，并恢复到一个尚不存在的数据库。它会检查目标中没有明文或私钥，并验证六种恢复拒绝场景以及备份自身的拒绝。恢复报告（id、数量、耗时）作为 workflow artifact 保留。
- Verified, rather than assumed, that recovery private keys are outside the backup scope. The schema has no column for them, a new unit test fails if a plaintext private-key column ever appears, and the backup aborts if the object directory contains anything that looks like a PEM private key. Offline custody without an HSM, and retention of every historical key, are documented as a proposal for the team to confirm.
- 核实（而非假定）恢复私钥不在备份范围内：Schema 中没有存放它的列；新增的单元测试会在出现明文私钥列时失败；对象目录中若有任何看起来像 PEM 私钥的文件，备份会中止。没有 HSM 时的离线保管方式，以及所有历史密钥的保留策略，已作为待团队确认的提议写入文档。

### Closing the two gaps #53 deferred / 补上 #53 遗留的两处缺口

- `mysql_release_gate.sh` now repeats the concurrent-migration race 8 times with random 0–400 ms start offsets. Each iteration must show one deployer applying all six revisions and the other reporting that it waited on the lock, then applying nothing. `migrate.sh` now prints a line when it has to wait, which is what makes real contention provable rather than assumed. A single un-fuzzed race could pass by luck, for example if the second deployer simply started after the first finished.
- `mysql_release_gate.sh` 现在以 0–400 毫秒的随机启动间隔重复 8 次并发迁移竞争。每一轮都必须表明一个部署者应用了全部六个 revision，另一个报告自己在锁上等待、随后什么都没有应用。`migrate.sh` 在需要等待时会输出一行，正是这一点让真实的竞争可以被证明，而不是被假定。单次、不随机化的竞争可能侥幸通过，例如第二个部署者恰好在第一个结束后才启动。
- The backup/restore phase now compares the restore with `backend/scripts/db_fingerprint.py`: per-table `CHECKSUM TABLE` plus a schema fingerprint of columns, indexes, CHECK and foreign-key constraints. It no longer compares only table and row counts. The gate changes one column of one row after restoring and requires the comparison to fail on exactly that table, so the check is proven to see content, not just counts.
- 备份/恢复阶段现在用 `backend/scripts/db_fingerprint.py` 比较恢复结果：逐表 `CHECKSUM TABLE`，加上由列、索引、CHECK 与外键约束组成的 Schema 指纹，不再只比较表数和行数。门禁会在恢复后修改某一行的一列，并要求比较恰好在该表上失败，从而证明该检查看到的是内容，而不只是数量。
- New gate phases cover #56's migration bullets. Rollback before upgrade: back up at `0005`, migrate, restore over the migrated database, and prove the revision and fingerprints are back. A multi-revision release interrupted between revisions resumes forward. A release interrupted inside a revision cannot resume forward: 0005 fails with MySQL error 3821 on a half-dropped constraint. That case recovers by restoring the pre-release backup and re-running the release. Local runs: all phases pass in 46–61 s.
- 新增的门禁阶段覆盖 #56 中与迁移相关的条目。升级前回滚：在 `0005` 备份、迁移、把备份恢复到已迁移的数据库之上，证明 revision 与指纹都已复原。在 revision 之间中断的多 revision 发布可以向前续跑。在 revision 内部中断的发布无法向前续跑：0005 会在已被删掉一半的约束上以 MySQL 错误 3821 失败。这种情况通过恢复发布前备份、再重跑发布来恢复。本地运行：全部阶段在 46–61 秒内通过。

### Required CI quality gates and a fixed Ruff baseline / 必需的 CI 质量门禁与修复后的 Ruff 基线

- Added `.github/workflows/ci.yml` with a `backend` job (Ruff format/lint, then the non-MySQL pytest suite) and a `frontend` job (`npm ci`, unit tests, `vue-tsc` typecheck, production build), triggered on every pull request and on push to `main`, with a concurrency group that cancels a superseded run on the same branch. Before this, a green release depended on every contributor remembering to run these commands locally; now a pull request cannot merge past a red check without a repository admin overriding branch protection. Dependency caching is keyed on the lockfiles only (`uv`'s download cache, `npm`'s cache); nothing under `.venv`, `.env`, or another generated secret is ever cached or uploaded.
- 新增 `.github/workflows/ci.yml`，包含 `backend`（Ruff 格式化/检查，随后运行不依赖 MySQL 的 pytest 套件）与 `frontend`（`npm ci`、单元测试、`vue-tsc` 类型检查、生产构建）两个 Job，在每次 PR 及推送到 `main` 时触发，并使用 concurrency group 取消同一分支上被覆盖的旧运行。此前绿色发布全靠每位贡献者自觉在本地执行这些命令；现在 PR 若检查未通过，除非仓库管理员手动覆盖分支保护，否则无法合并。依赖缓存仅以锁定文件为键（`uv` 的下载缓存、`npm` 的缓存）；`.venv`、`.env` 或其他生成的密钥文件不会被缓存或上传。
- Fixed the Ruff baseline for real instead of suppressing it: `ruff format .` reformatted the four files the 2026-09-03 baseline already flagged, and the three unsorted import blocks (`app/api/dependencies.py`, `app/api/v1/routers/auth_router.py`, `app/services/share_notification_service.py`) were reordered with `ruff check --fix .`. No `# noqa` was added.
- 真正修复了 Ruff 基线，而不是压制它：`ruff format .` 重新格式化了 2026-09-03 基线已标记的四个文件，三处导入顺序错误（`app/api/dependencies.py`、`app/api/v1/routers/auth_router.py`、`app/services/share_notification_service.py`）已用 `ruff check --fix .` 重新排序。未添加任何 `# noqa`。
- Documented the exact CI commands in a new "Continuous Integration" / "持续集成" section in `README.md` and `README.zh-CN.md`, so a contributor can reproduce a failing check locally before pushing.
- 在 `README.md` 与 `README.zh-CN.md` 新增“Continuous Integration / 持续集成”小节，列出与 CI 完全一致的命令，方便贡献者在推送前于本地复现失败的检查。

### MySQL 8.4 and schema-parity release gate / MySQL 8.4 与 Schema 一致性发布门禁

- Added a `mysql-gate` job to `.github/workflows/ci.yml` that provisions a disposable `mysql:8.4` service container, runs every migration from an empty database through `0006_chacha20_poly1305` (`alembic upgrade head`, `alembic current`, `alembic check`), and then runs the full MySQL integration suite (`pytest -m integration`). Before this, `AUTH_TEST_DATABASE_URL` being unset made the suite skip itself silently, so "tests passed" never meant the database was actually exercised. `backend/scripts/check_no_skips.py` now reads the JUnit report from that run and fails the job outright if a single test skipped, closing that gap.
- 为 `.github/workflows/ci.yml` 新增 `mysql-gate` Job：拉起一次性的 `mysql:8.4` 服务容器，从空数据库依次执行到 `0006_chacha20_poly1305` 的每一次迁移（`alembic upgrade head`、`alembic current`、`alembic check`），再运行完整的 MySQL 集成测试（`pytest -m integration`）。此前 `AUTH_TEST_DATABASE_URL` 未设置时套件会静默跳过，"测试通过"从未真正意味着数据库被实际验证过。`backend/scripts/check_no_skips.py` 现在读取该次运行生成的 JUnit 报告，只要有一条测试被跳过就直接判 Job 失败，补上了这个缺口。
- Added `backend/scripts/verify_mysql_tls.py`, which connects with a verified CA certificate and asserts `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Ssl_cipher'` returns a non-empty cipher, proving the release-gate connection actually negotiated TLS rather than merely being able to.
- 新增 `backend/scripts/verify_mysql_tls.py`，使用经过校验的 CA 证书建立连接，并断言 `SHOW STATUS LIKE 'Ssl_cipher'` 返回非空的加密套件，证明发布门禁的连接确实协商了 TLS，而不只是具备协商的能力。
- Added `backend/scripts/check_runtime_grants.py`, which applies the exact runtime `GRANT` statements from `database/mysql/02-schema.sql` against the disposable database and then connects as that account to assert it can do ordinary CRUD but not `CREATE`/`DROP`/`GRANT`, and not an unrestricted `UPDATE` on the append-only audit tables — while the administrator connection that applied the grants can still perform DDL. This is the live counterpart to `tests/unit/test_mysql_sql_deployment.py`, which only checks that the SQL file says this offline.
- 新增 `backend/scripts/check_runtime_grants.py`，将 `database/mysql/02-schema.sql` 中运行时账户的 `GRANT` 语句原样应用到一次性数据库，再以该账户连接，断言其可执行普通 CRUD，但不能执行 `CREATE`/`DROP`/`GRANT`，也不能对只增不改的审计表做无限制 `UPDATE`——而执行了授权的管理员连接仍可执行 DDL。这是 `tests/unit/test_mysql_sql_deployment.py`（仅离线检查 SQL 文件文本）的线上对应验证。
- Added `backend/tests/integration/test_mysql_schema_parity.py`, extending the offline SQL-file parity checks with live checks against the migrated database: foreign keys and their `ON DELETE` behaviour, unique constraints, and `CHECK` constraints are diffed against `Base.metadata` through `information_schema` rather than by parsing text.
- 新增 `backend/tests/integration/test_mysql_schema_parity.py`，在离线 SQL 文件一致性检查之外，补充针对已迁移数据库的线上检查：通过 `information_schema` 而非解析文本，将外键及其 `ON DELETE` 行为、唯一约束与 `CHECK` 约束与 `Base.metadata` 逐项比对。
- Added `backend/scripts/migrate.sh`, which wraps `alembic upgrade head` in a MySQL `GET_LOCK`/`RELEASE_LOCK` pair with a bounded timeout, and `backend/scripts/mysql_release_gate.sh`, which exercises four scenarios the pytest suite does not: a non-empty, non-Alembic-tracked database refuses `alembic upgrade head` instead of silently colliding; a migration resumed after stopping partway through the chain reaches head and a repeat `upgrade head` is a clean no-op; two concurrent `alembic upgrade head` invocations against the same database serialize through the new lock instead of racing; and a `mysqldump` backup taken before migrating restores into a fresh database with matching table and row counts. All four were run against a local MySQL 8.4 (`docker compose up -d mysql`) before being wired into CI.
- 新增 `backend/scripts/migrate.sh`，用带超时的 MySQL `GET_LOCK`/`RELEASE_LOCK` 包裹 `alembic upgrade head`；新增 `backend/scripts/mysql_release_gate.sh`，验证 pytest 套件未覆盖的四个场景：非空、未被 Alembic 追踪的数据库会拒绝 `alembic upgrade head` 而不是静默冲突；迁移链执行到一半后恢复执行能到达 head，且重复执行 `upgrade head` 是干净的空操作；两次并发的 `alembic upgrade head` 通过新锁串行化而不是相互竞争；迁移前的 `mysqldump` 备份能恢复到全新数据库，且表数与行数一致。这四项均已先在本地 MySQL 8.4（`docker compose up -d mysql`）上验证通过，再接入 CI。
- Deferred to follow-up work under issue #53: the concurrent-lock and partial-DDL-recovery checks above run as ordinary CI script assertions rather than as a fuzzed or long-running chaos test, and the backup/restore drill checks table and row counts rather than a full checksum. See `TODO.md`.
- 遗留后续工作，记录在 issue #53 下：上述并发锁与部分 DDL 恢复检查目前以普通 CI 脚本断言的形式运行，而非模糊测试或长时间混沌测试；备份恢复演练目前比对的是表数与行数，而非完整校验和。详见 `TODO.md`。

### Live end-to-end, concurrency, fault-injection, and coverage gates / 端到端、并发、故障注入与覆盖率门禁

- Added an `e2e-live` CI job and `backend/tests/e2e/run_live_stack.sh`, which migrate a disposable MySQL 8.4, seed per-run accounts with random credentials under `example.com`, start the real API (`backend/tests/e2e/live_server.py`), and run every frontend live-backend suite with `check_no_skips.py` over vitest's JUnit report. Before this, the four live suites (`crypto/mvp`, `crypto/upload`, `crypto/passphrase-reset`, and the new `e2e/live-stack`) skipped themselves on every CI run, so none of the MVP's cross-system behaviour was ever gated. The only substitution in the served app writes verification mail to a temporary directory, because the `console` provider would print codes into a kept CI log.
- 新增 `e2e-live` CI Job 与 `backend/tests/e2e/run_live_stack.sh`：迁移一次性 MySQL 8.4，以 `example.com` 下每次随机生成的凭据创建账户，启动真实 API（`backend/tests/e2e/live_server.py`），运行前端全部依赖真实后端的测试套件，并用 `check_no_skips.py` 检查 vitest 的 JUnit 报告。此前四个真实后端测试套件（`crypto/mvp`、`crypto/upload`、`crypto/passphrase-reset` 与新增的 `e2e/live-stack`）在每次 CI 中都会自行跳过，MVP 的跨系统行为从未被门禁覆盖。所提供的应用只有一处替换：验证码邮件写入临时目录，因为 `console` 提供方会把验证码打印到会被保留的 CI 日志里。
- Added `frontend/src/e2e/live-stack.integration.test.ts`, which needs no seeded users: registration with the emailed code (and refusal of a wrong one), login, refresh rotation and replay revocation, logout, unlock, all four file algorithms, share and revoke, audit visibility, role denial, and account deletion. It drives the browser's own client modules in Node rather than the markup; a real-browser DOM smoke test remains deferred under #58.
- 新增 `frontend/src/e2e/live-stack.integration.test.ts`，无需预先创建用户即可覆盖：邮箱验证码注册（及错误验证码被拒）、登录、refresh 轮换与重放撤销、登出、解锁、全部四种文件算法、分享与撤销、审计可见性、角色拒绝以及注销账户。它在 Node 中驱动浏览器端的客户端模块，而非页面标记；真实浏览器的 DOM 冒烟测试仍作为 #58 的遗留项。
- Replaced an unreviewed draft of the backend suites with `backend/tests/integration/test_concurrency_mysql.py` and `test_fault_injection_mysql.py`. The concurrency tests release real threads on separate connections together against session limits, the login-failure counter, refresh reuse, the audit chain with mid-transaction rollbacks, racing key rewraps, duplicate shares, share-versus-delete, duplicate recovery requests, and recovery-execution-versus-revocation; each was confirmed to fail once the row locks it depends on are removed. The fault tests use real mechanisms: a server `KILL` at COMMIT after publish (then `reconcile.py` removes the orphan), a store that refuses publish or unlink, malformed envelopes, forged signatures, and stale revisions. The draft's teardown revoked every recovery key in the database and has been removed.
- 用 `backend/tests/integration/test_concurrency_mysql.py` 与 `test_fault_injection_mysql.py` 替换了未经审阅的后端测试草稿。并发测试让处于独立连接上的真实线程同时出发，覆盖会话上限、登录失败计数、refresh 重用、带事务中途回滚的审计链、并发的密钥重新包装、重复分享、分享与删除竞争、重复恢复请求，以及恢复执行与撤销竞争；每一项都已确认在去掉其依赖的行锁后会失败。故障测试采用真实机制：发布后在 COMMIT 时由服务端 `KILL` 连接（随后由 `reconcile.py` 清理孤儿对象）、存储拒绝发布或删除、格式错误的信封、伪造签名以及过期 revision。草稿中会撤销数据库内所有恢复密钥的清理逻辑已删除。
- Added reviewed coverage floors a little below measured values: backend non-MySQL 78% (measured 79.91%, branch coverage), backend full suite in `mysql-gate` 82% (84.35%), frontend offline 55/53/52/55% statements/branches/functions/lines (56.86/55.57/54.25/57.50%), and frontend live 62/58/63/63% (64.19/59.91/65.18/65.31%). Every job now uploads JUnit and coverage artifacts, but only after `backend/scripts/check_report_hygiene.py` finds no PEM blocks, session or bearer tokens, non-example addresses, or fixture plaintext in them.
- 新增经审阅、略低于实测值的覆盖率下限：后端不含 MySQL 部分 78%（实测 79.91%，含分支覆盖）、`mysql-gate` 中完整后端测试 82%（84.35%）、前端离线 55/53/52/55%（语句/分支/函数/行，实测 56.86/55.57/54.25/57.50%）、前端真实后端运行 62/58/63/63%（64.19/59.91/65.18/65.31%）。每个 Job 现在都会上传 JUnit 与覆盖率产物，但前提是 `backend/scripts/check_report_hygiene.py` 未在其中发现 PEM 块、会话或 Bearer 令牌、非示例邮箱地址或测试夹具明文。

### Resetting a forgotten encryption passphrase with the key file / 用密钥文件重置遗忘的加密 passphrase

- Added a way back from a forgotten encryption passphrase. Settings now takes the private key file issued at registration, checks it belongs to this account, and sets a new passphrase. Before this, the key file could only unlock the current session; the next login asked for the same forgotten passphrase again, so a user who had lost it had to keep reaching for the file forever. Files, shares and the account key are untouched — the key pair and its id do not change — so nothing is re-encrypted and every existing wrapping keeps working. Part of #54; changing the passphrase while the old one is known and successor-key rotation remain open.
- 新增了遗忘加密 passphrase 后的补救途径。Settings 现在可以接收注册时发放的私钥文件，确认它属于本账户后设置新的 passphrase。此前密钥文件只能解锁当前会话，下次登录仍会要求输入那个已经忘掉的 passphrase，丢失它的用户只能永远依赖这个文件。文件、共享与账户密钥均不受影响——密钥对及其 id 不变——因此无需重新加密任何内容，所有已有的包装都继续有效。这是 #54 的一部分；在记得旧 passphrase 时修改 passphrase，以及后继密钥轮换，仍未完成。
- The reset needs a **new AMK**, not a rewrap. The passphrase protects the AMK, and the AMK seals the private key; forgetting the passphrase loses the AMK, and nothing can recover it. So the browser generates a fresh AMK, wraps it under the new passphrase, and re-encrypts the private key from the file under it. The existing `POST /keys/me/rewrap` cannot do this — it replaces only the AMK envelope and assumes the old AMK is still in hand — so this adds `POST /keys/me/reset-protection` (with its `/signing-payload`), which replaces both envelopes in one transaction. No schema change and no migration: both rows are updated in place and their ids are kept, so the AADs rebuild unchanged.
- 重置需要一个**新的 AMK**，而不是重包装。passphrase 保护 AMK，AMK 封装私钥；忘记 passphrase 就失去了 AMK，而且没有任何办法恢复它。因此浏览器会生成新的 AMK，用新 passphrase 包装它，再用它重新加密来自文件的私钥。现有的 `POST /keys/me/rewrap` 做不到这一点——它只替换 AMK 信封，并假定旧 AMK 仍然可用——所以本次新增了 `POST /keys/me/reset-protection`（及其 `/signing-payload`），在一个事务中替换两个信封。无 Schema 变更、无迁移：两行原地更新并保留原有 id，因此 AAD 的重建结果不变。
- Why letting a key-file holder do this is safe: the private key alone opens every file the account can reach, and the passphrase only protects a stored copy of it. Anyone holding the file can already decrypt everything, so re-sealing it under a new passphrase grants no new capability. `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` previously listed new-AMK reset as out of scope; it now records this reasoning and its limits.
- 为何允许密钥文件持有者这样做是安全的：只有私钥本身能打开账户可访问的每个文件，passphrase 只是保护它存储副本的那一层。持有该文件的人本来就能解密一切，因此在新 passphrase 下重新封装它并不会赋予任何新能力。`docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` 此前把新 AMK 重置列为不在范围内，现已记录这一论证及其边界。
- The server cannot check that the new envelopes open — it never sees the passphrase, the AMK, or the private key — so the reset is accepted only with an RSA-PSS signature by the account private key over a digest covering both envelopes and both expected revisions. Without it, a stolen session alone could overwrite the envelopes with garbage and lock the owner out of every file. Verified against a live backend: a signature from a different key is refused with `422` and leaves both rows unchanged, and a stale revision answers `409`. The digest has its own domain label, so a rewrap or key-addition signature cannot be replayed as a reset.
- 服务器无法检验新信封能否打开——它从不接触 passphrase、AMK 或私钥——因此只有附带账户私钥 RSA-PSS 签名的重置才会被接受，该签名覆盖两个信封及两个预期 revision 的摘要。若没有这一签名，仅凭偷来的会话就能把信封覆盖成垃圾数据，使所有者无法打开任何文件。已对真实后端验证：用另一把密钥做的签名以 `422` 被拒绝，且两行均未改变；过期 revision 返回 `409`。该摘要带有独立的域标签，因此重包装或添加密钥的签名无法被重放为重置。
- Accounts holding more than one key are refused with `409 key_protection_reset_unavailable`. One AMK seals every private-key bundle, so re-sealing with one key file would leave the others — and every file wrapped to them — permanently unopenable. Only `POST /keys/add` gives an account a second key and the browser does not call it yet, so today every account qualifies.
- 持有多把密钥的账户会以 `409 key_protection_reset_unavailable` 被拒绝。同一个 AMK 封装着所有私钥包，仅用一个密钥文件重新封装，会让其余私钥包——以及包装给它们的每个文件——永远无法打开。只有 `POST /keys/add` 会让账户获得第二把密钥，而浏览器目前尚未调用它，所以现在每个账户都符合条件。
- The browser does every check that can fail before anything is sent: the file must parse, must match the registered public key, and the new envelopes must reopen locally to the same bytes. After the server accepts, it fetches the stored envelopes back and opens them again, since what matters is what the next unlock reads. The confirmation step states that the old passphrase stops working, and the key file is cleared from the form on success and when the page is left.
- 浏览器会在发送任何内容之前完成所有可能失败的检查：文件必须能解析、必须与已登记公钥匹配，且新信封必须能在本地重新打开并得到相同的字节。服务器接受之后，浏览器还会取回已存储的信封并再次打开，因为真正重要的是下次解锁读到的内容。确认步骤会明确告知旧 passphrase 将失效，且成功后及离开页面时都会从表单中清除密钥文件。
- `sealAccountKey` now holds the envelope construction that enrolment and reset share, so the code that builds the two envelopes exists once and cannot drift between the two paths. Enrolment output is byte-for-byte as before.
- `sealAccountKey` 现在承载注册与重置共用的信封构造，因此构建两个信封的代码只存在一份，两条路径之间不会出现偏差。注册的输出与之前逐字节相同。
- Tests: backend `282 passed` (9 new, covering proof of possession, the multi-key refusal, stale and foreign bundles, and the digest binding every field); frontend `262 passed` (5 new, against a stand-in server that verifies the PSS signature — mutation-checked by breaking the signature, which fails three of them). A live end-to-end suite, `passphrase-reset.integration.test.ts`, passed against a real backend and MySQL 8.4: upload, reset, old passphrase refused, new passphrase opens, the file uploaded before the reset still decrypts, then reset back. It is skipped unless `E2E_RESET_*` is set.
- 测试：后端 `282 passed`（新增 9 条，覆盖持有证明、多密钥拒绝、过期及不属于本账户的私钥包，以及摘要绑定每个字段）；前端 `262 passed`（新增 5 条，使用会验证 PSS 签名的替身服务端——已通过故意破坏签名做变异检验，其中 3 条随之失败）。实时端到端测试 `passphrase-reset.integration.test.ts` 已对真实后端与 MySQL 8.4 通过：上传、重置、旧 passphrase 被拒、新 passphrase 可解锁、重置前上传的文件仍可解密，最后重置回原状。未设置 `E2E_RESET_*` 时该测试会跳过。

### Upload, download, and sharing feedback / 上传、下载与分享反馈

- Canonicalized only surrounding filename whitespace before browser encryption, while preserving internal spaces and arbitrary MIME types. Files selected from Finder with a hidden leading or trailing space now satisfy the authenticated metadata contract instead of failing after encryption.
- 浏览器加密前仅规范化文件名首尾空白，同时保留中间空格及任意 MIME 类型。从 Finder 选择的文件即使带隐藏的开头或结尾空格，也能满足受认证元数据约束，不再在加密后失败。
- Reduced the encrypted-download action to an icon with the hover label **Download encrypted file**, and added a persistent, non-error progress notice telling users to keep the page open while a large encrypted or decrypted download is in progress.
- 将密文下载操作精简为图标，并在悬停时显示 **Download encrypted file**；大文件密文或解密下载期间新增持续显示的非错误提示，明确提醒用户保持页面开启。
- A stale shared-file row now checks access before asking for a passphrase and turns into a disabled row with a clear red explanation when the owner has revoked access or removed the file. The same handling covers revocation that races with an already-started download.
- 共享文件列表未刷新时，下载前会先检查权限；若所有者已撤销访问或移除文件，该行会禁用并显示明确红色说明，不再先要求输入 passphrase 后无响应。下载已开始时发生撤销也采用相同处理。
- Sharing now unlocks the sender's account key before displaying the recipient form, eliminating duplicate recipient entry. The recipient can be resolved by exact SecureView username or email address; the former username endpoint remains as a deprecated compatibility alias.
- 分享流程现改为先解锁发送方账户密钥，再显示接收人表单，避免重复输入接收人；接收人可通过 SecureView 精确用户名或邮箱地址解析，原用户名端点作为已弃用兼容别名继续保留。

### Homepage screenshot walkthrough / 首页截图操作引导

- Replaced the static How it works cards with a six-step screenshot slideshow captured from the actual upload, file list, share, unlock, and recipient screens using fabricated local data. Numbered outlines, literal button names, and expected outcomes help a first-time visitor follow the product, including re-entering the recipient after unlocking. Autoplay starts at 8 seconds per step; visitors can pause, enlarge images, or use the mobile crops. Playback pauses off-screen and stops at the final step.
- 将静态 How it works 卡片替换为六步截图幻灯片，使用虚构本地数据从实际上传、文件列表、分享、解锁及接收者页面截图。数字定位框、实际按钮名称与预期结果帮助新用户照着操作，也明确说明解锁后需要重新填写接收者。默认每步 8 秒自动播放；用户可暂停、放大图片或使用手机裁切图。离开可视区域暂停，最后一步停止播放。
- Finished the remaining guide polish: dedicated mobile crops for Share and Unlock, autoplay on by default at 8 seconds per step with a visible progress bar (Pause / manual navigation turns it off), checkmarks on completed steps, cross-fade copy/image transitions, keyboard focus on the shell, and swipe between steps on phones. Help now leads with a link to `/#how-it-works` so video placeholders are no longer the only onboarding path.
- 补齐剩余引导打磨：Share/Unlock 独立手机裁切图、默认每步 8 秒自动播放并显示进度条（Pause / 手动切换可关闭）、已完成步骤勾选、文案/图片交叉淡入、幻灯外壳可键盘聚焦，以及手机滑动换步。Help 页优先链到 `/#how-it-works`，视频占位不再是唯一上手路径。
- Added an isolated screenshot refresh script so illustrations can be regenerated when the UI changes. Its backend responses are fixtures and do not constitute integration or live-deployment evidence. This preview is local and can be reverted independently of the Core Features and brand work.
- 新增隔离的截图更新脚本，方便界面变化后重新生成图片。脚本使用模拟后端响应，不作为集成测试或线上部署证据。本版为本地预览，可独立于 Core Features 与品牌改动回退。

### Contact us page with feedback preview / Contact us 页与反馈预览

- Renamed the Development Team nav label to **Contact us**, and placed a demo feedback form (name, email, topic, message) above the team mosaic on `/team`. The form only validates and shows a local success state — nothing is emailed — so the contact flow can be reviewed before a real inbox exists. The roster remains below under “Meet the people”.
- 已将导航标签 Development Team 改为 **Contact us**，并在 `/team` 团队拼贴上方加入演示用反馈表单（姓名、邮箱、主题、留言）。表单仅做校验并显示本地成功状态，不会发信，便于在真实收件箱接通前预览联系流程；团队名单仍保留在下方的 “Meet the people” 区块。

### Help page for product walkthroughs / 产品操作帮助页

- Moved How to Use out of Education into a dedicated `/help` route, and placed a Help nav item between Education and Development Team. Product walkthroughs are not cryptography lessons, and burying them inside Education made both destinations harder to find; Help keeps usage guidance on its own tab while Education stays focused on algorithms, playground, and knowledge.
- 已将 How to Use 从 Education 拆到独立的 `/help` 路由，并在 Education 与 Development Team 之间新增 Help 导航项。产品操作演示不属于密码学课程，埋在 Education 里会让两边都更难找；Help 单独承载使用指引，Education 继续专注算法、演练场与知识库。

### Header navigation wayfinding / 页头导航定位

- Active header tabs now use the theme primary colour, and the logo plus nav labels sit left-aligned with wider spacing. Without a colour change, a signed-in user could not tell Product, Education, Help, and Development Team apart at a glance; the active colour and left cluster make the current destination obvious while keeping actions on the right.
- 当前所在页头标签改为使用主题主色，Logo 与导航标签左对齐并加大间距。原先仅靠字重区分时，登录用户很难一眼分清 Product、Education、Help 与 Development Team；主色高亮加左侧聚合后，当前页面一目了然，右侧操作区保持不动。

### Landing hero and feature marquee polish / 首页主标题与功能滚动条打磨

- Fixed the Core Features loop boundary: three identical groups now move by exactly one group's width, including its trailing gap, inside a bounded viewport. Two groups could expose empty space on wider screens before snapping back. Opposing directions and hover pause remain; reduced-motion mode shows all six features without animated or duplicated cards.
- 修复 Core Features 周期交界处的跳闪：在限定宽度的显示区域内使用三组相同内容，每次精确移动一组宽度（含末尾间距）。原先两组内容在较宽屏幕上可能露出空白后突然补回。保留双向滚动与悬停暂停；减少动态效果模式静态展示全部六项功能，不播放动画或展示重复卡片。

- Simplified the landing hero to a static centred title **"Encryption and Sharing"** (matching the Development Team title treatment) and kept the Core Features dual-row marquee with softer rounded cards, extra vertical room for hover, and a lighter hover shadow. The previous scroll-driven title handoff and clipped hover effects made the first screen harder to scan and hid the card interaction.
- 首页主视觉改为静态居中标题 **"Encryption and Sharing"**（字重与 Development Team 一致），并保留 Core Features 双向横向滚动，卡片更圆润、上下留白更大、悬停阴影更轻。原先滚动切换大标题与被裁切的悬停效果，会让首屏更难扫读，也看不清卡片交互。

CT-1.0 is now accurately described as environment/documentation preparation and CT-1.1 as a baseline audit; implementation begins at CT-1.2. ### Account avatar menu, profile, and settings / 账户头像菜单、个人资料与设置

- Added a circular account avatar in the header for signed-in users. Hovering opens a menu carrying theme selection, Settings, and Log out; clicking the avatar opens the new `/profile` page. The header previously spent its right-hand corner on a standalone theme dropdown plus a bare "Log Out" button, and there was no way at all to see which account you were signed in as — the app knew, and never said.
- 已为登录用户在页头加入圆形账户头像。悬停展开菜单，内含主题选择、设置与登出；点击头像进入新的 `/profile` 页面。此前页头右上角被独立的主题下拉框和一个孤立的 "Log Out" 按钮占据，而且完全无法看出当前登录的是哪个账户——应用知道，却从不说出来。
- Added `/profile` and `/settings`, both behind the auth guard, reading identity from `GET /auth/me` through a new cached `user` in the auth store. The store clears that user on logout, because otherwise the next account to sign in on the same machine would inherit the previous one's name and email in the avatar until the request came back.
- 已新增 `/profile` 与 `/settings` 两个受登录守卫保护的页面，通过 auth store 中新增的带缓存 `user` 状态读取 `GET /auth/me` 的身份信息。登出时会清空该用户，否则同一台机器上下一个登录的账户会在请求返回之前，在头像中沿用上一位用户的用户名与邮箱。
- Made `useTheme` hold its preference at module scope instead of creating a ref per call. The header, the avatar menu, and the settings page are now three controls for one setting; with a ref each, changing the theme in settings would have left the header displaying the value it no longer had.
- 已将 `useTheme` 的偏好设置提升到模块作用域，而不再每次调用都新建 ref。页头、头像菜单与设置页现在是同一项设置的三个入口；若各持一份 ref，在设置页切换主题会让页头继续显示它已经不再持有的值。

### Education algorithms and software demos / 教育页算法与软件演示

- Removed DES/3DES from Algorithm Explorer because NIST withdrew 3DES in 2023, dropped AES-192 from the teaching set for now, and split AES into separate AES-128 and AES-256 topics under an AES family group. Algorithm Explorer now opens with a video/animation frame for how each algorithm works, and Education gains a How to Use tag for SecureView walkthrough clips so learners see both the crypto and the product flow.
- 已从 Algorithm Explorer 移除 DES/3DES（NIST 于 2023 年撤销 3DES），教学集合暂时去掉 AES-192，并将 AES 拆成 AES 分组下的 AES-128 与 AES-256 两个知识点。Algorithm Explorer 内容头部新增算法工作原理的视频/动画展示框，Education 页新增 How to Use 标签用于 SecureView 操作演示，让学习者同时看到密码学与产品流程。

The new explanations connect user actions to the trust and transaction boundaries behind them, making the repository usable as both an operating guide and a technical handover rather than only a setup sheet.

  In progress、1 个 In review、3 个 Ready 及 7 个 Backlog；
  `docs/PROPOSAL_TRACEABILITY.md` 逐项记录证据与尚缺证据。Jason（`jsonrar`）、Junyan

  写成未完成；同时代码已到 revision 0006，文档却仍报告 0004 与八月测试数字。新版本现准确描述

  并带 P0/P1/P2 与功能域标签。每个 Issue 均有明确验收标准；TODO 现在是带链接的优先级索引，
  不再是无人负责的重复 backlog。
- Added a pull-request evidence checklist and repository instructions that require every change
  to review changelog, README/TODO, Issues, tests, migrations, secrets, and deployment impact.
  Runtime work closes only after the exact merged SHA and affected live flow are verified, so

- 已增加 Pull Request 证据清单和仓库规则，要求每次变更检查 changelog、README/TODO、Issues、

  才能关闭，避免再把“已 merge”写成“已部署”。

### Swagger UI for team and supervisor evaluation / 面向团队与导师评审的 Swagger UI

- Fixed the white page that appeared after Swagger was enabled. FastAPI serves a small HTML
  shell whose JavaScript and CSS come from jsDelivr and whose bootstrap is inline; the main
  site's self-only Content Security Policy correctly blocked both. `/docs` and `/redoc` now
  receive a narrowly scoped documentation policy while the file, passphrase, and decryption
  pages keep the strict original policy. All other security headers are repeated explicitly
  because nginx stops inheriting server-level headers when a location defines one.
- 已修复 Swagger 开启后出现的白屏。FastAPI 返回的 HTML 外壳从 jsDelivr 加载 JavaScript/CSS，
  且使用内联脚本启动；主站仅允许同源资源的 CSP 因而正确地拦截了两者。现在仅 `/docs` 与
  `/redoc` 使用范围受限的文档策略，文件、passphrase 与解密页面继续使用原有严格策略。由于
  nginx 在 location 定义任一响应头后不再继承 server 级响应头，文档路由显式重复了其余全部
  安全响应头。
- The public staging deployment now explicitly enables `/docs`, `/redoc`, and
  `/openapi.json` so every team member and the project supervisor can inspect and demonstrate
  the live API without needing a VPS account. HTTPS, nginx request limits, application
  authentication, authorization, and audit controls still apply to requests made from the UI;
  publishing the schema does not grant access to protected data.
- 公网预发布环境现已明确开启 `/docs`、`/redoc` 与 `/openapi.json`，让所有团队成员及项目导师无需
  VPS 账号即可查看并演示线上 API。通过该界面发出的请求仍受 HTTPS、nginx 请求限流、应用登录、
  授权与审计控制约束；公开 schema 并不会授予受保护数据的访问权限。
- The deployment guide now records `APP_DOCS_ENABLED` as an explicit environment decision and
  names `https://secureview.tech/docs` as the canonical presentation URL. This closes the gap
  where the guide promised Swagger while staging deliberately returned 404.
- 部署指南现将 `APP_DOCS_ENABLED` 记录为必须明确决定的环境开关，并把
  `https://secureview.tech/docs` 指定为演示入口，修复了文档声称 Swagger 可用、预发布环境却刻意
  返回 404 的不一致。

### Writing down how a schema change actually reaches the server / 把 Schema 变更如何真正上线写下来

- Documented the manual procedure a release carrying a new Alembic revision needs, in both `DEPLOYMENT.md` files. `mcs21-deploy` never changes the schema — it backs up and verifies read-only — so the step is manual, and its order is forced rather than a matter of taste: `/health/ready` requires the code's `EXPECTED_SCHEMA_REVISION` and the stored marker to be **exactly equal**, so some window of `503` is unavoidable. Pushing the code first makes `verify_database` abort the deploy and quarantine the SHA, and a quarantined SHA is never retried, so every later commit queues behind it. Changing the database first costs a two-to-three minute outage that closes by itself.
- 已在两份 `DEPLOYMENT.md` 中写明：携带新 Alembic revision 的发布需要哪些手动步骤。`mcs21-deploy` 从不修改 Schema——它只做备份与只读核验——因此这一步是手动的，且其顺序是被迫的而非偏好问题：`/health/ready` 要求代码的 `EXPECTED_SCHEMA_REVISION` 与已存标记**精确相等**，所以一段 `503` 窗口无法避免。先推代码会让 `verify_database` 中止部署并隔离该 SHA，而被隔离的 SHA 永不重试，之后的每个提交都会排在它后面。先改数据库则只需承受两到三分钟、会自行关闭的中断。
- The section also covers rendering the SQL from the revision with `alembic upgrade <from>:<to> --sql` rather than hand-writing it, why the pre-check matters when MySQL DDL cannot be rolled back, and why `mcs21-deploy rollback` alone is not a rollback after a schema change — it moves the code and leaves the database, which is the `503` state it was meant to escape. `AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md` now links straight to it, since that is the document someone reads first.
- 该章节还说明了：用 `alembic upgrade <from>:<to> --sql` 从 revision 渲染 SQL 而非手写、在 MySQL DDL 无法回滚的前提下前置检查为何重要，以及为什么 Schema 变更之后单用 `mcs21-deploy rollback` 并不构成回退——它只移动代码而留下数据库，正好落进它本想逃离的 `503` 状态。`AUTOMATIC_DEPLOYMENT.md` 现在直接链接到该章节，因为那才是人们最先读到的文档。
- Migration guards now skip their queries when Alembic renders SQL offline. `op.get_bind()` has no connection in that mode, so the guard added in `0006` broke `alembic upgrade --sql` outright — which the release gate depends on. `tests/unit/test_migration_metadata.py` caught it; `0005` carried the same latent fault in its downgrade and is fixed alongside.
- 迁移中的保护性查询现在会在 Alembic 离线渲染 SQL 时跳过执行。该模式下 `op.get_bind()` 没有连接，因此 `0006` 中新增的保护直接破坏了 `alembic upgrade --sql`——而发布门禁依赖它。`tests/unit/test_migration_metadata.py` 捕获了这个问题；`0005` 的降级路径存在同样的潜在缺陷，已一并修复。

### A second encryption construction, not a fourth key length / 第二种加密构造，而非第四种密钥长度

- Added `ChaCha20-Poly1305` as a file encryption option beside the three AES-GCM strengths. It is a genuinely different construction — a stream cipher authenticated by Poly1305, where AES-GCM is a block cipher authenticated by GHASH — so the two rest on different assumptions and fail differently. It is often faster on devices with no AES hardware acceleration, which is the practical reason to offer it rather than a fourth key length.
- 已在三种 AES-GCM 强度之外新增 `ChaCha20-Poly1305` 作为文件加密选项。它是一种真正不同的构造——由 Poly1305 认证的流密码，而 AES-GCM 是由 GHASH 认证的分组密码——两者基于不同的假设，失效方式也不同。在没有 AES 硬件加速的设备上它通常更快，这正是提供它而非再加一种密钥长度的现实理由。
- WebCrypto has no ChaCha20-Poly1305; its only AEAD is AES-GCM. `@noble/ciphers` supplies it, chosen for having zero transitive dependencies, being audited, and being pure TypeScript with no WASM. The path costs about 4.7 KB gzipped. AES-CBC and AES-CTR are available in the platform and were not used: they are unauthenticated, and either one would discard the integrity guarantee the whole envelope rests on.
- WebCrypto 不提供 ChaCha20-Poly1305，它唯一的 AEAD 是 AES-GCM。该算法由 `@noble/ciphers` 提供，选择它的理由是零传递依赖、经过审计、纯 TypeScript 且不含 WASM，体积代价约为 gzip 后 4.7 KB。平台自带的 AES-CBC 与 AES-CTR 未被采用：它们不带认证，用其中任何一个都会丢弃整个信封所依赖的完整性保证。
- No envelope or AAD version bump, and no format change. The algorithm name was already framed into AAD-3, and RFC 8439 specifies the same 12-byte nonce and 16-byte tag AES-GCM uses, so the stored columns, the length half of the CHECK constraint, and every byte of the layout are untouched. Files already uploaded are unaffected.
- 未提升信封版本或 AAD 版本，也未改变格式。算法名此前就已被 frame 进 AAD-3，而 RFC 8439 规定的 nonce 与 tag 长度（12 与 16 字节）与 AES-GCM 相同，因此存储列、CHECK 约束中的长度部分以及布局的每一个字节都无需改动。已上传的文件不受影响。
- `AES-256-GCM` and `ChaCha20-Poly1305` both take a 32-byte key and a 12-byte nonce and produce a 16-byte tag, so **no structural check distinguishes them and the AAD binding is the only thing that does**. Two tests pin this in both directions: a file written under either name must fail authentication when opened as the other. Without them, dropping the algorithm from the AAD would leave every existing test passing.
- `AES-256-GCM` 与 `ChaCha20-Poly1305` 都使用 32 字节密钥、12 字节 nonce，并产生 16 字节 tag，因此**没有任何结构性检查能区分它们，唯一能区分的就是 AAD 绑定**。两条测试从两个方向固定了这一点：以任一名称写出的文件，被当作另一种打开时必须认证失败。若没有它们，把算法名从 AAD 中移除仍会让现有测试全部通过。
- Fixed a pre-existing weakness in `ck_files_encryption_envelope` while rewriting it: the column collates as `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`, so the name comparison was case-insensitive and a row could hold `aes-256-gcm`. That was never reachable through the API, whose `Literal` is exact, but the constraint read stronger than it behaved, and a row that did slip through would be refused by the browser at download time because the client's check *is* exact. The comparison now runs under `utf8mb4_bin`, and the upgrade refuses with a count rather than a bare error 3819 if any miscased row exists.
- 在重写 `ck_files_encryption_envelope` 时顺带修复了一个既有弱点：该列的排序规则是 `utf8mb4_unicode_ci`，因此算法名比较对大小写不敏感，`aes-256-gcm` 这样的值也能存入。通过 API 永远走不到这一步（其 `Literal` 是精确匹配），但这条约束读起来比它实际的行为更强；而真的漏进去的行会在下载时被浏览器拒绝，因为客户端的检查**是**精确的。现在比较在 `utf8mb4_bin` 下进行，且升级时若存在大小写不符的行，会给出数量并拒绝执行，而不是抛出一个光秃秃的 3819 错误。

### Choosing the AES key length / 选择 AES 密钥长度

- Made the file encryption algorithm a real choice: `AES-128-GCM`, `AES-192-GCM`, or `AES-256-GCM`, with AES-256 still the default. The upload page had carried a selector offering `AES-128 / AES-192 / AES-256 / 3DES` that changed nothing at all — it was removed rather than honoured, because 3DES has a 64-bit block, is broken by Sweet32, and was withdrawn by NIST in 2023. The three that remain are one construction at three key lengths, so they share a nonce length, a tag length, and every byte of the envelope layout.
- 文件加密算法现在是一个真实的选择：`AES-128-GCM`、`AES-192-GCM` 或 `AES-256-GCM`，默认仍为 AES-256。上传页此前那个 `AES-128 / AES-192 / AES-256 / 3DES` 下拉框完全不影响实际加密，当时选择删除而非实现它，因为 3DES 分组只有 64 位、已被 Sweet32 攻破，并已于 2023 年被 NIST 废止。保留的三者是同一构造的三种密钥长度，因此 nonce 长度、tag 长度以及信封布局的每一个字节都相同。
- The algorithm name was already framed into AAD-3, so this needed no envelope version bump and no format change: ciphertext written under one algorithm cannot be relabelled as another without the tag failing. `decryptFile` now also refuses a DEK whose length disagrees with the algorithm the metadata names, turning what would surface as an opaque tag mismatch — indistinguishable from tampered ciphertext — into a statement of what is actually wrong.
- 算法名此前就已经被 frame 进 AAD-3，因此本次无需提升信封版本、也不改变格式：某一算法下写出的密文无法被重新标注为另一种而不导致 tag 校验失败。`decryptFile` 现在还会拒绝长度与元数据所声明算法不符的 DEK，把原本表现为「tag 校验失败」（与密文被篡改无法区分）的情况，变成一条说明真实问题的报错。
- The browser is probed for what it can actually do before the menu is drawn. AES-192 is specified but absent from some WebCrypto implementations, and an unsupported key length fails at encryption time with a bare `OperationError` — after the user has chosen it and picked their files. AES-256 is required rather than probed: without it there is no default to fall back to.
- 菜单在绘制之前会先探测浏览器实际支持的算法。AES-192 虽有规范，但部分 WebCrypto 实现并未提供，而不受支持的密钥长度会在加密时以一个光秃秃的 `OperationError` 失败——此时用户已经选好算法并选好文件。AES-256 是必需项而非探测项：没有它就没有可回退的默认值。
- Widened `ck_files_encryption_envelope` as revision `0005_selectable_aes_key_length`. Only the algorithm name widened; the nonce and tag length checks are unchanged, because all three algorithms use 12 and 16 bytes. The account key envelopes — the wrapped AMK and the encrypted private-key bundle — stay AES-256-GCM and their constraints are untouched: they record no algorithm a reader could disagree with, so there is nothing to select and nothing to bind. Downgrading refuses while any AES-128 or AES-192 file exists, rather than dropping rows that are perfectly decryptable.
- 已以 revision `0005_selectable_aes_key_length` 放宽 `ck_files_encryption_envelope`。只放宽了算法名；nonce 与 tag 的长度检查未变，因为三种算法都是 12 和 16 字节。账户密钥信封（包装后的 AMK 与加密私钥包）仍为 AES-256-GCM，其约束未作改动：它们不记录任何读取方可能产生分歧的算法名，因此既无从选择，也无需绑定。只要还存在 AES-128 或 AES-192 的文件，降级就会拒绝执行，而不是删掉那些完全可解密的数据行。
- Added AES-128-GCM and AES-192-GCM known-answer vectors to `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json`, generated with python-cryptography rather than by calling the browser code. This is the check that matters most: the offline recovery path is Python, and an algorithm the recovery script cannot decrypt would produce files nobody can ever get back. The suite 1 vectors are unchanged.
- 已向 `docs/crypto/test_vectors.json` 增加 AES-128-GCM 与 AES-192-GCM 的已知答案向量，由 python-cryptography 生成，而非调用浏览器代码产生。这是最关键的一道检查：离线恢复路径是 Python 实现的，一个恢复脚本无法解密的算法会产生永远拿不回来的文件。suite 1 的原有向量未作改动。

### Header theme control and brand marks / 页头主题控件与品牌标识

- Unified the site's header/footer logo and standalone brand icon with the outlined favicon: a white shield, black border and eye, and transparent surroundings. Kept the wordmark theme-aware so its lettering remains readable on light and dark pages.
- 将网站页头、页脚 Logo 及独立品牌图标统一为与 favicon 一致的黑边白色盾牌、黑色眼形和透明外围；字标继续跟随明暗主题，保证文字在两种页面背景上均清晰可读。

- Changed only the browser favicon to a white shield with a black outline, black eye details, and a white keyhole on a transparent background. Versioned its HTML reference so browsers request the new icon instead of reusing the cached version; page logos keep their existing colours.
- 仅将浏览器标签页图标改为透明背景的黑边白色盾牌，保留黑色眼形细节与白色钥匙孔。为 HTML 图标引用增加版本参数，让浏览器请求新版而非复用缓存版本；页面内 Logo 保持现有配色。

- Refined the existing shield, eye, and keyhole logo with softer shield corners, more balanced eye spacing, and a single keyhole silhouette. Synchronized the wordmark symbol, standalone icon, and blue favicon while retaining outlined lettering and theme-aware colour so the identity remains consistent at small sizes and in both themes.
- 微调现有盾牌、眼睛与钥匙孔标识：盾牌边角更圆润，眼形留白更均衡，钥匙孔统一为一体轮廓。同步完整字标中的符号、独立图标及蓝色 favicon，并保留矢量文字与主题跟随颜色，使小尺寸及明暗主题下的品牌形象保持一致。

- Replaced the binary Light/Dark header toggle with a System / Light / Dark dropdown whose
  trigger stays a fixed width, so choosing an option cannot shove neighbouring Sign In /
  Get Started controls sideways. System follows `prefers-color-scheme` and updates when the
  OS theme changes.
- 将页头二态 Light/Dark 切换改为 System / Light / Dark 下拉，触发器宽度固定，选择任一项都不会把
  旁边的 Sign In / Get Started 挤歪。System 跟随 `prefers-color-scheme`，并在系统主题变化时更新。
- Header chrome text is black in Light and white in Dark so links stay readable on the
  translucent bar; primary CTAs keep their existing colours.
- 页头导航文字在 Light 下为黑、Dark 下为白，保证半透明栏上仍可读；主色 CTA 配色不变。
- Header and footer now show the SecureView wordmark SVG (inlined for `currentColor` theme
  tracking). The tab favicon is a permanent brand-blue shield (`#006DC7`) rather than a
  theme-tinted mark, because favicons cannot inherit page colour and a fixed blue stays
  recognisable in every theme. Detail: [`JY-changelog.md`](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/blob/50e15cf4a37544f0897c25c7091c3333cd26a528/changelog/JY-changelog.md).
- 页头与页脚改为展示 SecureView 字标 SVG（内联以跟随 `currentColor` 主题色）。标签页图标为固定
  品牌蓝盾牌（`#006DC7`），不再随主题着色——favicon 无法继承页面颜色，固定蓝色在任何主题下都更
  可辨认。明细见 [`JY-changelog.md`](https://github.com/jsonrar/fyp-secure-file-platform/blob/50e15cf4a37544f0897c25c7091c3333cd26a528/changelog/JY-changelog.md)。

### Totals that are actually totals / 让统计数字真的是总数

- The four figures on the records page summarised **one page** of the trail while looking like totals of everything. `GET /audit-logs` defaults to 50 rows and the page never asked for more, so on a test account holding 83 rows it reported four tracked files, twelve downloads and eight shares — against a true eight, nineteen and thirteen. Roughly half, presented as the whole.
- 记录页上的四个数字统计的是轨迹的**一页**，看上去却像是全部的总数。`GET /audit-logs` 默认返回 50 条，而页面从未要求更多，因此在一个有 83 条记录的测试账号上，它报告了 4 个文件、12 次下载、8 次分享——而真实值是 8、19、13。大约只有一半，却以全部的姿态呈现。
- Sign-ins made it worse. Twenty-two of those first fifty rows were `AUTH_LOGIN_SUCCEEDED`, so ordinary use of the site steadily pushed the file activity the page exists to show out of the window — which is why the figure shrank between two screenshots of the same account.
- 登录记录让情况更糟。那前 50 条里有 22 条是 `AUTH_LOGIN_SUCCEEDED`，因此对站点的日常使用会持续把这个页面本该展示的文件活动挤出窗口——这也是同一个账号的两张截图之间这个数字会变小的原因。
- The page now walks every page of the trail, bounded at 1000 rows so an account used for months does not issue fifty requests to draw four numbers. When that bound is reached it says the totals cover the most recent events, rather than under-reporting in silence.
- 页面现在会遍历轨迹的每一页，上限 1000 条，以免一个用了几个月的账号为了画四个数字发出五十次请求。达到上限时会明确说明统计仅覆盖最近的事件，而不是默不作声地少报。
- The walk lives in `utils/audit-trail.ts` with its own tests, because "a number that looks right and is not" is exactly the failure this page keeps producing, and it is not observable from the markup.
- 该遍历逻辑放在 `utils/audit-trail.ts` 并附带自己的测试，因为"看起来对、其实不对的数字"正是这个页面反复出现的失效方式，而它从 markup 上是看不出来的。

### Who may read the audit trail / 谁有权读取审计记录

Reported from a live test: a recipient could read the whole history of a shared file, and the owner's own Access Records reported zero downloads. Both came from the same place — the rule deciding which rows a caller may see.

来自一次真实测试的反馈：接收方能读取共享文件的完整历史，而所有者自己的访问记录却显示零次下载。两者出自同一处——决定调用方能看到哪些记录的那条规则。

- **A recipient could see what other recipients did.** The file-scoped query skipped the visibility filter entirely, on the reasoning that entitlement had already been checked. But entitlement is not ownership: a recipient holds an ACTIVE grant, so any recipient could read the whole trail — learning that other recipients existed and when each of them downloaded, and continuing to read it after the owner revoked someone else. Owning the file is what earns the right to see who touched it.
- **接收方能看到其他接收方的操作。** 按文件查询时完全跳过了可见性过滤，理由是"权限已经校验过了"。但**有权限不等于是所有者**：接收方持有一个有效授权，因此任何接收方都能读取完整轨迹——从而得知还存在哪些其他接收方、各自何时下载过，甚至在所有者撤销了别人之后仍能继续读到。是"拥有这个文件"才换来"看得见谁碰过它"的权利。
- **An owner saw zero downloads of their own files.** The account-wide query matched only rows where the caller was the actor or the target, and a recipient's download is neither. The owner was blind to exactly the thing the page exists to show.
- **所有者看到自己文件的下载数为零。** 账户级查询只匹配调用方作为操作者或被操作者的记录，而接收方的下载两者都不是。于是所有者恰恰看不见这个页面存在的意义所在。
- Both are now one rule, applied identically whether or not a file is named: **you see what you did, what was done to you, and everything done to a file you own.** An administrator is still unrestricted.
- 现在两者合并为一条规则，且无论是否指定文件都同样适用：**你能看到你做过的、别人对你做的、以及对你拥有的文件所做的一切。** 管理员仍不受限制。
- **"Total Views" was not counting views.** There is no viewer in this product — you download and decrypt, or you do not — so the stat counted every non-download event as one: uploads, shares, revocations, even sign-ins. It is replaced by Downloads and Shares, which are things that actually happened, and each file row now shows its download count and how many people appear in its history.
- **"Total Views" 统计的并不是浏览。** 这个产品里没有"查看器"——你要么下载并解密，要么不——因此该统计把所有非下载事件都算作一次浏览：上传、分享、撤销，甚至登录。现已替换为 Downloads 与 Shares 这两个真实发生过的动作，并且每一行文件会显示它的下载次数以及历史中出现过多少人。
- The privacy assertion is written as the rule itself: **every row a recipient can see must be something they did or something done to them.** A first attempt asserted that the sender's name must not appear at all, which is too strong — "waldo shared this with you" and "waldo revoked your access" name the sender as the actor and are the recipient's to read, because they are about the recipient. What must not appear is a third party's activity: another recipient's download, or the owner's own upload.
- 隐私断言直接写成规则本身：**接收方能看到的每一条记录，必须是他做的、或是别人对他做的。** 第一版断言写成"发送方的名字完全不能出现"，这过强了——"waldo 把文件分享给你"和"waldo 撤销了你的权限"都以发送方为操作者，但它们讲的是接收方，本就该让他看到。真正不该出现的是第三方的活动：另一个接收方的下载，或所有者自己的上传。
- The end-to-end suite gained both cases. Run against the deployment before the fix, they failed exactly as reported — the recipient saw the owner's actions, and the owner counted zero recipient downloads.
- 端到端测试套件补上了这两种情况。在修复前对着线上运行，它们的失败方式与反馈完全一致——接收方看到了所有者的操作，所有者数到的接收方下载次数为零。

### A presentable notification email / 像样的通知邮件

- The share notification is now a designed message rather than a stack of bare paragraphs: a branded header, the one sentence that matters set apart in a callout, a real button, and a muted footer. `email_templates.py` holds the shell so the verification-code email can adopt the same one later.
- 分享通知现在是一封经过设计的邮件，而不是一堆光秃秃的段落：品牌化的页头、把最要紧的那句话单独放进强调框、一个真正的按钮、以及低调的页脚。外层结构放在 `email_templates.py`，验证码邮件日后可以套用同一套。
- Built the way email actually has to be built: tables for layout because Outlook renders through Word, styles inline because Gmail strips most of a `<style>` block, and no images at all — a blocked remote image leaves a hole, and a tracking pixel is not something this product should be sending. A preheader gives the inbox a real preview line instead of a fragment of markup.
- 按邮件真正需要的方式构建：布局用表格，因为 Outlook 通过 Word 渲染；样式内联，因为 Gmail 会剥掉大部分 `<style>` 块；完全不用图片——被拦截的远程图片会留下一个空洞，而追踪像素不是这个产品该发的东西。preheader 让收件箱显示一句真正的预览文字，而不是一段markup 碎片。
- The plain-text alternative says the same thing rather than being a formality. Some clients show it, some people prefer it, and it is what a screen reader reaches first.
- 纯文本版本说的是同样的内容，不是走个形式。有些客户端只显示它，有些人偏好它，而且屏幕阅读器最先读到的就是它。
- Usernames are escaped into the markup and collapsed to a single line in the subject. A username is only length-constrained — there is no character restriction — so unescaped it is a way to put arbitrary HTML into a message the platform sends on someone else's behalf, and a newline in it has no business in a header. Both were live defects caught by writing the tests, not by reading the code: the preheader had been interpolating the raw name.
- 用户名在写入 markup 时会转义，在主题行中会被折叠为单行。用户名只有长度限制、没有字符限制——因此不转义就等于给了一条途径，把任意 HTML 放进平台代他人发出的邮件里；而其中的换行符更不该出现在邮件头中。这两处都是真实缺陷，且是写测试时发现的、不是读代码看出来的：preheader 此前一直在插入未转义的原始用户名。

### Telling someone a file was shared with them / 告知接收方有文件分享给了他

- There had been no notification of any kind. A recipient learned about a share only by opening their file list and noticing something new — which, for a file shared once and never mentioned in person, means never.
- 此前没有任何形式的通知。接收方只能通过打开文件列表、注意到多了个东西才知道有人分享给了他——而对于一个分享过一次、又没人当面提起的文件来说，这等于永远不知道。
- The file list badges how many shared files this browser has not shown yet, cleared when the shared list is opened. A file that stops being shared drops out of the marker, so re-sharing it counts as new again, and signing out clears it so the next person at that machine does not inherit it.
- 文件列表会标记有多少个分享文件是这个浏览器尚未展示过的，打开分享列表后即清除。不再被分享的文件会从标记中移除，因此重新分享会再次算作"新的"；登出会清空标记，使同一台机器上的下一个人不会继承它。
- Optional email, off by default (`SHARE_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL`), so a deployment without working mail does not queue messages nobody receives. It is sent **after** the share has committed and swallows its own delivery failures: a share that succeeded must not be reported as failed, or rolled back, because a mail server was unreachable.
- 可选的邮件通知，默认关闭（`SHARE_NOTIFICATION_EMAIL`），以免在邮件不通的部署里堆积无人接收的消息。它在分享**提交之后**才发送，并自行吞掉投递失败：一次已经成功的分享，不应该因为邮件服务器不可达就被报告为失败，更不该被回滚。
- Share notifications are capped per day (`SHARE_NOTIFICATION_DAILY_CAP`, 40) so they cannot crowd out verification codes. Both draw on the same provider allowance, and they are not equally important: a verification code that fails to send blocks registration and password reset outright, while a missed share notification costs the recipient one glance at their file list. If the count cannot be established the email is skipped rather than sent on a guess — the safe direction is the one that protects code delivery. For scale: the audit log records nine verification emails across the project's entire history against a 100/day, 3000/month allowance.
- 分享通知设有每日上限（`SHARE_NOTIFICATION_DAILY_CAP`，40），以免挤占验证码的配额。两者共用同一份服务商额度，但重要性并不对等：验证码发不出去会直接阻断注册与密码重置，而漏掉一封分享通知只是让接收方多看一眼文件列表。若无法确定当日已发数量，则跳过邮件而不是凭猜测发送——安全的方向是保护验证码的送达。给个量级参照：审计日志显示整个项目历史上共发出九封验证码邮件，而额度是每天 100 封、每月 3000 封。
- **The filename is deliberately not in the email.** The platform's threat model accepts that the server holds filenames, but an email leaves that boundary entirely — it sits in a third-party mailbox, gets indexed, and is forwarded. Naming the sender is enough to prompt the recipient to go and look; naming the file would move metadata somewhere the design never accounted for. The message does say the recipient opens it with their own passphrase, which is the single most confusing thing about this product for a first-time recipient.
- **邮件中有意不包含文件名。** 本平台的威胁模型接受服务端持有文件名这一事实，但邮件完全离开了那个边界——它躺在第三方邮箱里、会被索引、会被转发。写明分享者已足以促使接收方去查看；写明文件名则会把元数据带到设计从未考虑过的地方。邮件正文会说明接收方用自己的 passphrase 打开它，这是初次接收分享的人最容易困惑的一点。

### Taking a share back / 收回已经分享出去的权限

- The share dialog now lists who currently holds the file and can revoke each of them. `GET /files/{id}/shared-users` and `POST /files/{id}/revoke` both existed and neither was ever called, so a share was invisible and irreversible from the interface: no way to see who held a file, and no way to take it back short of calling the API by hand.
- 分享对话框现在会列出当前持有该文件的人，并可以逐个撤销。`GET /files/{id}/shared-users` 与 `POST /files/{id}/revoke` 两个接口早已存在且从未被调用过，因此一次分享在界面上既不可见也不可逆：既看不到谁持有这个文件，也无法收回——除非手工调用 API。
- The owner's own grant is not offered for revocation; the server refuses it as the canonical owner, and showing the button would promise something it will not do.
- 所有者自己的授权不提供撤销按钮；服务端会以"规范所有者"为由拒绝，把按钮摆出来等于承诺一件它不会做的事。
- The dialog states what revoking does and does not do: it stops further access and removes the file from that person's list, and it cannot recover a copy they have already downloaded and decrypted. `docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` has always said so; the interface now says it at the moment it matters.
- 对话框明确说明撤销做什么、不做什么：它会阻止后续访问并把文件从对方的列表中移除，但无法收回对方已经下载并解密的副本。`docs/SECURITY_FLOWS.md` 一直是这么写的；现在界面会在真正需要知道的那一刻说出来。
- Sharing no longer closes the dialog. The list of holders is the confirmation that it worked, and closing the dialog would hide the thing that just changed.
- 分享成功后不再关闭对话框。持有者列表本身就是"分享成功了"的确认，关掉对话框反而会把刚刚发生变化的那部分藏起来。

### Who opened this file, and when / 谁在什么时候打开了这个文件

- Every file row now offers its access history: who did what to it, and at what time, rather than a count. `GET /audit-logs/file/{id}` authorizes against the live entitlement and then returns the complete trail for that file **including events performed by other people** — the account-wide listing deliberately does not, returning only rows where the caller is the actor or the target. Nothing in the app had ever called it, so "who downloaded the file I shared" was recorded in full and unreadable.
- 现在每一行文件都可以查看它的访问记录：谁、在什么时间、做了什么，而不只是一个次数。`GET /audit-logs/file/{id}` 会先校验当前的有效授权，然后返回该文件的完整轨迹，**包含其他人执行的操作**——账户级的列表接口有意不这样做，它只返回调用者作为操作者或被操作者的记录。此前应用里从未调用过这个接口，因此"我分享出去的文件被谁下载了"被完整记录着，却无法读取。
- The owner therefore sees the recipients' downloads with timestamps; a recipient opening the same view sees the same trail for a file they legitimately hold, which is what the server has always been willing to answer.
- 因此所有者可以看到接收方的下载记录及其时间；接收方打开同一视图时，看到的是他确实持有的那个文件的同一份轨迹——这本来就是服务端一直愿意回答的内容。
- Separators live inside the text rather than between elements. Vue trims whitespace between elements, which silently ran "Downloaded" and "137.36 KB" together into one word.
- 分隔符放在文本内部，而不是元素之间。Vue 会裁剪元素之间的空白，这会悄无声息地把 "Downloaded" 和 "137.36 KB" 连成一个词。

### Files shared with you are no longer disguised as your own / 别人分享给你的文件不再伪装成你自己的

- The file list now separates "My Files" from "Shared with me". `GET /files` returns both in one list and has always marked which is which with an `owner` boolean — the page simply never read it, so a file someone else owned and shared sat in "My Encrypted Files" looking exactly like one you had uploaded, as though you owned and managed it.
- 文件列表现在把「My Files」与「Shared with me」分开。`GET /files` 本来就把两者放在同一个列表返回，并一直用 `owner` 布尔字段标明区别——只是页面从未读取过它，因此别人拥有并分享给你的文件就躺在「My Encrypted Files」里，看起来和你自己上传的一模一样，仿佛由你拥有和管理。
- A shared row names who shared it. `FileSummaryOut` gained `owner_username`, eager-loaded with the file so a page of a hundred rows does not become a hundred extra queries. "Shared with me" is not much use without saying who by.
- 分享来的行会写明分享者。`FileSummaryOut` 新增 `owner_username`，与文件一起预加载，因此一页一百行不会变成一百次额外查询。「Shared with me」不写明是谁分享的就没什么用。
- Share and delete are hidden on a file you do not own. A recipient's grant carries `can_share=false` and `can_manage=false` — both are hardcoded for non-owner grants — so those buttons could only ever produce a 404. Offering them promised something the server was always going to refuse.
- 对于不属于你的文件，分享与删除按钮会隐藏。接收方的授权中 `can_share=false`、`can_manage=false`——这两项对非所有者授权是硬编码的——因此这些按钮只可能得到 404。把它们摆出来，等于承诺一件服务端注定会拒绝的事。

### Staging hardening / 预发布环境加固

- Added nginx rate limits for secureview.tech. The application already throttles authentication — ten failures per account and fifty per IP inside fifteen minutes — but every attempt below that threshold still costs a database transaction and a full Argon2id verification at 64 MiB, which turns a deliberately expensive password hash into an amplifier. Credential routes now allow twelve requests a minute with a burst of eight; the rest of the API allows ten a second. Verified: nine requests pass and the tenth onward answer 429.
- 已为 secureview.tech 增加 nginx 限流。应用层本就对认证做了限流——十五分钟内每账户十次失败、每 IP 五十次——但在触发阈值之前，每一次尝试仍要付出一次数据库事务和一次 64 MiB 的完整 Argon2id 校验，这会把一个刻意设计得昂贵的密码哈希变成放大器。凭证类路由现在限制为每分钟十二次、突发八次；其余 API 为每秒十次。实测：前九个请求通过，第十个起返回 429。
- Added a fail2ban jail for this site. There was none: both nginx jails on this host read `monash-tracker.error.log`, which belongs to a different project. Twenty rejected credentials from one address in ten minutes now earns an hour's ban. The site also gained its own access log, so the filter reads this site's traffic rather than every site on the host.
- 已为本站增加 fail2ban jail。此前完全没有：这台主机上的两个 nginx jail 读的都是 `monash-tracker.error.log`，那属于另一个项目。现在同一地址十分钟内二十次凭证被拒即封禁一小时。本站同时有了独立的 access log，使过滤器只读本站流量，而不是主机上所有站点的流量。
- Added `Content-Security-Policy` and `X-Frame-Options: DENY`. The page holds decrypted file contents and a passphrase box, so it must never be framed. **`script-src` includes `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`, and must**: Argon2id comes from `hash-wasm`, so without it `WebAssembly.instantiate` is refused and registration, unlocking and every passphrase operation fail. A first attempt at the policy omitted it and did exactly that. `'unsafe-inline'` is allowed for styles only, because Vue writes inline styles for transitions.
- 已增加 `Content-Security-Policy` 与 `X-Frame-Options: DENY`。页面上存在解密后的文件内容和 passphrase 输入框，因此绝不能被嵌入 iframe。**`script-src` 包含 `'wasm-unsafe-eval'`，且必须包含**：Argon2id 来自 `hash-wasm`，缺了它 `WebAssembly.instantiate` 会被拒绝，注册、解锁以及所有 passphrase 操作全部失败。第一版策略漏掉了它，后果正是如此。`'unsafe-inline'` 仅对样式开放，因为 Vue 会为过渡动画写入内联样式。
- `APP_DOCS_ENABLED` now controls whether `/docs`, `/redoc` and `/openapi.json` are mounted. It defaults to true, because the schema costs the team nothing locally and is useful while building; it is set to false on the public staging host, where an anonymous visitor could otherwise read a complete, machine-readable list of every route, its parameters, and the exact shape each one accepts. Disabling un-mounts the routes, so they answer 404 rather than 403 — the absence does not confirm there is something to find.
- `APP_DOCS_ENABLED` 现在控制 `/docs`、`/redoc` 与 `/openapi.json` 是否挂载。默认为 true，因为本地开发时这份 schema 不产生任何成本且很有用；在公网可访问的预发布主机上设为 false——否则任何匿名访问者都能读到一份完整、机器可读的路由清单，包含每个接口的参数及其精确结构。禁用时路由会被整个卸载，因此返回 404 而非 403——这个"不存在"本身不会确认背后有东西可找。
- Checked and found already correct, recorded so nobody has to check twice: ufw denies inbound by default with only 80/443 and the SSH port open; SSH is key-only on a non-standard port with root restricted to keys; MySQL and the backend bind to localhost only; the backend runs as the unprivileged `fypapp` user; TLS is 1.2/1.3 with HSTS; unattended-upgrades is enabled. The pending OpenSSL security update was applied and nginx restarted onto it.
- 检查后确认本就正确、记录在此以免重复排查：ufw 默认拒绝入站，仅开放 80/443 与 SSH 端口；SSH 仅密钥登录且位于非标准端口，root 亦限制为密钥；MySQL 与后端仅绑定 localhost；后端以非特权用户 `fypapp` 运行；TLS 为 1.2/1.3 并启用 HSTS；unattended-upgrades 已启用。已应用待处理的 OpenSSL 安全更新并重启 nginx 使其生效。

### The audit page reads the API it actually has / 审计页改用真实的接口契约

- The records page had been reading fields the API has never sent. `AuditLogOut` in the browser declared `log_id`, `file_id`, `actor_username`, `device` and `location`; the server sends `action`, `resource_type`, `resource_id`, `actor_user_id`, `target_user_id`, `result`, `description`, `metadata`, `created_at`. Every one of the invented fields was optional, so type checking was satisfied and the page silently read `undefined` for all of them: every row collapsed into one nameless group, every actor rendered as "Unknown user", and the device and location columns were permanently "Unknown". The audit trail was on screen and told the reader nothing. The type now mirrors the server exactly.
- 访问记录页此前一直在读取接口从未发送过的字段。浏览器侧的 `AuditLogOut` 声明了 `log_id`、`file_id`、`actor_username`、`device`、`location`；而服务端发送的是 `action`、`resource_type`、`resource_id`、`actor_user_id`、`target_user_id`、`result`、`description`、`metadata`、`created_at`。那些凭空捏造的字段全都是可选的，因此类型检查毫无怨言，页面则默默地把它们全部读成 `undefined`：所有记录塌缩进同一个无名分组，所有操作者都显示为"Unknown user"，设备与位置两列则永远是"Unknown"。审计记录就摆在屏幕上，却什么也没告诉读者。现在该类型与服务端逐字段对齐。
- This also means the filename lookup added in the previous entry never fired: it read `file_id` on every row, got `undefined`, and resolved nothing. A row's subject is `resource_type` plus `resource_id`, and that is what the grouping uses now — with `SHARE` rows filed under the file they are about, since a share belongs in that file's history.
- 这同时意味着上一条记录里新增的文件名查询从未真正生效：它在每一行上读取 `file_id`，得到 `undefined`，因而什么也没解析出来。一条记录的主体是 `resource_type` 加 `resource_id`，现在的分组正是依此进行——并且 `SHARE` 记录会归入它所涉及的那个文件，因为一次共享属于该文件的历史。
- Account-level events — signing in, changing a password, closing an account — belong to no file and now appear under their own "Account activity" heading instead of being grouped beneath a file id they never had. They also carry proper labels rather than falling to the title-case fallback: "Signed in", not "Auth login succeeded".
- 账户级事件——登录、修改密码、注销账户——不属于任何文件，现在归入独立的「Account activity」标题下，而不再被塞进一个它们从来没有的文件 id 之下。它们同样获得了正式的标签，而不是退化到首字母大写的兜底格式："Signed in"，而不是"Auth login succeeded"。
- `AuditLogOut` gained `actor_username` and `target_username`. Both `User` rows are already joined and in hand in the router, so this costs no extra query — and without them the page can only say "someone did this", which is the one thing an audit trail must not say. A share now reads "Shared with junyan".
- `AuditLogOut` 新增了 `actor_username` 与 `target_username`。两个 `User` 行本来就已经在路由里 join 好并持有，因此这不产生任何额外查询——而缺了它们，页面只能说"有人做了这件事"，这恰恰是审计记录最不该说的话。一次共享现在会显示成 "Shared with junyan"。
- Each entry now shows what the action was, who did it, when, and whatever that action has worth adding — the size uploaded, the person shared with, the reason a download was refused — with failures marked. The two permanently-"Unknown" columns are gone rather than left as furniture.
- 每条记录现在显示操作是什么、谁做的、什么时候、以及该操作值得补充的信息——上传的大小、分享给了谁、下载被拒绝的原因——失败的记录会被标出。那两列永远是"Unknown"的内容被删掉了，而不是留作摆设。
- The hash-chain line no longer shows a permission refusal as a finding. `POST /audit-logs/verify-chain` is ADMIN only and recomputes every hash in the table, so restricting it is right — but the page called it for everyone and printed the 403 as red text, so an ordinary user opening their own history was met with what looked like a broken audit log. Not being allowed to run the check now hides the line; a genuine `INVALID` result is called out prominently, because it is the single most important thing this page can say.
- 哈希链那一行不再把权限拒绝当成检查结论来显示。`POST /audit-logs/verify-chain` 仅限 ADMIN 且会重算整张表的哈希，因此限制它是正确的——但页面对所有人都调用了它，并把 403 当作红色错误文本打印出来，于是普通用户打开自己的历史记录时，看到的像是一个坏掉的审计日志。现在无权执行检查会直接隐藏该行；而真正的 `INVALID` 结果会被醒目地标示出来，因为那是这个页面能说出的最重要的一句话。

### Filenames in the audit log / 审计日志中的文件名

- The records page now shows what each file is called instead of `File #<uuid>`. `GET /audit-logs` returns `file_id` and no filename, and the agreed resolution was for the browser to look it up rather than widen the audit response.
- 访问记录页现在显示每个文件的真实名称，而不再是 `File #<uuid>`。`GET /audit-logs` 只返回 `file_id` 而不返回文件名，团队商定的方案是由浏览器自行查询，而不是扩大审计接口的响应。
- Resolved with one list call before any per-file lookups. `GET /files` returns up to 100 summaries at once and covers essentially every file a person has audit entries for, so a user with eight files costs one request rather than eight; only ids that call misses — a file shared to them, most often — are fetched individually, deduplicated and at most four at a time.
- 解析时先做一次列表查询，然后才逐个补齐。`GET /files` 单次最多返回 100 条摘要，基本覆盖了一个用户会有审计记录的全部文件，因此拥有八个文件的用户只需一次请求而不是八次；只有这次调用没覆盖到的 id（最常见的是别人分享给他的文件）才会单独查询，且会去重、并发上限为四。
- Names are resolved after the rows are on screen rather than before. The history is readable without them, and a slow lookup should not hold it back. A file that has been deleted, or whose access was revoked, simply keeps a shortened id: audit entries outlive the files they describe, and what happened to a file is worth showing after the file is gone.
- 文件名在记录渲染之后才解析，而不是之前。没有文件名时历史记录依然可读，一次缓慢的查询不应该拖住它。已被删除、或访问权限已被撤销的文件会保留一个缩短的 id：审计记录的存续时间长于它所描述的文件，而"这个文件发生过什么"在文件消失之后依然值得展示。

### Deleting your own account / 自助注销账户

- Added `POST /auth/delete-account` and a "Delete account" control on the workspace. A forgotten encryption passphrase is unrecoverable by design — the offline recovery workflow cannot recover one, and `POST /keys/me/rewrap` cannot set a new one without the account master key, which is precisely what the forgotten passphrase protects. Starting over is therefore the only remaining path, and it should not require a teammate with database access to perform it by hand.
- 已新增 `POST /auth/delete-account` 接口以及工作台上的"删除账户"入口。遗忘的加密 passphrase 在设计上就不可恢复——离线恢复流程无法恢复它，`POST /keys/me/rewrap` 也无法在没有账户主密钥的情况下设置新的，而账户主密钥恰恰是被这个遗忘的 passphrase 保护的。因此重新开始是唯一剩下的路径，而这件事不应该需要某位有数据库权限的队友手工完成。
- The login password is required again on top of a live session, and the word `DELETE` must be typed. This is the one irreversible action in the application, and a session left open on an unattended machine should not be enough to trigger it. Owned files are soft-deleted with their access grants revoked — a share must not outlive the account that granted it — and the ciphertext is unlinked only after the database commit lands, matching `FileService.delete`: a process that dies midway leaves rows marked DELETED with their objects still present, which the reconcile job can clean, rather than live rows whose ciphertext has already gone.
- 除了有效的会话之外，还需要再次输入登录密码，并键入 `DELETE` 一词。这是本应用中唯一不可逆的操作，一个留在无人看管机器上的会话不应足以触发它。名下文件会被软删除并撤销其访问授权——共享不应比授予它的账户存活得更久——密文则在数据库提交落盘之后才解除链接，与 `FileService.delete` 保持一致：中途崩溃的进程留下的是状态为 DELETED 但对象仍在的数据行（对账任务可以清理），而不是密文已消失的存活数据行。
- The username and email are released so the same person can register again with them. `identifier_exists` does not filter by status, so leaving the original values in place would reserve them forever — and being able to start over is the entire point. The user row itself stays: audit entries reference the user by id, and removing it would take the record of what happened with it. The tombstone address deliberately keeps a real domain, since a reserved TLD registers fine and then breaks serialization wherever the address is read back.
- 用户名与邮箱会被释放，以便本人可以用它们重新注册。`identifier_exists` 不按状态过滤，因此保留原值会让它们被永久占用——而"能够重新开始"正是这件事的全部意义。用户数据行本身保留：审计记录通过 id 引用用户，删除该行会连带抹掉事情发生过的记录。墓碑邮箱有意保留一个真实域名，因为保留 TLD 虽然能注册成功，却会在任何读回该地址的地方导致序列化失败。
- The dialog points at the private key file first. Deleting is the last resort for someone locked out by a forgotten passphrase, not the first thing to try — the `.pem` saved at registration opens the files without the passphrase, and is worth ruling out before destroying anything.
- 对话框会优先引导用户使用私钥文件。对于因遗忘 passphrase 而被锁在门外的用户，删除是最后手段而非首选——注册时保存的 `.pem` 无需 passphrase 即可打开文件，在销毁任何东西之前值得先排除这条路。

### Audit log labels and the download counter / 审计日志标签与下载计数

- Downloads were being recorded all along; the records page just could not see them. It compared `log.action` against the literal `'download'`, while the backend writes `FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED` when a download is served. So the Downloads stat read zero no matter how many files had been downloaded, every event fell through to being counted as a view, and the raw enum name was rendered to the user. Reported as "there is no access or download record" — the records existed and were complete.
- 下载一直都有记录，只是访问记录页看不见它们。页面把 `log.action` 与字面量 `'download'` 比较，而后端在下载被授权时写入的是 `FILE_DOWNLOAD_AUTHORIZED`。因此无论下载过多少文件，"Downloads"统计恒为 0，所有事件都落到"view"分支被计为浏览，而且原始的枚举名被直接渲染给用户。此前被报告为"没有访问记录下载记录"——记录一直存在且完整。
- Actions now carry human labels ("Downloaded", "Shared", "Access revoked") from one shared module, and an action the browser has no label for is titled rather than dropped: the vocabulary grows on the server side, and a record nobody can name is still a record of something that happened.
- 各类操作现在由一个共享模块提供可读标签（"Downloaded"、"Shared"、"Access revoked"），浏览器不认识的操作会转成标题格式显示而非丢弃：这套词表在服务端会继续扩充，而一条叫不出名字的记录仍然是"发生过某件事"的记录。
- A blocked download (`FILE_DOWNLOAD_INTEGRITY_FAILED`) is deliberately not counted as a download. It is a record of an access that did not happen.
- 被拦截的下载（`FILE_DOWNLOAD_INTEGRITY_FAILED`）有意不计入下载数。它记录的是一次并未发生的访问。

### Session lifetime / 会话有效期

- The browser now renews an expiring session instead of failing the next click. The access token lasts 30 minutes and the refresh token 7 days, but the refresh token was discarded at login and never presented — so a tab left open for half an hour answered a bare authorization error to the next action, with nothing on screen saying the session had ended or that signing in again was the fix. A 401 now triggers one refresh and a retry of the request that failed, transparently.
- 浏览器现在会自动续期即将过期的会话，而不是让下一次点击直接失败。Access token 有效期 30 分钟、refresh token 7 天，但 refresh token 在登录时就被丢弃、从未使用过——因此一个开着半小时的标签页会对下一步操作回一句光秃秃的鉴权错误，界面上既不说明会话已结束，也不提示重新登录即可解决。现在遇到 401 会先续期一次并重放失败的请求，整个过程对用户不可见。
- Concurrent 401s share one refresh. The server rotates the refresh token on every call, so three requests failing together and each starting its own refresh would present an already-consumed token twice and turn a recoverable expiry into a forced logout. They now await the same promise.
- 并发的 401 共用同一次续期。服务端每次续期都会轮换 refresh token，因此三个请求同时失败、各自发起续期的话，第二和第三次会提交已被消费的 token，把一次本可恢复的过期变成强制登出。现在它们等待同一个 promise。
- When a session genuinely cannot be renewed, the tokens are cleared and the user is sent to the login page with an explanation and the page they were on, rather than left in a signed-in-looking shell where every request fails. Both tokens are cleared together through one module, so a logout cannot leave a usable refresh credential behind after the access token is gone.
- 当会话确实无法续期时，会清除令牌并把用户送到登录页，附带说明和原本停留的页面，而不是把人留在一个"看起来已登录、但每个请求都失败"的界面里。两个令牌通过同一个模块一起清除，因此登出不会在 access token 已消失后仍留下一个可用的 refresh 凭证。
- The account key's own 15-minute idle relock is unchanged: it is a separate mechanism, it only affects decryption, and it announces itself with the passphrase dialog. The confusing failure being fixed here was always the session, not the key.
- 账户密钥自身的 15 分钟空闲重锁保持不变：那是另一套机制，只影响解密，并且会通过 passphrase 对话框明确告知用户。这里修复的那个令人困惑的失败自始至终都出在会话上，而不是密钥上。

### Recovering with the private key file / 使用私钥文件恢复

- The `.pem` file the registration page hands out can now be used. It was exported once, described as the thing to keep safe, and then read by nothing: every unlock path derived the key from the passphrase, so a forgotten passphrase meant every uploaded file was unreadable even while the user held the key that opens them. The unlock dialog now offers "Forgotten your passphrase?", which takes the key file, verifies it against the account's registered public key, and unlocks the session without a passphrase.
- 注册页发放的 `.pem` 文件现在可以用了。它只导出一次、被郑重告知要妥善保管，然后就再也没有任何代码读取它：所有解锁路径都从 passphrase 派生密钥，因此一旦忘记 passphrase，即使用户手里正握着能打开文件的那把钥匙，已上传的文件也全都读不出来。解锁对话框现在提供「忘记 passphrase？」入口，接收该密钥文件，与账户已注册的公钥校验后，无需 passphrase 即可解锁本次会话。
- The key file is verified before it is trusted, by wrapping a random probe to the account's public key and unwrapping it with the candidate private key. `importKey` accepts any well-formed PKCS#8 RSA key, so without this check a key from a different account would import cleanly and then fail several steps later as an unwrap error on a file — which reads as "this file is damaged" rather than "this is the wrong key".
- 密钥文件在被信任之前会先校验：用账户公钥包装一段随机探针，再用待验证的私钥解开。`importKey` 会接受任何格式正确的 PKCS#8 RSA 密钥，因此若没有这道检查，来自其他账户的密钥会顺利导入，然后在若干步之后以"文件解包失败"的形式报错——读起来像是"这个文件损坏了"，而不是"你用错了密钥"。
- The file is read in the page and never uploaded, and the imported key is non-extractable and decrypt-only, matching what the passphrase path already guarantees. A PKCS#1 file (`BEGIN RSA PRIVATE KEY`, which `openssl genrsa` still emits by default) is now named as such instead of failing inside `importKey` with a bare DataError.
- 该文件在页面内读取、绝不上传；导入后的密钥不可导出且仅可用于解密，与 passphrase 路径已有的保证一致。PKCS#1 格式的文件（`BEGIN RSA PRIVATE KEY`，`openssl genrsa` 至今仍默认输出这种格式）现在会被明确指出格式不对，而不是在 `importKey` 内部抛出一个光秃秃的 DataError。
- The registration page now says what the file is and why it matters: that it is the account's RSA private key, that it is the only way back in if the passphrase is forgotten, that a `.pem` has no associated application on most systems and any text editor opens it, and that because it needs no passphrase the file itself is the secret and does not belong in the Downloads folder. The previous wording said only "save it somewhere safe", which left users unable to tell what they had been given.
